Chapter 3101: The Only Option

“Your idea might be correct, but it is too idealistic and impossible to realize.

The voice said casually, “The Third World War caused great pain to our civilization. Even a hundred years later, the wounds will only be scabs. There are still pus and sores inside that haven’t been fully healed yet. Maybe they will never be.

“Human beings were barely united under the threat of extinction. How could they turn into noble, pure, and selfless people with a few decades of ‘death education’ and give the hope of survival to the children of their enemies?

“On the other hand, the fat-headed, high and mighty nobles will only ask the civilians to die for their interests in the name of justice. Is it possible to do the opposite?

“The rich and powerful believed that all the immigration starships and the sea of stars were developed by them. There was no way that they would give the living space of the immigration starships to the ‘dirty’ civilians.

“You said that people were qualified to escape according to their age, but the social order at that time was far less stable than it was a hundred years ago. The Earth Council’s control was relatively weak, and it was impossible to conduct a large-scale census. Could it be that the age was determined by appearance?

“Even the bone age test has a lot of room for manipulation. The rich and powerful can always come up with all kinds of methods. Between an 18-year-old child of a noble family and a 16-year-old child of a commoner, who do you think is more likely to board an immigration starship?

“Besides, even if everything is pushed forward according to the most idealized expectations and a batch of ignorant children and teenagers are selected to board the immigration starships as the new hope of our civilization, do you think that they will be able to create a new era of ‘justice, kindness, and brightness’? “You’ve underestimated the cruelty hidden in the genes of us, or rather, all the carbon-based intelligent creatures.

“In the history of our literature, there was once a book called ‘Lord of Flies’, which told the story of a group of six to twelve-year-olds who were stranded on an island because of a plane crash. At first, they got along harmoniously, but soon, the extreme evil of human nature broke out. They killed each other and completely destroyed each other. There was even no need for such a story. Just by looking at the ignorant child who could smile and tear apart the wings of a dragonfly and roast a living ant with a magnifying glass, one could tell how terrifying natural evil was.

“If your idea comes true, you will only turn the migrating starships into bloody lone islands that kill each other and raise ‘fly kings’ that hover in the sea of stars.

Li Yao was not convinced. He was about to argue when he continued, “Yes. I know what you are trying to say. You are trying to say that there are a lot of women and fully-automatic self-disciplined education robots on the immigration starship, which are enough to take care of their offspring.

“However, I have to tell you that a lot of studies and experiments were conducted by the sociologists and psychologists at that time. In such an enclosed and unknown environment, the performance of the women was not much better than that of the children. Stimulated by the estrogen and the hormones, they even turned into the more terrifying females.

“As for the so-called ‘automatic self-discipline education robot’, which is similar to artificial intelligence, if we impose heavy restraints on it so that it absolutely cannot harm the children and can only impose ‘punishment’ that is neither painful nor ticklish, then the children will soon see through its bottom line, and they will ignore its earnest teachings. If we allow the artificial intelligence to do anything to the children, there is no way to prevent it from turning the children into its slaves, allowing the artificial intelligence to take over the nest and become the core of our civilization.

“All in all, we’ve considered your ideas very carefully and thoroughly. The outcome of our countless deductions is still not feasible. Pondering for a moment, Li Yao said, “Then, what about drawing lots? Everything depends on luck and is completely random. Didn’t you say that ‘luck’ is the most critical factor in the birth and rise of a civilization? Let the lucky ones continue the ‘fortune’ of the civilization!”

“You still don’t understand.” His voice was deep and heavy as he said, “It is impossible for any selection method that is made public in advance, from the bottom to the top, to work, because it is absolutely impossible for the losers to calmly accept their own doom. Even if a saint with a kind heart, a high moral quality, and a great personality can resolutely sacrifice himself, what about his child? What about his child’s child? What about his daughter who has learned to speak? What about his infant son? Can he personally extinguish the hope of his children’s survival?”

“There are always shady deals, and there are always frauds. There are always people who escape the selection in various ways and board the ‘vip’ channel leading to the immigration starships. The war, radiation, and plague over the past hundred years have completely divided the class of the nobles and the civilians. However ‘fair, open, and fair’ the nobles claim to be, it is impossible for them to win the trust of the civilians. As a result, the day after the ‘Escape List’ is announced, the entire Earth will be caught in endless riots.

“Tester, tell me, in the long history of the new civilization of mankind, have none of the planets experienced natural disasters such as meteoroids and solar storms? Are all the planets able to retreat calmly and orderly when faced with natural disasters?

Li Yao was rendered speechless.

The voice was not wrong at all. The human civilization of the Pangu Universe had experienced a similar situation, too, which was the ‘Flying Star Sector’ when Li Yao had just left his hometown to explore the sea of stars.

The natives of the Flying Star Sector had been attacked by meteoroids five thousand years ago, too. As a result, not long after the meteoroids hit them, the Flying Star Sector was divided into homers and spacers. They attacked each other and even killed each other. The fracture that was caused five thousand years later still changed the appearance of the Flying Star Sector profoundly, to the point that human beings were extremely fragile. The extraterrestrial devils and the Immortal Cultivators were quite popular among the dark

stars.

It must be noted that, even five thousand years ago, the Flying Star Sector boasted a very mature spiritual energy industrial system and abundant crystal energy. There were even a lot of starships left by the Star Ocean Imperium. The technology to build such starships could’ve extended to the Pangu civilization, which meant that the space residents at that time boasted a high ability to cruise in the sea of stars. Their situation was much better than that of the ‘poor’ ancient Earthlings.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

If space residents could not avoid the chaos before the apocalypse, how could the ancient earthlings be spared?

Therefore, the voice was right. Any public selection that was publicized in advance would not work. Instead, it would awaken the devil lurking in everyone’s heart. “Then—”

Li Yao felt that his voice was somewhat hoarse. “What should I do?”

“The bottom-up selection is never going to work. Only the top-down selection is likely to work.

The voice said, “That is to say, there is no ‘open, fair, and just’ selection at all. Instead, a highly centralized, iron-blooded man chooses the passengers who meet his standards according to his will and flees from Earth without most people knowing.

“What?”

Li Yao’s soul suddenly constricted. “How is that possible?”

“Why is it impossible?”

The voice said, “The technologies such as the refinement of starships, the sailing of the sea of stars, and the hibernation of the human body are all in the hands of the experts and scholars. Most of the resources are provided by the rich and powerful. Most of the citizens do not have the opportunity or the qualifications to know the progress of the entire project, nor do they know when the immigration starship will be completed and when it will set off. Therefore, as long as some of the people related to the ‘space migration project’ can board the starship, it will be fine.

“It is very fortunate that the experts, scholars, magnates, engineers, workers, and even soldiers who work day and night around the ‘Star Ocean Immigration Project’ are the smartest and strongest individuals of our civilization. They are most likely to carry on the civilization and flourish in the depths of the sea of stars.

“You can berate us for our coldness, mock us for our shamelessness, and feel sorry for the fate of the innocent people, but we have no other options. This is the only solution.

While he was talking, the thousands of options presented to Li Yao were either shattered or turned into lifeless greyness. There was only one option that was still emitting lackluster brilliance.

“Well—”

Li Yao gritted his teeth. His soul stirred anxiously and spread out ripples of uncertainty.

He was indeed not Jin Tuyi. He was even worse than Boss Bai and Li Jialing.

If it were Jin Tuyi—no, there was no need for Jin Tuyi-as long as it was Boss Bai, Li Jialing, Long Yangjun, and Li Linghai, they would not hesitate to choose the only ‘right answer’.

If Lu Qingchen and the mental devil had made it this far, they wouldn’t have made any mistakes. They would’ve chosen to hide the truth from the top and run away with a small group of the rich and powerful. But Li Yao was too frustrated to make a decision when faced with the ‘only choice’.

The voice was silent, waiting patiently for Li Yao’s choice.

What’s more, when Li Yao was caught in a dilemma when he entered the valley, he said, “Wait.”

Li Yao was dazed. “What’s wrong?”

“One of the testers failed.”

The voice said calmly, “We must get rid of him first.”

Before Li Yao realized what was going on, the left side of his vision turned translucent, and he saw ‘Obliteration’, the ultimate expert of the Pangu Clan who was floating not far away from him.

The commander of the remaining soldiers of the Pangu Clan at the end of the primeval war was one of the top experts in the Pangu Universe. His wisdom, determination, and combat ability were among the best. Even if Li Yao activated the full combat ability of the Dividing Soul Stage, he might not have been able to compete with him.

But right now, his miserable appearance could only make Li Yao think of one thing—”I am meat on a chopping block!”

Chapter 3102: Myriad Treasures Sea

He saw that the Sect of Destruction was struggling painfully in the bright gold ’embryo’ and trying to break through the barrier, but to no avail.

At first, veins and nerves as thick as earthworms that looked like boas and dragons appeared on his body. Then, pink blood leaked out from the wrinkles on his skin. The blood quickly expanded into foam and covered him. At first glance, he looked like a statue made of foam.

The bubbles were larger and larger, emitting glittering colors. With cracking noises, glamorous, colorful butterflies flew out of the bubbles.

It was like the collapse of the genes of the Sect of Immortals. The cells all over his body turned into independent entities that were out of his control.

Li Yao recalled the tyrannosaur that he had seen in the periphery of the ancient relics.

After the tyrannosaur collapsed and died, a lot of butterflies burst out of its body, too.

The boundary of life lost its significance at this moment. Even the concept of ‘life’ itself needed to be redefined.

Li Yao did not know whether what he saw was an illusion or reality. He could not help but shudder.

Gurgle, gurgle, gurgle… Deep swallowing sounds came out of the Cultivator’s throat. His brainwaves turned fuzzy like a gradually dispersing swirl. He tried to extend his arms, only to break them one after another. Before his arms hit the ground, they had already turned into countless bubbles and butterflies.

Very soon, he shrank, dimmed, and evaporated like water that had been splashed on a hot ground on a summer afternoon.

“It’s a pity that the tester performed quite well in the beginning. He realized that the form of human beings was the perfect direction for the evolution of carbon-based intelligent life. Before the civilization advanced into the third world war, he made only one mistake. He was among the best testers.

The voice said casually, “It’s a pity that he made an unforgivable mistake when he was trying to save our civilization in the face of the apocalypse. He was planning to develop the human brain, stimulate the brainwaves, and condense the consciousness of the bees with the strategy of ‘super collectivism’. He planned to turn our civilization into a giant swarm of insects so that most people would be willing to sacrifice themselves in order to save the lives of the queen bees, the soldiers, and the workers.

“From the very beginning, he devoted too many resources to the development of the human brain, the enhancement of brainwaves, and the transmission of the consciousness of the bees. Very soon, he wasted three precious opportunities and failed.

“Consciousness of the bees…”

Li Yao was aware that the Pangu Clan had a strong propensity for the civilization of the bees. Although every individual was essentially independent, the large-scale telepathic thoughts and the exchange of information had made them much more dependent and worshipful than the Nuwa Clan and the human beings. After the loss of their emotions and free will, they had become tiny screws on the machinery of the enormous civilization. The Exo was the supreme commander of the remaining soldiers of the Pangu Clan. The way he rescued the civilization had been marked by the Pangu Clan, too. It was not unusual that he thought of turning the entire Earth into a giant honeycomb.

Li Yao just didn’t understand. “Is the consciousness of the bees not allowed?”

“Yes, absolutely not.”

From the moment the voice appeared in Li Yao’s mind, it had been very calm and dull. But at this moment, it revealed intense disgust and even… fear. “The Primeval Civilization is a race that emphasizes free will. It is very appropriate to say that we are somewhat ‘selfish’. Even if every one of us decides to sacrifice for the collective, it should be done with 100% of our willingness. We cannot allow any ‘Queen Bee’, ‘Ant Queen’, or ‘Heart of Swarm’ to overstep their boundaries and make decisions for us. Can you understand the difference?

“All in all, evil existences such as the consciousness of the bees and the Insect Clan are the greatest enemies of the Primeval Civilization. Our successors can take any form. It doesn’t matter if they don’t have a body of flesh and blood, but it doesn’t matter if they upload their consciousness to the network or the supercomputing cloud. However, it is absolutely-absolutely not the consciousness of the bees or the insect clan. Absolutely not!

The intensity of the voice far exceeded Li Yao’s imagination. Li Yao also perceived the most important information from the soundwaves. “The consciousness of the bees is your biggest enemy… So, the ‘flood’ is a kind of consciousness of the bees, a large swarm of insects?”

There was a long silence.

“Of course, it’s not that simple.”

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

The voice said, “You don’t need to know now.

“Then, what about the civilization represented by the Cultivators?”.

Li Yao continued asking, “Will the Pangu Civilization be completely destroyed?”

“It depends on you, or rather, ‘you’.

The voice said, “Look, a new batch of testers are ready for the challenge. They seem to be your kind.”

In Li Yao’s vision, more than ten golden fetuses appeared one after another in the place where the Sect of Doom turned into foam butterflies. Countless humanoids were crouching inside them like babies.

Li Yao’s soul immediately shrank to the minimum.

Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, Yan Liren, Li Jialing, Li Linghai… The human experts had finally broken through all the obstacles and marched into the black stargate for the ultimate test!

Li Yao felt that his mouth was dry, and his heart was almost jumping out of his chest.

“What if…”

He stammered, “They’ve made more than three mistakes in the test. Will they make the same mistakes as the Cultivators?”

“Of course.”

The voice said, “Their corpses will raise the height of the tower by one millimeter, and the Pangu Civilization and your civilization will be the 101st and the 102nd civilization to fall here.

Unless I pass the ultimate test and receive the legacy of the primeval era, will I be able to stop their test?

Li Yao asked, “Is that so?”

“Yes.”

The voice said, “Are you ready to push forward our civilization?”

Li Yao was silent for a long, long time.

Then, his soul flowed toward the only option and touched the bright gold one.

As the bright gold options turned into streaks of brightness, all the options and deductions that followed were accelerated. Li Yao did not even need to pick any of them. Everything was rushing forward naturally.

No. This was not a test or a game at all. It was something like an animation. The voice was telling Li Yao the real history of Earth—the last events of the ancient Earth in the primeval era.

“You made the right choice, the only choice.

The voice said, “In the real history of Earth, the supervisor of the ‘pushing into the universe’ project was ‘Wan Zanghai’, the fifteenth chairman of the Earth Council. He was an iron-blooded man who was almost perfect.

“Before he embarked on the path of ‘Savior’ and ‘Devil’, he was active in the post-Wasteland Era as the ‘Ultimate Executive of the Water Purification Industry’.

“After the three wars, the entire Earth was polluted. Clean drinking water became a hard currency. The water purification technology, which was efficient, reliable, and low-energy, was also something that could determine life and death. At first, Wan Zanghai developed a lot of water purification technologies and equipment as an outstanding inventor. When he made his first bucket of gold, he displayed an amazing business acumen and united all the water purification companies into the second-to-none force on Earth at that time. What was even more remarkable was that he also had a noble personality and a holy heart. He did not monopolize the water purification technology to make excessive profits. Instead, he gave a lot of the technology and precious water purification technology at a low price to those who needed it for free. As a result, he established a good image and a distinguished reputation among the underground people, the Radiators, and the modified people. He was regarded as a ‘saint’ by almost everyone regardless of his faction or position-at least at that time.

“Therefore, nobody had any objection to Wan Zanghai becoming the Chairman of the Earth Council. Most people even believed that only ‘saints’ such as Wan Zanghai could supervise the grand plan to march into the universe.

“However, all this is just a disguise. The so-called holiness and kindness are just preparations for a deeper and darker purpose. Wan Zanghai is not a saint at all. He has been in cahoots with the nobles and the rich for a long time. He is even a spokesperson co-produced by the nobles and the rich. He is an ‘artificial saint’. His purpose is to win the trust of the ordinary people with his so-called generosity and selflessness so that they will believe that he is capable of supervising the plan to march into the universe. That way, he will know his place and not cause any trouble.

“Think about it. Without the support of the rich and powerful, how could Wanzang Hai squander the precious clean water resources without being hindered by any interest groups?

“In short, the rich and powerful have long been prepared to abandon the majority of the public and flee on their own. The Thousand Hide Sea is just a puppet that they carefully packaged for this purpose.

“However, the nobles and magnates who claimed that they were in control of everything underestimated the ambitions of Wan Zanghai.

“The rich and powerful make use of him, and so does he.

“At that time, an organization named ‘Deva Society’ rose up quietly. Its believers were mostly radiationers, modified human beings, and mutants who were influenced by spiritual energy.

“They advocate the ‘genetic superiority’ theory and believe that only those who are most sensitive to crystals and spiritual energy are qualified to survive with the best genes. “No matter whether it is money, status, or power, only genes are most important—this is the slogan of ‘Devaism’.

“I believe you must’ve guessed that the man behind the curtain of the Deva Society is Wan Zanghai. On one hand, he is using his power in the Earth Council to cover the activities of the Deva Society, and on the other hand, he is using the Deva Society to recruit a lot of superhumans and spiritual energy-sensitive Cultivators to serve as his personal soldiers. He is hiding his claws and wings in secret.

Chapter 3103: Retribution

Along with the description of the voice, a ‘saint’ in a white uniform appeared in Li Yao’s mind. He had a kind smile and a pure aura around him. Looking at his pure and innocent eyes and compassionate expression, Li Yao felt that he was the kind of person who would cut off his own flesh to help the desperate victims.

However, black devils were hiding behind the wings that were as white as angels.

He assassinated the upright politicians and soldiers who were truly determined, participated in the power struggles of the rich and powerful, imprisoned and corrupted the reporters who dared to speak the truth, and coerced the well-respected and renowned scholars into lying openly about their plans to march into the universe by threatening them with various methods, including kidnapping their family members. He also conducted unpardonable, inhuman experiments on human beings and condensed countless powerful training techniques with the blood of countless innocent people into countless loyal monster warriors… ‘Wan Zanghai’, the Chairman of the Earth Council, was simply a replica of Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, Wu Yingqi, and the rest of them. No. He was the ‘ancestor’ of the schemers and schemers. The meticulousness and the brutality of his methods dumbfounded Li Yao.

“What-what have I done exactly?”

Li Yao regretted his choice the moment he made it.

Although everything had happened billions of years ago and was an unchangeable history, Li Yao still felt that he was the one who had released the ferocious beast named ‘Thousand Treasures Sea’ and allowed it to trample on his mind and soul and corrupt his belief.

Then again, Wan Canghai was the same as Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, Wu Yingqi, and the other schemers and schemers. Other than his evil nature, he also boasted tenacity and leadership.

With his strategical planning and tireless endeavors, the plan to march into the universe was carried out at a lightning fast speed. Five hundred immigration starships, each of which could accommodate a million hibernators and allow fifty thousand of them to live and study in a clear state, were finally produced.

The hibernation technology and the space navigation technology were getting more and more mature, too. There were a lot of follow-up technologies that could be explored and developed after they escaped from Earth.

Everything was ready except for one thing.

At this moment, Wan Zanghai told a big lie to all the citizens.

There were only five hundred immigration starships, which could only carry five hundred million people. However, the Thousand Treasures Sea had bribed and threatened all the experts and participants of the project. It had even faked 2,500 non-existent immigration starships with shabby shells and sets, bringing the total number of the immigration fleet to three thousand.

Three thousand starships were enough to carry everyone to safety. When the news came out of ‘Saint’ Wan Canghai’s mouth, the entire Earth was drowned in a sea of joy.

As for Wan Canghai, he was promoted from a ‘saint’ to a ‘savior’. Everybody believed that he could save the civilization and everyone.

Naturally, it was a complicated and protracted task to move everyone to the starship. The authorities of the Earth Council under the leadership of Wan Canghai asked all the citizens to be patient and wait patiently at home. As long as social order was maintained, it would be their turn to board the starship very soon.

“All the earthlings are inseparable. I will never abandon anybody, just like I will never abandon my heart!

Wan Zanghai’s words were powerful and resonating

He had fooled everyone into trusting him with such a sordid trick.

Most of the civilians had been drained of their last drop of sweat after a hundred years of hard work. They were waiting patiently in the slums with hope.

In the meantime, a lot of ‘space training camps’ had been established in the Thousand Treasures Sea, which claimed that they would provide adaptive training for the ordinary people and pull them away one by one. Such methods further divided the public and calmed them down. Everybody believed that ‘I will be next’.

In the meantime, the nobles, the rich, the soldiers, the elites, and the demons boarded five hundred real immigration starships quietly and took away most of the valuable facilities and resources on Earth.

Of course, a lot of soul-stirring, twisted, and bizarre stories happened during the process. Some righteous journalists and scientists who had found their conscience tried to tell the truth, only to be captured and murdered by the subordinates of Wan Zanghai.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Finally,

When the five hundred real immigration starships spurted crimson exhaust flames into the sky and tore apart the atmosphere, only to leave the intense black smoke for Earth, the ordinary people finally discovered the scheme of the rich and the powerful.

But it was too late. Before Wan Canghai left, he had destroyed all the surface-to-air missiles in the military bases. The immigration starships were so fast that they soon got rid of the gravity of Earth. No matter how desperate and desperate the people on the ground were, they could not affect the refugees in the sea of stars at all.

“T-this!”

Although Li Yao could not feel his real body, he still found it hard to breathe.

He knew that the ‘no other choice’ was sordid and cruel, but he did not know that it was so sordid and cruel.

There was no picture of the fury, desperation, and sorrow of the people who stayed on Earth in his brain, but he could totally imagine that the Earth at this moment was a hundred times more dreadful and miserable than hell.

The picture inside the immigration starship, the nobles, magnates, and elites of all walks of life were dancing and celebrating with champagne in their hands, while they were singing and dancing—was still vivid in Li Yao’s mind. He couldn’t be more ashamed.

Then, Li Yao saw an unforgettable scene.

The migration fleet did not leave the solar system directly. Instead, they stayed outside of the orbit of Jupiter and tried to build a ‘gravity slingshot’ with the enormous gravity of the planet. They also opened enormous golden membranes toward the sun, which looked like an overwhelming sail.

They were waiting for the destruction of Earth.

Here it comes. The meteoroid shower.

Judging from the scale of the universe, the meteoroid showers were as insignificant as dust. Even the naked eye could not distinguish them.

It was not until the meteoroid shower carrying tremendous crystals and spiritual energy crashed into the sun and swallowed Mercury, Venus, Earth, and Mars that Li Yao had to admit that it was a real catastrophe.

The Earth had been destroyed.

The blue planet, which was the origin of the ancient human beings and one of the earliest sources of life in the sea of the universe, turned dry, black, and deformed under the coverage of the destructive flames, like a rotten apple.

Li Yao felt that his soul was going to be burnt to ash like the core of a burning apple.

The picture. The picture that he would never forget even if he was burnt to ashes. He had seen it before. He had seen it somewhere before. Yes. It was the ‘Earth in the Face’ in the uncanny dream!

At this moment, Li Yao could not tell which was the real Earth or the ‘original Earth’, and which was the illusionary Earth or the ‘derived Earth’.

The destructive solar storm swept over everything, but it also provided tremendous impetus for the migration fleet. The golden sails of the immigration fleet, having absorbed enough energy from the sun and accelerated by the gravity slingshots, marched out of the solar system like an arrow released from a bow, leaving their destroyed homes and countless innocent souls behind.

“Goodbye, Earth.”

“Here we come, the universe!”

Li Yao saw that, on many immigration starships, the fat heads of the rich and powerful were smoking cigars, sipping red wine, laughing, patting their bellies, and declaring their ambitions.

Then, he saw Wan Zanghai standing on the bridge of a starship, with most of his face hidden in darkness, looking lonely and depressed What happened next was beyond Li Yao’s wildest dreams.

Seeing that the five hundred immigration starships had escaped the range of the solar storm and were even accelerated by the solar storm and the gravity slingshot, the speed of the five hundred immigration starships was about to soar to the maximum, and they entered the stable and safe cruising state.

Most of the starships, more than four hundred of them, had exploded!

The scale of the explosion was not large enough to damage the overall structure and propulsion system of the starship, but it was enough to destroy all the life-support systems inside the starship and burn out the oxygen so that the passengers, be they hibernating or celebrating, would be killed instantly.

The picture switched to the inside of the starships that had exploded. The ‘escape party’ that had been singing and dancing a moment ago was now a burning hell. The rich and powerful who had survived the disaster were either riddled with holes, with broken limbs flying everywhere, turned into dancing candles by the unstoppable flames, or held their throats so hard that the corners of their mouths were almost torn apart, only to find that they could not breathe any precious oxygen.

Very soon, the artificial gravity field was gone. The bodies of the nobles and the rich were floating in the cold and silent space. They did not know what had happened even until their death. Their bulging eyes were full of confusion and fear, as if their starship had been assaulted by the ghosts from Earth. Of course, some of the rich and powerful had already considered the possibility of being hit by meteoroids or other natural disasters during their voyages. They had specially installed ‘safety rooms’ for their starships and put on their spacesuits in time.

However, it was useless.

When the flames and the fog gradually dispersed, what appeared in front of them were the hideous-looking Radiators, the mutants, and the sharpest blades made of minerals from Earth.

Chapter 3104: A Little Revenge

The next picture in Li Yao’s field of vision was back to the starships in the Thousand Treasures Sea. The Chairman of the Earth Council, the saviors and the destroyers of the earth civilization, was still lurking in the darkness, watching the brutal scenes of slaughter on dozens of starships on the computer screens while he poured himself a glass of wine. He held the glass in midair and did not move for a long time, before he slowly poured the wine that was as red as blood on the ground.

Right now, Li Yao could see that the destruction of the other starships and the slaughter of the passengers were all part of Wan Zanghai’s scheme.

“Wan Zanghai is so brutal!”

Li Yao found it hard to believe. “What—what is he trying to do?”

“Isn’t it obvious?”

The voice said, “According to the era of Earth, even after the third world war, the super battles, and the meteoroid attacks, the migration fleet fled from Earth in the ’22nd century’. “You have detailed data on the technological level and the resource reserves of the twenty-first century. Do you really think that, in the twenty-second century, it is possible to transport 500 million people to the unknown depths of the sea of stars thousands of lightyears away in search of hope?

“It’s—it’s impossible. It’s one thing to transport 500 million people out of the solar system, but it’s another thing to pass through the vacuum of the universe, which is desolate, barren, and dead, through radiation, meteoroids, and space turbulences, and travel in the darkness for tens of thousands of years. The difficulty of the latter is hundreds of times higher!

“Even though 99% of the people are in the hibernation state and barely consume any energy, it has become an astronomical figure after tens of thousands of years of accumulation. The hibernation drugs have to be replaced every once in a while, and the consumption of high-nutrition liquids can’t be stopped for even one day. Also, the daily wear and tear of the starships can’t be stopped for more than twenty lightyears without backup food, fuel, and components, let alone the source of the meteoroid shower.

“In fact, according to the most optimistic estimation of the secret research team of the Thousand Treasures Sea, in order to cruise in the sea of stars at 1% the speed of light for more than ten thousand years and ensure that 90% of the hibernating passengers can be revived in a healthy and rational state, an immigration starship will need to carry at least ten times the amount of spare fuel, resources, and maintenance parts. In other words, five hundred starships will need to carry at least five thousand supplies, or fifty starships will need to carry five hundred supplies, or the capacity of each starship should be reduced by ten times, not a million people but a hundred thousand.

“Only in such a way can we possibly find a glimmer that can save our civilization in the deepest part of the dark universe. Do you… understand?”

“You mean—”

Li Yao’s soul waves were almost frozen. His voice was even colder than the wind in the deepest level of hell. “Since the very beginning, the rich and powerful have been deceiving all the citizens, and the Hidden Sea has been deceiving all the rich and powerful. Of the three billion people on Earth, 500 million will not be saved, but 90% will be saved.

“Of the three billion people, only fifty million can be saved!”

“Yes.”

His voice remained unchanged as he said, “However, such a conclusion is too pessimistic. It might even lead to the suspension of the entire plan to march into the universe, because there are far more than fifty million nobles, magnates, their families, and the elite scientists and engineers who built the starship.

“The rich and powerful escort the projects, the rich and powerful provide funding for the projects, and the scientists and engineers solve the practical problems of the escape plan. It’s impossible to stop them from boarding the starships. If Wan Zanghai had told them that only 50 million people could leave, I’m afraid that all the rich and powerful would’ve fought each other to the death or even hugged each other’s thighs. They would rather perish together than see a lucky guy leave the Solar System openly. This is the evil of human nature.

“Therefore, Wan Canghai only coerced and coerced the elite scientists and the engineers at the behest of the rich and powerful. Even if he slightly changed the content of his threat, would it matter?

“Understood. Everybody has been fooled by Wan Canghai.”

Li Yao smiled bitterly. “The rich and powerful thought that Wan Zanghai was just their puppet and their lackey and that they were the protagonists of the escape plan. They didn’t know that, in Wan Zanghai’s eyes, their starships were nothing but ‘transfusion bags’, ‘tool bags’, and ‘resource storage boxes’. When the birds are gone, the bows are hidden; when the rabbits are killed, the dogs are cooked. When the migration fleet fled from the solar system, Wan Zanghai was supposed to be exploited and kicked away by the rich and powerful. But he made the first move and ‘cook’ the rich and powerful all at once. What a smart plan! What a smart move!” “The rich and powerful are all parasites bred by the old system. They can only exploit the weaker people with the rules, but they do not have the capability to face the vast and unknown sea of stars and make their own rules. They do not have the courage to explore, explore, and conquer, either. Therefore, it is impossible for them to be the real leaders and complete the sublimation from the ‘Earth civilization’ to the ‘Primeval civilization’. Only the iron-willed experts such as Wan Zanghai can do that.

The voice continued, “Besides, it was for the revenge of the billions of compatriots who died miserably on Earth. It must be noted that most of the soldiers in the Thousand Treasures Sea were radiationers, modified people, and superhumans. They were the descendants of the civilians who used to live miserable lives on the wasteland. Many of their family members had been abandoned on Earth and burnt to ashes by the solar storm. They died without a grave. “Right now, there is fury flowing in the pores of the ghosts who are surrounded by their family. If they don’t raise their weapons at the culprits and let them have a taste of ‘blood for blood’, what are they waiting for? “Revenge?”

Li Yao couldn’t help but laugh. “Wasn’t it all done by Wan Zanghai? Maybe he was not the real schemer, but he was the most loyal lackey for the nobles and the rich. Many sordid plans were carried out by him, and he was shameless enough to talk about ‘revenge?

“Only a hundred times more despicable and shameless can be dealt with. The purpose of the Thousand Treasures Sea is to cleanse the civilization and create a completely different era for our civilization. He has no other options except to walk on this dirty and rugged path.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

The voice said, “If he doesn’t serve as a puppet or a lackey for the rich and powerful, he won’t be able to become the Chairman of the Earth Council at all. And after he becomes the Chairman, if he shows the slightest bit of mercy and doubts the plans of the rich and powerful, then he will step down from the position at any moment. He might even be directly killed and replaced with a second puppet. He will still have to carry out the will of the rich and powerful. Besides demonstrating his integrity, what benefits can he get?

“Faced with reality and analyzed calmly, of the three billion Earthlings at that time, at least 2.95 billion were doomed and could not be saved. As an excellent commander-in-chief, he should not waste his time thinking about how to save the people who were destined to die, but how to make their sacrifices more valuable.

“Wan Zanghai was an excellent commander-in-chief and the best leader that a civilization could meet in a crisis. He did not disappoint his billions of compatriots and made the best use of their deaths.

“The nobles and magnates who were too fat to adapt to the new era were all swept away. The survivors were all iron-blood warriors who were strong, determined, and possessed all kinds of special abilities. After countless rounds of screening and elimination, they all had the best genes and were naturally sensitive to spiritual energy. They were out-and-out supermen.

“In the meantime, after eliminating 90% of the users of the inferior genes who were not qualified to participate in the great march, the remaining 10% of the survivors would have ten times the resources. Even though a starship was worn and broken during the long journey, nine components of the starship could be replaced, which significantly improved the odds of finding new hope. “The most important thing is that nobody is allowed to ride on Wan Canghai’s neck, talk to him imposingly, and come up with bad ideas. Some of them even stab him in the back and drag him down. Wan Canghai’s will can be carried out to every nerve ending of the entire migration fleet. It is of paramount importance for the people who have just experienced the destruction of their mother planet and are living in anxiety in the dark sea of stars.

Li Yao truly didn’t know how to comment on the actions of the Myriad Treasury Sea.

It was just like how he found it hard to judge Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, Wu Yingqi, and the rest of them based on their actions.

“Of course, other than ‘purification’ and ‘improvement’ for the civilization as a whole, the ‘Slaughter of the Outer Solar System’ has another small, insignificant purpose. It is the revenge of Wan Zanghai himself, isn’t it?

The voice said, “This is a story recorded in Wan Canghai’s will a long, long time later about his birth.

“Before the suicide note was released, everybody thought that Wan Zanghai was born in a rich family in an underground shelter. His parents were elegant and elegant people of the high society. No matter how polluted and dirty the ground was, they were able to maintain a decent life in the underground and even let him receive a good education. Unfortunately, his parents died in an explosion in the shelter, and his family declined.

“It was because of his identity as a descendant of the high society that he entered the circle of the rich and powerful and became their spokesperson.

“However, in the will, Wan Zanghai admitted frankly that he was not a member of the ‘high society’ at all, or rather, he was not the real ‘Wan Zanghai’, but a radiationer who grew up in the heavily polluted wasteland, grew up in the smoke, rust, and sand, and even had his own mutation.

“His parents were not elegant, gentle, or decent people. On the contrary, they were weeds, ants, and cockroaches. They were the ‘pariahs’ who struggled to survive in the wasteland and died miserably in the mouths of the radiation disease and the mutant beasts.

“He was involved in the big explosion of the underground shelter by accident. He met a lot of people from the high society who had escaped from the shelter. He killed a young man named ‘Wan Zanghai’ who looked similar to himself and assumed the identity of the young man. That was how he got the ticket to the high society.

“Therefore, the massacre in the periphery of the solar system is his revenge for his parents, for the wasteland where he grew up, and for the ants, cockroaches, weeds, and ‘pariahs’ who are suffering from radiation, virus, tumor, and animal bites in hell, struggling in desperation, and being silently obliterated, right?

Chapter 3105: Ghost Fleet

“From… the ‘criminals’ living on their own in the desert to the supreme leader of the civilization on Earth, the first commander-in-chief to march into the universe!

Li Yao was greatly moved. “Such an incredible life?”

His heart was filled with mixed feelings. He was deeply shocked by Wan Zanghai’s indescribable path to fame.

Naturally, it was easy to despise and criticize other people’s shamelessness. Li Yao had once righteously berated many schemers and ambitious schemers similar to those in the Myriad Hidden Sea.

But what was more important than the criticism was to come up with a solution to continue the hope of the civilization.

Li Yao had no time to consider whether or not the Myriad Treasures Sea was good or bad, nor did he have time to polish the dirt and dust that had fallen on his Dao heart.

It was because, following the demise of the rich and powerful, the newly-born fleet of immigrants had rushed out of the solar system and was sailing to an unknown place.

The civilization of Earth had ushered in a brand-new page. It had officially sublimated or ‘transformed’ into the Primeval Civilization. All the earthlings had been destroyed by the solar storm. Right now, after being corrupted by the cold vacuum and the dark universe, they had a new name—the Primeval Race!

The animation came to an end. Li Yao was faced with countless options that were falling from the sky again. They hit his soul like a waterfall.

On one hand, he had to plan the hibernation and wake-up time of the scientists and the engineers in order to ensure that all the technologies necessary for sailing in the sea of stars could be advanced in an orderly manner. On the other hand, he had to ensure that the new generation on board received the best education in order to produce a research reserve that was inspired by new knowledge. On the other hand, he had to plan and adjust the route so that he would not lose his way in the boundless sea of stars. On the other hand, he had to pay close attention to the scourge of the meteoroid showers and cosmic radiation. Even a meteoroid the size of sand or dust could cause minor damage to the shell and the power unit of a starship. After tens of thousands of years of accumulation and enhancement, the smallest damage could lead to the fission and disintegration of the entire starship, which were all life-and-death events.

Other than that, threats that were more serious and secretive than cosmic radiation and the damage of starships were the changes of human hearts.

Earthlings used to live in the blue sky, on the solid land, bathed in the warm sunlight, and were a carefree planet species.

But right now, they had completely lost their home and roots. They had to spend tens of thousands of years and thousands of generations wandering in darkness and desperation. Maybe they would become homeless children in the sea of stars forever.

The narrow space, the enclosed environment, the deep sea of stars… There was no telling where the destination was exactly, and they might be killed in the very next second… Such cruel torture was like a vat of black venom that was corroding people’s hearts and turning them into two completely different lives from the past.

Similar changes had happened in the Flying Star Sector before.

As Xiao Xuance, leader of the Immortal Cultivators and a Cultivator of the Flying Star Sector, had said, it was very easy to turn an unwavering Cultivator into an Immortal Cultivator in the black sea of stars. It usually took only… one second.

The people of the Flying Star Sector, at the very least, still had their history and remembered the glory of the Star Ocean Imperium. They also knew that there were countless Sectors and their own kind outside of their small world. Such knowledge was enough to be a comfort and hope to resist the invasion of darkness.

But those Earthlings—the Primeval Lord of Heaven—had lost everything. From then on, although the sea of stars was vast, they could not find any other compatriots or homes that they could trust anymore. How painful, how terrifying, and how desperate it was!

Such venom, after hundreds of years, thousands of years, or even tens of thousands of years of long and aimless journey, could twist the humanity into any kind of deformity or hideousness. It was not strange at all.

Faced with all kinds of challenges, Li Yao was somewhat overwhelmed.

More importantly, it was not necessary for him to make a choice that was in the best interest of the moment, but rather, he had to consider the development of the immigration fleet in tens of thousands of years and make a choice that was in the best interest of the future. In many cases, it was inevitable that he would hurt the interests of the passengers at present and even sacrifice a lot of things ruthlessly.

After such a test, Li Yao was certain that he was not qualified to be a commander-in-chief.

Maybe he was only suitable to play the role of a hot-blooded high school student, or he could play the role of a flapping flag in the wind and shout some idealized sacred slogans. But when faced with complicated, practical problems, they needed a calmer, rational, and even darker role, such as Jin Tuyi, Boss Bai, Lei Chenhu, Li Linghai, and even Long Yangjun.

Li Yao felt that he had made a lot of mistakes.

Under his command, a series of serious problems broke out inside the immigration fleet. There were even several internal conflicts.

Outside of the starship, they had passed through a belt of cosmic dust that was as thick as ink, endured a few unexpected radiation storms, and almost crashed into another meteoroid shower. They managed to cope with each crisis with great difficulty, causing a lot of damage to the starship and consuming a lot of valuable resources.

Thousands of years had passed since the test. They had only sailed for dozens of lightyears, but more than half of the immigration starships had been destroyed. More than 30% of the hibernators had been killed in their sleep because of mechanical faults, lack of resources, and internal strife. Even the ‘leader’ that Li Yao played had been killed by the insurgent who suffered from claustrophobia during the civil strife.

The people who were still alive had long lost their vigor and vitality when they rushed out of the solar system thousands of years ago. They were unshaven, their skin was loose, their eyes were sunken, and their faces were lackluster. They were like the walking dead trapped in steel coffins, only moving forward numbly driven by their last instinct for survival.

Li Yao was on tenterhooks, thinking that he had made a series of wrong choices and his performance must’ve been terrible.

However, the voice remained silent and did not comment on his performance.

When the ‘leader’ he played was killed by the crew members of the rebellion, he even comforted Li Yao that it was not a ‘mistake’ because, in the real history, Wan Zanghai, as the commander-in-chief of the immigration fleet and the first chief of the ‘Primeval Clan’, had ended up like that, too.

Naturally, Wan Zanghai’s commanding ability was a hundred times higher than Li Yao’s. However, the countless practical problems and fatal threats that he was faced with were a hundred times more complicated and difficult than the large-scale options in the test.

No matter how resolute and wise Wan Canghai was, he was just a mortal. After decades of lurking and training, he had almost reached the limit of the life span of the earthlings when he left the solar system. It was impossible for him to maintain a clear mind for a long time to command the fleet. He could only enter the hibernation state and wake up every five to ten years to deal with the problems that had happened over the past ten years.

Such a way of leadership was destined to be unable to govern the brutal, disobedient Radiators, the modified people, and the superhumans for a long time.

Time could change many things, including the prestige of a tough commander-in-chief.

The immaturity of the first-generation hibernation technology had a serious influence on the wisdom and personality of Wan Zanghai.

Gradually, he became unpredictable and temperamental. He was even suffering from claustrophobia and paranoia like many crew members.

He had once pointed at the deep pathway on the starship and said that he had seen countless ghosts from Earth wandering there.

He had ordered the fleet to accelerate without caring about the cost of fuel, too, because he had seen that the ghosts of everyone on the furiously burning Earth were not annihilated. Instead, they had been condensed into a new… ghost civilization by the solar storm and the spiritual energy magnetic field. The ghost civilization was following them quietly, planning the cruelest revenge.

He did not even dare to enter the hibernation state, because he would be drowned in the eyes of countless innocent souls and dragged to the deepest part of hell by the burnt hands of the victims.

Under such circumstances, a fierce conflict between the commander-in-chief and the crew was only a matter of time.

Wan Zanghai was dead. He had been torn apart by the crew members who had been loyal to him and thrown into the engine furnace, fulfilling his dream of being the spark that pushed the civilization forward.

The journey continued. After all, there was nothing that could stop them in the cold, dark vacuum.

In the thousands of years of testing that followed, Li Yao felt that his performance was getting worse and worse, and all the crew members were more desperate, cruel, and irritated.

They even felt that they had given up on themselves. They were determined to destroy themselves in a riot or natural disaster and end the never-ending nightmare.

This wasn’t some sort of expedition or escape; it was merely a “death sentence” that would be carried out over 10,000 years. Quite a few crew members believed this.

When Li Yao drained the last bit of his braincells, overdrafted all his computational ability, and tried his best to maintain the immigration fleet for ten thousand years, all that was left of them were forty-nine battered, miserable starships, fourteen million frustrated, angry passengers, and empty fuel and resource capsules.

Right now, they had not grasped the ability to shuttle through the four-dimensional space yet. No matter how much they improved their regular sailing speed, they had only flown five hundred lightyears away. In a galaxy hundreds of thousands of lightyears in diameter, their speed was even lower than that of a snail. It was impossible for them to find the source of the meteoroid shower.

They had run out of resources and food. This was where they would be buried.

“Did it fail?”

Li Yao was not a man who would give up easily. But now that he was running out of options, he did not know how to continue the test anymore.

Maybe he should have been crueler in the beginning?

No. It was useless. Even if the potential of the crew members was exploited, what was the difference between the exploration distance from five hundred lightyears to six hundred lightyears?

Li Yao found it odd. Didn’t the voice say that it would give him three chances? But he was already at a dead end after only one suggestion. Was the voice going to go back on its word?

While he was hesitating, an unbelievable and appalling picture suddenly appeared in Li Yao’s brain!

In the front of the refugees’ fleet, which had run out of ammunition and food, a cluster of bright purple waves suddenly rose up in the empty sea of stars. The waves surged to the two sides like tides, only to tear open a giant crevice from which a fat tentacle crawled out!

Chapter 3106: Void Hunter

Li Yao watched everything in bewilderment.

He did not know whether it was a real picture of the primeval era or an exaggerated depiction of a symbol. In short, the tentacles that extended out of the void tied up the immigration starships one after another. No matter how the particle cannons and vacuum torpedoes of the starships attacked, it did not care. Instead, all the starships, together with the Primeval Lord of Heaven, were dragged into the purple gap.

Then, Li Yao’s vision seemed to have turned into a bizarre dream. In the middle of the surging tides that were as splendid as a kaleidoscope, he saw an enormous monster that seemed to have occupied half of the sea of stars. It had ten thousand tentacles and a hundred million translucent organs that looked like eyes. They were emitting vague brightness and were filled with inexplicable weirdness and fear.

Then, the colorful pieces condensed again, and the chaotic space and time were stabilized again. The migration fleet of the Primeval Lord of Heaven was brought to a brand-new world, a ‘paradise’ that was full of spiritual energy and crystals and inexhaustible energy!

“This—this is—”

Li Yao’s soul was shivering in excitement, as if he could taste the endless spiritual energy in the picture. “What is this?”

“As you can see, it is a foreign species that can jump freely in the four-dimensional space. We call them ‘Void Hunters’.

The voice explained, “Although the ‘Primeval Civilization’ was one of the earliest civilizations to be born in the sea of the universe, we are not the first intelligent creatures to be born. Long before us, countless intelligent alien species were born in the natural paradises where the spiritual energy was abundant, forming a giant ‘cosmic ecosphere’. The Void Hunters were one of them.

“However, although they wasted billions of years, they did not create a highly developed civilization. Most of the alien species in space were ignorant animals who lived on instinct like the ‘Void Hunters’. Their IQ could not have been higher than that of a seven-year-old child. Only a few alien species in space developed a society with slavery.

“Why?”

Li Yao found it hard to believe that a civilization had not developed despite billions of years of precious time despite the abundant spiritual energy and the enlightenment of preliminary wisdom!

“On the ancient Earth, many species thrived for billions of years and resisted multiple mass extinctions. They were known as ‘living fossils’, such as turtles, crocodiles, and cockroaches, but they never developed wisdom or civilization.

The voice said, “The bottom line is, the so-called ‘civilization’ is not necessary for life, and evolution is definitely not an eternal path. The so-called ‘evolution’ is just a helpless evolution resulting from the drastic changes of the environment.

“Life is a very lazy thing. As long as the environment hasn’t changed much and it can survive, it will never think of changing or evolving.

“Take turtles, crocodiles, and cockroaches for example. They are the kings of their respective fields, or the top predators at the top of the food chain, or they boast unparalleled defense and unbelievable survival ability. They don’t need wisdom or civilization. They can live a good life and pass on their genes, too.

“Therefore, what motivation do they have to change and ‘evolve’?

“The same goes for the Void Hunters. You can simply imagine them as giant octopuses that boast the ability to jump in the sea of stars and survive in vacuum. They chase after the meteorites and asteroids that contain mother lodes of crystals in the vast sea of stars by instinct, extend their tentacles out of the space crevices, tie up the meteorites, and absorb the energy inside the crystals with the specially-constructed negative-pressure suckers. Such instincts have allowed them to reproduce comfortably in the center of the sea of stars for billions of years. Their enormous size and unpredictable abilities make them invincible. Even if they run into enemies that they cannot afford to mess with, they can always crawl into the four-dimensional space and run away to millions of lightyears away. What can wisdom and civilization do for them?

Li Yao thought carefully for a long time.

He had to admit that the voice was right.

A creature born in a natural paradise with abundant spiritual energy who was born with the ability to absorb crystals, absorb energy, and cruise in the air did not need to develop an intelligent civilization.

Just like the children of rich and powerful families, there was no need for them to work hard.

Only human beings, the hairless monkeys from the Spiritless Space, who crawled and struggled to survive, the weak and pitiful primates, had the need to evolve into super intelligent beings and create a prosperous civilization.

“Then—”

Li Yao felt that his mouth was dry. “All the remaining immigration starships were brought to the ‘paradise’ in the center of the universe by the ‘Void Hunter’? Didn’t it swallow you?”

“Part of it.”

The voice said, “The Void Hunters are the most adept voyagers in the sea of stars. They are born with the ability to perceive the shadow ripples when a four-dimensional storm is projected into the three-dimensional universe. With the shadow ripples, they can create fleeting wormholes and perform super long-distance space jumps to chase after the erratic meteoroid showers and resource planets.

“The Void Hunter, on the other hand, sensed the exhaust flames released by the immigration starship and regarded our fleet as a meteoroid belt that contained abundant energy. That was why it showed up in the four-dimensional space and swallowed us.

“However, as you may have discovered, at that time, our fleet of immigrants was running out of ammunition and food. There was barely any fuel left in the fuel warehouse. The ‘Void Hunter’ crushed and swallowed three immigration starships. Finding that it could not squeeze out much energy except for the rusted garbage, it gave up eating.

“Although it did not evolve into a civilization in the truest sense, the Void Hunters were, after all, an intelligent species that had thrived in the center of the universe for billions of years. Very soon, it discovered that the Primeval Beings inside the starship were actually a tiny life form, too.

“At that time, in the center of the universe, there was a standard to determine the strength of life and the risk.

“Basically, the thicker the spiritual energy stored inside the body, the more proficient one is in manipulating spiritual energy, and the stronger one is, the more dangerous one is.

“Although the Primeval Lord of Heaven has been exposed to a few crystals that fell from space on Earth and practiced preliminary techniques, we are still new to the technology and barely know anything about it. Most of the propulsion systems, including the starships we are on, are based on chemical energy and atomic energy, too.

“The Void Hunters cannot understand our complicated social form and civilization, nor can they perceive the potential and danger behind our feeble appearance, nor do they understand the advanced technology and complicated industrial system that our starship represents. In their eyes, we are just… harmless bugs.

“Harmless bugs?”

Li Yao couldn’t help but laugh.

Human beings, the toughest species in the sea of stars, had been mistaken for bugs by a giant octopus. How interesting.

“The Void Hunters regard us as ants. It doesn’t matter whether we are stomped to death or ignored.

The voice said, “But for the survivors of the Primeval Clan, the appearance of the Void Hunters is tantamount to an apocalypse. Our world view and scientific outlook have been shattered. The endless surging spiritual gas in the natural paradises that are coming at us makes our survival instincts, which are on the verge of extinction, expand again and unleash the fiercest flames.

“All the survivors immediately took action and racked their brains to figure out how to survive in such a weird and fertile environment.

“Not long after, a few wise men whose brains had evolved greatly and could amplify and release brainwaves freely found a solution. They tried everything they could to reach an agreement with the Void Hunters. The two parties formed a harmonious symbiotic relationship, and the Primeval Lord of Heaven turned into the ‘biotics’ and ‘parasites’ of the Void Hunters.

“Well—”

Li Yao was dazed. “How is that possible?”

“The Void Hunters are travelers who travel in the vast universe by themselves. They don’t have a sense of community or society. However, during the long, desolate journey, they inevitably feel lonely and bored, too. Since they often travel in the four-dimensional space and tear apart their neurons, their nerves will be affected eventually.

The voice continued, “In order to survive, a special profession named ‘Dream Maker’ was born in the Primeval Lord of Heaven. It performed ‘soul massages’ on the Void Hunters with its complicated brainwaves and weaved all kinds of glamorous dreams. Naturally, the Void Hunters could not understand the memories and feelings of the Primeval Lord of Heaven, but the bizarre dreams that poured into their nerves made them feel very comfortable, as if they were being tickled.

“Just like that, the Primeval Lord of Heaven parasitized the Void Hunters and managed to survive with the ‘remnants’, or fragments of crystals, that it leaked out of their tentacles. The Void Hunters, on the other hand, massaged our hearts with our abundant emotions and imagination to eliminate the loneliness of the long journey and the side effects of space jumps.

“The symbiosis seems to be very harmonious, but there are subtle dangers in it.

“One day, when the Dream Makers on a starship were performing the ‘soul massage’ for the Void Hunters, their starship was suddenly minced into pieces by the Void Hunters. The millions of survivors who managed to reach the center of the universe perished on the spot.

“The rest of the survivors were greatly shocked. They did not know how they had pissed off the Void Hunters.

“It was not until almost ten million people on the other four starships were casually killed by the Void Hunters that the remaining people gradually came back to themselves. It was because the ‘dreams’ that the Dream Makers on the starships created for the Void Hunters were too outdated and boring. They were repeated too many times that the Void Hunters grew impatient.

“It was the most humiliating and dangerous moment for the Primeval Civilization.

“In order to survive, the Primeval Beings had to rack their brains and weave beautiful, brilliant dreams in order to help the Void Hunters scratch their itch. They could not stop for a moment and had to send out the most interesting brainwaves every second. When the brainwaves were dull and lackluster, it was the day of the total destruction of an immigration starship!

Chapter 3107: Fatal Parasite

This was truly an incredible interstellar journey!

Li Yao seemed to have seen countless ancestors of the Primeval Race crouching under the tentacles of the Void Hunters, shivering in the broken starships. They were living every second of their life in anxiety, while they were trying to come up with new dreams to please the gargantuan nightmare creature below their feet and above their heads.

One moment of carelessness, and the starship would be minced to pieces by the tentacles. Millions of people would be swallowed by the sucking discs that looked like black holes. There would be nothing left of them at all. They would truly be obliterated!

Other than that, the Void Hunters often travelled in the raging tides of the four-dimensional space.

The ancestors of the Primeval Race were apparently not prepared for the impact of the four-dimensional storm.

Although they were protected by the tentacles of the Void Hunters, countless starships were damaged every time they passed through the storm in the four-dimensional space. The passengers were sucked out of the gaps and smashed into the gaps of time and space, ending up in a fate worse than death.

Even if they returned to the three-dimensional universe safely, countless people would be melded with their starships and explode violently, or suffer from serious sequela and become mentally deranged. It was as if their bodies had returned, but their souls were still left in the terrifying four-dimensional storm!

How thrilling, how humiliating, how desperate!

However, in order to survive, a weak species had struggled from the lifeless space tens of thousands of years ago to this day, to this place, to the center of the Universe Ocean, and to the dawn of dawn. Their will and ambitions would not be crushed by any difficulties.

When the Void Hunters and the four-dimensional storms crushed their flesh and blood, leaving them heavily wounded and on the verge of death, the purest spiritual energy in the center of the universe gave them the warmest comfort.

The Primeval Lord of Heaven absorbed and digested the spiritual energy crazily. He was like a sponge that had been tossed into water after being dried, hoping to absorb all the seawater in the ocean.

In order to survive, they had tempered every organ in their body with spiritual energy, reorganized the gene strands, refined the cell structure, and optimized the efficiency of the mitochondria. Their internal organs, skin, and brain had undergone tremendous changes and gradually adapted to the coldness of the three-dimensional universe and the brutality of the four-dimensional space.

In order to survive, their computational ability soared. Almost every Primeval Lord of Heaven had become a qualified Dream Maker, weaving weird, brilliant dreams for the Void Hunters.

In order to better instill the dreams into the collection of nerves of the Void Hunters, the Primeval Lord of Heaven’s ability to manipulate brainwaves and telepathic thoughts was improving day by day, too. The neurons in their brains were mutating and strengthening. The number of neurons that were condensed in a tiny brain was even higher than the total number of neurons on one of the enormous tentacles of the Void Hunters.

Of course, even though they were fully prepared, it was inevitable that a lot of their compatriots would be devoured by the Void Hunters every few days. There was no time for sorrow or desperation. With the help of their spiritual energy, the Primeval Lord of Heaven activated their strong reproductive ability and expanded their species crazily like rabbits.

In the universe at that time, most of the intelligent creatures were tough individuals without natural enemies or competitors like the Void Hunters. They boasted a long life and absolute dominance. Their urge to continue their genes was not strong, and they would only reproduce once every hundreds or even thousands of years.

The female creatures such as the Primeval Lord of Heaven could reproduce when they were fifteen or sixteen years old. Then, they could reproduce once a year. Every time, they could even be twins or triplets. They were even crazier than cockroaches, mice, and rabbits.

With the help of advanced genes and medical technology, it was not difficult for the multiple births and the rapid recovery after the birth.

At that time, the reproduction of the species was dependent on the bellies of the females and was bound to be restricted. But after the artificial uterus and the incubation tank were developed, the Primeval Lord of Heaven was no longer restricted by the gender restriction. As long as they had enough energy and genes, they would be able to produce offspring continuously, thereby freeing all the women and doubling the combat ability of the entire species.

As long as they were willing and had enough energy, their species could explode exponentially and reproduce in every corner of the infinite universe.

And that was exactly what they did—the devils that were about to sweep across the entire Universe Ocean!

The Void Hunters were ignorant of everything.

It was not that it did not notice the subtle changes of the bugs attached to its body, but that there were more and more of them.

However, its body was too enormous, and its perception of time was different from that of the Primeval Lord of Heaven. It did not realize the danger of the Primeval Lord of Heaven at all. Instead, it felt that the bugs were scratching its itch more and more. What was wrong with that?

Not long after, the Primeval Lord of Heaven showed another ‘ability’ to the Void Hunters.

The Void Hunters lived by swallowing meteoroids and asteroids, crushing the crystals stored inside, and absorbing the energy stored inside.

However, its body was too large, and its movements were relatively clumsy. During the process of swallowing the meteoroids and asteroids, it was inevitable that a lot of the debris would be left behind.

A lot of precious energy was still stored inside the residues. As the saying goes, a little makes the world go round.

In the past, the Void Hunters would often leave behind a large number of meteorites and asteroid fragments after devouring everything in their way.

But right now, the Primeval Lord of Heaven was hovering around the scene of the void hunter’s absorption in his latest small-scale spiritual airship. He was collecting the scattered scraps carefully and smelting the scraps that contained mother lodes of crystals into the purest spiritual crystals before he offered them to the void hunter.

For the Void Hunters, the manually-refined spiritual energy crystals whose purity was close to 100% were as delicious as dragon liver and phoenix marrow.

The Void Hunter sensed a feeling of beauty that was completely different from the glamorous dream but with the same approach. He was immediately hooked and found his original method of absorption boring and tasteless.

From then on, the Primeval Lord of Heaven joined the absorption and digestion process of the Void Hunters and became the ‘tentacles’ in a different form. They helped the Void Hunters crush the meteoroids, smelt the spiritual minerals, extracted the purest spiritual energy crystals, and tossed them into the suckers of the Void Hunters respectfully.

Of course, the Primeval Lord of Heaven had to take a step back during the process. About 5% to 10% of the spiritual energy had fallen into their control.

The Void Hunters, who had been well served by the Primeval Lord of Heaven, did not think too much of it. As far as they knew, the Primeval Lord of Heaven was their tentacles, teeth, the flagella in their digestive system, and even the probiotics inside their body. Who would doubt the probiotics inside their body?

Just like that, the ancestors of the Primeval Clan lurked in the shelter of the Void Hunters and drifted in the dark sea of stars for a second ten thousand years.

Over the past ten thousand years, the Void Hunters and the Primeval Lord of Heaven had become more and more inseparable. The primeval cosmic creature that was as enormous as a deity was more and more feeble in appearance and under the control of the Primeval Lord of Heaven.

Its cluster of nerves had been analyzed, studied, paralyzed, and corrupted by the dream creators of the Primeval Clan. Its wisdom, like that of a seven-year-old child, had been played by the wisdom of the Primeval Clan.

Its digestive ability weakened as the Primeval Lord of Heaven continued supplying the purest spiritual energy crystals. It did not even bother to wave its tentacles. Everything was done by the Primeval Lord of Heaven.

The Primeval Lord of Heaven had even learned how to sneak into one of its tentacles and cut off the transmission of information between the tentacle and the nerve cluster before it was connected to the mainframe crystal processor of the starship so that the tentacle could be controlled.

After all, the Void Hunters had so many tentacles, but they did not even have a real brain. The neurons all over their bodies were too few to notice such tricks.

If it had been discovered, it might’ve praised the loyalty of the Primeval Lord of Heaven and helped it get rid of the tentacles and the troublesome feeding process!

Although it was not unusual for countless people to die every day, the artificial uterus and the technology of the large-scale hatching pool, together with the fully-automatic education system that Li Yao mentioned just now, could allow ten times more of his compatriots to be produced continuously. Then, with the scraps of meteorites that the Void Hunters despised, he could refine metals and build components into new starships—the real warships that could expand the black glory of the Primeval Lord Clan to the ends of every tentacle of the Void Hunters!

The Void Hunters had never discovered the ambitions of the Primeval Lord of Heaven.

In the meantime, the Primeval Lord of Heaven had only encountered one life-and-death threat, not because of the original intention of the Void Hunters, but because the life cycle of the behemoth had come to an end.

Void hunters were asexual creatures.

It was obvious. Such top predators in the universe did not need to change their genes aggressively. Besides, it was quite rare for them to run into their own kind in the vast sea of stars. It was unnecessary to ask for trouble by evolving genders.

However, the reproduction method of the Void Hunters had caused a lot of trouble for the Primeval Lord of Heaven.

Whenever a Void Hunter sensed that its ten-thousand-year-long life was coming to an end, it would devour a lot of meteoroids and asteroids crazily driven by its instincts and store all the overloaded spiritual energy inside its body. It was like a gargantuan biological bomb that would explode in the end.

In the big explosion, the old Void Hunters would be blown to pieces, and their broken limbs would be burnt by the intense spiritual energy reactions. Part of the hibernating genes would be activated, too. In the end, of the thousands of tentacles that were tossed away, the few largest limbs would be revived and grow out of their body parts, turning them into new Void Hunters.

The process was called ‘explosion’.

It was not hard to imagine that, for the Primeval Beings who were living on the bodies of the Void Hunters, it was as good as another apocalypse.

However, the Primeval Lord of Heaven at this moment was no longer an earthling who could not resist the catastrophe twenty thousand years ago.

Chapter 3108: Mad Dog Released

After ten thousand years of development, with the nourishment of abundant spiritual energy, the Primeval Lord of Heaven had a small yet capable fleet of spiritual energy miners. They even boasted several warships that boasted tremendous attack and defense ability. The Void Hunters trusted them enough to move away from the waving tentacles of the Void Hunters. They had even grasped the technology of space jumps by studying the neurons of the Void Hunters.

The Primeval Lord of Heaven had made preparations in advance. Most of his compatriots had armed themselves with crystal suits and spiritual shields. Their starships had also been modified and moved far away from the Void Hunters.

The destruction of the Void Hunters had destroyed about 10% of the Primeval Lord of Heaven’s strength, but it had brought new hope, too. After the explosion, three enormous broken limbs were revived, and under the care of the Primeval Lord of Heaven, they gradually grew into the new generation of Void Hunters. Different from the old ones, they did not encounter the ‘little bugs’ such as the Primeval Lord of Heaven in their prime years. Instead, they were entangled with the Primeval Lord of Heaven the moment they were born and took the existence and care of the Primeval Lord of Heaven for granted. They did not know the ambitions and threats of the Primeval Lord of Heaven at all.

Just like that, the Primeval Civilization, which used to live on one Void Hunter, had three more obedient ‘mounts’ whose capability had tripled. They even tamed the Void Hunters slowly and stimulated their growth with technology and industry, turning the ignorant animals into terrifying man-made monsters.

Just like that, the Primeval Lord of Heaven completed the transformation from a parasite to a dominator and took the first step to conquer the universe!

A similar rise was taking place at the same time in countless natural paradises and worlds in the center of the universe.

The Void Hunters were never-ending travelers in the sea of stars.
