Over the past ten thousand years, the Void Hunter who had been living on the body of the Primeval Lord of Heaven had travelled through countless natural paradises and dealt with thousands of alien species and even the low-level civilizations. However, he had spread the Primeval Lord of Heaven to every corner of the sea of stars.

Most of the alien species in space were similar to the Void Hunters. They boasted unparalleled combat ability and unbelievable techniques, but they did not have extraordinary wisdom or ambitions. Their IQ could not have been higher than that of a seven-year-old child. Their treatment of the Primeval Lord of Heaven was more like “a child who found a new toy and could not wait to show it off and share it with his companions”.

The Void Hunters left the ships of the Origin Clan in various natural paradises in exchange for other precious resources or rare gadgets before they left in satisfaction.

The primitive demons that had been stranded in the natural paradises were like their compatriots who were still living on the bodies of the Void Hunters. They lurked, hid their ambitions, and struggled to survive.

They were faced with all kinds of creatures as strong as gods and devils and as weird as nightmares. One moment of carelessness, and they would be crushed and swallowed by the enemy, who did not even mean them any harm. It was like they were merely yawning when they crushed an ant.

They had to rack their brains and try their best to please the dominators of the paradises so that they could show the value of their existence. Only in such a way could they live on and endure the inhuman pain!

On a planet whose atmosphere was extremely thin and full of hard rocks, a foreign species named ‘Mountain Giant Crab’ lived in space. Such creatures boasted shells as hard as iron and a sense of time that was close to stagnation. They could absorb the metal elements and the geothermal energy in the mother lodes directly and be self-sufficient in such a way. Their enormous size and too-hard shells made them the strongest existences in the sea of stars. Even the Void Hunters could not digest their exoskeletons. Therefore, there was no need for them to develop wisdom and civilization.

They had no ill intentions toward the Primeval Lord of Heaven. It was even difficult for them to understand the concept of ‘slaughter’. But they loved to flip their bodies and create earthshaking quakes. Every time the quakes took place, countless Primeval Lords living on their bodies would fall into the abyss and be squeezed to death by the gaps between their shells.

In the long symbiotic relationship, the Primeval Lord of Heaven had learned how to clean the ‘Mountain Crab’s’ breathing holes that had been blocked by the creep. He had also learned how to carve glamorous patterns on the surface of their shells to please the dull and stupid beasts. After he discovered that the Mountain Crab was particularly sensitive to sound, a special profession named ‘Singer’ even appeared. Just like the ‘Dream Maker’ on the Void Hunters, it was designed to calm and anesthetize the souls of the giant gods, if the giant gods really had souls.

For survival, such compromises and grievances were just the minimum.

In other natural paradises, the carbon-based intelligent creatures did not take the form of animals but plants. The Primeval Lords who accidentally ended up there even had to let the roots of the plants grow into their bodies and meld with their veins and internal organs, turning themselves into the ‘vehicles’ of the dominators!

Just like that, in countless natural paradises, the Primeval Lord of Heaven entertained the rulers who seemed to be deities in countless humiliating and painful ways.

Generally speaking, the dominators were very satisfied with the performance of the ‘little bugs’ that the Void Hunters brought. They did not realize that there was anything wrong with the exponential growth and expansion of the Primeval Lord of Heaven. They thought that the Primeval Lord of Heaven was a very interesting and docile creature and that there was nothing wrong with it except that it liked to play with metal gadgets.

Only some low-level civilizations were vaguely aware of the danger of the Primeval Clan.

They were the carbon-based intelligent life forms that were relatively weak and could not survive on their own. They had to get involved with their own kind and develop social relationships or even civilizations.

They looked very similar to the ‘demons’ in human beings’ eyes.

As a low-level civilization, the wise ones among them could vaguely sense the potential and threat of the ‘high-level civilization’ that was the Primeval Clan.

Although the Primeval Beings that the Void Hunters brought to them were merely haggard, shivering, and funny-looking hairless monkeys, the wise men of the low-level civilization could still see the burning flames deep inside the eyes of the ‘brow monkey’.

The wise men of the low-level civilizations were spreading the news and shouting, asking people to be wary of the arrival of the devils and to make predictions such as ‘the gate of endless hell has been opened, and the devils from the blue planet are about to arrive and destroy the universe’.

It was a pity that, in the primeval era when the universe was just divided, the intelligent creatures living in the center of the universe were far from monolithic. The life forms that grew freely under the abundant spiritual energy naturally lacked channels of communication. The low-level civilizations with weak individuals and the super-large spiritual beasts with strong individuals were even more vigilant and hostile than trusting and friendly.

For the Primeval Lord of Heaven, the Void Hunters were ‘universe species’. For the low-level civilizations, the Void Hunters were also ‘universe species’. At best, they were relatively familiar with each other and only appeared occasionally.

What was even worse was that, in the middle of the low-level civilizations, the ‘stronger are always stronger, and the weak are always weaker’ phenomenon caused by the spiritual energy had intensified the social conflicts and stifled the development of technology and systems, making them unable to move forward anymore. They were suspicious and wary of each other, and they were caught in the endless war of ‘everyone versus everyone’. They were like duplicates of the larger-scale Ancient Saint World.

In such a swirl of struggle, all the individuals prioritized their immediate interests and would never unite to consider the interests of the civilization as a whole.

When the Ancients, who were scattered to the low-level civilizations, showed amazing wisdom and complicated strategies, they were immediately awed by the experts of the low-level civilizations and started making use of the Ancients to fight against their old enemies. During the process, the ‘mercenaries’ and the ‘slave soldiers’ of both sides were also the Ancients. Such a ridiculous and dangerous scene took place.

The old wise men were crying in grief and indignation on the peak of the mountain. The Primeval Lord of Heaven was attacking each other with the advanced spiritual weapons on the battlefield. The experts of other species in space were laughing in the forest of wine and meat, thinking that their plan had worked out. Nobody heard the earsplitting waves coming from the depths of the sea of stars. Thunder was breaking out in the atmosphere of every habitable planet. It was the ambition of the Primeval Lord of Heaven that he had been hiding for ten thousand years. It was the roar of a high-level civilization against the low-level civilizations and the stupid beasts. A war had broken out for the glory of the Primeval Lord of Heaven!

“A hundred thousand years ago, after eating all the large animals on the planet, including the most brutal animals, our ancestors walked out of Africa. They scattered the animals on every continent of the planet in about eighty thousand years and exterminated more than 90% of the animals. It was the only mass extinction that was not caused by natural disasters in the history of the planet. Our species was more efficient in slaughtering than the natural disasters such as meteoroid strikes, volcanic eruptions, super earthquakes, and prolonged glaciers.

The voice said calmly, “The mammoth weighs more than ten tons and boasts thick hair and fat. The tusks of the male elephants are more than three meters long, much longer than those of an adult man. They are the unquestionable dominators of the prairie during the long Ice Age.

“The saber-toothed tiger possesses teeth as sharp as daggers and an extremely concealed hunting ability. In order to resist our threat, some subspecies have even evolved into the form of teeth specialized in gnawing the human brain. Their powerful bite force can easily crush a complete skull.

“Whale are the largest and heaviest animals. Their range of survival does not overlap with ours at all. They live in the vast ocean far away from crowds.

“So what? So what? So what? Is there any reason to stop our extinction?

“Civilization means a million times more barbarism. When a civilization encounters barbarism, no matter how great the difference in individual capability is, there is no suspense about the outcome. Whether it is the Void Hunters, the giant crab, or other alien species in space, no matter how much spiritual energy they control or ‘waste’, in our eyes, they are just large mammoths, saber-toothed tigers, and blue whales. A hundred thousand years ago, we walked out of Africa. Today, we conquer the universe!”

Chapter 3109: Great Destruction Order

Along with the high-pitched roars, Li Yao seemed to see that, on the bizarre planets, groups of bizarre-shaped creatures were tearing off their disguises of shame and humor. War machines made of crystals and metals were emerging from the deep sea, from the valleys, and from the chaotic stone belts.

The horns of war were blown. People were praying to their ancestors and the gods in the languages that had been passed on on Earth ten thousand years ago, hoping that they could bring victory to the war. No. Prayers to the gods were unnecessary, because they were waging a war against the gods.

The Primeval Clan—human beings—would represent the blue planet that had been destroyed twenty thousand years ago and declare war on all the species as strong as deities in the universe.

“The vast sea of stars is the journey of our race. There will be no gods or devils after us!”

No species or civilization was a match for the Primeval Lord of Heaven.

Even if the Void Hunters could destroy ten warships of the Primeval Lord of Heaven in their fury, even if the rolling of the giant crab could tear apart the crust of the planet and swallow dozens of infantry legions, the military bases and hatching centers that the Primeval Lord of Heaven had secretly established in the sea of stars would be able to produce brand-new starships and soldiers continuously. Even if they suffered setbacks in certain battlefields, an army of iron ten times larger would soon drown the entire battlefield, the planet, and the entire space zone. It was the great power of the great industrialization, the combination of spiritual energy and technology. Such supreme power was incomprehensible for the idiotic creatures and the low-level civilizations who had enjoyed billions of years of peace in the warmth of spiritual energy!

Just as the voice said, the process of the Primeval Lord of Heaven conquering the universe was a replica of the process when human beings walked out of Africa and conquered the Earth.

The Void Hunters, the Mountain Giant Crab, and the thousands of other species in space perished for the same reason as the mammoths and the saber-toothed tigers.

As top predators, they had lost the determination and courage to evolve. Without natural enemies, there was no need to change. The abundant spiritual energy gave them a long life. Even the genes that they inherited were not as urgent. The word ‘life’ had been corrupted by the spiritual gas to the point that they were no longer able to fight back when a new, fiercer, more determined, and ambitious species appeared. It was too late for them to make any changes!

Besides, the Primeval Lord of Heaven, who had developed from human beings on Earth, had experienced countless plots and schemes. In order to annihilate their own kind, they were willing to wage three world wars. After ten thousand years of seclusion and ten thousand years of forbearance, their brutality and sordidness were beyond the imagination of the alien species in space. They would not be foolish enough to fight against the alien species in space!

“When the golden horn was blown, billions of starships and tanks charged at the alien species in space, and countless primordial creatures built the glory of civilization with their own blood and bodies. It was just an exaggerated rhetoric.

Most of the time, the war was carried out in a more secretive and dark way.

The sharpest weapons of the saber-toothed tigers and the mammoths were their claws and tusks, but the sharpest weapons of the Primeval Lord of Heaven were not the cannons and warships but their brains and their minds.

Corroding the nerves of the Void Hunters with the dreams that were full of black venom and raising their greed and hatred for the giant alien species such as the Hill Crab, dozens of Void Hunters were gathered to sweep the planet of the Hill Crab.

Or, they could steal an egg of a fast-breeding alien insect that was specially designed to gnaw on plants and, after genetic modification, throw it into the natural paradises that were full of primitive forests on a large scale. The ecological system of the natural paradises would be destroyed, and the top predators living there would be forced to migrate to other natural paradises. Conflicts between the top predators would break out, and the Primeval Lord of Heaven would reap all the benefits.

Their double-dealing, double-dealing, and sowing discord… The schemes and plots of the Primeval Clan ignited the flames of war in the entire Universe Ocean. Compared to the cunning Primeval Clan, most of the alien species in space were as childish as the children in kindergarten. Even though the children all boasted tremendous strength, how could they not be played by the adults?

In the end, only the alien species that were relatively weak and thus developed a complicated social system and a low-level civilization were able to resist the march of the Primordial Beginning Clan.

It was as if the voice had once told Li Yao that the thinner the spiritual energy of a Sector was, the more complex its social form would be, the more advanced its civilization would be, and the more powerful its combat ability would be. It was also possible for them to carry out certain schemes.

But right now, the Primeval Civilization had conquered a lot of natural paradises and tamed almost a thousand alien species. They had millions of fully-automatic military bases and space docks. Almost every second, dozens of brand-new warships would be refined and sent to the frontline. The resistance of the low-level civilizations was meaningless except to postpone their doom.

As part of the test, Li Yao, as the supreme commander, commanded all the fleets of the Primeval Civilization and swept across the entire Universe Ocean.

Compared to the struggles of the spores and the reptiles in the ancient era, the desperation to face the apocalypse before the destruction of the planet, and the prolonged exploration in the darkness for ten thousand years, the glorious battle of conquest right now was unbelievably easy.

Whatever choice he made, it was the right one. Even if he suffered a setback in a battlefield, three to five times the number of reinforcements would arrive very soon. There was no alien species that could not be resolved by ten thousand starships roaring at the same time. If they did exist, he would increase their combat ability by ten times and ask for a hundred thousand of them!

Li Yao thought of a saying: As long as you learn the operations of the Primeval Clan, nothing else will be a problem. All you need to do is sweep across everything.

Although the boundless sea of the universe was countless times larger than that of Earth, it only took the Primeval Lord of Heaven several million years to become the well-deserved dominator.

Crushing mountains and destroying temples, slaying demons and devils, sweeping away all kinds of monsters. The blood-stained epic had been created and carved on billions of shining stars. There was no power in the entire sea of stars that could erase it!

In the long history of conquest, after absorbing the advantages of all the alien species and the low-level civilizations, the technology level and individual capability of the Primeval Civilization had soared, too. The Supreme Emperor was right. They had embedded the strongest genes of countless alien species into their gene strands and evolved, evolved, and evolved. They now boasted the ability to absorb spiritual energy, summon wind and rain, and even tear apart the four-dimensional space!

While deducing the progress of the Primeval Civilization and reflecting on the formidability of the ancient human beings, Li Yao found it odd, too.

Judging from the history of conquests that lasted millions of years, the Primeval Lord of Heaven had explored hundreds of space zones with abundant spiritual energy and discovered a lot of low-level civilizations and alien species, but he had never found any traces of the ‘flood’.

At least, the flood did not exist when the Primeval Civilization rose. Then, where did the ultimate destructive force that intimidated the Primeval Civilization come from?

Li Yao even felt that what the Primeval Lord of Heaven had done over the past millions of years was more like an out-and-out flood!

Such ancestors awed, worshipped, and terrified him.

While he was confused, the progress of the test had reached the point when the Primeval Civilization conquered most of the enemies and almost reached the entire sea of stars. All the options in front of his eyes suddenly froze. Then, they turned gray and vanished. The only thing left was an option that Li Yao had overlooked a moment ago, which was emitting bloody brilliance.

It was a policy-type option named ‘Obliteration Order’.

The content was…

“Are we going to annihilate all the carbon-based intelligent creatures except the Primeval Lord of Heaven?”

Li Yao was greatly alarmed. “Even those who have surrendered and been tamed will not be spared? Are they going to go extinct in every space zone and planet? What—”

“You’ve just made your second mistake. It is very likely that the entire Primeval Civilization will perish.

The voice said gloomily, “Now, correct your mistake and implement the ‘Obliteration Order’!”

“Why—why?”

Li Yao stammered, “The Primeval Civilization has conquered so many fertile worlds with abundant spiritual energy and defeated all the enemies. Even the most brutal alien species in space were tamed and became the absolute dominators of the entire sea of the universe. Isn’t it enough? Do you have to destroy all the carbon-based intelligent creatures except yourself? Do you have to destroy the diversity of life forms and the endless possibilities of evolution? Is it necessary?”

“Of course it is. Can you imagine two kinds of intelligent creatures living on the ancient Earth living in harmony and developing together?”

The voice said casually, “The ancestors of the Primeval Clan were Homo sapiens. They did not even let go of their own kind, the Neanderthals, and ate them alive. That was how they took control of the planet.

“However, the Universe Ocean is billions of times larger than Earth!

“Can’t there be two kinds of intelligent creatures in such a vast universe?” Li Yao asked.

“No matter how vast the universe is, there are limits, but our ambitions are limitless.

The voice said, “Two tigers cannot live on the same mountain. The roots of the grass must be removed. It is a simple logic that all the ancient earthlings know. Can’t you understand it?”

“But they are… intelligent creatures!”

“Some of them even established their own civilizations,” Li Yao said. “Even the lowliest civilizations have their own emotions, arts, and countless things beyond their natural instincts, don’t they? Are they going to be wiped out and erased?”

“You got to the point.”

The voice was calm. “If they are truly ignorant animals, it is not a bad idea to keep them alive under the strict supervision in order to maintain the diversity of the species of the Primeval Civilization. But the intelligent creatures, especially the intelligent creatures that evolved into civilizations, all boast immeasurable potential and threats. Every one of them might remember the deep hatred for the extinction of a nation and its species and brand the hatred toward the Primeval Race into their genes and soul so that it can be passed on forever. They are like time bombs that might explode at any moment and destroy the hard-earned dominion of the sea of stars and the entire Primeval Civilization.

“Don’t put such a brilliant and great civilization in unnecessary danger just because you are softhearted.

Chapter 3110: Leading

“Your argument is too arbitrary!

Li Yao said solemnly, “I believe that the Primeval Race a hundred thousand years later should be different from the early homo sapiens a hundred thousand years ago. Otherwise, wouldn’t our evolution over the past hundred thousand years have been in vain? The Primeval Race, which boasts extraordinary technology and wisdom, should have developed a higher level of ethics and the social laws of the universe and come up with a way to make multiple civilizations live in harmony. That is the pursuit of a god-level civilization!

“An arbitrary decision?”

The voice said coldly, “Don’t forget that the communication of civilizations is two-way. Wars are the best catalysts for a civilization to catch up. The alien species and the low-level civilizations may seem weak, but they have immeasurable potential, too. It’s just that they never needed to be activated before.

“Right now, with the conquest of the Primeval Race, the potential and desires of all the surviving alien species and the low-level civilizations have been fully activated. It is more likely that they will learn more about the high-level civilizations from the captives of the Primeval Race, including the war machines that they captured, and see the direction of their evolution clearly.

“From the Homo sapiens who wielded bone spears and stone axes to the Primeval Lord of Heaven who dominated the sea of stars, it only took us hundreds of thousands of years. Who knows whether or not the alien species in space who were born with powerful techniques could ‘mutate’ in such a short period of time and turn their civilization hundreds of levels higher into our greatest enemy again?

“Don’t say that it’s impossible. Tens of thousands of years ago, the Primeval Lord of Heaven was merely lurking in the tentacles of the Void Hunters and around the breathing holes of the giant crab. He was shivering and trying to curry favor with us in exchange for some leftovers that he could barely keep alive. Who would’ve thought that we would defeat the previous dominator after only tens of thousands of years?

“If we don’t set ten thousand feet right now and stop the dominators of the past from making a comeback, who knows whether or not they will reenact our rise and overthrow the reign of the Primeval Race? By then, I’m afraid that the Primeval Civilization will not get a second chance.

“Well—”

Li Yao was lost for words.

“Besides, how do you intend to ‘peacefully coexist’ with the alien species and the low-level civilizations?

The voice continued, “It must be noted that, when the Ultimate Order of Destruction was about to be issued, the Primeval Civilization had already conquered countless fertile universes, demolished the nests of thousands of alien species and the temples of the low-level civilizations, and reproduced in the natural paradises as the rightful masters. Should we return the new worlds that we seized at the cost of billions of warriors and our blood and sweat? Impossible! But if we don’t return the conquered territories, how can we talk about peace?

“Even if the issue of living space can be negotiated, what about the lives lost? Many species have lost more than half of their population in the war. How are they going to pay back and forget such hatred?

“Should we say to the alien species and the low-level civilizations that are full of hatred, ‘I’m sorry that we destroyed your home, killed your family, and wiped out your brilliant civilization’? But it’s all in the past now. We can lay down our weapons and talk about peace now. Although we are not going to return your home to you, nor are we going to revive your family who died miserably, we can prepare a new reservation for you in the desolate land where resources are scarce. You can start a new life there. As long as you don’t try to develop a higher civilization in case you create a weapon that can threaten us, we can turn a blind eye to it and simply ignore you.

“Do you want the Primeval Civilization to become such a hypocritical ‘cosmic police’?

“Or should we castrate the alien species in space and the low-level civilizations to a certain extent and modify their brains and nerves? Or should we even alter their physiology and force them to forget their past and transform them into something more docile, cute, and non-aggressive with our ethics and social rules so that they can live a ‘happily’ life with us?

“Of course, ideally speaking, we shouldn’t have started a war of conquest against the Universe Ocean in the first place. In such a case, there wouldn’t have been an unresolvable grudge, and peace, communication, and development would’ve been possible. However, if we hadn’t started the war, how would we have been able to survive? How could the humiliation of our race be washed away? How could the glory of the past be restored?

Li Yao thought for a long time and was rendered speechless.

“Admit it. Aggression and the desire to conquer are the most precious things for a civilization. A civilization that has lost its aggressiveness and the desire to conquer is like a louse or a castrated pet dog. It has no dignity, no pride, and no meaning of existence at all. Compared to forcibly depriving a civilization of its aggressiveness and achieving false peace through brainwashing, it is more satisfying and dignified to destroy the civilization directly.

The voice said, “Survival of the fittest. Such a rule is still valid in the universe. We and them have no other options.

“If—”

Li Yao took a deep, deep breath on the level of his soul. “What if I don’t want to choose the ‘Great Extinction Order’?”

“Then—”

The voice asked, “Do you have a better way?”

“No.”

Li Yao said, “I’m not a wise man, nor am I the leader of a broad-minded universe. I’m just an ordinary human being. I can’t think of a better way, but I’m not willing to make such… the worst choice, either. It is like I would rather starve to death than kill my own kind and eat their meat even if I can’t find anything to eat.

“But the alien species and the low-level civilizations are not our kind at all.

The voice said, “It is understandable that you are not willing to abandon your compatriots on billions of Earths in your first mistake, but your persistence right now is completely unreasonable. Are you going to project your brilliance that is as brilliant as the Virgin Mary on things such as the ‘Void Hunters’ and the ‘Mountain Giant Crab’? Even the hideous monsters that look like devils are objects of your overwhelming sympathy?

Li Yao was silent.

He did not know what he was trying to do.

Maybe because he had been an orphan since childhood, although he seemed to enjoy solo adventures, he was particularly scared of loneliness.

He liked the joy of being surrounded by his family and friends, and he liked the feeling of fighting for everyone even more!

The sea of stars was too vast and too dark and cold. It would be too lonely if the civilization of mankind was the only thing that existed.

If a civilization existed alone, who would it show its brilliance to? What was the significance of the persistence, progress, creation, and prosperity of a civilization? Even if such a civilization could exist forever, was it really ‘happily’?

However, the voice did have a point. The war had broken out, and the damage had been dealt. At the very least, there had been no evilness in the war in the beginning. Even Li Yao, as stubborn as he was, could not say that there was anything wrong with the struggle of the Primordial Beginning Clan for their own survival.

The war had reached the point of extinction. The seeds of anger, hatred, and suspicion had been planted in the hearts of all the survivors. How was the dark forest bound by the chains of suspicion going to be resolved?

Li Yao could not understand what was going on.

The voice was surprisingly silent for a long time. When it appeared again, it was apparently tired.

“I’m sorry. I’ve wasted a lot of my computational ability on other ‘testing fields’.

The voice said, “Another tester has failed.”

“What?”

Li Yao was so shocked that he almost jumped up. “Who is it? Is it—”

“Rest assured. It is not your own kind, but the Nuwa Clan named ‘Long Lianzi’.

The voice said, “The subspecies named ‘Nuwa’ is very similar to the aggressive and high-spirited Primeval Clan. It’s a pity that they are too undisciplined and sentimental. They lack the ruthlessness and tenacity to survive no matter what. In the end, they were wiped out by the counterattack of the alien species in space.

Li Yao sighed with mixed feelings.

The two commanders of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan were both godlike figures. If it were a different time and situation, they both had the potential to be the dominators of an area and fight against him for three hundred rounds.

He did not expect that he would die so quickly and miserably in the ultimate test.

“Then…”

His mouth was dry. He didn’t know whether or not he should ask, and he didn’t know whether or not his voice would answer. “How are my companions?”

“Rest assured. Up until now, your compatriots have been performing quite well. After all, in terms of genes, you are all 100% Primeval Beings. You just haven’t activated all your abilities yet.

The voice said, “Two of the testers performed exceptionally well and have surpassed the level of Dustless. It is very likely that they will pass the test and receive the heritage of our race.

“Really?”

Li Yao was overjoyed. “Which two?”

“In your names…”

The voice replied, “Lu Qingchen and the mental devil.”

“What?”

“Especially Lu Qingchen.

His voice couldn’t conceal his satisfaction and admiration. “Compared to you, he is simply a natural-born leader. He is a commander-in-chief who can lead his clansmen to lurk and explore in the darkness of ten thousand years and soar into the sky in the end to conquer the universe. During the entire process of evolution and conquest, he barely made any mistakes. He didn’t need my advice at all. Even before the test system came up with the strategy of ‘abandoning 95% of the Earthlings’ and ‘Great Extinction Order’, he had already thought of it on his own. Moreover, he was much more thorough and resolute than us. The series of strategies and arrangements he showed in the war of conquest also proved that he was absolutely capable of making good use of our heritage and was one of the most suitable candidates to fight against the Flood Tide.

“The mental devil was not bad, either. Although it was not as good as Lu Qingchen in regard to the big picture and leadership, it was even better in regard to strategical schemes and brutality. He did not make any mistakes and made the choice to ‘abandon most of the Earthlings’ and the ‘Obliteration Order’ without any hesitation.

“Right now, the two testers are in the lead and about to reach the finish line. If you want the legacy of the Primeval Civilization, you will have to try your best!

Chapter 3111: Too Ashamed To Show His Face

“How is that possible?”

Li Yao was greatly shocked. “The two guys who were as ugly as pigs and as despicable as dogs, who had been beaten and suppressed by me time and time again, were able to pass the test earlier than me? Are you really not lying to me?”

“I don’t have to lie to you.

The voice said, “I’ve existed for too long. Billions of years are enough for the flood tide to search every ripple and corner of the sea of the universe. It is not far away from its arrival.

“Although I cannot lower the standard of the test just because the flood is imminent, I do hope that a real heir can show up as soon as possible and complete the test that has lasted billions of years. I’m not willing to destroy more testers.

“Then—”

Li Yao’s soul was shivering violently like a torch that was about to be burnt out in a storm. “Can you tell me, if Lu Qingchen or the mental devil pass the test first, will they really receive the legacy of the Primeval Civilization? What are the so-called ‘ancient secret treasures’ exactly? Can they really command the entire Pangu Universe and dominate our civilization?”

“There are a lot of things beyond your imagination in the legacy of the Primeval Civilization.

His voice was neither too fast nor too slow. “As far as you can understand, if Lu Qingchen or one of the mental devils becomes the heir of the Primeval Civilization, they will get a new body first.

“I’ve noticed that they do not have bodies of flesh and blood. Instead, they exist in the form of special energy waves and unstable magnetic fields. Although the form has a lot of benefits, it is not very convenient for them to move in the three-dimensional universe made of materials.

“Within the technological limits of the Primeval Civilization, it is not a big problem. They can find a new body in our warehouse of war machines at their will, be it carbon-based or silicon-based, made of flesh and blood, or made of precise machines and the most powerful crystals, or even bodies of alien species such as the ‘Void Hunters’ and the ‘Mountain Giant Crab’. Together with the most advanced biochemical technology of the Primeval Civilization and the attack units made of machines and metals, they will become the most terrifying emperors of the universe. We have such technology, too.

“Other than that, we have fully-automatic military bases buried deep inside almost every habitable planet and resource planet in the Pangu Universe. They can be collected, smelted, and refined automatically. With enough energy and resources, we can produce a steady stream of battle puppets and even an army of warships. There is no need to manipulate them manually. One brainwave from the commander-in-chief is enough to run them until death.

“Of course, I believe that it won’t be a problem even if they need to recruit a lot of manpower. As long as their identity as the ‘Inheritor of the Primeval Civilization’ is made public and they arrive in the magnificent, sky-blocking bodies of the deities and devils, most of your compatriots will worship them and listen to their every command.

“What’s wrong? It seems that you don’t want the two of them to inherit the legacy of the Primeval Civilization?”

“That’s right.”

Li Yao managed to say, “However, I don’t want to see the Primeval Civilization falling into the abyss of horror, turning into the cruel butchers, or a bunch of space cockroaches!

“Your stupidity is regrettable, and your persistence is admirable.

The voice said, “Then, if I tell you that, on a certain level, the remnants of the alien species and the low-level civilizations are part of the ‘flood’ that will destroy the Primeval Civilization in the near future, will you change your mind?”

“What?”

Li Yao was greatly shocked. He grasped the key of the voice keenly. “Are you saying that the so-called flood is the coalition army of the alien species and the low-level civilizations against the Primeval Civilization? But what do you mean by ‘on a certain level’? What is the flood exactly?”

“I can’t tell you exactly what a flood is, because it is impossible for you to see its full appearance and horror with your current understanding ability. But I can guarantee that I’m not lying to you, because providing false information will make you make false judgments, which is inconsistent with the purpose of the test.

The voice said, “All in all, in the real history, the ‘Great Extinction Order’ for the alien species and the low-level civilizations, due to various objective factors, mainly because of the vastness of the sea of stars and the inefficiency of information transmission, was not implemented at 100%, and a lot of fish escaped the net. Maybe it was the fish that formed the flood tide and overthrown the reign of the Primeval Civilization.

“Therefore, in the ultimate test, if the ‘Obliteration Order’ had been issued earlier and implemented more resolutely, the fate of the Primeval Civilization might have been reversed.

“A long time later, on the road of collapse and escape, the remaining experts and wise men of the Primeval Civilization ran thousands of simulations. They all believed that the period around the issuance of the ‘Great Extinction Order’ was the most critical turning point for the Primeval Civilization. If they missed the best opportunity, there would be no power to stop the rise of the flood tide and the destruction of our race.

“If you want to continue the test, to receive our heritage, to save your people, and to defend your civilization, then make a choice… that you have no other options!”

The voice contained infinite magical power that corroded Li Yao’s soul silently.

For the first time, Li Yao was caught in such confusion and hesitation.

The vivid, immersive pictures were shuttling in his brain and interweaving into a complicated mess.

If he did not choose the ‘Great Extinction Order’, the ultimate test would most likely fail. By then, either Lu Qingchen would get the ancient secret treasures or all the human beings in the Pangu Universe would be obliterated.

However, if he chose the ‘Great Extinction Order’, what was the difference between him and Lu Qingchen? Would the gradually growing scales completely engulf him and turn him from a ‘dragon slayer’ into a ‘dragon’?

If he had received the legacy of the Primeval Civilization in such a way, would he have been able to control the legacy instead of being enslaved by it and becoming a duplicate of the Primeval Race?

No…

He was not a duplicate of the Primeval Race. He was a new human being!

However, the problem could not be solved with just a few drops of hot blood and a few roars. Ding Lingdang and the others were still taking the test. If he didn’t break through soon, sooner or later, Ding Lingdang and the others would also encounter such a difficult and dangerous choice. And with Ding Lingdang’s unyielding personality, she would absolutely not compromise with the voice.

Then, would Ding Lingdang follow in the footsteps of ‘Exterminate All’ and ‘Dragon Lotus Seed’? Would she be obliterated by the sound and turn into ten thousand burning butterflies?

The vivid scenes lingered in Li Yao’s brain, making his every brain cell shiver.

There was also the truth of the flood, as well as the biggest secret in Li Yao’s heart—the ‘Earth’ that he was born in. It seemed that all the answers could not be unlocked until he passed the ultimate test.

What should he do exactly? What should he do?

Although he had never been faced with a real, real, invincible enemy and only a seemingly simple test, Li Yao had never felt so lonely and helpless before.

He wished that he had a few comrades who could fight side by side with him. He wished that his seniors such as Jin Tuyi or Boss Bai could give him some pointers. He hoped that his bad friends such as the mental devil and Long Yangjun could ease his anxiety by chitchatting with him. It would be best if Ding Lingdang could appear next to him right now and punch his shoulder heavily so that he would not have to think about anything else and could simply follow his heart. However, he did not. His family and friends were not around. He was the only one facing the deep universe and the world inside his heart that was deeper than the universe.

Cultivators. Li Yao had once thought that he had reached the ultimate level of the best expert of the human civilization in the Pangu Universe after one to two hundred years of Cultivation. He had even practiced three thousand reincarnations in the world of mortals in the virtual space, and he had seen through his heart a long time ago. It was not until this moment that he realized that he was wrong. He was terribly wrong. The mind of a man was immeasurable. Even if he spent his entire life searching, who would dare boast that they could truly see through his heart?

Nobody knew what they would do until the ‘no other choice’ arrived.

Li Yao even regretted his choice before he made it.

But he still chose to continue the test for… everything.

Inside his brain, the slaughtering machine of the Primeval Civilization started functioning. Blood dyed the entire sea of stars red.

Such was the contradiction of human beings. A moment ago, Li Yao had been deeply awed by the magnificent army of the Primeval Civilization and the soul-stirring rise of the civilization. He felt honored and complimented them. But right now, he felt that the compatriots who looked exactly like himself were so cruel and terrifying.

Just now, when the ‘Void Hunters’ and the ‘Mountain Giant Crab’ devoured and destroyed the Primeval Lord of Heaven as if they were ants, Li Yao had felt extremely helpless and furious, too. He had been looking forward to the day when the Primeval Lord of Heaven would counterattack and soar into the sky.

But when the day came and the cubs of the alien species in space were all shivering under the bombardment of the Primeval Civilization, when their nests and all traces of their existence were obliterated, Li Yao felt pain and regret beyond description.

Many individuals of the low-level civilization looked hideous or cunning. Li Yao was wary and disgusted when he saw them. But when they were wounded, bleeding, or charging at the most advanced war machines of the Primeval Civilization in order to protect their family and home, their fearlessness aroused the surging tides in Li Yao’s heart, too. Li Yao was too ashamed to look them in the eyes.

“It… shouldn’t be like this.

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “What—what have I done exactly?”

Chapter 3112: Fighting Back!

In Li Yao’s vision, the hot blood turned into stinky pus. The noble warriors turned into brutal butchers. The warriors who had fought for survival and glory sank into the abyss of desire one by one. In the countless slaughters and destruction, the last bit of brightness in their hearts was wiped out, too.

The sea of stars was too vast and too dark. The journey of the Primeval Lord of Heaven was too far away. Everybody lost their way and forgot where they originated from. They did not know where their destination was, either.

They had been drowned in the blood of the innocent and corrupted by too much radiation. They had been deformed beyond recognition and completely forgotten the reason for the war. Driven by the instinct of killing that had been decided billions of years ago, they were slaves of genes and burnt, killed, conquered, and destroyed!

The process lasted a long, long time. The Primeval Civilization expanded and expanded into countless ‘subunits’ in the universe that were similar to the Pangu Universe.

According to the features and rules of the subunits, they were greatly influenced by the local natives, too. The Primeval Lord of Heaven was in various forms, too.

Some of the sub-universe’s spiritual energy was mainly in the form of ‘ripples’. Some of the sub-universe’s spiritual energy was in the form of ‘particles’. The most efficient way to utilize spiritual energy in different forms determined the evolution of the Primeval Lord of Heaven.

The spiritual energy of certain sub-universes was relatively pure and violent. It was hardly utilized by the war machines. On the contrary, it could be absorbed and released directly by the human body. A training system named ‘battle aura’ was born in the sub-universes, with a series of dazzling combat skills and the likes.

As for the sub-universe, as Li Yao knew, the barriers between the three-dimensional space and the four-dimensional space were not very solid. They could absorb the power of the four-dimensional space easily, and a training system named ‘magic’ was born. Cultivators of spiritual energy fought with the help of the supreme power of the four-dimensional storms.

Of course, there were also the Ancients who insisted on ‘technology’ and claimed to be the rightful rulers of the civilization.

In some of the more remote sub-universes, the Primeval Lord of Heaven was best at taming alien species in space and turning apocalyptic beasts into their mounts. They could even find a way to meld with the apocalyptic beasts and turn them into powerful biochemical warriors such as the ‘Beast God Soldiers’ and the ‘Dragon God Generals’.

In the remote, remote universes, the ‘Obliteration Order’ was not implemented effectively. People castrated the alien species and the low-level civilizations according to a different method according to the voice but kept them alive.

The Primeval Lord of Heaven’s conquest of the Universe Ocean was not something that could be accomplished overnight. The millions of years of conquest and the vast distance were enough for the civilization of the Primeval Lord of Heaven to be divided into hundreds of different political entities whose strength systems and even appearances were completely different. However, everyone acknowledged that they were the Primeval Lord of Heaven and that they had come from an ancient blue planet a long time ago—Earth.

After a long period of division and alienation, it was impossible for the Primeval Race in the various sub-universes to be united forever. There were minor conflicts among them, too. However, when they were faced with the alien species and the Primeval Civilization, their attitude was the same, which was ‘conquer and destroy’.

“Wait—”

Li Yao had always felt that something was stuck in his throat. At this moment, he finally threw out the doubt in the bottom of his heart. “There’s one thing that I can’t figure out. According to the characteristics of the sub-universe, the Primeval Civilization will develop different evolution directions, social forms, and strength systems. The appearance of the ‘Cultivator Universe’, the ‘Magical Universe’, the ‘Battle Aura Universe’, the ‘Mechanical Universe’, the ‘Technology Universe’, and so on are all natural and easy to understand.

“I assume that my ancestors, namely you, came from a certain ‘Cultivation Universe’?

“My question is, why is it that most of the residents of the ‘Cultivation Universe’ have black hair and black eyes, while the ‘Magical Universe’ has blonde hair and blue eyes? It doesn’t make any sense!

“After hundreds of thousands of years to millions of years of conquest, the bloodlines of the ancient Earth have been diluted to almost zero. The concepts such as nationalities should not still exist, should they? Everyone has abandoned the concepts of ancient nationalities and bloodlines. They live together and give birth to a new species of human beings. The ‘Cultivation Universe’ also has a lot of residents with blonde hair and blue eyes. The ‘Magical Universe’ also has a lot of residents with black hair and black eyes. It is only reasonable.

Faced with Li Yao’s questioning, his voice remained silent.

Maybe, it had been challenged by hundreds of generations of testers, including Lu Qingchen and the mental devil, but it had never encountered such a problem before.

“Or is it…”

Li Yao’s soul, because of the burning of shame and anger, became hotter and sharper. “You have never forgotten the hatred on the ancient Earth. Or rather, in the wandering sea of stars, in the era when we were on the same boat and struggling for survival, you once forgot the ancient hatred. But as your strength continued to grow, you raised your butcher’s knife against all the carbon-based intelligent life in the universe. In the process of conquering and destroying, you destroyed your own ability to ‘trust’. You destroyed your ‘belief in kindness’ and your ‘pursuit of justice’. The more you conquered, the greedier you became. The more you were destroyed, the more terrified you became. At first, you feared the counterattack of the low-level civilizations and the alien races in space. Then, you feared your own kind—no, for the black-haired, black-eyed, and primitive people, are the golden-haired, blue-eyed, primitive people really your own kind? On the ancient Earth, in order to fight for the space to live, didn’t everyone fight to the death and almost destroy each other’s common homes? Now, although your home has expanded by billions of times, your ambitions have also expanded by billions of times. Who knows if the ‘World War IV’ will play out in an even crueler way in the endless sea of stars?

“The hearts of those who are not of our species must be different. That’s a good saying. But who are my species and who are the ‘outsiders’? When all the alien species and the low-level civilizations fall and become the ghosts that haunt the saber of the Primeval Lord of Heaven, the greatest threat will be each other, won’t it?

“It’s hilarious. I should’ve thought of such a simple logic a long time ago. You are trying to justify the expedition and the slaughter of the Primeval Lord of Heaven by asking the Homo sapiens to march out of Africa and expand to the entire Earth. But don’t forget that, even though human beings exterminated mammoths, saber-toothed tigers, and almost all the large animals, they did not usher in an eternal peace. On the contrary, an even more terrifying enemy appeared below the corpses of mammoths and saber-toothed tigers, which was mankind itself!

“The only thing that can destroy mankind is oneself. The saying is valid in the endless universe, isn’t it?

“The black-haired, black-eyed Primeval Lord of Heaven is deeply wary of the golden-haired, blue-eyed Primeval Lord of Heaven. The golden-haired, blue-eyed Primeval Lord of Heaven is also looking at the black-haired, black-eyed Primeval Lord of Heaven with the sharpest eyes. Even if one party has never thought of bringing up the past of the Earth Era, how can you be sure that the other party will not bring up the past and reignite the flames of war? At the end of the day, the old grudges of the Earth Era, including the differences in appearance, are just excuses. You have long lost your way in the bloody journey. You have become slaves of your killing instincts and become pitiful creatures bound by the chains of suspicion!

“In the end, is it really just a ‘tiny friction’ between two different universes? Or is it a ‘Primeval Civil War’ that is ten times more intense than a war of conquest?

“Do you remember what you said in the beginning? Most of the prosperous civilizations did not perish because of foreign invasions, but because of internal strife when they reached the limits of expansion. The civilizations that lived in the Pangu Universe for hundreds of generations were the same. Was the Primeval Civilization not the same?

“Therefore, I made a bold assumption that, after conquering most of the alien species and the low-level civilizations, the Primeval Civilization split into hundreds of different forces and waged a brutal civil war. It is appropriate to call it the ‘Fourth World War’.

“This war has completely destroyed the trust between the two of you. The suspicion and wariness between the two of you have been raised to the extreme, to the point that even the slightest difference between the two of you is unbearable. During this period, countless checks and purges must have taken place. All the fellows who are not of my race have been purged. The entire ‘Cultivator Universe’ only has black hair and black eyes, while the ‘Magical Universe’ only has blonde hair and blue eyes… Tell me, is that right? Tell me!”

There was a long silence.

“That’s not important.”

It said, its voice was extremely hoarse. It was apparently an answer that had never been heard in billions of years.

“No. This is very important.

Li Yao smiled miserably. “If that’s the case, the greatest enemy of the Primeval Clan is the Primeval Clan, and the greatest enemy of mankind is mankind. Then, what is the significance of the so-called ‘Great Obliteration Order’?

“On the other hand, I believe that such a brutal, super-civil war does exist. The civil war has cut off the bonds between every sub-universe. The close comrades-in-arms who used to be as close as brothers turned into strangers or even enemies again. After a long time of fermenting, the so-called ‘Primeval Civilization’ only existed in name. It evolved into countless alien and vigilant independent civilizations. Maybe they still remember the blue planet and the story of Earth, but it is already an ancient legend and an imaginary myth.

“In the end, when the ‘flood’ really rose up and lunged at you murderously, the so-called ‘Primeval Civilization’ could no longer be united against the enemy and could only be swallowed by the tides one after another. I’m even certain that, when a certain ‘magical universe’ was swallowed by the tides, the ‘Cultivators’ in the ‘Cultivators’ universe who had fought against it were still laughing and clapping their hands!

“The starting point of all this is the ‘Great Extinction Order’. It can even be traced back to the time when you abandoned 95% of your compatriots on Earth. From that moment on, you have thrown away what you are most proud and protective of as human beings. You have thrown away the soul of the civilization on Earth and turned into space cockroaches in human skin!

Chapter 3113: Super Bee Swarm!

Faced with Li Yao’s berating, the voice was silent for a long, long time. It even gave Li Yao the feeling that it was running away.

The voice… More precisely speaking, the Primeval Beings who had established the program billions of years ago had never thought that such a bold tester would appear one day and castigate them.

Li Yao heard vague sighs inside his brain, as if the ghosts of the Yuanshi Clan were sighing in regret.

“You may have a point, but it is too late.

Finally, he managed to say, “The mistake that was made thousands of years ago has never been corrected. We can only walk on the bloody, thorny road without looking back.

“In order to correct a mistake, the best time is the moment when the mistake takes place, followed by the present billions of years later!

Li Yao said resolutely, “Even if it was a mistake made by your predecessors, you never tried to change or make up for it. How do you know you can’t?”

“Because there is no time.

The voice smiled bitterly. “Just like the Primeval Civilization did not give the alien species and the low-level civilizations time to evolve, the flood did not give us the opportunity to change or make up for it at all. The terrifying flood swept across the entire universe almost instantly and spread to every sub-universe ruled by the Primeval Civilization.

“Oh?”

Li Yao was refreshed. “This should be the final test, right?”

A new challenge had begun in his brain.

As he expected, although the territory of the Primeval Civilization had expanded billions of times and almost covered the entire Universe Ocean, the resources and fleets that he could control had shrunk. Also, judging from the picture, the planets that used to be prosperous and peaceful had all been savaged by the flames of war and seemed to be in dire straits.

It was apparently because of the fourth world war, the super civil war of the Primeval Civilization, which led to the ‘great division’ and ‘great decline’.

Since the branch of the Yuanshi that had fled to the Pangu Universe came from the ‘Cultivation Universe’ with black hair and black eyes, Li Yao could only manipulate the branch of the Yuanshi to deal with the challenges. The challenges were mostly related to the sub-universe nearby. The development of the ‘Magical Universe’ and the ‘Battle Aura Universe’ and even real-time communication had been blocked by the fierce war and the long distance.

No one knew the origin of the flood.

Maybe it was because several different Guilds of Provenance developed new ‘ultimate weapons’ during the civil war and lost control.

Maybe it was because a certain alien species or a low-level civilization that had been driven to the edge of the sea of stars by the Primeval Civilization had finally experienced a technological explosion, or maybe it was because the so-called ‘Children of Destiny’, unparalleled experts that could not be seen in billions of years, had emerged and led the civilization, leading the drastic mutations that had taken place in only several hundred years. Then, they had united all the other intelligent carbon-based creatures who had lost their homes, family, and glory into a terrifying coalition army.

Of course, it was also possible that the expansion of the Primeval Civilization had significantly changed the sea of the universe and finally attracted the interest of a certain powerful existence beyond the sea of the universe, which cast an intimidating shadow on the sea of the universe.

In short, because of the isolation of information and distance, when the branch of the voice discovered the ‘flood’, the trend of destruction had already been established. The flood had become an unstoppable force.

It was hard to tell what the true form of the tide was. Most of the time, it appeared in the form of an invisible mist that blocked the sky and the earth, and it was the subordinates and claws of the tide, or the so-called ‘Flood Tide Legion’, that brought slaughter and destruction.

It was not hard to guess that the alien species and the low-level civilizations that had to flee to the edge of the sea of stars because their homes had been destroyed by the Primeval Civilization were the main force of the Flood Tide Legion.

They had been defeated by the Primeval Civilization in the first place, and there were only a few of them left. Logically speaking, they were not enough to deal fatal damage to the Primeval Civilization. The Primeval Civilization even hoped that the remaining soldiers could be gathered so that they could catch all of them.

But the alien species and the low-level civilizations who had come for revenge with the flood tide were no longer as weak and stupid as they used to be. They had grasped advanced technology, extraordinary tactics, and a continuous industrial production system. They even had their own starships, which were even more mysterious, weird, and powerful!

What the Primeval Lord of Heaven feared most had happened.

Sure enough, their enemy had experienced a ‘technology explosion’. In only several thousand years or even hundreds of years, the technological gap between the two parties had been closed, and they were now even closer to the Primeval Civilization.

The alien species in space boasted tremendous individual combat ability in the first place. Together with the enhancement of high-tech technology and industrialization, the combat potential that they boasted was too much for the Primeval Civilization to deal with. Even though they occupied the most prosperous sub-universe and abundant resources in the sea of stars, and they had an initial army that was hundreds of times larger than that of the Flood Tide Legion, the Primeval Civilization could not be united. They were all loose sand and did their own things. The war was extremely difficult.

As the battle went on, another shocking truth was discovered—the Flood Tide Legion was mixed with a lot of the Primeval Clan, who had provided a lot of technologies to the alien species and the low-level civilizations and helped them realize the ‘technology explosion’!

The unbelievable news was like a bomb that was tossed into the hearts of all the self-proclaimed ‘Supreme Beings of the Universe’.

They could not figure out why their compatriots would degrade themselves, cooperate with the alien species and the low-level civilizations, and even transmit valuable technology to each other to deal with their own compatriots.

Even though an earthshaking civil war had just broken out and the flames of war in many sub-universes had not died down yet, there was no way that they could cooperate with the alien species and the low-level civilizations. Wouldn’t that be digging their own graves?

The Primeval Civilization had captured some ‘betrayers’, too, who had joined the Flood Tide Legion.

They had thought that the Primeval Beings joined the Flood Tide Legion because of the hatred of the civil war or because of conflicts of interest. But the results of their interrogation and research were far from that.

It seemed that the defectors who joined the tide, as well as the alien species and the low-level civilizations, had been brainwashed by a mysterious yet invincible existence and believed that they were part of the tide. They had been connected to a certain enormous network or consciousness and formed a new civilization of bees!

From the moment they joined the Flood Tide, the hatred of the past had been forgotten. The estrangement between the two species was gone, too. They were no longer independent entities but tiny cells inside the Flood Tide. Everything was for the Flood Tide, which was above everything else. All the technology, techniques, and information could be shared without any reservation.

“I see!”

Li Yao suddenly realized why the voice hated the ‘swarm civilization’ so much. The flood was actually a strengthened version of the bee civilization!

However, the ordinary civilization of the bees, such as the ‘Houyi Clan’, originated from the same species of intelligent creatures on the same mother planet. They could only mutate after absorbing the genes of other species, but it was difficult for them to transform other species into their own.

On the other hand, the tides could be transformed and absorbed regardless of their species. It seemed that they retained the natural talents and wisdom of their original species. On second thought, if they had the techniques and wisdom of a thousand or even ten thousand different species, they would be invincible!

“The most terrifying thing about the flood is the power of brainwashing and transformation.

He gnashed his teeth, but there was nothing he could do about it. “If they are just the coalition of the defectors, the alien species, and the low-level civilizations, they are just a bunch of mobs. No matter how divided we are, we can always crush them.

“But the Flood Tide is not an enemy in the conventional sense at all. It is a virus, a plague, and a burning flame! Often, after a protracted battle is over, after we’ve achieved a difficult victory at the cost of countless sacrifices, we will find that a lot of our own people have been corrupted by the Flood Tide and become members of the Flood Tide Legion. They will input all our technology and secrets into the Flood Tide, leading to the collapse of the rear defense line. How are we going to fight the war if such a collapse is repeated thousands of times?

Along with the lamentation, countless planets under the control of the Primeval Civilization appeared in Li Yao’s mind.

Li Yao had mixed feelings when he saw such scenes. He didn’t know where to stand.

The war in which the Primeval Civilization was corrupted by the tides was like a replica of the war between the Star Glory Federation and the Star Ocean Imperium—or rather, an enhanced version.

The expansion of the Primeval Civilization to the entire Universe Ocean was not glamorous at all. It was even cruel and brutal.

However, during the prolonged colonization and development, the different species of the Primeval Race had developed a new civilization in the ever-changing sub-universe. The new generation born and raised in this place had the right to live a happy life and free will, too.

Li Yao could not bring himself to say that the new generation of the Primeval Clan born in such an environment must be carrying some sort of ‘original sin’.

Even if they were really going to repent for everything that their ancestors had done and ‘abandon their civilization, abandon their free will and self-awareness, join the flood tide, and become a cell in the flood tide’, such a punishment was too miserable and unreasonable!

At this moment, Li Yao finally understood why the civilization of the Fist King, including the ‘voice’ in front of him, was so scared of the arrival of the flood.

It was true that free will was the most precious thing for a human being. If he had been in the situation at that time, he might have scorned the actions of the Primeval Civilization. But he would rather die than join the flood. He would definitely fight against the enormous ‘hive’ until the end!

Fourthly, human beings would never be slaves.

Chapter 3114: Real And Fake Testing?

Li Yao was faced with the toughest challenge.

If the reenactment of the Primeval Civilization’s conquest of the Universe Ocean was a game of ‘no matter what you do, you will never fail. When you meet an enemy, you only need to gather ten times the number of soldiers to crush them’. The battle against the tide right now was tantamount to the difficulty of the game being upgraded to ‘hell’.

Even if he maximized his poor operation and commanding ability, squeezed out every resource under his control, and inspired the courage of every Primeval Lord of Heaven, even if he finally united more than ten wary sub-universes with his glib tongue, even if he made every choice right and made the Primeval Lord of Heaven evolve again under the pressure of the flood, even if the legendary technology and training system broke out in only a few hundred years, it would still not change the final outcome.

A typical battle between the Primeval Civilization and the flood tide often began when countless bizarre, nightmarish monsters suddenly appeared in the periphery of the galaxy controlled by the Primeval Civilization.

The nightmare beasts were based on the enormous bodies of the alien species in the past and based on the cutting-edge technology of the Primeval Civilization. They were a perfect combination of flesh and metal and could wield even more terrifying power than a starship.

They were often followed by the servants of countless low-level civilizations. With the help of flood modification and traitors, the low-level civilizations boasted strong reproductive ability and a social system in the industrial era. They were highly organized and well-disciplined.

Even if the warriors of the Primeval Civilization rose up and paid a heavy price in order to defeat the enemy, it would be to no avail, because they would soon be infected or brainwashed and lose their self-awareness. They would be connected to the network of the Flood Tide and turn into a tiny cell of the Flood Tide Legion!

Just like that, one planet after another fell into the abyss, and one galaxy after another was drowned by the tides. From the sub-universe to the lower-level universe, the splendid brilliance of the Primeval Civilization was eclipsed by the flood.

The tide was like an expanding sponge that was about to occupy and swallow the entire sea of the universe!

Li Yao was both mentally and physically exhausted.

All the choices were wrong.

All hope was lost.

There was no force that could stop the flood. Those who were unwilling to be ‘flood cells’ in the sub-universe did not even have the time to run away. They could only watch an outcome that was even more unacceptable than destruction befall them.

In the end, almost all the options were shattered.

For the third time, a ‘only option’ popped up in front of Li Yao’s eyes.

It was the Sun Defense Line Plan.

After the flood swept almost half of the sea of stars, the members of the Primeval Clan scattered in the various sub-units finally woke up and joined hands again. Just like the earthlings who were faced with the meteoroid disaster in the past, they put aside their hatred and worked together for the common purpose of survival.

Perhaps only when mankind and the Primeval Beings were faced with an irresistible threat could they truly unite and trigger the wisdom and courage in the deepest part of human nature. It was like a curse or a cruel joke.

After studying the features of the flood tide in depth and deducing countless battle plans, the Primeval Clan, which had been united again, proposed the ‘Sun Defense Line’ plan.

Stars, scorching light balls, the origin of life, the most furious energy in the universe, and the most powerful weapons!

With the scourge of the tide as the boundary, thousands of stars nearby would be detonated in one breath, and thousands of destructive torrents would crash into the tide!

Even such an impact was not enough to deal fatal damage to the flood. But if all the stars nearby were destroyed, a large area of ‘no man’s land’ where spiritual gas was extremely thin, resources were scarce, and there was not even the tiniest bit of brightness would be created!

Although they did not know what the Flood Tide was made of, the Flood Tide Legion was an out-and-out intelligent, carbon-based life form. Even if they turned into one of the cells of the Flood Tide, they would still need to absorb a lot of energy and a complete and complicated ecological system in order to survive. Without a star, it would be impossible to establish a large-scale ecological system. The man-made ecological system on a few starships was simply not enough to satisfy such a large-scale Flood Tide Legion. In the end, a large batch of Flood Tide Legion would starve to death.

Of course, with the help of the traitors, the Flood Tide Legion had grasped the advanced technology of space jumps, too. But the technology of space jumps was based on the stability of the three-dimensional universe and the precise coordinates. The energy waves resulting from the destruction of thousands of stars were enough to shake the entire sea of stars. Accurate and safe space jumps would not be performed for a long time. At the very least, large-scale, organized space jumps would be suicidal!

In other words, the so-called ‘solar defense line’ was a replica of the ‘scorched earth strategy’ and the ‘iron wall defense line’ in the war on Earth. On one hand, they were trying to empty all the resources in the range of the enemy’s attack, and on the other hand, they were building an impregnable defense line that would block the enemy and trap them inside!

“Detonate thousands of stars and form a defense line of destruction, darkness, and defense!

Li Yao was deeply shocked by the marvelous technology and craziness of the Primeval Civilization.

Although he also believed that intelligent life could only be called a ‘civilization’ if it made unerasable changes to the universe, such changes were still too unbelievable and shocking.

Was this the most furious roar of a god-level civilization at the moment of life and death?

“Yes. This is my last and only suggestion for you.

The voice said, “Although the odds of success are not very high, it is the only effective way for us to strike the flood. Even if we cannot destroy the flood, we can at least stall it for a while. If we stall it for billions of years, we will find new hope in the billions of years to come.

Li Yao was silent. He studied the option carefully.

Before he read the detailed explanation of the ‘Sun Defense Line Plan’, he had already found the third ‘right answer’ repulsive and repulsive because he had no other options.

When he browsed through the files of the thousands of stars that were about to be destroyed, including the planets that were affiliated to them, Li Yao was greatly shocked.

“I’ve noticed that the thousands of stars that you want to destroy are all located in the sub-units in the center of the sea of stars where the spiritual gas is extremely dense. In other words, they are all ‘paradise’ that are full of vitality and colors, right?

Li Yao asked.

“Yes. Those places are the main targets of the flood. Only by destroying them in advance can the flood be stopped.

The voice replied.

“Alright. Let’s not talk about whether or not destroying almost half of the natural paradises in the center of the universe is more evil than the scourge of tides. Let’s just talk about all the primitive demons living in the sub-universes.

Li Yao said, “Judging from the time of the arrival of the flood, it must’ve been tens of thousands of years, if not hundreds of thousands of years, since a lot of the Primeval Lord Clan migrated to the sub-universes and evolved into different civilizations. They thrived in the sub-universes and built the sub-universes as their new home. They spread to almost every corner of the universe.

“Although the ancestors of the Primeval Beings were cold-blooded butchers, it is very likely that they were civilians who did not even have the strength to truss a chicken. It is also possible that they were kind-looking old people, children who learned to talk, and men and women who lived a peaceful and happy life. Their entire life, including the civilization consolidated by billions of lives, was based on the illumination of the stars.

“My question is, what are you going to do with your ‘compatriots’ after you destroy thousands of suns?

“Should we send them all to other places on the starship?

“Let’s not talk about whether or not they are willing to leave their new home, which has been built for tens of thousands of years. Let’s just talk about the number of them, which is far beyond ‘billion’, shall we? Do you have such a powerful transportation ability that can transport everyone and support the daily consumption or hibernation of so many people? I doubt it.

“It must be noted that the center of the Universe Ocean has been occupied by the flood tide. You can only run to the edge of the Universe Ocean, a remote village that lacks spiritual energy. You don’t even know where your destination is. It is impossible for you to bring so many civilians with you, right?

“In the end, what happened to most of the civilians?

The voice fell silent again.

“You are right. The defense line of the sun will detonate a lot of natural paradises and destroy the new homes that countless people have been working on for tens of thousands of years. Besides… we don’t have enough transportation and resources to bring all our compatriots away.

The voice said, “That’s why I said that this is another ‘no other choice’. The flood tide is drawing close. If we don’t make a quick decision, a lot of our compatriots will fall into the hands of the flood tide. They will lose their most precious free will and become mindless walking dead, turning from the noble king of the universe into a tiny ‘cell’. Don’t you think that their dignity and pride will be preserved more if they die a painless death instantly?

The answer froze Li Yao’s soul instantly, turning him into a lifeless ocean of ice.

But a moment later, cracks appeared on the icy ocean, through which unconcealed laughter was echoing.

“Haha. Hahahaha!”

Li Yao laughed miserably, angrily, and ironically.

“What are you laughing at?”

The voice asked.

“I laughed at myself for being too silly. At first, I really thought that it was a test. I was wrong from the beginning, and I kept making mistakes and falling deeper and deeper. It was not until this moment that I realized what was going on.

Li Yao’s laughter came to an abrupt end. His soul waves were as sharp as blades as he said, “In fact, this is not a test at all, but ‘possession’ or ‘corrosion’ on a certain level, isn’t it?”

Chapter 3115: A Battle To The Death

“Why do you say that?”

The voice said, “It is a piece of cake for us to corrupt the soul of the tester. If our purpose was so simple, we could’ve corrupted the tester tens of millions of years ago. Why did we wait so long?

“Is it really that easy?”

Li Yao sniffed and sneered again. “Then, do you dare to tell me the real purpose of the ‘Sun Defense Line Plan’? Is the seemingly magnificent plan really designed to destroy and block the flood just as you said?

“It’s impossible. Even the most ordinary galaxy has a hundred million stars, a billion stars, or even ten billion stars. Even the Primeval Civilization, which has developed to its peak, cannot destroy a billion stars in one breath, can it? As you said, your plan is to destroy ‘ten million stars’. Hehe. Compared to the astronomical number of stars, ‘ten million stars’ are nothing but a drop in the ocean!

“Is it really possible to seriously damage and block the flood, or even disrupt the stability of the entire sea of stars by destroying millions of stars? I doubt it very much.

“The Flood Tide Legion boasts a lot of ‘Void Hunters’ that can travel in the four-dimensional storms. They also boast technology that is as advanced as your space jump. No matter how large the scorched land you create is, the Flood Tide will be able to bypass it.

“Then, what is the real purpose of the ‘Sun Defense Line Plan’? I couldn’t figure it out in the beginning, but when you were about to make the necessary sacrifices again, I suddenly realized something.

“The so-called ‘necessary sacrifices’ must be the real purpose of the ‘Sun Defense Line Plan’.

“Since the very beginning, you have never thought of fighting to the death with the flood tide. Violence and cowardice are two sides of the same coin. As the dominators of the universe, you have long lost the tenacity and bravery that you once had when you were hibernating. However, you have been scared out of your wits by the overwhelming flood tide and are only thinking of fleeing.

“However, the escape will take time. Also, due to the lack of transportation and resources, it is impossible for you to take away all the primitive demons. What’s worse is that, if most of the civilians are left behind, they will be swallowed by the flood, which will increase the strength of the flood by a hundred times. Very soon, they will catch up to you.

“The so-called ‘Sun Defense Line Plan’ was implemented in such a way as to kill three birds with one stone.

“First of all, although detonating thousands of stars is not enough to deal heavy damage to the tide, it can indeed buy you millions of years of escape and development.

“Secondly, most of your compatriots will be buried in the flames of the shattered stars. They will not be captives of the tides, nor will they continue living with hatred for you.

“In the end, the ripples from the explosion of thousands of stars may not be enough to shake the stability of the entire sea of stars, but they will be enough to erase your traces of your escape. In the brilliant fireworks where thousands of supernova explosions are taking place at the same time, even the strongest exhaust flames of the fleeing fleet will leave no trace of radiation at all.

“Therefore, the ‘Solar Defense Line Plan’ is not a risky offensive plan at all, but a despicable and shameless escape plan.
