Your reaction is just a replica of the time when the rich and powerful on Earth were faced with a meteoroid disaster. You are abandoning 99% of your compatriots, the innocent and the weak, and walking away! This time, you are even more shameless than last time. You are going to ignite the hope that your compatriots rely on to help you stall the enemy and cover your tracks!

“Speak, scumbag. Don’t you love jabbering? Aren’t you best at concealing your cowardice and hypocrisy with righteousness? Tell me the truth!”

He was rendered speechless.

Squeak! Squeak! Squeak! Squeak! Squeak! Noises were echoing inside Li Yao’s brain, as if the programs that had been stored in the primeval secret treasures had run into the underlying logic and did not know how to respond.

“We… have no other options.

He repeated dryly, “We can’t just wait to be killed. We can only let the wisdom and the elites of our civilization go first in order to preserve the spark of our civilization. The rest of us are destined to die. We might as well give them a quick release and trade their sacrifices for the greatest benefits.

“Haha. Hahahaha. What a great ‘wise people and elites go first’. What a great ‘pleasant release’. What a great ‘trade with sacrifices for the greatest benefits’. Bah!”

Li Yao burst into laughter at first. Then he burst into fury. “I’ve heard enough of your nonsense. I’ve had enough of your corruption. I can’t hold back the ‘no other choice’ any longer! Let me, ‘Vulture Li Yao’, tell you my ultimate choice. If I were really the commander-in-chief of the Primeval Civilization at that time, I would’ve stayed with the strongest, bravest, and wisest adults of our civilization. We would’ve faced the tides and fought side by side until one of us was killed. We would’ve built an iron wall that was sturdier than the ‘solar defense line’ with our flesh, blood, and soul. We would’ve given the chance of survival to the weak and the children. Before they ran away safely, the tides wouldn’t have been able to step over our dead bodies. At the last moment, maybe we would’ve detonated the star, too. But all the people who made the ‘solar defense line plan’ would’ve stood with me in our new home and welcomed the ultimate glory!

“Therefore, you are not a qualified commander-in-chief.

The voice replied, “Just a hot-blooded boor.”

“Before I become a qualified commander-in-chief—”

Li Yao bellowed, “First of all, I am a qualified, real, human being with pride and integrity!

“Then, your resistance and escape plan will likely fail.

The voice said, “You will destroy the entire civilization.”

“Only your shameless, inhuman methods can destroy a civilization that was once great!

Li Yao roared, “The universe is vast and time is endless. If we look at it from the perspective of the universe, how can there be a civilization that can live forever in fifty billion years and a hundred billion years? If my civilization and I are about to fall, I only hope that we can blossom in the most brilliant brilliance! We will either live like human beings or die like human beings. We will never become zombies, viruses, and cockroaches in the sea of stars!

“Tester, you are wasting your precious last chance.

His voice turned stern. “If you continue to be so obstinate, it is very likely that you will be eliminated and your civilization obliterated.

Accompanied by the undisguised threat in his voice, the brilliant sea of stars inside Li Yao’s brain gradually boiled and turned into a venomous black swamp.

The black venom seemed to have condensed into overwhelming tentacles and smashed at Li Yao.

“If the soft approach doesn’t work, are we going to use the hard approach?”

Li Yao burst into laughter. Faced with the threat of the mysterious voice, his belief, which had just been contaminated by dust and dirt, suddenly turned clear and bright. All his hesitation, hesitation, and confusion were burnt away by his burning fighting will. “I was the one who was stubborn when I made the first two choices. Right now, I’m completely clear-headed!

“Your words are contradictory. If this is really a test for ‘different possibilities’, why don’t you let me make my own choice every time it comes to a critical juncture? You keep saying that ‘free will’ is the most important thing in the world. Why did you set up so many obstacles to interfere with my free will? Such a test is merely a replay of the real history from the human beings on Earth to the Primeval Civilization. Even if I follow your three suggestions and pass the entire test, I’m afraid that my belief and thinking patterns will be completely corrupted and assimilated by you, turning me into a copy of you. What kind of ‘civilization reboot’ is this?”

“You are just copies of us.

The voice waved the black tentacles inside Li Yao’s brain and raised a tsunami. “Don’t forget that our genes are identical!

“So what? Human beings share part of the same genes as cockroaches and maggots, but real human beings are never as dirty as cockroaches and as lowly as maggots!

Li Yao roared, “Genes and bodies of flesh and blood are merely carriers that carry our hearts. The heart is the most important thing! Even if we are 100% identical to your genes, we still possess wisdom, courage, morality, justice, and the determination to protect everything that you don’t have! We are your children and grandchildren, but we are definitely not duplicates of you. We are not duplicates of anybody else. Whether it is the Primeval Civilization, the ancient deities, or some other messy, unparalleled existence, we don’t care. We are who we are. We are the insignificant yet proud human beings of the new generation!”

“So—”

The thousands of black tentacles of the voice seemed to have opened countless eyes that looked like suckers at the same time. Death rays that looked like cosmic radiation were beaming out of every eye as they stared at Li Yao’s soul. “Are you determined to turn down our suggestions and be our enemy?”

“If the only ‘no other choice’ other than to become your enemy is to become your clones, claws, and slaves and live your life in your own way, living your life as ruthlessly as a zombie and a steel cockroach, what’s the point of such a life?

Li Yao burst into laughter. “That’s right. To hell with all the ‘no other choices’. It doesn’t matter whether it is the ‘Primeval Civilization’, the ‘Flood Tide’ that is even more terrifying than you, or other demons, devils, devils, ghosts, or even the ultimate existences in the four-dimensional space. Come on. The new human beings are waiting for your arrival. Let’s fight to the death!”

Chapter 3116: Still Testing?

Ten thousand golden flames of war leapt up from the deepest part of his brain and exploded into thousands of supernovas in midair, illuminating Li Yao’s entire sea of consciousness in a clear and splendid light.

Illuminated by the infinite brilliance, the voice—the body formed by billions of black tentacles of the Primeval Civilization—appeared so ugly, feeble, and hilarious.

It was stunned by Li Yao’s soul power.

It was shivering, as if it could not believe that its son billions of years later, a humble tester, dared to defy its will.

It wriggled and rolled its hideous eyes that were full of venom, as if it were considering new schemes.

“Even if you are truly unafraid of death…”

The voice said, “You should think about your wife, your civilization. Do you want all your family, friends, and home to be buried together with your recklessness?”

His words only made Li Yao hesitate for half a second.

Half a second later, Li Yao’s fighting will blossomed into an unstoppable brilliance again. It turned into an invincible saber and slashed at the black tentacles.

“Don’t try to scare me with my wife. I know what kind of person Ding Lingdang is better than you do. If she were standing next to me and faced with the same choice, she would certainly be more determined and resolute than me. Maybe she wouldn’t even hesitate to make her first choice!”

Li Yao condensed the flames of billions of telepathic thoughts into one finger and pointed at the voice, while he said brutally, “She is a more innocent and straightforward person than me. She will not be trapped by your fancy words or your cunningness. She will fight you to the end without any hesitation!

“If I abandon my principles, my belief that I’ve been training for hundreds of years, and my bottom line as a human being because of her, it will not only be my incompetence but also the greatest insult to her!

“I believe, I believe deeply, that she will approve of my choice right now and fight side by side with me until the last breath!

“No. Maybe right now, in another test field, she has seen through your scheme and declared war on you, hasn’t she? If so, as a man, how could I desert and leave her to face such a sordid and sordid you?

“You…”

The twitching of his voice grew more and more frantic and powerless. “Are you so confident in her?”

“Of course!”

Li Yao burst into laughter. “How can a couple crawl into the same bed if they don’t even trust each other?”

“…”

The voice was rendered speechless.

“Besides, I’m not just confident in Ding Lingdang. I’m also confident in Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, Li Jialing, and even the ‘Punching King’, ‘Small Ming’, and ‘Wen Wen’. I’m confident in my thousands of compatriots!

Li Yao’s voice was louder and louder, and the fire of his will was more and more dazzling, too. “In the past, although it was full of gloom, although we suffered setbacks, although we groped and crashed into each other countless times in the dark labyrinth, in the end, all my family, friends, and compatriots showed their brightest hearts, their most brilliant fighting will, and their greatest strength, which saved me, themselves, our common home, and us, the new civilization of mankind in the Pangu Universe, and the boundless brilliance of hope. This time, it is the same!

“As for the mental devil and Lu Qingchen, hehe, although they are evil, they are definitely not humble or stupid. I believe that they will not be willing to be your puppets and subordinates and give up all their pride and free will. They will certainly counterattack in their own way and show you the strength of the new human beings!

“You—you don’t even stand a one in a billion chance!

He roared, or rather, screamed, “You are like moths to a flame. You are asking for your own doom!

“I’ve said the same sentence countless times over the past hundred years. But every time I said it, it was exactly the same as the first time. My blood was boiling and surging. Even if the odds were only one billionth, it was definitely not ‘zero’!”

Li Yao took a deep breath. All the flames of war that were dancing crazily in the sky condensed into a golden blade. The love for his wife, the loyalty to his friends, the love for his home, the loyalty to the civilization of mankind, the yearning for the unknown universe, the hope for the endless future… Everything was condensed in the golden blade and turned into billions of rolling sharp edges.

“Who says that a moth darting into a flame is seeking its own doom? As long as there are enough moths, even the most furious flame can be extinguished.”

Li Yao said resolutely, one word after another, “Then, billions of burning moths fell into the dark forest without caring about their own lives. No matter how vast the dark forest was, no matter how cold and terrifying it was, a brilliant new world would have been created, wouldn’t it?

“I firmly believe that.

“Therefore, cut the crap and let’s fight!”

The golden blade slashed at the ‘sound’.

At this moment, there were no distracting thoughts in Li Yao’s mind. He had forgotten the fear that the civilization of mankind was about to be destroyed. Even the outcome of the attack was not important.

There was only one thought in his mind.

Human beings could be destroyed, but they could not be defeated.

BAM!

Li Yao had thought that, after the attack, he would either be counterattacked by the unparalleled strength of the voice and be completely suppressed, or he would end up like ‘All-Exterminating Sect’ and ‘Dragon Lotus Seed’, whose genes collapsed and turned into foam.

Some of them were uttering earsplitting screams, as if they had been crushed by him and vanished into thin air like the weird forces that had crawled into his brain and tried to possess him.

But out of his expectation, he heard a sound that sounded like a broken crystal. Countless bright gold cracks appeared in his vision. The black universe was falling apart, and the ugly body of the ‘voice’ was gone, replaced by…

“What—what the hell is this?”

Li Yao was dumbfounded. The golden blade was floating in midair, staring at the weird picture in his field of vision. He felt that he had punched nothing but air with all his strength.

He seemed to have boarded a crystal warship of the Primeval Clan again, surrounded by countless translucent, bright gold shadows of men and women, young and old. Although their faces were full of gentleness and wisdom, and there was not the slightest hint of hostility, they did not look like living people at all but more like regretful ghosts.

“Congratulations, tester ‘Li Yao’.

The ‘ghost’ in the lead, who looked like a kind, middle-aged woman, floated to the center of Li Yao’s vision and smiled. “After billions of years of waiting, we’ve finally found the most suitable heir. You’ve passed the test successfully and represented your civilization. You are qualified to receive all the ‘legacies’ of the Primeval Civilization and bring you a new life.

“What—what?”

Li Yao stammered. He thought quickly and realized what was going on. He exclaimed, “Everything that happened just now… was a lie just to test my personality? Everything that the voice said was a lie, the defense line of the sun was a lie, and so was the degenerate Primeval Clan that turned into cockroaches and maggots in order to survive?”

“No. The voices are real. The defense line of the sun is real, too, including the ‘Corrupted Origin Sector’, which is as sordid as a cockroach and as sordid as a maggot.

The middle-aged female ghost was still smiling, but there was intense regret, regret, and shame in her smile. “However, don’t tell me that you don’t think that the dirty mud can’t grow bright stamens, the dark night sky can’t produce shining stars, and the descendants of the criminals must be equally sordid and evil. They don’t know how to reflect on what their ancestors did, do they? In particular, the idiotic crimes of our ancestors did not bring us any good. Instead, they pushed us deeper and deeper into the swamp of destruction. Under such circumstances, will we really go our own way and repeat the decadence and disgrace of our ancestors?

“What do you mean?”

Li Yao was baffled. “I don’t understand!”

“It’s simple. The three ‘no other options’ that have been bothering and angering you do exist, and they are indeed the true history of the Primeval Civilization. But when we drafted the procedure, or rather, when we left the ‘last will’, it had been thirty thousand years since countless stars were destroyed in the ‘Solar Defense Line Plan’.

The middle-aged female ghost—she might be the captain of the starship of the Primeval Race—said, “The Primeval Civilization is not monolithic, and it does not have the unified will of the Flood Tide. The Primeval Race, which is scattered in the various sub-universes, can have their own social norms and ethics. The Primeval Race thirty thousand years ago and the Primeval Race thirty thousand years later have completely different opinions when they look at a problem. Even on my starship, everyone has their own ideas and ideologies to save the civilization.

“You mean…”

Li Yao thought for a moment and said, “The ‘aristocrats, magnates, and elites’ who carried out the ‘Solar Defense Line Plan’, sacrificed most of their compatriots, and destroyed the natural paradises in the center of the sea of stars, and struggled to survive in the most sordid ways are in fact your ancestors from thirty thousand years ago. It has been thirty thousand years since you left your… will, and you don’t agree with your ancestors?”

“How could they agree?”

The female captain smiled bitterly. “Let’s put ‘justice’ and ‘evil’ aside. The ‘Solar Defense Line Plan’ is no different from the ‘Advancement Plan’ when we fled from Earth. It is a death sentence that will be extended to thirty thousand years.

“Thirty thousand years. For thirty thousand years, we’ve been drifting in the sea of stars, suffering the erosion of lack of resources and claustrophobia. We’ve been living in anxiety every day.

“In front of us is the darkness that is getting more and more desolate. Sometimes, we can’t even find a space zone with thin spiritual gas after sailing for hundreds of years. Behind us are the legions of the Flood Tide who are chasing after us and trying to crucify us on the pillar of shame of the universe. We are also burdened by the sins of our ancestors and the original sin that we were born with. It seems that every one of us was born with the miserable deaths of our compatriots thirty thousand years ago.

“In such an environment, living an ignoble life is not luck at all but endless torment. We don’t know how to put an end to it. We are even complaining about why our ancestors did what they did in the first place. As you said, fighting against the tides in the center of the universe will be a great relief for us, won’t it?

Chapter 3117: The Answer Is So Simple?

Li Yao finally understood.

He could not help but want to laugh.

In the era when the ‘Solar Defense Line Plan’ was thrown out and implemented, the Primeval Lord of Heaven had been a bunch of shameless guys who had no bottom line for their own survival. Maybe they had declared that they were going to preserve the tinder of their civilization and the hope of the next generation. But it never occurred to them that the blood descendants thirty thousand years later would resent their actions.

The older generation went on a rampage under the banner of ‘it’s all for your own good’, but the new generation didn’t appreciate their kindness at all and was even completely against them. From ancient times to now, were such incidents not rare?

Besides, the new generation of the Primeval Beings had been wandering in the dark sea of stars and living a miserable life like stray dogs. Such a miserable life indeed gave them reason to hate their ancestors.

Li Yao was well aware that a lone starship drifting in the sea of stars had nowhere to go and that there were pursuers behind it. Its resources were running dry, and there was no hope at all. ‘Firefly’, the last flagship of the Star Ocean Imperium, had drifted for a thousand years and almost perished. It was not redeemed until it encountered the Star Glory Federation.

After wandering for only a thousand years, Firefly had almost completed the transformation from a ‘Cultivator’ to an ‘Immortal Cultivator’ on its own. After the remnants of the Primeval Civilization destroyed their home, they went into exile for thirty thousand years under the pressure of the flood. The journey was even longer than their ancestors’ escape from Earth.

What had happened to their starships in the process? What had the ‘cockroaches and maggots’ who had killed their compatriots, destroyed their homes, and cut off all their hope turned themselves into?

It wouldn’t feel good to be a cockroach or a maggot, would it?

“With the surging tide, the escape of our ancestors was very hasty. Even if we reduce the population and fill the resources, it is not enough for everybody to sail for thirty thousand years.

The ghost of the female captain said sadly, “Besides, don’t forget that an earthshaking civil war took place in the various sub-universes before the flood. The wounds of the war are not so easy to heal.

“In an environment where you have lost your fighting spirit and all your morals, where you don’t know where you are going, where you don’t know when your pursuers are coming, and where your resources are quickly running out, all the things that you can think of and can’t imagine… despicable, evil, cruel, insidious, sleazy, dirty, and dark, scheming, overt and covert, and killing each other, are all a piece of cake. Every second is a play. Every starship is a small, barbaric tribe. We have become each other’s most dangerous enemy. Even every person on every starship is sharpening their blades, hiding their claws and teeth, and piercing their evil and vigilant eyes at everyone around them, including their so-called ‘family’.

“In some starships with particularly harsh environments, swallowing the dead bodies of their own kind was even a natural custom. The starships robbed their compatriots for a living and lived through ten thousand years of darkness.

“As a result, as you said, our ancestors turned themselves into cockroaches in space, maggots in the sea of stars, and monsters beyond recognition!

“Uncomfortable. Such a life is too uncomfortable. In the near-eternal mutation and torment, part of the Primeval Beings had a total mental breakdown. They laughed weirdly and adjusted their route, only to turn around 180 degrees and fall into the arms of the tides. They abandoned their rationality and free will and became muddleheaded cells.

“Some of the starships mutinied. The sailors and crew members who rebelled had nothing else to ask for. When they took control of the bridge, they would activate the ‘self-detonation procedures’ of the starships and end their shame with brilliant and scorching fireworks.

“Only a few crew members and passengers on the starships were able to endure the humiliation and suffering. But they started reflecting on what their ancestors had done and the true meaning of ‘civilization’.

“It doesn’t matter if you say that we can’t stand such a humiliating, dark, and meaningless life anymore. In short, it’s time to put an end to everything.

“At the beginning of the test, you’ve seen a picture. Countless members of the Primeval Race were lined up neatly and fell into the engine furnace one after another. Such a scene is not fiction. But our state of mind might be different from what you imagined. We did not turn ourselves into the fuel to push the civilization forward with the tragic mindset of ‘sacrificing for the civilization’. Instead, we were burdened with billions of tons of shame and guilt. We felt ashamed to face all our compatriots who had been killed by our ancestors in the past. We were ashamed to face the past and future of the glorious Primeval Civilization. We could not find a way to make up for it or change it. It was because of such desperation that we decided to end everything.

“Ah…”

Li Yao had been curious. According to the style of the Primeval Civilization, it should not have been so righteous and full of sacrifice.

As it turned out, it was not sacrifice but shame and guilt.

It did make sense now that he thought about it. If he were a member of the escape fleet and had such… evil and sordid ancestors who lived a miserable, desperate life, he would have no other options except to die.

“However, before we decided to go there together, the discovery of the ‘Pangu Universe’ gave us a slim hope.

The ghost of the female captain smiled and said, “Our civilization is truly hopeless. The fixed mindset, the heavy burden of history, the pursuit and absorption of the flood tide… We’ve been homeless for thirty thousand years. We are too tired. Death is the best relief for us.

“But what if our civilization starts from scratch?

“If we can restart our civilization and let it be reborn in a brand-new, completely different appearance, will we have the opportunity to find a new way out and make some minor but crucial changes to wash away all the sins that we and our ancestors have committed? We might even be able to let the entire Universe Ocean get rid of the trauma caused by us and the flood?

“Therefore, there was the ‘black wall’, the ‘ancient relics’, and the ‘ultimate test’.

“Hehe. The correct answer to the ultimate test is simple. There is no correct answer at all.

“Because everything we do is wrong, and we don’t know what the right choice is at all. Then, who are we to judge or even manipulate your choice?

“Therefore, for the testers, it doesn’t matter what you choose or what you do, as long as you don’t make the same choice as us at the most critical juncture.

“Ah…”

Li Yao was dumbfounded at first. Then he realized what was going on. “So, that’s how it is!”

“Besides, we’ve given you three chances. If you make a choice that is completely against our will and make the opposite choice, you will pass the test.

The ghost of the female captain smiled and said, “It seems simple, doesn’t it? But for billions of years, none of the testers of a hundred generations of civilizations passed the test. In order to keep the test running on the surface, they listened to our suggestions and obeyed our will without any exception. In the end, they became our puppets, slaves, claws, teeth, and duplicates. How hilarious. If even we are no match for the Flood Tide, why do we need a bunch of puppets, slaves, claws, teeth, and duplicates?

“Now that I think about it, it does make sense. A seemingly simple test requires not only indestructible willpower but also fearless sacrifice, as well as absolute trust in our comrades and compatriots. All three of them are things that we are missing and that the hundred generations of testers did not have. But today, you have all of them.

“Is that so?”

If his soul had teeth, Li Yao would’ve been grinning from ear to ear.

“Yes. Therefore, you are the man we’ve been waiting for billions of years. You are destined to inherit the legacy of the Primeval Civilization.

The ghost of the female captain looked at Li Yao with scorching eyes. All the bright gold ghosts nearby were cheering for Li Yao. Dazzling sounds and effects were flying everywhere. Then, the catalogs and detailed files of countless ‘ancient secret treasures’ appeared in Li Yao’s brain and turned into glittering turbulences.

“This is the invincible fleet that we have docked in the ‘black wall’. It is tens of thousands of years more advanced than your active starships. This is our strengthened exoskeleton and super-large humanoid war machine. One of them is enough to fight against a hundred of your Giant Soldiers. This is our fully-automatic war factory. It can complete the entire procedure from the collection of resources to the refinement of war puppets. You don’t even need to command it. All you need to do is input a command remotely, and you can occupy or destroy a planet.

The female captain smiled. “Of course, you will also need the technology trees, techniques, and training systems from Earth to the Primeval Civilization. With them, your civilization will have a pair of wings that surpass the speed of light, which will save you at least a hundred thousand years.

“Come on. Everything is yours now. Take it away and become the master of the Universe Ocean to make up for our mistakes. Build the new glory of the Primeval Civilization and create a brilliant future that belongs to… the new generation of mankind!”

All the sound effects behind the female captain—the splendid technology trees, the colorful techniques and training systems, the awe-inspiring war machines, the countless secret treasures, the legacies, and the destructive forces—all turned into thousands of streaks of brightness and congregated in her palm, turning into a translucent, bright gold key.

The ghost of the female captain handed the gold key to Li Yao, her eyes full of eagerness.

Li Yao’s soul was deeply shocked by the thousands of techniques displayed by the female captain, the technology tree, and the war machine. He subconsciously reached out to grab them.

But the moment his fingertip was about to touch the gold key, he suddenly felt that something was wrong, as if a needle had pricked his fingertip.

“Wait!”

Li Yao’s soul suddenly shrank. His finger stopped less than one millimeter away from the gold key.

—

Chapter 3118: The Real Answer

“What’s wrong?”

The female captain’s expression was somewhat complicated. Even the bright gold ghosts behind her were getting anxious. All the ghosts of the Primeval Lord of Heaven were staring at Li Yao, as if they could not wait to see him take the key that contained billions of techniques and endless technology.

Li Yao immediately sensed that something was wrong.

This made the alarms in the depths of his sea of consciousness even louder.

Calm down. I must calm down!

“It’s not that simple,” Li Yao warned himself. “It can’t be that simple. The sighs about the past of Earth, the hatred for the Primeval Civilization, the appreciation for the human civilization that was reborn in the Pangu Universe, the love for my family and my home, and the boiling blood… All of them will interfere with my judgement. I have to calm down, freeze all the irrelevant thoughts, and find the most crucial answer!

His eyes suddenly turned sharp. He stared at the female captain for a long time, as if he were going to poke two holes in her body.

“You seem to want me to take over the key and inherit the legacy of the Primeval Civilization?”

Li Yao suddenly asked.

“Of course.”

The female captain was still smiling, but there was a hint of reluctance in her smile. “We’ve waited for billions of years and wiped out the testers of hundreds of generations of civilizations. If nobody gets the legacy, the flood will come at any time, and it will be too late.

“Therefore, there is no need to doubt it. Of course, I hope that you can get the legacy of the primeval era, and I sincerely hope that you can bring the legacy to greater heights.

“Is that so?”

Li Yao found it more and more odd. He sniffed and said, “Then, are there not other suitable candidates besides me? What happened to Lu Qingchen and the mental devil? Were they obliterated?”

The female captain was silent for a moment. She shook her head and said, “Not yet. They are still being tested and lost in the bloody mud.”

“That’s unreasonable.”

Li Yao was calmer and calmer. Looking at the golden key that was right in front of him, he felt that he was not looking at the legacy of a god-level civilization but a viper that was burning and full of thorns. “If the correct answer to pass the ultimate test is not to make the same choice as you, then Lu Qingchen and the mental devil should’ve failed and been killed by you a long time ago. Didn’t you say just now that the two of them had surpassed me in progress? There’s no reason that they haven’t entered the life-and-death decision of the ‘Sun Defense Line Plan’.

“Since they haven’t been wiped out yet, it means that they might pass the test even if they make the darkest choice three times. Maybe, the so-called key to the ultimate test is not here at all. It is not ‘justice’ or ‘evil’, or ‘darkness and brightness’.

“My head is a mess. I need to think, think hard…”

“What are you hesitating for?”

The female captain hurried to say, “The flood is coming. You will only have a slim chance of survival if you receive the legacy of the Primeval Civilization!”

“What’s the hurry?”

Li Yao was more and more certain that his intuition was correct. “Even if the flood is really coming, I don’t mind thinking for another three to five minutes, do I? Something is wrong. Something is definitely wrong!”

“What is it?”

The female captain’s smile was about to collapse. All the ghosts around her were about to turn into death rays and cling to Li Yao.

“Don’t get me wrong. It’s not your problem, but mine.”

Speaking up to this point, Li Yao simply withdrew his hand. In the mental world, he held his chin with his two hands and squatted down. He muttered, “My problem is that perhaps the heavens are jealous of my talent and have cast a curse on me, right? Every time I appear in a mission, the difficulty of the mission will suddenly increase by ten times. Every time I appear in a duel, the great devil will grasp at least three to five transformations. And every time the mission ends, it is inevitable that there will be twists and turns, causing great waves… To be honest, I am already used to this kind of miserable adventure.

“Right now, I’ve passed the test that the elites and experts of hundreds of civilizations failed to pass for billions of years with nothing but my passion, and I’ve received such… an unbelievable super luxury package? It’s too wonderful. So wonderful that it does not seem real but a dream.

“What are you talking about?”

The female captain cracked a smile. “You are overthinking.”

“No. I didn’t think too much about it. Although I like to let my imagination run wild and brag about myself most of the time, I am actually very clear-headed and self-aware when my computational ability is maximized.

Li Yao pointed at his head and said, “In fact, I have an extremely clear and calm understanding of myself. I know exactly what kind of person I am. I’m actually not as tall, majestic, righteous, far-sighted, and broad-minded as other people imagine me to be. I’m really, really just a handsome and confident ordinary person.

“When it comes to the commanding ability of a fleet, I’m nothing special. At the very least, there are hundreds of people around me who can hang me and beat me. When it comes to the operation and management ability of a civilization, I’m even more mediocre. I might not be able to manage a planet even if I was given one. As for the development of technology, the reformation of social structures, the settlement of interests in various aspects, and even the blueprint of a civilization ten thousand years later… I don’t have any of those abilities.

“Other than my appearance, the only thing I possess is nothing more than hot-bloodedness. But the so-called hot-bloodedness is something that can be casually triggered by a few high school students in their prime who are not satisfied with their desires. Don’t you think it’s too simple, too child’s play, and too absurd to rely on hot-bloodedness to pass the ‘Ultimate Test’?”

The female captain looked at Li Yao’s face and thought hard. In the end, she shook her head and hesitated.

“Thankfully, before the final test, I received a lot of files about the ancient civilization from the Supreme Emperor and the Observer.

Li Yao’s train of thought became clearer and clearer, and his speech became faster and faster. “There were a hundred ancient civilizations that followed a variety of evolutionary directions and moral principles. There were bloody, brutal, and selfish dark civilizations, but there were also benevolent civilizations that were kind-hearted and full of brightness. There were humble and cowardly civilizations, but there were also hot-blooded and fearless warrior civilizations. There were even ‘super-brained civilizations’ that were full of schemes and schemes.

“The most extreme civilization of kindness destroyed most of their weapons after they developed. They did not even have the heart to trample on flowers and grass.

“Every individual of the most hot-blooded warrior civilization has been pursuing the most glamorous and glorious way to die their entire life. Their only desire is to find a stronger enemy and engage in a great battle with him, dying in the most brilliant fireworks.

“The computational ability and deduction ability of the ‘super-brain civilization’ are dozens of times higher than ours and the Pangu civilization’.

“By logic, the most extreme civilization of kindness would never have been tempted by you and chosen the dark and evil path three times in a row.

“As far as the warrior civilization is concerned, the so-called ‘Sun Defense Line Plan’ is simply not something that they can sully their beliefs with. They will definitely fight the tides to the death.

“Also, with the computational, analytical, and deduction ability of the ‘super-brain civilization’, there’s no reason why they can’t see through such a simple scam. The so-called ‘the only correct answer is that there’s no right answer at all’ is more like a cliché. It’s more like a scam to fool the middle school students who are still wet behind the ears. It’s impossible to fool the elites, wise men, and madmen of a hundred generations of civilizations!

“Therefore, I make a bold assumption that you lied just now. I’m not the only one who has made it this far in the past billions of years. Thousands of testers have walked to this place and stood in front of the… golden key. At the very least, the extremely friendly civilization, the fearless warrior civilization, and the super-brain civilization that has made thorough plans. Their testers should have stood where I am right now and extended their hands to the golden key.

“It was a pity that they failed. They perished and were obliterated.

“Because… this is not the destination. We are still being tested, aren’t we?

The female captain was silent for a long time.

Then, he applauded Li Yao together with the other bright gold ghosts.

“Don’t underestimate yourself. You are indeed very smart. You are the first person in billions of years to notice that the test is still ongoing.

The female captain smiled. “However, I can’t tell you the real purpose of the ultimate test yet. You have to find the right answer yourself and figure out the significance of the test in order to learn everything about the Primeval Civilization.

She held the glittering gold key that contained thousands of technologies and techniques in her hands and offered it to Li Yao.

Li Yao stared at the gold key for a long time.

The key seemed to be able to open countless invisible labyrinths.

Every maze hid destructive forces, unbelievable technology, and unparalleled power that could make a person the king of the universe.

Of course, if he was selfless enough, the key could also unlock the seal of the civilization of mankind and allow the civilization of mankind to leap from the path of evolution of hundreds of thousands of years to a god-level civilization, or at least a ‘quasi-god-level civilization’.

It seemed to be a tempting legacy that nobody could resist.

Li Yao stared at the gold key for a long, long time.

His eyes were filled with the desire for strength, the hope for the evolution of civilization, and the excitement for the final battle.

Several times, his fingers and his telepathic thoughts were restless again. He was about to take the gold key and clutch it.

But in the end, the obsession and craziness on his face and in his eyes were gone. His eyes were as clear and pure as before.

Li Yao took a long breath in relief. His lips curled into the purest smile that had not appeared for a long time. He made up his mind and suddenly felt relaxed.

“So complicated? Then forget it. I don’t want it.”

Li Yao crossed his arms and stopped looking at the gold key, as if it were a transparent cloud. “Captain, I refuse to accept the legacy of the Primeval Civilization. Please take it back.”

Chapter 3119: A Bitter Lesson!

The ghosts of the Primeval Civilization were all frozen.

“What did you say?”

The female captain found it hard to believe. “You came to this place after a lot of trouble and passed all the tests. Wasn’t it for the legacy of the primeval era? It was the only hope to save your civilization. But you are going to… refuse it?”

“That’s right. I refuse.”

Li Yao was calmer and more at ease. He smiled and said, “Don’t get me wrong. It was not a spur-of-the-moment decision to risk the survival of the entire civilization, nor was it a game of cat-and-mouse. I thought that the so-called ‘ancient heritage’ would do my civilization no good. Or rather, my civilization was not ready to accept such a generous gift. The time was not right yet!

“Why?”

The female captain stared at Li Yao and asked, “Can you explain?”

“Since the very beginning, I’ve been pondering what the real purpose of the ‘ultimate test’ is. What are you testing and weighing exactly? Are ‘justice’ and ‘evil’ really black and white?

Li Yao shook his head and said, “No. I don’t think so. On the path of civilization, the concepts of good and evil are changing all the time. There is more than one way to save a civilization. Who can say that they are always right?

“For example, in the civilization of mankind, there are people like Ding Lingdang and me, but there are also people like Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, Li Jialing, Li Linghai, Lei Chenghu, and the Spring Breeze… There are thousands upon thousands of different personalities. Everyone adheres to different ideologies and temperes their different beliefs. In their own way, they strive for the rise of the entire civilization. Do I have to be more right than them? Do I have to be more able to save the civilization? Or do I have to change everyone’s personalities and ideologies to be like me? Do I only have one voice, one ideology, one path? Even if it seems like a path of light and justice, is such a world necessarily better than the present?”

“Even I wouldn’t dare say that.

“I’m afraid that’s why you said ‘no correct answer’ in the beginning.

“I believe that, even after thirty thousand years of suffering and reflection, the survivors of the Primeval Clan will not agree unanimously that their ancestors were wrong, will they? Just as there are ‘Cultivators’ and ‘Immortal Cultivators’ among human beings, there must be two or even many of you who believe that their ancestors were not wrong. The ‘Great Extinction Order’ and the ‘Sun Defense Line Plan’ should have been more brutal.

“Therefore, ‘righteousness’ and ‘goodness’ are not what you value most at all. At the level of a ‘quasi-god civilization’, your belief has long surpassed the concepts of ‘righteousness’ and ‘goodness’. You may know that there is no way out if you dwell on the issues of good and evil.

“What you care about is not the difference between good and evil, but the attitude of a civilization when faced with a sudden expansion or a falling force.

“From the past on Earth in the beginning to the struggle and rise of the Primeval Lord of Heaven in the sea of the universe, you’ve hinted to me countless times that the manipulation and utilization of any kind of power is not without a price. The ‘Heavenly Dao’ has marked the price of every kind of power in secret. The more powerful the power is, the heavier or even more miserable the price will be. If I am too obsessed with the strength, not only will I not become the master of the strength, I will also become the slave of the strength.

“The mammoths and the saber-toothed tigers seemed to have received the blessing of evolution and grasped unparalleled strength in the environment at that time. They became the dominators of their respective regions, but the price they paid was that they shut themselves in and lost the motivation to continue evolving. In the end, when the ‘mutated species’ of human beings appeared, they were exterminated in the blink of an eye.

“The same tragedy happened to the alien species in space such as the ‘Void Hunters’ and the ‘Mountain Crab’. They originated from natural paradises with abundant spiritual energy. Their strength was thousands of times higher than that of the saber-toothed tigers and the mammoths, and the price they paid was thousands of times more painful. Had the flood not appeared, they would never have known what real wisdom and civilization looked like.

“What about the earthlings who entered the industrialization and the information society? They seemed to be in control of the advanced technology and the extraordinary civilization that no other living creatures on Earth could grasp, but the price they paid was that they lost their self-control and stimulated the self-destruction tendencies deep in their genes to the extreme. The first world war, the second world war, and even the thermonuclear war that turned the atmosphere into a black curtain and turned the earth into a wasteland. The third world war was the price that the earthlings paid for their strength.

“What about the Primeval Race, the descendants of the earthlings? Of course, you paid a higher price than the earthlings, the saber-toothed tigers, the mammoths, the void hunters, the giant crab…

“Too fast. Your civilization has evolved too fast. You are not the natives in the center of the universe but ‘mutated species’ that have invaded. Before you came to the center of the universe, you had never been in contact with spiritual energy on such a large scale. Isn’t the abundant and intense spiritual energy in the paradises a free source of power for you?

“With the help of the ‘completely free strength’, you completed the transformation from an ordinary civilization to a quasi-god civilization in only tens of thousands of years. You were also lost in the overwhelming strength without exception.

“In the fleeting moment just now, I also scanned your social form and technological development. I discovered that you are a rather abnormal civilization with a lot of faults. In certain fields, especially the military technology of bloody slaughter, you have developed to the point of perfection and even insanity. However, in some other fields related to the humanities, the legal system, and the ethics, you are not much better than those of the Earth. In fact, because of the coldness and darkness of the sea of stars, you have regressed to the feudal and tribal level of the Middle Ages on Earth. The division of technology and humanity is too weird.

“I believe that the forces that corrupted the alien species such as the Void Hunters and the giant crab and kept the low-level civilizations in the slave society have started corrupting the Primeval Civilization, too.

“Therefore, why did you behave so badly on the road to the rise of a civilization? Why did you issue the ‘Great Armageddon Order’? Why did you start another brutal civil war when you forgot the lessons of the Earth Era after slaughtering most of the civilizations? Why did you throw out the ‘Sun Defense Line Plan’? It is meaningless to blame it on the moral standards of certain people. The root of the reason is that you are lost in forces beyond your control and cannot free yourself. Your growth is so fast that your moral integrity cannot keep up with the progress of technology. You are too greedy. You tried to control what you could not control and achieved billions of years of glory in only tens of thousands of years.

“In the face of absolute strength and fatal temptations, is it possible to keep a clear head and make rational judgments? This is what you are really concerned about, right?”

The female captain listened quietly. Hearing that, she and the ghosts of the Primeval Lord of Heaven finally put on ashamed, regretful, and painful expressions.

The female captain’s lips were trembling. There was no telling whether it was because there was no corresponding answer in the pre-programmed procedure or because there was a certain truth that was higher than the procedure, but she remained silent.

“The new generation of human civilization in the Pangu Universe is lucky.

Li Yao’s smile was brighter and brighter. “Although we are a man-made civilization by accident, and although our ‘father civilization’, the Pangu Clan, and the Nuwa Clan, have left a lot of legacies for us, for a long time, the legacies have been buried under the yellow sand and floating in the sea of stars. We have made our way to where we are today by overcoming all obstacles with nothing but our own hands. Not only have we developed advanced technology and developed powerful techniques, our citizens also have a sound mind and body, a stable moral code, and a general consensus between the strong and the wise. Our civilization has been thriving and full of fearless hope.

“Under such circumstances, we discovered the relics left by the Pangu Civilization one after another. Since the gap between the Pangu Civilization and us is not too large, and the technology of the relics is sporadic, it will not have too much of an impact on our social form and ethics. Even if there are some subversive things, we can slowly digest them.

“It is safe to say that such a legacy is the most beneficial thing for the civilization of mankind at this moment.

“However, what about the ‘ancient heritage’ from the Primeval Civilization? According to you, it is at least hundreds of thousands of years ahead of us. It is unbelievably advanced and can bring our civilization to the level of a quasi-god civilization. Hehe. That’s too unbelievable. Just like it is impossible for an ordinary person to turn into a Nascent Soul Stage Cultivator after taking an elixir, I can’t imagine what it would be like if an ordinary civilization suddenly turned into a quasi-god civilization.

“Let’s say, for example, that a poor man suddenly won a lottery worth tens of millions. Do you think he has enough knowledge, ability, and willpower to control the windfall instead of losing himself in the materialistic world?

“The ordinary people who used to live a simple life, although mediocre, had a drastic change of personality after they became rich overnight. Corrupted by their own desires, they went on a rampage and returned to poverty. They could not accept such an ending and fell into an even more painful abyss. I’ve heard a lot of similar stories.

“Even myself, who used to be a healthy, cheerful young man, is now suffering from ‘chronic mental derangement’ because of the immense strength that I gained after only a hundred years of training. My brain is seriously damaged, my wisdom is fluctuating, and I’m even suffering from schizophrenia. Those are all painful lessons!

Chapter 3120: The Road Is Beneath Our Feet!

The female captain and all the ghosts looked at Li Yao in disbelief, not knowing whether they were looking at a genius or an idiot.

“Think about it. If we really inherited such an enormous ‘legacy’, we would have grasped countless mysteries and rules overnight and possessed the power to destroy the world.

Li Yao opened his arms and continued, “There is no need for our experts and scholars to think independently, because all the cutting-edge technologies, cutting-edge theories, and possible directions have been pointed out by you. There is no need to think and explore at all. At the same time, it is impossible for them to raise any doubts or rebuttals about your theoretical system, because the gap between us is too large. It is like the confrontation between a primary school student and a college student. They can only be led by the nose by you obediently.

“And once our experts and scholars, the smartest, the most paranoid, the least blind believers of authority, the people who are filled with the desire to explore and the curiosity to explore, worship your theories and laws, they will no longer have their own opinions, and their imaginations will be killed prematurely. Wouldn’t that mean that our civilization has completely lost the spirit of independent exploration?

“As for our warriors… Right now, all the human experts have gone through all kinds of hardships in order to possess great strength. Our strength all comes from ourselves. Even the children of the noble families, who have accumulated their strength with tremendous resources, require their own tenacious will and a thousand times of tempering in order to digest and absorb it. Even the scheming and cruel internal strife between the Immortal Cultivators is also a form of tempering!

“If our experts possess ‘strength a hundred times greater than that of the Giant God Soldiers’ overnight, I don’t know what kind of impact such an impact will have on the belief of the experts and whether or not it will bring a destructive blow that will turn all the human experts into powerless, willless waste!

“After all, as long as you have the golden key, you can directly inherit the legacy. Why do you need to suffer? Why do you need to play with your life? Why do you need to cultivate?

“As for our people, our social system, and our moral code, everything about the civilization of mankind has been based on the current training system, the resources available, and the productivity level. What will our society, our ethics, and our entire civilization become if everybody has the strength to split a mountain in one night? Will the entire civilization be mentally deranged and collapse overnight?

“In conclusion, the civilization of mankind has completely fallen to the ‘greatness, glory, and advancement’ of the Primeval Civilization. We worship you and follow your lead. Our thinking patterns and evolution are completely limited in your framework. It is impossible for us to question your path, your technology, and your ethics, let alone change anything.

“As a result, as I said just now, a smaller version of the Primeval Civilization will appear. Is it meaningful? Is it useful? Can it resist the flood and find new hope?

“Therefore, I’m not playing any tricks. I’m sincerely rejecting your gift. The legacy of the Primeval Civilization might be a very good thing, but it is not suitable for us right now.

Finishing his speech, Li Yao smiled and bowed to the female captain and all the ghosts of the Primeval Clan, expressing his sincerest gratitude.

“You said…”

The female captain hesitated for a long time before she smiled, too. “Fair enough. Maybe it’s too early to inherit the legacy of the Primeval Civilization. The right path of a civilization is to develop slowly with independence and hard work. But have you ever thought that time waits for no man? A flood is coming at any moment. Its claws and teeth might have already reached into the small universe. There might not be enough time for you to develop peacefully for thousands of years.

“It was not easy for you to come this far and see a glimmer of hope to save the civilization. Are you really going to return empty-handed?”

“It does seem a pity.”

Li Yao scratched his hair and grinned, revealing his shining teeth. “However, after so many adventures, I’ve figured it out. Whether or not a civilization can be saved does not depend on one person, much less a so-called ‘gold key’. If all human beings are united and unleash all their wisdom and courage, is the ‘gold key’ really important?

“Besides, I never said that I would return empty-handed. Thanks to you, I’ve found… the real treasure.

“What?”

The female captain was slightly surprised. “You rejected the ‘gold key’ of the Primeval Civilization and said that you’ve found the ‘real treasure’. What is it exactly?”

“Before I started the ultimate test, I heard the last words of a certain tester of the ‘super-brain civilization’. He said—’Be vigilant, my friends. There are no ancient treasures at all. We are the greatest treasures of all’.

Li Yao smiled like a trumpet flower that had bloomed to the maximum. “Thanks to those words, I was able to stop at the last moment and see through the so-called ‘ultimate test’. Right now, I finally understand the full meaning of those words.

“Yes. The tester of the super-brain civilization was right. Compared to the primeval treasures of the Primeval Civilization, we are the treasures that are worth cherishing and exploring.

“Before the civilization of mankind and the Pangu Civilization, the corpses of a hundred generations of pioneers had been lying here. I believe that the pioneers gathered the essence of their entire civilization—the most advanced magical equipment and war machines, the bravest and most intelligent warriors, and even the last bit of their strength for the ultimate test. When the test failed and their civilization was about to be wiped out, they must’ve left the sincerest last words and the most abundant information.

“All the magical equipment, the war machines, the remains of the warriors and the wise men, and even their entire history are buried around the tower, constituting the main body of the primeval relic.

“Isn’t the civilization of a hundred generations of pioneers more worth studying than the Primeval Civilization?

“In terms of the degree of evolution of their civilization, they are almost the same as the civilization of human beings. The highest level of their civilization is not much higher than that of the Pangu civilization, and the lowest level of their civilization is no lower than that of the civilization of human beings thousands of years ago. Not only are they valuable enough for us to study them and provide abundant nutrition for us, we also don’t need to worry that their influence will be too strong and lead our civilization into a ditch.

“In the meantime, the civilizations of a hundred generations of pioneers have been growing without any restraints. There are all kinds of weird evolutions and social forms. The history of their rise and fall can give us countless enlightenment and lessons. They can help us discover countless unexpected problems and even inspire us to embark on an unprecedented path!

“If we can calm down and absorb the civilization of a hundred generations of pioneers together with the essence of the Pangu Civilization, wouldn’t it be safer and more efficient to meld the hundred and one civilizations into one civilization than to swallow the Primeval Civilization recklessly?

“I think this is the answer that the tester of the super-brain civilization thought of before his death. That’s why he said that the real treasure is not the Primeval Civilization but ourselves. We are the pioneers who were born in the Pangu Universe and eventually fell to the Heaven’s Tower. It’s a pity that there are so many corpses and last words of the civilization of the pioneers that we can study. They are more than enough for the civilization of today. There is no need for us to break into the Heaven’s Tower and run any tests at all. It’s a pity that the previous pioneers were too unconfident in their own civilization and were too greedy about the so-called ‘ancient heritage’. They always believed that the older they were, the stronger they would be. The ancient heritage would certainly help them solve all their problems. There is no such thing as a bargain in the world!”

“You…”

The female captain exclaimed, “He is really different from other testers.”

“It’s probably because my luck hasn’t been very good. Every time I want to take a shortcut, I will suffer a big loss. Slowly, I will understand that there are no shortcuts in the world, right?”

Li Yao shrugged his shoulders and opened his hands as he said, “The shortest shortcut is to step on solid ground. It’s better to have one’s own than to have one’s own. Compared to the legacy that falls from the sky, it’s more comfortable to enjoy the strength that one has painstakingly earned!

“If I had understood the logic earlier, I wouldn’t have had to climb the damn towers and run the tests at all. But this is good, too. At the very least, during the tests, I saw the history between Earthlings and the Primeval Lord of Heaven clearly. Even though the history you presented to me was false, the lessons I learned were real. Also, I saw the vastness of the sea of stars, the brilliance of civilization, and the brilliance of all kinds of species. These are the more precious secret treasures compared to pure strength, right?”

“Maybe.”

The female captain smiled noncommittally. “Do you really not want the legacy of the Primeval Civilization, not at all, not ever?”

“Who says I ‘never want to’? I’m not that stupid. I didn’t want to look at something that had already been studied by my predecessors and had to study it from the beginning—as I said just now, the time just hasn’t come yet.”

Li Yao said confidently, “I firmly believe that the civilization of mankind has enough wisdom to analyze and digest the essence of a hundred generations of pioneers, to carry on their legacy, and to become the strongest, wisest, and brightest super civilization in the history of the Pangu Universe. By then, we will be qualified to inherit the legacy of the Primeval Civilization. As long as your legacy does exist somewhere in the Pangu Universe, be it in a secret space zone, the space surrounded by black walls, or even in the folds of time, we will definitely find it.

“Do you understand? Of course, we yearn for the legacy of the primeval era, but we don’t want anyone’s charity. We have to explore and excavate with our own hands—the road is below our feet. This is how we walk!”

Chapter 3121: Guess?

While he was shouting at the top of his lungs, a shining, golden path seemed to have appeared below Li Yao’s feet. The path that extended to infinity seemed to be a coldly-shining saber that slashed at the hearts of the female captain and all the ghosts of the Primeval Clan.

The female captain and the ghosts of the Primeval Clan were all awed by Li Yao’s determination. They were like smoke under the scorching sun, blurred and unsteady.

“I didn’t expect that a brand-new civilization of mankind would emerge today after billions of years, and that there would be someone like you in the civilization of mankind.

The female captain smiled in the most desolate and regretful way. She seemed to be talking to herself, as if she were talking to the ghosts of her compatriots, too. “If only people like you had existed in the beginning… in the beginning, at the end of the era of Earth. If the leader of the civilization of Earth at that time, ‘Wan Zanghai’, had someone like you by his side and achieved a balance between good and evil, light and darkness, maybe our civilization would have developed in a completely different direction. Maybe the flood would not have been born, and maybe the entire Universe Ocean would have been ten thousand times more prosperous and beautiful than today.”

“That was in the beginning. Today is coming to an end, too. But billions of possibilities are still waiting to be created in the future, including the possibility that it is ten thousand times better.

Li Yao clenched his fists and raised them high. “We will create our own future!”

“True.”

The female captain smiled and looked back at her compatriots.

All the ghosts of the Origin Clan smiled in relief. Bright gold chimneys were spreading out of their bodies, as if they could not wait to enter the eternal sleep after billions of years of suffering and waiting.

“Your performance is better than all the possibilities preset by the program.

The phantom of the female captain gradually turned thinner and thinner, like an ice block melting in warm water. But the smile on her face was getting wider and wider. “In the ‘passing requirements’ that the procedure presupposes, we mainly test three points. Firstly, whether or not the tester has absolute freedom and tenacity to persist in their beliefs until the end.

“You are right. The concepts of good and evil, light and darkness, are not things that we care about. Who says that evil and darkness in the conventional sense is definitely not the way out? The key is balance and persistence.

“If the tester is inclined to light and kindness, then we will give him dark and evil options. If the tester comes from the Asura World where the strong prey on the weak and brutal battles, then we will give him light and kindness options. It doesn’t matter how he chooses. The key is to persevere and not be influenced by us—anyone.”

“Secondly, on the basis of self-awareness, the tester must have the spirit of sacrifice, both for his compatriots, his home, and his civilization, and for his own ideology. Those who commit crimes in the name of justice cannot lead a civilization to glory. Only the real martyrs, even the evil and the dark ones, can be the leaders of a strong civilization.

“Thirdly, and most importantly, his head is absolutely clear, and he knows when to give and take and when to hold back. Even the temptation of the legacy of a god-level civilization cannot shake his belief. Only in such a way can he avoid the problems resulting from the sudden expansion of the civilization, avoid the tragedy of ‘spinning and vanishing’, and even resist the scourge of the flood.

“Originally, according to the standard of the procedure, as long as one had the courage to refuse the ‘Gold Key’ of the Primeval Civilization, one would be considered to have succeeded. However, we, who designed the ultimate test in the beginning, did not expect that there would be such a thing as ‘a hundred corpses of the Pioneer Civilization’. Our original plan was that, as long as the tester refused the ‘Gold Key’, his civilization would be preserved, and we would pass on the knowledge of the Primeval Civilization bit by bit, step by step, through the ‘Tower of Babel’. Of course, we wouldn’t do such a stupid thing as feeding them with tremendous technology and training systems.

“However, now that I think about it, we were too hasty in our settings. We only raised a weaker, less developed version of the civilization of the primordial era instead of a new future. Your ideas are still valid.

“Then, Li Yao, the tester, hopes that the new generation of human beings can absorb the essence of a hundred pioneering civilizations and carry on the legacy of your ‘brothers’ and ‘sisters’. In your own way, march forward in the boundless sea of stars without looking back!

“We’ve been waiting for billions of years. We are too tired. Finally, we can take a good rest. I hope that tomorrow will be ten thousand times better when we wake up, and you will find a solution to the flood.

“On behalf of the last will of the Primeval Civilization, I sincerely wish you success. Human beings… civilization…”

The female captain and all the ghosts of the Primeval Clan bowed to Li Yao with a smile, both as a sincere wish and as a way of thanking him for a better answer and eternal relief.

“Wait!”

Li Yao felt that there was more to the captain’s words, but he was sweating hard in the mental world. “Don’t leave just yet. There are still a lot of questions that I haven’t answered yet!

“You said just now that, according to the different natures and moral values of the testers, the scenes presented by the ultimate test are ever-changing, too. Then, I would very much like to know what the so-called ‘Conquering History of the Primeval Civilization’ looked like to the testers who were born dark and evil, the tragedy at the end of the Earth Era, the ‘Great Extinction Order’, and the ‘Solar Defense Line Plan’… Have any of these things ever happened in real history? What was the truth of the voice’s words? What was the truth of the real history?

“Also, was the civilization of a hundred pioneers really destroyed by you, or was there something else?

“Also, how are my wife and the others? Are they alright? Now that I’ve passed the test, can you let them out now?”

“Also, what about the Vicious Beast Tide that is charging at the Heaven’s Origin Sector? I feel that the arrival of the Vicious Beast Tide is too weird. It can’t be that the tentacles of the tide have reached the Pangu Universe, can it?”

“History was created by human beings, but it has been embellished, smeared, and altered, too. Even the history of a hundred years ago is full of different opinions. There is no telling what is good and what is evil. Who can say for sure what is right and what is wrong?

The female captain had already turned into a cluster of gold brightness whose facial features could not be seen clearly. A calm voice came from the cluster of light. “Even for us billions of years ago, the Great Armageddon Order and the defense line of the sun belonged to the category of ‘ancient history’. Who knows what the truth is exactly?

“Besides, didn’t you say it yourself? The past is the past. What matters is the present and the future. Everybody is the past, and everybody is the amalgamation of the present and the future. You are exactly what you are today, and history and the future are exactly what they are. There’s no need to overthink it.

“We have already answered the question as to how the civilization of the hundred pioneers was destroyed. Of course, there are still a lot of details, a lot of twists and turns, and a lot of soul-stirring stories. Hehe. The interesting information is all recorded in their remains. If you want to know, you might as well explore it yourself.

“As for the final test…

“Now that you’ve successfully broken through the barrier, there is no need for you to continue. However, I still hope that all the human testers can persist until the moment when they fail or succeed. However, even if they fail, they will not receive any punishment. Except for the mental interrogation, I think that it will leave a deep impression on them and make them realize that ‘any power has a price’. In the end, you will have to reveal the truth to them in person. It is the best arrangement.”

“You mean…”

Li Yao thought for a long time. “For example, let’s say that one of the testers successfully passed all the previous tests and came to the ‘Gold Key’ phase. He saw so many destructive magical equipment, marvelous techniques, and unimaginable technologies. Naturally, he was overjoyed and snatched the Gold Key, only to find that he was dreaming. Then, I appeared in front of him and told him with a smile that everything was false, that there was no ancient legacy, and that I had rejected the legacy. How about this?”

“Understood.”

The female captain asked, “What do you think?”

“I think—”

Li Yao said, “I will be killed by them.”

Soft laughter came from the golden cluster.

“What about the beast tide?”

Li Yao said anxiously, “Don’t you think that the beast tide outside is too weird?”

“We are just a small program. Our last words have become mottled after billions of years.

The female captain said, “We don’t know anything about the Vicious Beast Tide. Even if it is the vanguard of the Flood Tide Legion, there is nothing we can do about it. If it is within our capability, we can destroy the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the ancient relics instantly. All the carbon-based creatures and energy-based creatures that are still here will be swept into the abyss of space and time and be obliterated forever.

“But I believe that this is not the solution you want, is it?

“It’s been billions of years. It’s not strange that Hong Chao has come to this place. We’ve done our best. The rest is up to you. Go now, Li Yao. Prove that you are not bluffing and that you are not capable enough. Fight in your own way!”

“Are you really just a program?”

Li Yao asked and gave his own answer. “I don’t believe it. How can a program be so vivid and fluent that it can even answer questions beyond its settings? Even an ordinary artificial intelligence wouldn’t be able to do that!

“If the Pangu civilization could evolve into a super artificial intelligence such as ‘Fuxi’, there’s no reason that the Primeval Civilization couldn’t have done it billions of years ago. What are you exactly? A super artificial intelligence similar to the Fuxi civilization? Or are you a literal ‘ghost of the Primeval Race’? What did you mean when you said that you would wake up again one day? Hey, don’t play tricks with me. There can’t be any other tricks. This is a game within a game. All my reactions were within your expectations. What you said right now is partly true, too. In a long, long time to come, we are going to have a second confrontation with you, aren’t we?”

Laughter was echoing inside the golden waves, as if countless men, women, and children were laughing together.

Before the golden waves completely dispersed, the laughter of countless ghosts converged into one word:

“What do you think?”

Chapter 3122: Ding Lingdang'S Way

Li Yao wanted to vomit on the golden wave’s face.

But before he cursed, the ghosts of the Primeval Lord of Heaven and the translucent super warship vanished. He seemed to have been freed from a broken world of crystals and returned to a virtual world that was as vast as the universe.

Pictures, sounds, and historical figures, some heroic, some sordid, some righteous, and some evil, congregated into billions of glittering pieces, symbolizing the path of evolution from the civilization of mankind to the Primeval Civilization and from the tiny Earth to the endless stars. The path of evolution was like a brilliant galaxy that surrounded Li Yao.

In the middle of the starry universe, dozens of translucent, bright gold crystal balls were floating like glittering eggshells. Countless of Li Yao’s family, friends, and even enemies were taking their own tests in them like babies.

Ding Lingdang, Li Jialing, and Boss Bai were not the only standard human experts. The mental devil and Lu Qingchen, who were similar to energy life forms, were among them. Long Yangjun, who was a warrior from the Grand Desolate Plateau, and even the boxing champion, Xiao Ming, and Wen Wen, who had surpassed the civilization of mankind, were among them.

Faced with the choice of life and death and the enormous legacy left by the Primeval Civilization, what would they choose exactly?

Looking at Ding Lingdang, who was deep asleep with her eyes closed, inside the crystal ball, Li Yao clenched his fists. He wanted to break the crystal ball and rescue his wife at any cost.

However, he did not know whether or not the Primeval Lord of Heaven had set up other traps, and whether or not his initiative to help other people pass the test would be deemed as a “fault”, which would only make things worse.

Besides, the real experts had to face the interrogation of the soul all the time. Nobody could compete in their place.

Li Yao could only wait, anticipate, and believe!

Finally—

Out of Li Yao’s expectation, Ding Lingdang’s crystal ball was the first to crack. The interconnecting cracks soon spread to the entire ‘eggshell’, as if a new life that had been bred inside the crystal ball was eager to break out.

“Honey!”

Li Yao was both surprised and delighted. He did not expect Ding Lingdang to be the second person to break through after him. He had thought that Lu Qingchen or the mental devil would be faster!

BAM!

After a crisp sound, the bright gold ‘eggshells’ that covered Ding Lingdang’s body all shattered. The strongest female warrior of the Star Glory Federation, no, perhaps the entire Pangu Universe, had woken up from the ultimate test. She roared in fury and punched Li Yao, who was lunging at her, brutally, almost blowing his soul away.

“Honey, it’s me!”

Li Yao shouted, “Wake up! It’s over! Everything is over!”

“…Li Yao?”

Furious flames that looked like magma surrounded Ding Lingdang. Even in the virtual space, her fighting will seemed to be real, as if she was wearing invisible armor. Her consciousness was still caught in the weirdness of the ultimate test. It was not until a long time later that she realized who was in front of her.

“Careful, this is not a test. Don’t be fooled by them!”

Ding Lingdang was slightly relieved. Then she raised her eyebrow again. “This is a trap. A trap similar to the possession of a civilization!”

“Uh…”

Li Yao did not know how to explain himself. He opened his hands and comforted her. “Honey, calm down first. Of course, I know that it is not a regular test or legacy. But how should I put it? Just tell me what you encountered in the ‘ultimate test’. It seems that you did not inherit the legacy of the Primeval Civilization.”

“What legacy?”

Ding Lingdang still didn’t know what was going on. She pulled out her fist and glared at the surroundings. “Where is the old witch?”

“Old witch?”

Li Yao was confused, too. “Where did the old witch come from?”

“The golden, kind-looking old witch!”

Ding Lingdang gritted her teeth. “Be careful. She is very cunning!”

“Well…”

Li Yao felt a headache coming on. “What did you encounter in the ultimate test?”

Ding Lingdang calmed herself down and told Li Yao about her experience in the ultimate test.

Alright, Li Yao discovered that the content of Ding Lingdang’s test was almost the same as his. Perhaps the two of them belonged to the “bright” and “righteous” camp and were more hot-blooded and impulsive?

However, Ding Lingdang was apparently more hot-blooded and impulsive than him. When the Primeval Lord of Heaven proposed an ‘immigration plan’ in which 99% of the ordinary people on Earth and only 1% of the rich, powerful, and elite could survive, Ding Lingdang turned hostile on the spot and quit the game.

“He tricked 99% of the ordinary people into tightening their belts for the escape plan with the most sordid methods. In the end, only 1% of the rich and powerful boarded the escape capsule. What a f*cking civilization!”

Ding Lingdang said angrily, “What’s the difference between this approach and Wu Yingqi sacrificing an entire planet of innocent people to realize her ambitions? What future can there be in such a civilization that lives on the edge? It will definitely become what you once called ‘space cockroaches’! If I make such a choice, wouldn’t I become a second Wu Yingqi? A greedy, bottom-lineless animal that is worse than pigs and dogs!”

“I would rather die than choose such a shameless path. I would never let myself become a scumbag who is as cruel and unscrupulous as snakes and scorpions. I believe you are the same as me, aren’t you, husband?”

“Well…”

Li Yao scratched his head for a long time. “Then?”

“I didn’t want to do that. Then, the ‘voice’ coerced me into making some stupid ‘no other choice’ and even created all kinds of creepy illusions to threaten me. You know me very well. I can be persuaded by reason but not by force. The more the ‘voice’ threatened me, the more I felt that there was something wrong. Come to think of it, the answer is obvious!”

Ding Lingdang smashed her fists hard. “This is not a test of inheritance at all, but a trap similar to ‘possession of a civilization’. Just like what happened to you on Iron Plateau of the Flying Star Sector, if I made the choice that the ‘voice’ enticed me to, I would have fallen into the trap, made a lot of mistakes, and eventually degenerated into the appearance that the ‘voice’ desired, an incomparably dark and evil zombie!

“Therefore, the ‘voice’ is most likely not an ancient civilization. Even if it is an ancient civilization, it is a dark civilization full of evilness and something even more evil than the extraterrestrial devils.

“Now that you know that your opponent is a ‘super extraterrestrial devil’, what else is there to say? One word—fight!”

“In any case, I don’t like to use my brain in the first place. Fighting is what I like the most! Instead of wasting my mental energy and brain working blindly in a field that I’m not good at, it’s better to drag a battle into a field that I’m good at, right?”

“Is it… right?”

Li Yao did not know what to say. “Then, what’s with the glittering, kind lady? Why do you call her an old witch?”

“After I roared and prepared to fight the ‘voice’, I entered a deeper illusion.

Ding Lingdang thought for a moment and said, “A middle-aged woman who looked like the captain of a starship looked at me with a fake smile, talked to me, and offered me something.

“A key?”

Li Yao asked.

“It seems… to be a key.”

Ding Lingdang shrugged her shoulders. “You know my personality very well. Now that I’ve determined that the other party is an evil existence like a ‘super extraterrestrial devil’, I don’t care what you look like, what you say, or what you do. In any case, it’s all fake. It doesn’t matter if it’s a f*cking key or not. I’ll just punch the old witch in the face.”

“…”

Li Yao really wanted to wipe off the sweat on his forehead from the soul ripples. “You—you were so simple and crude. You really just punched it. Did it never occur to you that the key might represent the legacy of a god-level civilization?”
