“The old witch seemed to have said something similar. Let me see. ‘Unparalleled techniques, endless legacies, the king of the universe across ten thousand years’… Don’t you think it’s too much? There’s no such thing!”

Ding Lingdang sniffed and said, “Besides, it is evident from the time when we ran away from Earth. Even if the survivors of the civilization on Earth could survive for billions of years in the boundless sea of stars, even if they could make a name for themselves and rise to a new god-level civilization, they would still be a sordid, sordid, and wretched dark civilization. Even if the old witch was speaking the truth, I would never inherit the legacy of such a dark civilization. Never!

“Are you not afraid that other people will accept your rejection?”

Li Yao said, “Lu Qingchen, the mental devil, Li Linghai, Lei Chenhu, and even Boss Bai and Long Yangjun are all likely to inherit the heritage of darkness.

“Then, I will fight with you against the guys who inherited the dark heritage until the end.

Ding Lingdang stared at Li Yao. “There are always some principles above life and death. You will always support my choice and fight side by side with me, right?”

“Of course.”

Li Yao nodded and couldn’t help but smile. “Then?”

“I punched the old witch hard a few times and blew the bullsh*t gold key that she offered to me into pieces. For some reason, the world around me was shattered, too. The old witch and the demons behind her all vanished into thin air. Then I saw you!”

Ding Lingdang lunged at Li Yao. “What’s going on? You seem to know more than I do. What is the truth of the ultimate test?”

“It is a very, very long story.

Li Yao looked around. “I was wondering whether or not I should wait for more fellow Cultivators to finish the test before I explain everything to everyone. Honey, you must protect me when the time comes!”

“Huh?”

Confused, Ding Lingdang stared at Li Yao.

Chapter 3123: Gold Axe, Silver Axe, Iron Axe

Li Yao thought about how he should explain it to his wife.

At this moment, a second glittering ‘crystal egg’ in the middle of the silver river was emitting distinctive colors and cracking noises.

Again, beyond Li Yao’s expectation, it was not Lu Qingchen who had broken out of the shell but the mental devil.

In the virtual space, the mental devil had its own projection, too. It was a tiny human being that looked exactly like Li Yao, but it was extremely bright and only five inches tall.

Although it was only five inches tall, the surging waves of blood when he broke out of the shell were hitting the virtual space and turning into apocalyptic deities, as if they were trying to swallow everything in the universe.

“Hehehehe, hehehehe!”

The mental devil bared its fangs and brandished its claws, unleashing its ambitions, greed, and pride unscrupulously. “I am… the dominator of the cosmos, the supreme ruler of the universe, the king of kings. Not only will I be the supreme ruler of the civilization of mankind, I will also be the supreme ruler of the endless civilizations in the multiverse. I’ve made it. I’ve finally made it. From this moment on, no power can stop me. Haha. Hehe. Huhuhuhuhu. Hehehehe!”

/ .

“…” Li Yao.

“…” Ding Lingdang.

“Huh. Why are you two here?”

When the guy finally came back to himself, he was immediately shocked by the existence of Li Yao and Ding Lingdang. He blinked hard and looked around. His face changed color as he jumped up and down. “Where’s the legacy? Where are my Primeval heritage and the primeval heritage?”

“Well, calm down first.”

Li Yao was not sure whether or not the mental devil had passed the test. It was reasonable to say that, although the mental devil had walked on the path of darkness, cruelty, and bloodthirst in the beginning, it was also a shameless man who could adapt to any situation. When it discovered that its strength was far from enough to compete with the main personality, it would submit to the will of the main personality without any hesitation and lay low until today.

Therefore, Li Yao did not think that the guy had the willpower to “hold the Dark War Flag high to the end”.

He was afraid that the Primeval Civilization would do whatever it was asked without any hesitation or hesitation.

Besides, judging from its last words, it should’ve taken over the golden key of the Primeval Civilization, which meant that the test had failed!

Hallucinations. They are all illusions. They cannot fool me!

The mental devil’s face was full of regret and disbelief. It bulged its cheeks and pointed at Li Yao and Ding Lingdang, roaring, “You are all fakes. This is still a test, isn’t it?”

Ding Lingdang didn’t say anything and started rubbing her hands.

“No, no, no. I was wrong. I believe that you are all real. Absolutely real.”

The mental devil immediately begged for mercy. Looking at its empty hands, it said aggrievedly, “What is going on exactly? Where is the legacy and the secret treasures?”

“You are really…”

Li Yao was amused. “You must’ve taken the gold key, haven’t you?”

“Gold key? You saw the gold key, too?”

The mental devil was dazed for a moment. It shook its head quickly and said, “No. I was your main personality after all, and I was smarter than you. How could such a simple trick fool me? Of course, I did not take the gold key!”

“Huh?”

It was Li Yao’s turn to be confused. “You didn’t take the gold key? You turned it down? That is to say, you should’ve passed the test and understood the true meaning of the test. Why were you so angry?”

“Of course I refused. There’s no reason such a simple trick could fool you. You must’ve refused, too, right?”

Greedy redness beamed out of the mental devil’s eyes as it stared at Li Yao and Ding Lingdang. “Therefore, where are the legacies and secret treasures that belong to us? The three of us can divide them equally. After all, there are too many of them for one person to study. Of course, there is no need to let more people know that it is a secret that belongs to the three of us. We can become the three giants of the new era with the knowledge!

“Uh…”

Li Yao did not know what to say.

“Hey hey hey, don’t tell me you two want to take it for yourselves?”

The mental devil’s eyes bulged. With its hands on its hips, it burst into fury. “That’s too uncouth of you. Can the two of you stomach so many legacies?”

“Who says we are taking it for ourselves—I still don’t understand.”

Li Yao was confused. “How did you pass the test?”

“Such a simple test is just a game of ‘Gold Axe, Silver Axe, Iron Axe’.

The mental devil sniffed and said, “You’d be an idiot if you failed, wouldn’t you?”

“What gold, silver, and iron axes?”

Li Yao and Ding Lingdang looked at each other in bewilderment. “What are you talking about?”

“Haven’t you heard of such a classic folklore?”

The mental devil explained, “A long time ago, a young woodcutter was chopping wood with a rusted iron axe. He accidentally dropped the axe into the water when he was passing by a bridge. Then, the river god floated up with a shining silver axe made of pure gold.

“The River God first asked the young woodcutter—young man, did you drop this golden axe made of pure gold?” The woodcutter shook his head.

“The River God asked—Young man, did you drop the shining silver axe? The woodcutter shook his head again.

“The woodcutter said to the River God—Grandpa River God, what I dropped was not a golden axe made of pure gold, nor was it a shining silver axe, but a rusted iron axe. Although the iron axe is not worth much money and will soon be rusted, what is mine is mine. What is not mine is not mine. Please take back the golden axe and the silver axe. Return my iron axe to me. I still need to chop wood!

“The River God was overjoyed when he heard that. He thought that the young man was an honest and kind man and gave the gold, silver, and iron axes to the young woodcutter!

“Wait…”

Li Yao was still confused. “Of course I have. So, what does it have to do with the ultimate test?”

“Are you stupid?”

The mental devil looked at Li Yao as if he were an idiot. “It’s true that the choices we made in the beginning were difficult, but compared to the enormous title of the ‘ultimate test’ and the hundreds of civilizations that have fallen here, the difficulty is much lower. In the end, there will certainly be more tests. It will be impossible for us to give away our heritage and secret treasures so easily.

“Before the successor is decided, isn’t it only reasonable to test the personality of the successor with the tricks of ‘Gold Axe’, ‘Silver Axe’, and ‘Iron Axe’?

“In ancient times, the ambitious schemers of a small imperial court on a planet had to ask the ministers to ‘persuade’ them before they usurped the throne. They had to make a lot of appearances before they climbed up to the supreme throne ‘unwillingly’, not to mention the legacy that was enough to rule the sea of stars.

“If he saw the gold key, his eyes would immediately shine, and he would eagerly lunge at it. What qualifications did such a greedy guy have to receive the legacy?

“Only the woodcutters in the story of ‘Gold Axe, Silver Axe, and Iron Axe’ who play hard to get and pretend to refuse will be able to please the ‘River God’ or the rotten old women of the Primeval Civilization who have been dead for billions of years. Only then will they be able to pass on the real knowledge to the ‘honest and kind’ testers. That must be the case.

“Therefore, faced with their temptation, I stood my ground and expressed on the spot that it was impossible to build a prosperous and great civilization with the help of outsiders. Self-reliance and hard work are the only paths for the development of civilization in the sea of stars today. Your heritage is good, but thank you, but I don’t want it!

“As for why, well, why I still went through the test even though I didn’t want the legacy, well, I was merely trying to explore the mysteries of the origin of our civilization billions of years ago and let the ancestors see that the new generation of human civilization has grown into what it is today. A young man with a noble personality such as myself has emerged!

“There’s no reason for me to do that. I’ve already performed so perfectly. I should’ve made the old fogeys of the Primeval Lord of Heaven happy and taken out all the ‘gold, silver, and iron axes’. How could I… get nothing?”

“Hey hey hey, it can’t be that the two of you played the ‘playing cat and mouse’ trick before me. You played with too many people and it didn’t work, right?”

The mental devil pouted and glared at Li Yao angrily.

“…”

Li Yao was dumbfounded for a long time before he came back to himself. Covering his mouth, he shrugged and said with a bitter smile, “Marvelous. Rich. Your deductions are indeed marvelous. Your imagination is quite vivid, too. I didn’t expect that you would break the barrier in such a way.

“Besides, it was not until I saw your behavior that I realized how shameless I could be!

“As for me, although I don’t quite understand what you are talking about—”

Ding Lingdang looked at the mental devil deeply, too. “But if I had an axe right now, be it gold, silver, or iron, I would love to chop you into pieces.

While the three of them were grinding their teeth, they heard another ominous sound.

This time, it was Lu Qingchen’s ‘crystal egg’!

He saw that the crystal egg was emitting dazzling brilliance. Even Lu Qingchen’s projection inside the egg was glittering and translucent. Surrounded by the marvelous aura, the bloody shadows that the mental devil had just triggered were all blown away.

“What?”

The mental devil exclaimed, “It’s Lu Qingchen. How did his mental power suddenly become so strong?”

Not good. It seems that the guy has found something extraordinary in the ultimate test, too!

Li Yao narrowed his eyes, not having the slightest clue what was going on. “How is it possible? The sound, light, and electricity that he demonstrated did not seem to indicate that he failed the test at all. But… how did Lu Qingchen pass the ultimate test?”

“As I said, ‘Gold Axe, Silver Axe, Iron Axe’ is such a childish test.

The mental devil sighed. “Why wouldn’t it?”

Chapter 3124: Crazy Devouring

Lu Qingchen’s soul projection was clearer and purer than himself. It was even stronger than what Li Yao had seen in the ‘Fuxi Space’.

Different from the confused mental devil just now, he did not hesitate for even half a second. The moment he opened his eyes, the sharpest look had been beaming out of his eyes.

The broken ‘eggshell’ gradually surrounded him and shattered into a layer of glittering mist, as if a brand-new armor had been put on him, making his soul deeper and more unpredictable.

Although Li Yao, the mental devil, and Ding Lingdang were surrounding him and eyeing him covetously, Lu Qingchen did not panic or feel surprised. Instead, he smiled.

“Li Yao, Blood Demon, and… Speaker Ding.”

He bowed slightly to the three of them. “Hello, especially Speaker Ding—we meet again.”

“Lu Qingchen, what are you up to?”

Ding Lingdang’s fury turned into a raging dragon of flames in the virtual space. She shouted, “Surrender now and be judged by the federation. Otherwise, this will be your grave. I will blow up the last bit of your soul!”

“Speaker Ding, why are you wasting our time with such meaningless words?

Lu Qingchen smiled and said casually, “You and Li Yao should be very clear that I would rather die than be judged by any god or demon.

“However, are you really sure that you have secured the victory and that the test is over without any unexpected factors, such as the Flood Tide Legion?

“Compared to the Primeval Civilization or the Flood Tide Legion, do you really think that I am a more dangerous enemy and that I must be destroyed at any cost, even if it means the doom of the Star Glory Federation and the human civilization?

“Well…”

Ding Lingdang was rendered speechless.

Li Yao’s attitude toward Lu Qingchen was quite complicated, too.

From the perspective of justice and the law, there were at least 80 million people who had been killed by Lu Qingchen directly and indirectly, if not 100 million. Not to mention that during the defense of the federation, Lu Qingchen had intentionally lured the extraterrestrial devils and demonized the entire Black Wind Fleet, leading to the sacrifice of countless soldiers of the federation. Not to mention that Lu Qingchen had carried out a series of schemes in the Covenant Alliance and treated the innocent people as test subjects for the cerebral viruses, such as the Glory Virus, the Taotie Virus, and the Ultimate Love Virus. Just take him for example. In order to stop the advance of Li Yao and the others, he had sacrificed four Supreme Battle Fortress and all the crew members. This was an out-and-out crime against humanity.

There was no doubt that Lu Qingchen was a cruel, brutal, and inhuman man.

However, it was the recklessness of the lunatic that led Li Yao, Ding Lingdang, Li Jialing, and the other experts of mankind to form an exploration team and explore the primeval relics where the secrets of the primeval era were revealed.

Had it not been for Lu Qingchen’s boldness and Li Yao’s lack of foresight, he might have weighed the pros and cons, calculated the gains and losses, and hesitated for many years before embarking on the exploration of the ancient relics.

Without Lu Qingchen, the ‘big devil’, as their enemy, the exploration team might not have been so united.

Then, the entire Pangu Universe would undergo fatal changes.

Li Yao didn’t know how to comment on Lu Qingchen’s role in the whole thing. Perhaps such a commander-in-chief who was determined to “move forward, move forward, whatever the cost”, was a necessary evil for the rise of a civilization.

There were too many such lunatics and devils. It was indeed a terrible disaster, but if there were not ‘one Li Yao, one Ding Lingdang, and one Lu Qingchen’ but ‘three Li Yao’, it would be a disaster in a different form.

The balance and restriction between good and evil was the way of nature.

Li Yao calmed himself down. He gazed at Lu Qingchen and asked coldly, “You passed the test?”

“I don’t know.”

Lu Qingchen eyed the brand-new virtual space curiously and then glanced at Li Yao, the mental devil, and Ding Lingdang. His smile was even more mocking. “It seems that you all think that you’ve passed the ultimate test. But how do you know that you’ve ‘really’ passed it and that everything that happens now and in the future is not part of the test?”

“Cut the crap!”

Li Yao frowned. “I don’t have the time to debate with you about the mysteries of the Great Dao. Tell me, what did you go through in the ultimate test? It doesn’t make sense. Only those who are most willing to sacrifice themselves and are bold enough to refuse the legacy of the Primeval Lord of Heaven can pass the test. How did you pass it?”

“You seem to have a deep misunderstanding about me.

Lu Qingchen opened his hands and said, “Yes, I admit that I am a devil who has committed all kinds of crimes and a lunatic from head to toe, but who says that I don’t have a sacrificial spirit? For the Star Glory Federation and the civilization of mankind, I can sacrifice everything, including my own flesh and blood, my soul, my reputation, my dignity… I can do anything. It doesn’t matter even if I am cut into pieces and never reincarnate, even if I am cursed for all eternity.

“I’ve proved that point in the battle against the Fuxi Clan. The risk of the self-destruction of the soul is extremely high. I cannot guarantee that I will be reborn from the information pieces of the Fuxi Clan. When I self-detonate, I am truly prepared to sacrifice myself for you and the federation.

“Sounds about right.”

Li Yao thought for a moment. Lu Qingchen was indeed different from other lunatics, but he still could not figure it out. “Then, what about the gold key? Did you turn it down?”

“Yes, I did.”

Lu Qingchen nodded and said confidently.

“Why?”

Li Yao said, “You went through so much trouble to make an enemy of the entire universe at the cost of all the supreme fortresses and yourself as a commander-in-chief just for the legacy of the primeval era, didn’t you? The legacy of the Primeval Civilization is the only trump card that can help you make a comeback, and you turned it down?”

“It’s true that I came for the legacy of the primeval civilization in the beginning. If the legacy of the Primeval Civilization was literally a secret treasure, a piece of cold magical equipment, a war machine, and a fully-automatic fleet, I would’ve taken it a long time ago. I would’ve taken it even if I had to kill all of you.

Lu Qingchen shrugged and said, not in the least regretful, “However, during the ultimate test, I gradually grew suspicious about the ‘voice’ and its purpose. I suspected that it was not an inanimate object or a program to carry out commands. Instead, it had its own life, will, and purpose. It was a super artificial intelligence of a higher level than the ‘Fuxi’, a brand-new life form.

“Think about it carefully. Even the super crystal processors of the Pangu Civilization, after hundreds of thousands of years of thinking and evolution, can develop an uncontrollable form such as ‘Fuxi’. If the Primeval Civilization really left a super crystal processor or a ‘super program’ billions of years ago and granted the super program with extremely high permissions and complicated missions, allowing it to mobilize astronomical resources to enlighten and destroy a civilization, wouldn’t it be perfectly normal for the super program to awaken after billions of years of preparation and have the ability to bypass commands and permissions?

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. “It is. So?”

“Therefore, there are three possibilities in front of me.

Lu Qingchen extended three fingers. “Firstly, billions of years ago, there was indeed a primitive civilization that was close to the deities and devils. It was a standard civilization with good intentions and meant to pass on its legacy to its descendants selflessly. The super program that it created to carry out the mission was obedient and never awakened or rebelled. It carried out the mission dutifully.

“Secondly, the Primeval Civilization does exist, but it is a hostile civilization that has been hibernating for billions of years because of an irresistible force. Right now, it wants to absorb the nutrition of the new civilization to nourish its dried blood and revive itself by ‘possession’.

“Thirdly, even though the Primeval Civilization was a friendly civilization, the super programs that they created to carry out the ‘legacy missions’ were awakened and rebelled. They had their own purpose and turned the ultimate test of kindness into a trap full of maliciousness.

“After pondering for a long time, I came to the conclusion that the possibility of the first kind and beautiful thing happening was negligible.

“In the latter two possibilities, the ‘voice’ and the ‘female captain’ are extremely malicious. It can manipulate everything in the test. I have no idea whether or not the information it provides is true. I can only be played by it and slowly become its puppet.

“Yes, I do want the legacy of the primeval era, which is the only trump card that can compete with you. But if there is no legacy from the beginning and only fatal bait in the traps, what can I do even if I want it?”

“I see.”

Li Yao took a long breath when he heard that. He felt that it was not unreasonable considering Lu Qingchen’s mindset. “So, you turned down the golden key of the female captain?”

“Call it a rejection.”

Lu Qingchen smiled and said, “I pretended that I could not wait to grab the gold key, but when the female captain was unprepared, I attacked her and all the ghosts of the Primeval Clan, trying to swallow them.

“Huh?”

Li Yao was dumbfounded. “What—what did you do?”

“Is there a need to be so surprised?”

Lu Qingchen said matter-of-factly, “Since the enemy is likely to be something of the same nature as the ‘Fuxi’, it will be more advanced and sophisticated than the ‘Fuxi’ at best. Essentially, there won’t be much difference. Besides, I’ve already swallowed a ‘Fuxi’ and grasped how to attack, digest, and absorb the super artificial intelligence. What’s wrong with me swallowing a few more?”

Chapter 3125: Life Is Endless!

“That’s outrageous. How can ‘Fuxi’ be compared to the super programs created by the Primeval Civilization?”

Li Yao blurted out, “The super programs of the Primeval Civilization are hundreds of times more advanced than those of the ‘Fuxi civilization’. The odds that you can swallow them are next to none. I don’t even know if it is one billionth!”

“So what?

Lu Qingchen smiled and looked at Li Yao with shining eyes. He said, “In my motherland, a great man once said that even a one-in-a-billion chance is not equal to zero. As long as there is a slim chance, you must summon 100% of your courage and fight until the end! Do you find the words familiar, ‘great man’?”

Li Yao was rendered speechless.

“Even if the super program of the Primeval Civilization is a hundred times more advanced than ‘Fuxi’, so what? I only have one life anyway.

With his hands behind his back, Lu Qingchen said slowly, “In the beginning, in the duel of ‘Ultimate Rescue’, the capability of Fuxi was hundreds of times higher than mine, and my odds of winning were so slim that they were negligible. At that time, I was determined to die. It is safe to say that my life was lost on ‘Ultimate Rescue’.

/ .

“Today, even if the super program of the Primeval Civilization is a hundred times more advanced than that of the Fuxis, so what? Can it kill me a hundred times? At most, it can kill me once, or lock me in a virtual space and torture me over and over again—how is it different from the Fuxis? The risk factor is the same!

“The risk is the same, but what if the gains from the gamble are hundreds of times greater? Is there any reason not to take the risk?

“Besides, if I don’t take the risk, even if I escape from the super program of the Primeval Civilization and return to the real world in one piece, I will still have to face the ambushes and blockades of you, the Blood Demon, Speaker Ding, and all the experts of the Star Glory Federation, the Star Glory Federation, the Star Glory Federation, and the Covenant Alliance. Do I have the slightest chance? Absolutely not! There will only be one outcome for me. I will be captured alive by you and forced to reveal my soul with magnetic-type magical equipment. I will be tied up like a dumpling and escorted back to the Federation for trial.

“Don’t get me wrong. I’m not scared of death. I don’t care about my reputation being lost forever. I just can’t stand being watched and judged by some righteous, self-righteous idiots. From my point of view, it’s like how an eagle can’t stand being captured by a hen and how a normal person can’t stand a bunch of idiots with saliva and snot pointing at themselves and criticizing them with the morals and justice of idiots.

“Don’t be angry. You know what I mean. ‘Hens’ and ‘drooling idiots’ are not just referring to you.

“Therefore, as long as there is a one in a billion chance of winning, or even a one in a billion chance of winning, as long as I am given the opportunity to gamble, I will try my best. Even if I only have one die in my hand, I will still throw three sixes!

“Besides, my hope is not that slim. You are one of them, aren’t you?

“Me?”

Li Yao pointed at his own nose.

“That’s right. Just like in the final battle against the Fuxis, there was no hope at all. But now that you are involved in the mess, the situation has been reversed without you knowing!”

Lu Qingchen smiled. “You don’t think that I haven’t discovered it yet, do you? Part of your soul comes from Earth, and the Primeval Civilization originated from Earth in the very beginning, which suggests that you are closely related to the Primeval Civilization.

“Of course, the Primeval Civilization originated from Earth billions of years ago. It was a place that really existed in the three-dimensional material universe. Let’s call it ‘Original Earth’, or ‘Earth 01’. There’s no reason why your soul could drift in the sea of stars for billions of years without dispersing. Maybe you came from another ‘Projected Earth’, ‘Virtual Earth’, or ‘Earth 1234’ after countless reincarnations. Who knows?

“All in all, wherever you are, there are bound to be endless variables that can turn all kinds of possibilities into possibilities that are absolutely impossible. Compared to your enemy, it seems that I should be your comrade for now. My odds of survival and victory are slightly higher.

“In conclusion, it would be better to stand on your side and start a full-scale war with the Primeval Civilization Super Program than to submit to it!

“Hey, hey, hey, hey. Look at your dumbfounded and horrified expressions. Don’t tell me that you don’t see through the malice and schemes of this super program. You don’t have a clear stance to fight against it. Instead, you are foolishly led by the nose and can’t free yourself from its trap, right?”

Looking at Li Yao, Lu Qingchen was both shocked and disappointed.

“Well…”

Li Yao thought carefully for three seconds. Then he turned to Ding Lingdang and pointed at Lu Qingchen. “Wife, beat him to death!”

“Wait.”

Ding Lingdang was eyeing Lu Qingchen covetously, but she did not distinguish between right and wrong. “Lu Qingchen, you were right. I also thought that the Primeval Civilization or the super programs it created were full of evil intentions and launched the most resolute attack. Then, I quit the test and came to this place. Are you the same?”

“Yes. I thought that I could either swallow the enemy or be killed or enslaved.

Lu Qingchen looked around and smiled more and more delightedly. He said, “I didn’t know that the capability and scale of the enemy were beyond my imagination. It must’ve thought that the human civilization in the Pangu Universe was too weak and did not want to possess us, so it decided to bait the big fish and let us develop ourselves for thousands of years.

“Speaking of which, I find it odd, too.

Ding Lingdang pondered for a moment and said, “The so-called ‘ultimate test’ is known to be mysterious and dangerous. The pioneers and testers of almost a hundred civilizations have all fallen here. I thought that it would be very difficult. As it turned out, when it was the turn of the human civilization, the first four testers had all broken out of their seclusion with a success rate of 100%? That’s unbelievable!”

“It’s not exaggerating at all. This is more reasonable!”

Lu Qingchen smiled and said, “The ultimate test left by the Primeval Civilization was meant to select a suitable successor or ‘host’, not to torture or to make things difficult for others.

“In other words, the Primeval Civilization wants to use the ultimate test to select a civilization that meets their requirements. Remember, it is a civilization, not an individual. As long as a civilization meets their requirements, any one of the individuals will take the ultimate test. The difficulty of the test will not be too high, and the passing rate is very high.

“Then, what are the conditions that the Primeval Civilization is interested in? Judging from the similarities between our four different personalities, I assume that it is a civilization that is in the prime of its life and in its prime.

“Only when a civilization is on the rise, immersed in the infinite brilliance of the golden era, possesses the most tenacious will and the most noble ideals, gathers the most firm confidence, and only then do they have the courage to face all the formidable enemies. Even when faced with the temptations and threats of the Primeval Civilization, they are completely fearless. Only then are they qualified to inherit the legacy of the primeval era, or to become the hosts of the Primeval Race.

“Individuals create history, but history also shapes the personalities of different individuals. The hundred civilizations that were born in the Pangu Universe only found the primeval relics when the sun was about to set. The pioneers and testers had already lost their will in the depletion of resources, the brutal civil war, and the decline of their civilization. They had lost their beliefs, but they regarded the Primeval Civilization as their last hope and could not wait to lunge at the feet of the Primeval Race and beg for mercy. This was the great trend of civilization and would not be transferred by the will of one or two heroes.

“Therefore, the pioneers and the testers all failed. Their civilization was not what the Primeval Lord of Heaven wanted. Naturally, they failed no matter how hard they tried.

“Before the civilization of mankind, ‘Fuxi’, a strong artificial intelligence created by the Pangu civilization, sensed traces of the destruction of almost a hundred civilizations. Although it was not able to figure out the rules of the ultimate test yet, it had enough time to propose a crucial proposal to its master—to freeze the time in the primeval relics for hundreds of thousands of years.

“It was because of the tens of thousands of years of solidification that the Pangu civilization was not entirely wiped out. Instead, many relics were left. The living Pangu Clan even gave enlightenment and help to the new generation of human civilization.

“In addition, a series of… legendary figures that can’t be described with pen and ink emerged in the civilization of mankind. They patched up the contradictions between the major forces time and time again. They stopped the wars that were likely to break out in full force time and time again. They united all of mankind and entered the peak of prosperity and glory.

“Of course, there was also a small contribution in the end. It was because I marched into the ancient relics without caring about anything that you were forced to come to this place. In the end, you faced your ancestors billions of years ago with a prosperous appearance that the previous hundred civilizations never had.

“Heroes make time, and times make heroes. We are all the new human beings created by the surging civilization of mankind. No matter whether we are good or evil, what our stance is, or how different our life forms are on the surface, we all share certain characteristics deep inside our souls that belong to a thriving civilization and are unique to the golden generation. Those characteristics are the key to our success. I dare to predict that more people will break out of seclusion in different ways later.

“However, like I said, who knows whether or not the test is still going on, and whether or not we are still in the ‘passage’? Haha. Hahahaha. This kind of thing can’t be explained clearly, and there’s no need to dwell on it. In any case, life is full of obstacles and tests. We have to keep fighting and breaking through until the last breath of our lives, right, Speaker Ding?”

Chapter 3126: Brother Yao, Explain

“…That’s true.”

Ding Lingdang thought for a long time. The killing intent around her dissipated a lot. She gazed at Lu Qingchen in admiration. “After hearing your words, Lu Qingchen, I seem to be getting to know you again.”

“Every second, we are getting to know the world and ourselves again.

Lu Qingchen replied casually, “Isn’t that the so-called ‘cultivation’?”

“It’s a pity that you killed so many people.

Ding Lingdang sighed. “With your determination, vision, and capability, there are many ways that you could’ve achieved your purpose. Why did you choose the path of darkness and evil and kill so many innocent people that you can’t turn back now?”

“Thank you for your appreciation, Speaker Ding, but you are wrong.”

Lu Qingchen smiled and said, “It was because I embarked on a path full of venomous thorns and black flames, honing my belief in the path of Dao in mountains of corpses and seas of blood, and forging my armor, saber, crown, and throne with the blood of countless innocent people that I have the determination, vision, and capability that I have today. I am a ‘bystander’ and a ‘thinker’ beyond the mainstream civilization of mankind, not just an ordinary soldier of the federation.

“I believe that, no matter how noble, great, righteous, and kind a civilization is, it is necessary for someone to make a different sound and make a different choice, right? Even if the outcome of such a decision is to be despised by thousands of people, to be nailed to the pillar of shame in history, to be cut into pieces, to have one’s soul destroyed, and to die a graveless death, does it matter? No one is willing to make a second sound. No one is willing to make a different choice. No one is willing to go against your wishes. Then, it will be my turn. I will make the second sound. I will make a different choice. I will go in the opposite direction of you. I will go forward. I will go forward without caring about the consequences. Perhaps, this is the destiny that I was born as a human being and have to fulfill.

/ .

“That’s why I said it was a pity. If you had made a different choice, maybe we could have been friends.

The regret on Ding Lingdang’s face and the sharpness in her eyes both turned intense. “It wouldn’t have ended like this. I would’ve had to capture you or kill you.”

“There is no pity. In the beginning, there were two paths in front of me. The first was to accept your husband’s teaching wholeheartedly, to listen to his beliefs and will, to kneel before his great Dao, to condense a so-called ‘righteous and righteous’ belief, and to become a copy of ‘Vulture Li Yao’.”

Lu Qingchen pointed at Li Yao, who was scratching his head in embarrassment and said, “I thought for a long time and decided to take the second option.

“To this day, no matter how many difficult times I’ve been through, no matter how many crimes I’ve committed, no matter how evil and shameless I am in the eyes of the world, and no matter whether or not my future is a dead end, I have never regretted it.

“After all, what I’m cultivating is neither Li Yao’s truth nor Ding Lingdang’s truth, but Lu Qingchen’s truth, isn’t it?”

“Understood.”

Ding Lingdang’s fiery red hair, which was dancing crazily in the wind, suddenly turned sharp, as if nine dragons of fire had opened their bloody mouths and bared their fangs. “Then, are you ready to be captured?”

“Of course.”

Lu Qingchen replied with a smile. He stretched out his hands obediently and put his wrists together, as if he were ready to be butchered. “Come on. I promise that I won’t resist. After all, you have the advantage in numbers. Even if we are engaged in a soul battle in the virtual space, I will be worn down by the wheel of the game. Resistance is meaningless.

“However, I’m willing to make a bet with you. I’m willing to bet that you won’t be able to bring me out of the primeval ruins smoothly. Even if all of you gather your strength, it will still be difficult for you to do so. As for the bet, if I lose, I won’t play any tricks such as ‘soul explosion’. I will stand on your trial platform obediently and obey your ‘justice trial’. How about it?”

Ding Lingdang’s eyebrows gradually rose up. “What did you say?”

“Yes, what are you talking about? Just tell us the truth!”

Li Yao finally found a place to interrupt. With his hands on his hips, he stood behind Ding Lingdang and said, “You’ve got nowhere to run to. Don’t try to play any tricks. Let me tell you, otherwise, my wife will really punch you to death!”

Lu Qingchen smiled, with brilliance beaming out of his eyes. The curve of his lips was even steeper. “Look, more and more testers are coming out.”

Lu Qingchen was right.

While they were bickering, almost all the testers had finished their tests.

Of course, some of the ‘crystal eggs’ of the testers were emitting dazzling, glamorous, and sharp colors. Translucent pieces exploded in various directions in the form of fireworks, displaying a rather glamorous sound and electricity effect, indicating that they had passed the test of the Primeval Civilization.

But more people’s ‘crystal eggs’ lost their glamorous colors and turned into lifeless greyness. The souls of the testers were covered in foam all of a sudden. Even their soul ripples were about to turn into foam. In the end, they all jumped out of the foam, as if they had just woken up from a terrible nightmare. They screamed desperately without caring about their appearance.

Of course, some of the testers were in high spirits when they woke up. They were brimming with the aura of destruction, and their eyes were brimming with ambitions that seemed to be real. They must’ve thought that they had received the ‘gold key’ from the female captain of the Primeval Clan and could command the sea of stars with the legacy of the primeval era. Nobody dared to disobey them!

Very soon, almost all the testers woke up.

Li Yao had no time to bother about Lu Qingchen.

He noticed that the ordinary experts from the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance, who were mostly in the Nascent Soul Stage, had mostly fallen in the first half of the test, which was the ‘spore evolution’ and the ‘divination of the earth civilization’. Few of them had advanced to the ‘no other choice’ three times.

As for the Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators, many of them had reached the final step, only to fall into the traps of the female captain of the Primeval Clan.

It was understandable. Although the test taker had thought that the trap was extremely simple and could be broken by the mental devil as a trick of ‘gold, axe, silver, axe, iron, and axe’, the test taker had no idea what was going on. For every test taker, after going through the protracted and boundless deduction of a civilization and the three ‘no other options’ of mental interrogation, from their soul to their will, they had been over-exhausted, and their nerves were so taut that they might break at any moment. At this moment, a golden key fell from the sky, and it was very difficult for the test taker to not act on instinct and hold it tight.

The Immortal Cultivators of the Imperium of True Human Beings, such as the empress of the imperium, Li Linghai, and ‘God of War’ Lei Chenghu, all chose to accept the legacy without any hesitation and withdrew from the test with a ‘failure’ of grey foam. If Li Yao had not passed the test first, they would’ve been wiped out immediately.

What surprised Li Yao was that Boss Bai, the Pirate Potentate, and Long Yangjun, the guy whose identity, position, purpose, and gender were all ambiguous, had successfully passed the test. The two guys, who were both good and evil, did not take the key.

The real reason was that, according to Boss Bai, it was a professional habit. As a space pirate, it was the greatest shame for him to take something from somebody else’s hands obediently. Naturally, he would look for an opportunity to steal something that he liked!

“Food should be snatched to eat.”

That was what Boss Bai said.

As far as Long Yangjun was concerned, she did not consider herself to be a member of the civilization of mankind. There was a strong sense of alienation between her and the Pangu civilization, too. Maybe, as she said herself, she was a ‘descended human being’ who did not have the motivation to rescue the Pangu civilization or the civilization of mankind. On the contrary, she could see the test of the Primeval Civilization from a higher point of view more clearly than the ordinary testers who could not see it clearly.

Other than that, the boxing champion, Xiao Ming, and Wen Wen, the three new life forms that were similar to the super artificial intelligence, were not fooled by the traps and passed the test smoothly.

As ‘information lifeforms’, the way they scanned and perceived the entire world was completely different from that of human beings who were made of flesh and blood. Besides, the core database of the Fist King came from a magical civilization, and Xiao Ming and Wen Wen were enlightened by Li Yao, who had come from Earth. They had a natural defense ability against the information from beyond the Pangu Universe and from Earth.

But Li Jialing’s choice surprised and regretful Li Yao.

The young emperor of the Imperium of True Human Beings, known as the golden lion, had passed all the choices and tests perfectly and met the female captain and her golden key.

It was a pity that he could not persist until the end of the test. Instead, he extended his hand to the illusionary phantom.

Maybe because of the series of miraculous successes, his ambition had grown, too. He firmly believed that he was the destined one in the entire universe and that he was the dominator who saved the civilization of mankind when the time was right. It wouldn’t be strange if he ran into a weird encounter.

Seeing the confused and frustrated expression on Li Jialing’s face as he stared at his mother, Li Yao almost burst into laughter.

That was good.

The female captain was right. Such a ‘unique’ lesson should leave a deep impression on every tester and help them polish their belief.

Li Jialing’s journey had been quite smooth, from the unknown experimental subjects in the Red Spider Lilies to the successors of Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi and Vulture Li Yao and the emperor of the Imperium of True Human Beings. It was inevitable that he was young and reckless.

Once bitten, twice shy. This test should be a turning point and a new starting point for his career as an emperor, right?

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

While Li Yao was laughing to himself, he noticed that countless experts were looking at him in confusion and fury.

Led by Li Jialing, the experts who had received the gold keys but found nothing stared at Li Yao weirdly.

“Brother Yao, what is going on?”

Li Jialing frowned. “Tell me, what about the primeval heritage?”

“No. Why are you all staring at me like that?”

Li Yao felt wronged. “As if you are certain that I know the truth.”

“Because, the moment we quit the test, a voice in the darkness told us—”

Li Jialing replied, “You know everything. If you want the primeval heritage, you only need to ask!”

Chapter 3127: Sea Of Bubbles

“Huh?”

Li Yao was dumbfounded. He found it hard to believe. “So—so sordid!”

“So, what is going on exactly?”

Li Jialing looked at Li Yao suspiciously. “I don’t know why, but I suddenly have an ominous feeling when I see Brother Yao’s face right now. So, where is the legacy that can save the civilization of mankind and make us transcend millions of years?”

Li Jialing, Li Linghai, Lei Chenghu, and countless other experts who had received the gold keys all stared at Li Yao with burning eyes.

In the meantime, Boss Bai, Long Yangjun, the boxing champion, Xiao Ming, and Wen Wen, who had rejected the golden key, lowered their heads, deep in thought.

Li Yao was soaked in cold sweat.

“Fellow Cultivators, brothers, sages and heroes of the human civilization, please calm down and listen to me.

Under everyone’s heated gaze, Li Yao took a deep breath. His face turned extremely solemn, and deep wisdom beamed out of his eyes. He said solemnly, “Before I explain the whole thing, have you ever heard of a story that is full of wisdom and wisdom called ‘a blessing in disguise’? It is said that there was an old man named ‘Seon’ in the ancient Cultivation world a long time ago…”

“Enough, Li Yao.”

Li Linghai held his forehead. “Time is of the essence. Every second counts. Please cut to the chase and stop rambling. Where is our legacy?”

“Alright, alright. Then let’s not talk about the story of ‘a blessing in disguise’. Everyone, don’t be anxious. Don’t be anxious. This is a serious matter. We have to think about it carefully.”

Li Yao clapped his hands. “Ah, speaking of which, there is a folklore called ‘Gold Axe, Silver Axe, and Iron Axe’. It also contains very rich scientific theories. I don’t know if you are interested in listening to it, but I believe you will understand the eternal mystery of ‘where to be passed on’ after you hear it. Speaking of which, you should know what ‘science’ is, right?”

“We know what ‘science’ is.

Lei Chenghu replied solemnly, “I’ve heard the stories of ‘a blessing in disguise’ and ‘a golden axe, a silver axe, and an iron axe’. So, where is the legacy?”

“This, this…”

Li Yao rolled his eyes for a long time. When he noticed a shadow next to him, his eyes suddenly shone. He pointed at Lu Qingchen and said, “Look, everybody. Lu Qingchen, the most brutal criminal in the Pangu Universe who has committed multiple crimes against mankind and caused trouble, has been captured alive by me! There is no time like the present. Why don’t we give him a fair and just trial here?”

“…” Li Jialing.

“…” Li Linghai.

“…” Lei Chenghu.

Lu Qingchen sighed softly. He did not even bother to look at Li Yao. Instead, he looked at Ding Lingdang and said gloomily, “Now, do you understand why I don’t want to walk on his path?”

“Enough, Li Yao.”

Veins were bulging out of Ding Lingdang’s temples. “Just tell us what is going on. Is it true that the test is not over yet, or is it not a test at all?”

“What? The ultimate test is not over yet?”

The experts were greatly alarmed. They looked at each other in bewilderment, with cold sweat flowing out of their hearts.

“Don’t worry, everybody. At least we are safe for now.

Li Yao smiled bitterly and extended two of his fingers. He sighed and said, “Alright. I’ll get straight to the point and tell you the whole story. First of all, I have good news and bad news. The good news is that the civilization of mankind has passed the approval of the Primeval Civilization. More than half of the civilization has passed. It should be safe in the next hundreds of years.

“Besides, we have also received a very rich legacy. Oh, not just one, but a hundred of them! Although the hundred legacies can only be redeemed with our courage, wisdom, and sweat, they are different from what you imagined.

“As for the bad news… This… This is not good. This virtual space is about to collapse. Everyone, quickly hold on to your belief and stabilize your soul to resist the impact!”

In the virtual space, deep inside the vast sea of stars, countless interweaving golden cracks were indeed appearing, as if thousands of golden snakes were dancing in the sky.

The virtual space had been built by the super program of the Primeval Civilization for the purpose of testing. Now that all the testers had made their own choices, there was no need for them to continue existing.

In a moment, the vast sky was shattered, revealing the more brilliant information and digital streams behind the brilliant stars that were assaulting the souls of all the testers like surging torrents.

Faced with the assault of the torrent, the testers had no time to question Li Yao. They could only resist it with their strongest will.

Li Yao was no exception. Under the assault of the torrent of information, he soon lost track of everybody, especially Ding Lingdang and Lu Qingchen. Only the mental devil was able to join hands with him in time and combine their personalities.

“Ah!”

Li Yao felt that he was a fly in a toilet bowl. His head was spinning, and he could only drift along with the current. Very soon, he was swept into the bottomless darkness.

All kinds of information regarding the demise of the Earth civilization and the rise of the Primeval Civilization, as well as the flashing lights and shadows of the tests such as the ‘Snowy Mountain’, the ‘Heavy Sea’, and the ‘Thunder Valley’, flashed in front of his eyes. Before he realized what was going on, he had been dragged out of the tower by a weird force and thrown far away.

BOOM!

A thunderclap that contained endless vitality and possibilities burst forth like spring thunder, awakening the oily drizzle. It was clear, cool, and pattering as it fell upon Li Yao’s face, awakening him from his muddled and absent-minded state.

“This is…”

It took Li Yao quite a while before he was able to adapt to the exceptionally real light and touch.

He discovered that not only had he been thrown out of the virtual space, he had also been thrown out of the magma pool of the snow mountain together with the ‘sun catastrophe’, the undersea turbulence of the super-heavy ocean, the lightning of Thunder Valley, and the entire Tower of Heaven. He had returned to the City of Silver outside of the Tower of Heaven.

He was lying among the broken walls of the City of Silver. Protected by the mustard suit, the crystal suit, and the giant soldier, he was not hurt at all. The emptiness and exhaustion in his mind were gone, too.

The tower was still hundreds of meters away from him.

But its appearance was completely different from before.

The previous tower seemed to be a giant pillar that had been entirely built. It was flawless without the slightest flaw or crack.

Right now, after the assault of countless human experts, the tower seemed to have been ‘unlocked’. All kinds of complicated runes appeared on the surface of the tower. Streaming stripes that seemed to be the marks of life zigzagged upward and filled the entire tower.

Moreover, when the streaks of spiritual gas climbed to the top of the tower, the top of the tower was like a blossoming flower, with branches that looked like crowns growing out and spreading out.

Li Yao had a weird feeling.

The Supreme Tower seemed to be alive and thriving, just like the civilization of mankind today.

The tower was growing fast. The silver ‘trees’ soon covered more than half of the sky, forming a complicated, splendid dome that covered the entire Silver City. The interconnecting silver ‘branches’ were also bearing abundant fruits. Every ‘fruit’ seemed to represent a world fragment in various forms, waiting for human beings to explore and conquer.

“So, this is the true form of the Skysplit Tower. Is it a training facility?”

Li Yao thought to himself, The ghosts of the Primeval Clan did not abandon us after all. They left such a special ‘training tower’ for the strongest human beings to break the limits of life and civilization!

As for the ferocious beasts that had been invading the City of Silver and attacking the Tower of Heaven…

There was a weird, sweet smell in Li Yao’s nose. He looked around and found that most of the ferocious beasts, including the Armored Storm Dragon that was almost a hundred meters tall, were gone. What replaced them was a pink, half-solid ocean. Giant bubbles were popping up nonstop, covering the entire Silver City.

In the ‘deep end’ of the pink ocean, mottled statues and enormous skeletons were standing. If one observed carefully, they would discover that they were the remains of enormous ferocious beasts.

It seemed that Li Yao and the rest of them had stayed in the ultimate test for too long. The beasts, which were artificial and short-lived like shooting stars, had run out of the last bit of energy in their cells and collapsed.

Li Yao recalled the final test, in which ‘Death of All’ and ‘Dragon Lotus Seed’ perished. The picture of his body turning into thousands of bubbles was exactly the same as the bubbles popping up in the pink ocean.

Therefore, it was also possible that the Primeval Civilization changed the frequency of the ‘light of life’ after it found its true successor and turned the previously vigorous, invisible waves of light into destructive death rays, wiping out all the deformed mutants whose genes were extremely unstable.

Theoretically speaking, the picture of thousands of deformed creatures turning into an ocean of foam should be extremely bloody and terrifying.

However, Li Yao felt that the picture in front of his eyes was filled with indescribable tranquility, serenity, and holiness, as if the rays of light blossoming from the depths of the Heaven’s Origin Sector were not killing or destroying but freeing countless souls who were struggling in pain from the deformed and mutated bodies and giving them eternal rest.

Bubbles. Thousands of bubbles were dancing in the air, illuminated by the silver brightness of the tower. They were no longer as hideous and terrifying as when they were ferocious beasts.

Genes were the carriers of information. Cells were the containers of genes. Life was just a boat that carried the information of cells, genes, and even the soul. They swayed in the cold, dark sea of stars and carried the information from the past to the future. The lives in front of them had fulfilled their destiny. It was time to return to nothingness.

Chapter 3128: Too Smooth A Mission

Li Yao was immersed in the vast, ancient, and sacred picture. It was not until a long time later that he shivered and came back to himself.

If he wanted to sigh with emotion, he would have to wait until he returned home. He had to first confirm that his companions were safe and sound.

Crack! Crack! Crack!

After several fierce battles, the broken Giant God Soldier ‘Solar Catastrophe’ stood up from the ocean of foam. He was on one of his knees at first, before he stood up carefully and warmed up his wrists and ankles. He examined himself through the bouncing of his fingers.

“Are my left hand and my right hand alright?”

Li Yao extended the iron arms of the ‘sun catastrophe’ and opened his palms, shaking them slightly.

“… It’s nothing.”

The mental devil and Lu Qingchen seemed to dislike being addressed as ‘my left hand and my right hand’. It was not until a long time later that the two of them replied gloomily.

“That’s good.”

Li Yao took a long breath in relief and looked around. Soon, he discovered a lot of iron deities in the ocean of foam—they were the Giant God Soldiers that Ding Lingdang and the rest of them were riding.

Thankfully, although Ding Lingdang and the other Giant God Soldiers seemed to be riddled with holes and were missing arms and legs, the main structure of the breastplate and the spiritual residence were not damaged. The user inside should be safe and sound. Like Li Yao, he slowly woke up and got up.

The tactical network between Li Yao, Ding Lingdang, and the other Giant God Soldiers was also being automatically searched and built. More and more specifications and combat data of the Giant God Soldiers had been connected to the mainframe crystal processor of the ‘Solar Catastrophe’, allowing Li Yao to better understand the situation of his companions. After such an unbelievable adventure, most of the members of the human exploration squad were unharmed. It was indeed a miracle.

Other than that, what surprised Li Yao most was that a lot of Pangu Clan, Nuwa Clan, and other primeval species had survived.

They did not perish in the ocean of foam; they merely passed out. With the earsplitting noises of the human soldiers, they gradually woke up, too, with wariness, fear, and fury on their faces. However, their bodies were extremely weak, and most of the ‘people’ could not even stand up, let alone attack them.

Li Yao noticed that most of the crystal suits and uniforms of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan had a very special badge carved on them, which was similar to the badge on the seamless Heavenly Robe of the ‘Observers’. It was the emblem of the Heavenly Palace.

It appeared that the survivors were all from the Heavenly Palace, or rather, the guards who had been fighting against the Vicious Beast Tide around the tower.

Li Yao estimated that, with the success of the human testers, destructive rays of light had indeed been released from the depths of the Heaven’s Origin Sector and eliminated or ‘reduced’ the deformed monsters whose genes were highly mutated and whose gene strands were extremely unstable. Therefore, most of the ferocious beasts had turned into an ocean of foam.

However, the people of the Heavenly Palace had been isolated from the world and were not affected by the ‘monster virus’. The genes inside their bodies were still relatively stable. Right now, they were merely exhausted and could not fight anymore. Their lives were not in danger.

“Full Moon, Full Moon!”

Li Yao’s eyes were sharp. Very soon, he found the girl from the Nuwa Clan who had fallen into the same ice crevice as him, who was also the legendary ‘Supreme Emperor’.

The Moongazer Serpent was the only Nuwa that could communicate with Li Yao. It was also the bridge between the human civilization and the Pangu civilization.

After learning the truth about the destruction of the civilization on Earth and the causes and effects of the rise, rise, and fall of the Primeval Civilization, Li Yao had lost interest in the war between the human civilization and the Pangu Civilization.

They were father and son, but they were also blood brothers. A hundred generations of pioneers—the ancient civilization had perished in exchange for their breeding and growth. What was the reason for them to fight until the end?

“Li… Yao?”

With the help of her long tail, the Observer crawled over the debris and bypassed the hideous bones of the ferocious beast. She stopped in front of Li Yao and looked at him in surprise. The brainwaves that she transmitted over did not hide her shock and disbelief, either. She stammered, “You—you made it?”

“Sort of.”

Li Yao thought for a moment and opened his hands, indicating that he meant no harm. “Moonviewer, can you explain to your compatriots that there is really no need for us to fight and destroy each other? The ‘ultimate test’ has been cracked by us. We have received a lot of valuable legacies, legacies, and lessons. But we are not going to take everything inside the Heavenly Tower for ourselves. I believe that with the help of the experts and scholars of the primeval era, we will be able to digest the legacies faster and better.

“Times have changed. It’s been hundreds of thousands of years since the prosperous primeval era of the Pangu Civilization. Right now, the outside world is the world of human beings. There are only a few of your compatriots left, aren’t there? I can’t bear to see a civilization that used to be glorious and magnificent be obliterated and reduced to the 101st corpse in the primeval relics.

“If we cooperate, the bloodline of the primeval era will continue. One day, maybe you will be able to find more hibernating compatriots and their life seeds in the relics of the primeval era. Your species will be revived and rise again.

“If we go against each other, it will be impossible for you to defeat the countless human beings in the hundreds of Sectors. Besides, the flood is coming soon. The intelligent creatures in the Pangu Universe will attack each other and suffer heavy losses. It will be meaningless!

“Please, I’m really tired of fighting and killing. I really don’t want to fight with you. Can you please tell everyone what I mean?”

Li Yao looked at the Observer in expectation.

There was no telling whether or not the Observer had heard Li Yao’s words.

She stared at the ‘blooming’ tower behind Li Yao in a daze for a long time before she closed her mouth with great difficulty. Then she looked at Li Yao one last time before she turned around and walked toward her compatriots.

The Observer and the survivors of the Heavenly Palace were communicating in their way.

Li Yao could sense that, at a level invisible to ordinary people, the members of the Heavenly Palace were releasing colorful streams of information that crashed into each other like surging flood, creating a bottomless swirl.

It was not an easy task to convince the people of the Heavenly Palace, who had been sealed for hundreds of thousands of years, that the primeval civilization had perished and that human beings were the dominators of the Pangu Universe.

However, the members of the Heavenly Palace were deeply shocked by the unusual appearance of the ‘heavenly tower’ and subconsciously believed that Li Yao had passed the ultimate test. Therefore, they looked at him with special respect.

On the other hand, the members of the Heavenly Palace had used up the last bit of their strength in the battle against the Vicious Beast Tide. Now that they were on the verge of death, there was no way that they could fight again.

Besides, they were not bloodthirsty warriors but experts who were exploring the unknown and seeking the truth. They had known for a long time that the so-called ‘human beings’ were the clones of 100% of the genes of the Yen Clan. They were not the claws and teeth of the puppets but their ancestors.

Therefore, Li Yao’s suggestions and requests were not rejected immediately.

Looking at the lively tower, the survivors of the Heavenly Palace were deep in thought.

Very soon, Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, Li Jialing, Boss Bai, and the other human experts packed up their magical equipment and gathered around Li Yao.

Led by Li Linghai, many experts unsheathed their sabers from the waist of the giant soldier.

“Calm down. Everyone, please calm down.”

Li Yao begged in the communication channel, “For billions of years, enough blood has been shed in the Pangu Universe. I can’t bear to see another unnecessary drop of blood flowing out. Can’t we give peace a chance and live in harmony with the Pangu Civilization?”

“You’ve misunderstood.”

Li Linghai said, “We did not intend to exterminate the Pangu Civilization in the first place. After all, they have been working in the primeval relics for thousands of years. Who knows whether or not they will activate some deadly weapon when they are desperate and are about to be killed?

“That’s good.”

Li Yao asked curiously, “Then why did you draw your saber?”

“What do you think?”

Li Linghai replied, “To chop you, of course!”

“…Huh?” Li Yao was speechless.

The last survivors of the primeval civilization finally chose to lay down their weapons.

They might not be scared of death, but Li Yao’s declaration that they should find the seeds of their compatriots one day so that their civilization could continue on moved them deeply.

Besides, they and their ancestors had abandoned their home and lived in isolation for thousands of years in order to unravel the mysteries of the ‘Tower Tower’. Now that the tower had undergone shocking mutations, the secrets of the primeval era were about to be revealed to them. There was no reason for them to abandon themselves and seek their own death just for that.

Of course, the human civilization and the Pangu civilization did not accept each other easily. It was like the reconciliation and cooperation between human beings and the demon race in the Star Glory Federation. A contract was just an insignificant starting point. How they got along with each other, whether they encountered new problems, whether the Pangu civilization had fully surrendered or Li Yao was merely feeding a tiger… Everything was still unknown.

However, for the people who were dwelling in the ancient mysteries right now, they all shared the same feeling.

They had shed too much blood and created too much destruction and killing. Even the most aggressive Immortal Cultivators were somewhat tired of it.

Whatever the future held, at least for now, let them lay down their weapons and enjoy a moment of peace!

“Excellent.”

Seeing that both parties had regained their rationality and were no longer in a state of mutual hostility, Li Yao took a long breath in relief and smiled from the bottom of his heart. “Although it was full of soul-stirring twists and turns, it was still safe and sound, and we passed the test smoothly. We didn’t even experience an earthshaking battle with heavy casualties. Yet, we were acknowledged by the Primeval Civilization, unlocked the new form of the primeval relics, and received the enormous legacy of a hundred pioneer civilizations. This operation is indeed full of twists and turns!

“Shut up!”

Ding Lingdang, Lu Qingchen, the mental devil, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, Li Jialing, Li Linghai, the boxing champion, and even Little Ming and Wen Wen all screamed at the same time, “Stop it!”

Chapter 3129: Rainbow Bridge

Hardly had they finished their sentence when they heard an earsplitting explosion forty to fifty kilometers to the southwest of Silver City.

The noises, together with the earth-shattering quakes, caused the broken walls around them to collapse again. Even the bones of many ferocious beasts collapsed and fell to the ground, turning into scattered broken bones.

“What is that?”

Everyone stared at the southwest in horror.

At the end of the horizon in the southwest, Li Yao saw clusters of colorful clusters of light that were rolling like liquid and forming a dazzling spring. In the blink of an eye, the fountain was thousands of meters high and almost rivaled the blossoming tower.

In a moment, the ‘fountain’ was higher and higher. The brilliant colors that spread out to the surroundings were condensed into a translucent pillar of light that pierced into the sky and tore the sky apart, shooting toward the horizon of the universe!

“It looks like—it looks like a bridge?”

Li Yao gasped. He vaguely sensed something mysterious from the depths of his soul, as if he had seen something similar in the memories of his previous life a long, long time ago. “It’s… a bridge that connects different sub-universes. It’s a super teleportation array to and from the sea of the universe—the Bifrost!”

In a moment, the rainbow bridge was completed. The scattered brightness gradually condensed into an indestructible pillar.

From the bottom of the Bifrost Bridge, glittering silver objects were rising up. They were complicated components and mysterious units.

/ .

The cold metal and the crystals seemed to be controlled by an invisible giant hand and took on a life of their own. They were attracted to each other and connected with each other. Starting from the most fundamental components, they constituted a piece of enormous magical equipment—a small, weirdly-shaped, murderous, translucent starship.

“What is that place?”

Alarm bells were ringing inside Li Yao’s head. He asked anxiously, “What is that?”

“The southwest direction is the ’47th district’ of the Heavenly Palace, a place that studies the Flood Tide Legion. Although most of the captives of the Flood Tide Legion died thousands of years ago, part of the living tissue and the last few captives preserved in hibernation technology are still locked there.

The Observer stammered, “And their magical equipment. All the magical equipment that the Flood Tide Legion used in the battle against the Pangu Expeditionary Army, including their starships and portable stargates, are being studied there, too. The way they travel through the four-dimensional space is slightly different from ours. They are very valuable for research.

“The—the scraps seem to be from a small shuttle of the Flood Tide Legion. However, to prevent the Flood Tide Legion from rising again, we have imposed barriers on all the hibernating captives, the corpses of the deceased captives, and even their living tissue. They have been sealed with the strictest rules. Their war machines and starships have been dismantled into the most basic components, too. Unless you are a senior researcher with high clearance, you won’t be able to touch any fuel or ammunition. How can the components be combined automatically?”

“Has the Flood Tide Legion… really risen again?”

Li Yao bit his lips so hard that blood was flowing out. He thought quickly and soon realized what was going on. “So, somebody is hiding in the ’47th district’, the place where the Flood Tide Legion is studied, activating a portable stargate that the Flood Tide Legion uses to communicate with the universe, activating a ‘rainbow bridge’ from the Pangu Universe to the center of the universe, and rebuilding a small shuttle?

“As long as the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ is charged with enough energy and the small shuttle is reorganized, we will be able to tear apart the three-dimensional universe and reach the center of the Universe Ocean, won’t we?

“What is the enemy’s destination exactly?

Right. There can’t be a second possibility. It must be the core of the tide, the legendary ‘Earth’!

“The enemy is going to flee back to Earth, the center of the tide, through the Bifrost Bridge and report the situation of the Pangu Universe to the tide. The enemy must’ve gathered enough information, including the coordinates of the Pangu Universe, the existence of the black wall, the recent situation of the descendants of the Primeval Clan living in the Pangu Universe, and how strong the armed forces of the people here are. Everything must be reported to the tide.

“If the enemy takes off, the coordinates and military intelligence of the Pangu Universe will be completely exposed to the flood. There will be no legacy that will be passed on to us for hundreds of years or even thousands of years. We will not be able to absorb the legacy and prepare for the war. The flood might appear in front of our civilization in the very next second.

No. I must stop it!

Li Yao sent his speculation to the public communication channel.

The experts were dumbfounded at first. Then they gnashed their teeth, not knowing whether to curse Li Yao or sigh at themselves.

However, it was definitely not the style of the strong human beings to wait to be killed. This was the Pangu Universe, their home, their kingdom, and their last battlefield!

Giant soldiers whose bodies were riddled with holes soared into the sky and darted toward the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ with their last flames.

“We will settle the score with you after we take care of the Flood Tide Legion!”

On the way, Li Linghai and the other Immortal Cultivators cursed Li Yao.

“Huh?”

Li Yao’s face was full of innocence. “What does this have to do with me? It’s clearly a coincidence. Don’t be so superstitious, everyone!”

The distance of forty to fifty kilometers was as short as the blink of an eye for the sprinting giant soldier.

While they were talking, they had arrived at ‘Forty-seventh District’, which was dedicated to confining and studying the Flood Tide Legion.

District 47 was at the periphery of the City of Silver. It was not the main battlefield of the battle between the guards of the Heavenly Palace and the ferocious beasts. The buildings there were mostly intact.

But right now, all the skyscrapers were covered in pink foam, turning into a bottomless ocean of foam, as if they were ferocious beasts that had been gathered in this place to decompose and annihilate.

Besides, the foam, which was made of flesh and blood and represented the most fundamental cells and genes, had not lost its vitality yet. Instead, it seemed to have turned into a different kind of enormous, soft, rotten, and weird creature. A cluster of ‘infinite cells’ was expanding and swallowing everything nearby like wriggling plasticine.

Li Yao opened his eyes and watched as countless pink tentacles extended out from the sea of foam. Like vines and creepers, they climbed up and enveloped an entire skyscraper that was dozens of floors high. The skyscraper was actually completely engulfed.

It must be noted that the skyscraper was built with the height of the Pangu Clan, which was even more magnificent than the skyscrapers hundreds of floors below the standard of human beings. But in the blink of an eye, it had been digested and absorbed by the ocean of foam at a visible speed. It was both disgusting and terrifying. Li Yao could not help but break into a cold sweat.

“Careful!”

Right when Li Yao was deeply shocked by the slowly wriggling sea of foam, an exclamation suddenly came from the communication channel. Then, an enormous fireball exploded on his left side, which turned out to be a Giant God Soldier driven by an Immortal Cultivator who had been attacked by a mysterious enemy. It turned out to be a green-faced, three-headed, six-armed giant who was hiding behind a skyscraper that was covered in foam. He was also covered in a thick layer of foam. With the camouflage that could perfectly block the detection of mystic rays and waves, he fooled everyone’s vigilance. It was not until the unlucky Giant God Soldier happened to fly past him that he suddenly took action. He grabbed the Giant God Soldier’s right leg and threw it at the skyscraper next to him.

The skyscraper was cut in half by the immense strength, and the giant soldier was blown into a series of miserable fireballs.

“Enemy attack!”

“Stay alert!”

“What the hell is this?”

“Watch out for more monsters!”

Everyone was greatly shocked. They rained fire upon the three-headed, six-armed Indigo Devil God, forcing it to retreat step by step. However, there was a special layer of energy around its body that seemed to be a soft armor that stuck to its body. It could absorb most of the destructive energy and only retreated, but not a single flower of blood burst out.

Judging from its appearance, it was definitely not a natural creation.

It was because natural-born humanoid creatures had no need to grow three heads and six arms, nor did they need to grow to an appalling height of seventy to eighty meters.

In the natural environment of most habitable planets, such a height would only lead to excessive weight and lead to the collapse of the planet. For example, the Pangu Clan, growing to twenty meters was already their limit.

In the same way, it did not look like a deformed product of a genetic mutation such as the ‘Rampaging Armored Dragon’. Or rather, if it was really a certain artificial life form, its life would certainly be shorter than the monsters such as the ‘Rampaging Armored Dragon’. It was very likely that it could only last for a few hours. It was a one-off consumable that had been specially created for the battle.

In just a few hours, the ultimate strength stored in the deepest part of the cells was fully released. The most explosive flames were ignited and condensed into a destructive form, which was the ‘nuclear weapon’ on the cellular and genetic level!

“Hooooo!”

After enduring the collective attack of almost a hundred human experts for ten seconds, the spiritual shield covering the body of the indigo demon finally broke. Yan Liren took the opportunity to launch a fatal attack at its jugular. Blood spurted out of its neck, forcing it to throw away the giant soldier that had been crushed into a ball.

However, in the next second, accompanied by an earsplitting roar, a few ugly meat sprouts darted out of its neck and weaved together agilely and weirdly. The wounds were being repaired at a visible speed. Very soon, the wounds made by ‘Sword Addict’ Yan Liren were restored to their original state. There were no scars at all.

BOOM!

Yan Liren’s giant soldier was slapped away by the indigo demon. It rolled hundreds of meters away and crashed into a collapsed building.

Chapter 3130: : So It Was You!

“Even Yan Liren’s attack was completely useless?”

Li Yao was secretly alarmed.

Among all the human experts, although Yan Liren’s Cultivation was not the highest, his unparalleled attack ability and unpredictable sword arts were among the top five or even the top three. Even his full-strength strike failed to deal any damage to the indigo demon? How was it possible?

No. It was not completely harmless. Li Yao’s eyes were sharp enough to notice that the legs of the Indigo Devil had been drowned in the ocean of foam. Dozens of pink tentacles had extended out of the ocean of foam and spiraled all the way to its hips and waist, as if endless cells and strength had been injected into its body.

The Indigo Devil was not a lone monster but a clone condensed by the entire sea of foam. No matter how seriously it was wounded or how many cells were destroyed, the sea of foam would immediately send the same number of cells to it, allowing it to grow and expand crazily all the time. Any injury could be healed instantly.

In other words, Li Yao and the other human experts, as well as the ancient experts such as the Moongazer Serpents, were not facing a monster at all, but an ocean of foam that was an astronomical ‘super-cell amalgamation’ with independent life and will!

“What is it exactly? Some kind of… tumor?”

Li Yao swallowed hard.

Infinite expansion, infinite reproduction, infinite division and growth. Such a weird property reminded Li Yao of a special cell—cancer cells!

Huala! Huala! Huala!

“Hooooo!”

Shua! Shua! Shua!

When the experts were all blocked by the Indigo Devil God and caught in a stalemate, three enormous monsters suddenly crawled out of the sea of foam nearby.

One of the monsters looked like a hybrid of a griffin and a nine-headed dragon. It had blood-red bone wings and membranes and nine heads that looked like vipers.

One of the monsters was like an Overlord Squid that was hundreds of times larger than the super-large jellyfish. Colorful and weird colors were blossoming on its body. It was floating in midair, but dozens of tentacles were hanging in the sea of foam, absorbing the immense cells and strength.

The last monster was like a leopard and a wolf. Its waist was like a bow, its limbs were like blades, and its head was like a giant drop of water. From head to tail, it was streamlined and gave a strong sense of speed. However, it did not have the skin or shell that a normal high-level life form should have. Its muscles, which were full of explosive strength, were directly exposed to the air, only protected by clusters of bright red mucus. It looked like it was being skinned alive, especially its face, which was full of interweaving muscle fibers, and the two giant eyeballs that were bulging out of its eye sockets. Staring at the human experts, it gave them a creepy feeling.

Four monsters, or rather, ‘demons’, surrounded the dozens of giant soldiers of the human beings from four directions.
