Although the dozens of giant soldiers of human beings had the absolute advantage in terms of numbers, they did not have any psychological advantage when they were faced with the surging ocean of foam.

One indigo devil was already enough to attract the attention of all the Giant God Soldiers. How was he going to deal with three more brutal-looking ones?

If all the monsters had indestructible spiritual shields and the terrifying ability to heal themselves instantly, they would be the clones of the entire ocean of foam. How could the battle be fought?

Besides—

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently as he stared at the ‘Venomous Jellyfish’ floating in the air in the southwest.

On the head of the monster, there was a slightly smaller shadow that looked somewhat familiar. It was Gu Wuxin, the leader of the Heavenly Palace who had suffered a joint attack from the ‘Exterminator Sect’ and the ‘Dragon Lotus Seed’. One of his arms had been torn off, and he had fallen into the depths of the magma!

Li Yao had thought that Gu Wuxin had perished in the magma and been burnt to ash. He did not expect that, at this moment, not only was there no trace of burning magma on his body, even his broken arm had grown back. It was even thicker and more hideous than before. The surface of the arm was covered in bone blades that could cut through iron as if it were mud and crystals that contained tremendous energy, which were emitting visible heatwaves.

“How did this happen?”

The Observer could not believe her eyes. Li Yao could clearly feel her shock and sorrow. “You are our leader and the most distinguished researcher. How did you—”

“I should’ve realized it earlier. The weirdness and mutations that took place inside and outside the primeval relics, from the location of the captives of the Flood Tide Legion in the beginning to the weird teleportation arrays that corrupted the Heaven’s Origin Sector, and of course, the endless tides of ferocious beasts. The real schemer behind all of this could not have been outsiders such as ‘Exterminate All’ or ‘Long Lianzi’. The only person who was familiar with the situation inside the primeval relics and closest to the captives of the Flood Tide Legion was you, ‘Gu Wuxin’, leader of the Pantheon of Immortals!

Gold brightness and blood were swirling slowly inside Li Yao’s eyes. He had never been so solemn before. “It seems that, when the Pangu Fleet and the Flood Tide Legion met for the first time hundreds of thousands of years ago, they seemed to have defeated the enemy. But in fact, they were corrupted by the enemy and decided to put the two research bases together.

“In the thousands of years that followed, the Flood Tide’s strength was gradually influenced by the captives or magical equipment of the Flood Tide Legion bit by bit. Finally, thousands of years later, a critical minion, namely you, the leader of the Heavenly Palace, was transformed!

“A lot of important intelligence in the Heavenly Palace was leaked by you, wasn’t it? You told the situation of the Heavenly Palace, including advanced technology and powerful magical equipment, to the two ambitious, brutal commanders named ‘Slaughter Sect’ and ‘Long Lianzi’, so that they would be tempted to attack the ancient relics.

“The so-called ‘monster virus’ has turned most of the ancient, intelligent species into inhuman, crazy monsters. I’m afraid that you or your ‘senior’ will have to fan the flames.

“As for your purpose, let me see. I’m afraid that you don’t care about the ‘ultimate test’ at all. From the very beginning, you’ve been trying to muddy the waters and attract everyone’s attention to the ‘ultimate test’. It would be best if the forces of ‘Death of All’ and ‘Dragon Lotus’ as well as most of the guardians of the Heavenly Palace suffered heavy casualties in the tower and perished together. You, on the other hand, pretended to be dead and escaped from the tower to District 47, where you repaired the teleportation arrays and starships unscrupulously and sent messages to the depths of the flood in the center of the universe beyond the Pangu Universe!

“What a cunning plan.

“You almost succeeded… In your scheme, no one could resist the temptation of the ‘ultimate test’. Whether it was the ‘Exterminate the masses’, the ‘Dragon Lotus Seed’, or everyone else in the Heavenly Palace except for you, under the circumstances where there was no other choice, you could only treat the ancient legacy as your only life-saving straw. All the experts were gathered inside the Heaven’s Path Tower, so no one would notice the anomaly in District 47. I’m afraid that, when the ultimate test failed again and all the living creatures were wiped out, you would have already sailed away on the Flood Tide Battleship across the Bifrost.

“It’s a pity that you overlooked an unexpected factor, which was the ‘other half of the Fuxi’, the mainframe crystal processor of the Heavenly Palace!

“I finally understand why the ‘half of the Fuxi’ suggested that you activate the ‘time-warp magical equipment’ and seal and freeze the entire primeval relics.

“At first, I thought that the other half of the ‘Fuxi’ had calculated that there would be new hope in hundreds of thousands of years, namely human beings. But now that I think about it, the idea is too narcissistic. Even though the other half of the ‘Fuxi’ has amazing computational ability, it is impossible for them to foresee the rise of the human civilization hundreds of thousands of years later.

“It was not until then that I learned that the other half of the ‘Fuxi’ did not foresee the rise of the human civilization but captured your anomaly.

“It should’ve warned everyone in the Heavenly Palace, but I estimate that, as the leader of the Heavenly Palace, you must have the highest clearance to manipulate it, which makes it impossible for it to point out that you are a puppet of the Flood Tide, nor can it leak such important information to other people. Any hostility from it will be detected and tampered with by you. It cannot stop you and can only stall for time.

“Therefore, it selected the only feasible strategy out of all the targeted strategies and postponed your evil plan for hundreds of thousands of years.

“Although you are the leader of the Heavenly Palace, it is impossible for you to reject the rationalization proposal of the mainframe crystal processor without a good reason. It will only raise the suspicion of more researchers. Besides, you don’t think that there will be any setbacks if you delay for hundreds of thousands of years. After all, the Pangu civilization outside has already collapsed, and all the intelligent creatures inside the primeval relics will be sealed. Even if you wake up one day, you will be one of the first to wake up. Nobody can stop you from transmitting information to the outside of the Pangu Universe.

“You didn’t expect that the civilization of mankind would rise to such an extent in only a hundred thousand years.

“You did not expect that the ‘ultimate test’, which had not been cracked for billions of years, would be broken by us so quickly that we appeared in front of you before your ‘Rainbow Bridge’ and your ‘Tidal Warship’ were ready.

“Just admit it, Gu Wuxin. You’ve completely lost yourself. You’ve become the lackeys and claws of the tides, a pitiful worm without a soul!

Li Yao condensed the questions and the boundless fighting will into spiritual waves that seemed to be real and slashed at ‘Gu Wuxin’, the leader of the Heavenly Palace, like an unstoppable axe.

Chapter 3131: Super Body

The super-large jellyfish below Gu Wuxin’s feet was emitting colorful ripples.

The sea of foam behind him was surging, too.

However, his tranquil face was not affected by Li Yao’s questioning at all.

“Excellent analysis.”

Gu Wuxin’s voice popped up in the minds of Li Yao and the other experts. Together with the voice, deep and ancient music echoed, as if it were a call from the universe. “I can tell from your analysis that your civilization is quite advanced and developed. You have learned a lot of preliminary information about the Universe Ocean and are qualified to return to the embrace of the main civilization. Starting from the resistance and the escape billions of years ago, the people who have been wandering in the periphery of the sea of stars have been away from the main civilization for too long. It’s time for you to return and join me in the sacred universe.

“In your dreams!”

Li Yao roared, “We would rather die than become the puppets and claws of the flood. We would rather fight to the death in the form of the proudest human beings than be the walking dead without a soul!

“You seem to have a misunderstanding about the so-called ‘flood’. It’s understandable. The Primeval Civilization must’ve instilled a lot of radical and false information into you during the ‘ultimate test’ and brainwashed you, twisting and tampering with the truth.

Gu Wuxin pointed at his temples and said casually, “What if I told you that everything the Primeval Civilization said was false and that I was not brainwashed but maintained an independent will and a clear mind? What if I made the decision to ‘return to the tide’ because I analyzed all the factors under the control of absolute rationality? What if the one who was truly brainwashed was not me but you? How would you determine whether or not it was true?

“It’s up to you whether you want to believe it or not. This is the truth. The so-called ‘flood’ is simply an insulting title that you made up yourself. Of course, there are also a lot of people—those who cling to their old ways and live in seclusion, those who dwell on the old ways, and even those who have fallen into darkness—who have come up with names that are even more evil and terrifying. They want to distort our true appearance and escape from our embrace, but no matter what you call us, our essence will not change.

“There is no such thing as a flood. We are the civilization of mankind—the real, orthodox civilization of mankind that originated from Earth.

“What?”

Li Yao was rendered speechless for a moment. He could barely believe his hearing cells. He stammered, “What did you say? You are the civilization of mankind? What about the Primeval Civilization?”

“I believe that you’ve learned the so-called ‘path of rise’ of the Primeval Civilization through the ‘ultimate test’, haven’t you? No matter how much the Primeval Race tried to disguise themselves, it was impossible for them to wash off the blood all over their bodies. Do you think that such a dark, decadent species is qualified to be called the civilization of mankind?”

Gu Wuxin smiled. “No. Since they abandoned 99% of their compatriots and evolved in the dark sea of stars, they are no longer worthy of the title ‘human civilization’. They are just a bunch of animals in human skin, a bunch of cockroaches who have learned how to build weapons and steer starships. They are not qualified to be the successors of the civilization of mankind in the past.

Li Yao quite agreed with Gu Wuxin’s point.

“However, luckily, although the guys who fled from Earth gradually changed, fell, and forgot the spirit and soul of the civilization of mankind, the civilization of mankind on Earth was never cut off. Instead, it was passed on in a brand-new form and continued to maintain the dignity, glory, and spirit of the orthodox civilization of mankind. It also learned the lessons of the Third World War and embarked on a new path of peace and communication, exploring and modifying the sea of the universe in entirely different ways.

Gu Wuxin replied, “No. We are not raging monsters, we are not soulless walking dead, and we are not the so-called ‘flood’. We are the ‘civilization of mankind’. If you must change the way you address us, you can call us the ‘unified super information entity of the universe’.

“A super body?”

Li Yao mumbled to himself and glanced at the four monsters around Gu Wuxin. Then he glanced at the surging ocean of foam below his feet and said, “Not a raging monster? Not a walking dead without a soul? Hey, hey, hey. Your words do not seem very convincing! What damn universe? What damn super information? What damn unification? No matter what you say, isn’t it still an upgraded version of the Swarm Civilization?”

“It will be a shame if you think of us only as a civilization of bees—the parasites that barely have free will.

Gu Wuxin repeated, “We are not bees. We are ‘supernaturals’.

“Then what exactly—”

Li Yao asked, “What is it?”

“A summer insect cannot talk about ice. I know it is difficult for you to understand with your current wisdom, but I will still try to explain it to you.

Gu Wuxin sighed, as if he were feeling regretful. “In fact, the best way to understand what a ‘super entity’ is is to connect to the ‘super information network of the universe’ in person and sense the endless information from the endless universe. I guarantee that, if you connect to it for even one second, you will feel that your life has undergone tremendous changes. Even the definition of life and the universe itself will be broken. All the misunderstandings and barriers will be cleared instantly. There will be no reason for us to fight and be wary of each other. You and your compatriots will be willing to return to the universe and the main civilization.

“But I also knew that you wouldn’t understand or agree to it right now, just like I did before I was connected to the super body. My head was full of childish, hilarious ideas and cold, dark conspiracy theories. I didn’t want to believe that there were truly ‘kind intentions’ in the boundless universe without asking for anything in return.

“Even so, I’m still willing to do everything I can to explain it to you—because you are my last compatriots in the Pangu Universe. I chose to link myself to you in order to save the Pangu Universe and to quell the internal strife between my compatriots. How could I bear to destroy you myself?

“How can I explain the difference between ‘supernaturals’ and ‘swarm swarms’? Sigh. They are two vastly different concepts. The former is thousands of times more advanced than the latter.

“You should know that, in a typical civilization of bees, only individuals such as the ‘mastermind’ and the ‘queen’ boast the ability to think, analyze, and make decisions. They are known as ‘free will’. Thousands of soldiers, worker bees, and all kinds of bizarre mutations are merely extensions of the mastermind. They are super-large cells that cannot be regarded as real intelligent creatures at all.

“Take the Houyi Clan of the Pangu Civilization Alliance for example. The most fundamental individual of the Houyi Clan is a very low-level beetle. In the legal definition of the Pangu Alliance, there is no such thing as ‘human rights’. Only when the number of the beetles of the Houyi Clan exceeds a hundred will incredible changes take place and a high-level wisdom burst out. Even such a high-level wisdom has to obey the arrangement of the mainframe. Although it has the ability to make logical judgements, it lacks the initiative and the free will.

“Joining such a civilization of bees and turning into muddleheaded cells was a choice that no intelligent creature was willing to make. Even the members of the Houyi Clan were unwilling to be separated again after they gathered more than a hundred individuals and unleashed their wisdom and will.

“But look at me. Look at me carefully. Do I look like a muddle-headed ‘soldier bee’ or ‘worker bee’? Do I look like I’ve completely lost my emotions and will? Do I look like a puppet that obeys the commands of a certain ‘mastermind’ without any self-awareness?

“No. That is not the case at all. Although there is no proof, you will know that I cannot be controlled by the so-called mainframe after you think about it with the simplest logic.

“If such a ‘main brain’ does exist, it will not be able to lock onto the coordinates of the Pangu Universe, nor will it be able to control an intelligent creature in the Pangu Universe precisely. If it has such an ability, what are we waiting for? We will be able to command the magnificent army in the so-called ‘flood’ and swallow the entire Pangu Universe, won’t we?

“Therefore, look, I did not lie to you. I made the right judgement when I was absolutely rational, free, and clear-headed and chose the best path for the Pangu Universe and all the intelligent creatures living in it.

Li Yao was confused by Gu Wuxin’s long speech.

He felt that his head was full of stars, and all the stars were still exploding, blowing his brain cells into a mess.

“Wait, wait, wait. Let’s start over.”

Li Yao took a deep breath and said, “Are you suggesting that the so-called flood is the people who were stranded on Earth billions of years ago? But weren’t they burnt, melted, and obliterated by the meteoroid shower?”

“Life is the most incredible miracle in the universe. It should have the most splendid forms.

Gu Wuxin said, “Even if the apocalypse comes, life will still have the opportunity to find a new way out and even unleash greater strength.

“Just like the meteoroid shower that assaulted Earth and wiped out 90% of the species, including the dinosaurs, did not end the light of life on Earth. Instead, it promoted the rise of mammals and gave birth to a real civilization, the super spiritual meteoroid shower that almost destroyed the entire solar system did not destroy the civilization of mankind, either. Instead, it ushered in the most glamorous and brilliant new life for the civilization of mankind. In a way that was completely different from the Primeval Civilization, the civilization evolved from the ‘planet era’ to the ‘cosmic era’. The magnificent path of cosmic exploration and integration was opened.

Chapter 3132: Super Information Society

“What do you mean?”

Li Yao suddenly remembered that, in the ‘ultimate test’, the voice of the Primeval Lord of Heaven had told him that ‘Thousand Treasures Sea’, the leader of the civilization who fled from Earth, had gone crazy in a trance and claimed that he had seen countless ‘ghosts from Earth’ following their fleet.

At that time, Li Yao had disapproved of the sentence. He had thought that it was just an illusion caused by Wan Zanghai’s old age and guilt.

However, what if it was not an illusion?

Li Yao shuddered hard. “It’s impossible. The meteoroid shower did not hit Earth directly. Instead, it lunged at the sun and triggered a solar storm that affected the entire solar system. The water planet, Venus, Earth, and even Mars were all swallowed. All the water on Earth was vaporized, and the ground was wreathed in flames. For days, weeks, or even months, no life could survive in such an environment.

“For a carbon-based life, yes.

“But who says that there is only one form of life? Who says that the ‘sun’ cannot be a different form, a different level, or a life in a different sense?”

Li Yao was greatly alarmed. “What? What do you mean?”

“If you want to know the truth of the Earth’s destruction, you will have to wait until the bridge of information between the Lord Civilization and me is built. We will take over the civilization together, read the tremendous information, and even experience the desperation, struggle, and sublimation when the Earth is destroyed!”

Gu Wuxin opened his arms and said, “I just want to tell you that the feeling of being connected to a super entity is… unique and incomparable. Besides, there will be no consequences at all. You don’t need to worry that your self-awareness and emotions will be erased at all. All you need to do is open your soul completely and write a special super information interactive protocol in it so that your soul will be one of the endless computational nodes in the ‘unified super information of the universe’. You will share all the information, thoughts, emotions, and wisdom with other people.

“After you join the super body, your life will be upgraded. Just like a single-celled life evolving into a multi-celled life, you will experience an earth-shaking, rebirth-like upgrade. From then on, you won’t just be yourself. You won’t just be living in such a lonely and ridiculous shell. You will become all the computational nodes in the super body. You will become all the compatriots around you. You will live on the network. In the ‘cloud’, you will tear open the void and cross the galaxy with the help of the Great Unification Super Information Interaction Agreement. You will live in the bodies of 10,000,000 or 100,000,000 or 100,000,000,000 compatriots almost without any delay. Within a second, you will be able to feel 100 million different emotions and ways of thinking. You will be able to absorb the life experiences and beautiful memories of 100 million people. Moreover, you will be able to share your emotions, your will, your way of thinking, and your memory fragments with these 100 million people… Your soul will even be able to exist simultaneously on countless planets with completely different environments. You will be able to live in the bodies of countless bizarre-shaped creatures. You will be able to enjoy a life of a thousand forms in the manner of these ‘monsters’. You will be able to obtain brand-new philosophies and comprehensions. You will be able to condense your own meaning of life.

“In the end, you will no longer be a tiny, weak, stupid, selfish, impulsive, dust-like human being. Instead, you will live as the amalgamation of billions of lives. You will be fully integrated into the civilization of mankind and the universe. You will be the civilization of mankind and the universe!

“…I don’t understand.”

Li Yao was silent for a long time. He breathed heavily and said, “I only know that anyone who wants anything in the universe will have to pay a corresponding price. Also, every time a guy like you shows up and says that everything is perfect, he is most likely lying.

“I knew you wouldn’t understand. That’s why I said ‘summer insects shouldn’t talk about ice’.”

Gu Wuxin was silent for a moment, too. Then he said, “Before I was connected to the super body, I didn’t understand it, either, and I was very proud of my insignificant, lonely, enclosed self. What a blind and idiotic pride it was!

“But in the end, I was connected to a small super entity made of thousands of people from the Heavenly Palace as well as the captives of the Flood Tide. Our small-scale super information exchange agreement is far from the vastness, depth, and unpredictability of the real main civilization super entity in the sea of the universe, but it has already made me feel extremely happy and touched. I feel that I’ve lived in vain in the past. I’m just a muddle-headed, single-celled life. The things that I’m proud of and protect are meaningless. Right now, this is the real beginning of my life.

“I am Gu Wuxin, living in such a weak body.

“I can be a monster, too. My life cycle is only a few hours. I have to burn the fire of life deep inside my cells in a moment.

“I was also the first generation of researchers of the Heavenly Palace thousands of years ago. When the Heavenly Palace was being built, I was invited by the so-called captives of the Flood Tide and established a local super information exchange agreement in the isolated Pangu Universe. In the days to come, I absorbed all the life information of thousands of elite researchers so that everyone could live in the mind of Gu Wuxin in the form of information.

“Look. Flesh, cells, genes. Everything is superficial. Only information is the foundation of life. Information, information, information, information constitutes everything in the Universe Ocean. As long as you have information, you can control everything at your will.

Gu Wuxin’s voice sometimes came from his real body, sometimes from the body of the Indigo Devil God, sometimes from the nine-headed griffin, sometimes from the Mystic Color Jellyfish, sometimes from the skinned jackals, sometimes from the depths of the sea of foam where the voices of thousands of people overlapped.

Tremendous tides were raging inside the sea of foam. In the tides almost a hundred meters high, pink tentacles were wriggling nonstop, as if thousands of different figures were staring at Li Yao and the human experts while they screamed, “Join us! Join us! Share all the information with us!”

Li Yao felt goosebumps all over his body.

“What kind of pervert do you think I am?”

He rubbed his hands hard. “You want me to release all the information in my brain so that any Tom, Dick, and Harry can see my most private memories and read my mind? This—this is even more terrifying than wiping out my free will and emotions. You are forcing me to publicize the browsing and downloading records of my crystal processor! How can you think of such unpardonable, inhuman things? Lunatics! You are a bunch of out-and-out lunatics!”

“Judging from the magical equipment and crystal processors you’ve been using, you’ve entered an information society and even have a unified super light speed network in every space zone of the Pangu Universe, haven’t you?

Gu Wuxin’s voice came from his own body, the monsters, and the squirming tentacles deep inside the sea of foam. “If so, you should be very clear that the so-called evolution of civilization is the process of informationization and sharing.

“In the primitive society, each individual received and outputted very little information. The primitive people did not have their own names. Such important identification information was rarely exchanged with other cave tribes. They lacked the urge to transmit their own information, nor did they have the desire to pry into other people’s information. Each individual was a node that was cut off from each other and sealed itself. Survival had drained all their energy. There was no need to talk about uniting everyone’s information, because the entire civilization was a pile of loose sand.

“By the medieval era, the intelligent creatures had conquered the entire continent and even the entire planet, becoming the well-deserved ‘spirit of all things’. However, their information exchange was still extremely poor. There was no need for information transmission, nor did they have the means to read information. Other than their own family or their own village, they were completely uninterested in the information of the outside world. This was the so-called ‘darkness and ignorance’.

“However, as the technological boom brought about tremendous changes to the society, it only took a moment for the industrial society to evolve into an information society. You will find that, as the civilization surged forward, every individual’s demand for information exchange soared exponentially, hundreds of times higher than their ancestors hundreds of years ago.

“I don’t know what the civilization of mankind is all about, but in the history of the Pangu civilization, almost all the carbon-based intelligent creatures experienced such information explosions when they jumped from the industrial era to the information era.

“During the information explosion, everyone would be immersed in the ‘carpet-type information bombardment’ of the official news, public forums, and the self-media of the experts when they woke up in the morning. Every terminal would bombard people’s brains like a never-ending rapid-fire cannon. The information that they read during breakfast was almost the amount of information that their ancestors could only receive a year and a half ago. Even so, they were still in an insatiable ‘information hunger’. Even if they were on their way to work and study, they would have to bury their heads in various terminals and read and share the information through various channels. As long as they were away from the terminal for one second, they would feel abandoned by the entire society.

“This is part of the information reception. There is still the output of information.

“Also relying on the terminal and various network channels, they were able to display and export the details of their daily life in great detail. What they ate, what they wore, who they met, and even their shallow and barren thoughts… There was no need for anyone to force them. They were born with the urge to expose themselves and strip themselves of everything. All the information that constituted their entire life was completely uploaded to the terminal and the cloud, using their own hands!

“My question is, have you ever wondered how the information society will naturally evolve when it continues to develop for thousands of years?

Chapter 3133: Supernatural Era

“Well…”

Li Yao thought for a long time before he understood what Gu Wuxin meant.

It was true that, in the human civilization that Li Yao lived in, both the Star Glory Federation and the Imperium of True Human Beings had entered the era of informationization. Almost everybody was connected to the network by crystal processors. Work, studies, and daily life were inseparable from the network. There were even ‘spirits’ such as Lu Qingchen who tried to connect everybody to the virtual space through the network in order to create a new civilization.

It was needless to say that ‘Fuxi’, the main brain of the Holy Accord Alliance, was a crystal processor and a spirit of the Spiritual Nexus. Like Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, and the Fist King, it was a new life of information.

Then, was Gu Wuxin’s so-called ‘superbody’ the same as Lu Qingchen, Fuxi, Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, and the boxing champion?

Li Yao did not think so. Gu Wuxin and his super body had touched upon fields that were deeper than Lu Qingchen, Fuxi, Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, and the Fist King. Maybe, that was the essence of life.

“You have to admit that human beings—or rather, all the carbon-based intelligent creatures—are animals of information. Since our oldest ancestors, the bacteria and viruses born in the primeval ocean, appeared, they have existed for the purpose of replicating, transmitting, and weaving information. They devour information, devour more information, output information, and transmit information to larger groups of people and farther away. Those are our instincts and the significance of our existence.

Gu Wuxin continued. “In the ancient times when technology was underdeveloped, the individuals with carbon-based intelligence had to seal themselves and live a dull, boring, and lack-of-information life. But it did not mean that they liked it. Their natural instincts to absorb and transmit information were merely suppressed.

“When the technology is mature and the social environment allows, the carbon-based intelligent life will immediately be transformed into information. Later, in the super-information direction, they will transform themselves and the entire world. They will connect all the individuals together with a network of various forms.

“They craved for information and devoured information that was of no significance to them. From the changes of the universe and national affairs, to the gossip of celebrities and the social news of the neighboring towns, to the trivial affairs of the parents around them… In fact, what did the information have to do with their lives and how would it affect them? But they just wanted to know, even at the cost of their precious lives.

“In the same way, they uploaded their information to the network without stopping. They didn’t care what they ate, what they wore, how they felt, or what problems they encountered in their daily life. Nobody cared about that. They knew that nobody cared about that, but they just couldn’t control the urge carved in the deepest part of their genes. They were like a high-power radio station that was releasing information, information, and information to the outside world nonstop. With the information, they reconstructed a better, more brilliant, and more ‘real’ version of themselves in the network and the virtual world.

“This is what happened to every individual when the Pangu civilization entered the information society.

“Of course, since the Pangu Clan boasts the ability of large-scale telepathic thoughts and has passed it on to other species in the Pangu Civilization Alliance, the way we build a network and exchange information might be different from yours.

“But I believe that the essence is the same. There are bound to be more and more ‘net addicts’ and ‘informators’ in your civilization, and your society is slowly moving toward informationization, networking, and even virtualization, right?

“There’s no need to answer. I have already sensed the answer from your brainwaves.

“In fact, what is the difference between reality and illusion? What is the difference between me and other people?

“As I said just now, once I was connected to a super body, I could live in the brain of billions of people, and billions of people could flood into my brain to share their wisdom, emotions, and memories. You seemed to be very vigilant and disgusted, but on second thought, it was not as exaggerated as you thought.

“I don’t know whether or not there are similar things in your civilization… In the words of Earth, there are novels, games, and movies. Then, when you are intoxicated by a beautiful novel, a wonderful movie, and a thrilling game, aren’t you also substituting yourself into the characters of novels, movies, and games to experience another life? When technology is constantly upgraded, allowing the online world to gradually have all the beauty of the real world and to be improved by a hundredfold, who would refuse to live a more brilliant, beautiful, and rich life in the online world? After all, compared to our exponentially explosive wisdom and desires, how weak are our brains and bodies? How lonely and boring is the real world we live in?

“The tide of history is vast and mighty. Those who go with it prosper, and those who go against it perish. Do you understand? This is the inevitable trend of the evolution of a civilization. It does not shift according to the will of a certain ‘hero’ or ‘demon’. At first, a primary network of various large and fixed terminals was established. Then, the terminal became miniaturized. It could be moved and worn, allowing the network to cover the entire civilization. Following that, the terminal was further miniaturized and could be implanted under the skin. It became an artificial organ that everyone was born to wear. Gradually, the boundary between the body of flesh and the network of the terminal became more and more blurred. Along with the development of biotechnology and quantum technology, the brain cells would become a biological crystal processor. The crystal processor would also shrink to the level of mustard seeds and cells. It would be perfectly integrated into the body of flesh and blood. It would not be damaged or scrapped for life.

“In every development phase, the efficiency of information exchange, including the amount of information they can absorb and output every second, will be hundreds of times, thousands of times, or even tens of thousands of times higher than in the past! For an ordinary person living in the early years of the information era, the news information that they can read through the network terminal in a day might be as much as the news information that their ancestors, who lived in the villages of the dark and obscure era thousands of years ago, have access to in their entire life. And since he lived in the later years of the information era, which is the offspring of the ‘super information era’, the information that he can absorb in one second is billions of times more than what he can absorb in the past. If such a beautiful super information era does arrive, wouldn’t it be only natural and natural for their brains to be connected and share their wisdom, emotions, and memories so that they can form a ‘super body’?

“Of course, I believe that you will bring up the old-fashioned question of how to maintain the ‘free will’. It is not your fault. It is a question that the carbon-based intelligent creatures ask when they are limited by their rigid thinking. Years ago, when my ancestors decided to build a small super information network and connect it to our own super bodies, they had considered the question multiple times.

“Don’t worry. Even if you are connected to a super entity, you can still maintain your small, futile, and ridiculous independence. If you want to, you can turn off your super information interactive protocol, cut off your connection with other people, and drift to the edge of the super entity. There are no coercive commands that forbid you from doing so. It’s just that few people will do such a thing.

“Why?”

Li Yao could not help but ask.

“Why?”

Gu Wuxin raised his eyebrow, as if his brainwaves, which were full of mockery, were still not enough to express his surprise. “Isn’t it obvious and self-explanatory? Human beings—carbon-based intelligent creatures—are animals of information. Information is our mission and significance. Nobody can return to the low-level information interaction state of ‘100,000 orders of magnitude’ or even ‘10,000 orders of magnitude’ after they are used to the super-information exchange of ‘billion orders of magnitude’.

“Imagine this. A person who is used to immersing himself in the online world day and night, who carries several information terminals with him, who has countless accounts in countless forums, who has experienced countless kinds of lives in countless novels, games, and movies, is thrown back to the isolation and underdeveloped medieval era where there was no network, no newspapers, no books, and no 10,000 wonderful lives. What he is faced with is the monotonous sky and barren land, as well as the high mountains and lofty mountains that surround the village. The only entertainment he has all year around is to drink himself drunk with inferior narcotic drugs… Can he endure such a life? If it was you, how long can you endure a life without entertainment if you cut off the network?

“The logic is the same.

“For the people living in the ‘super body era’, they have to read 10,000 pieces of information every second, from the supernova explosion millions of lightyears away to the experts and scholars of another Sector who have developed a brand-new life form to the glitzy atmosphere that the explorers have just discovered on a certain planet… At the same time, they have to express their opinions about the 10,000 pieces of information, generate their own feelings, and upload the opinions and feelings to the super bodies, sharing them with everyone. Information is as essential as air and water for them. Not to mention going back to the ancient times when there was no information at all, even going back to the early years of the information era and processing three to five pieces of information every minute with various portable terminals would be unimaginable. If such a ‘tragedy’ does occur, they will either have a mental breakdown or suffocate to death.

“So, do you understand now? Severing yourself from the super body and maintaining your ridiculous independence is tantamount to committing mental suicide.

“Besides, communication and isolation work both ways. If you want to maintain the independence of your mind, it means that you cannot pry into and share other people’s wisdom, emotions, and memories. You will become a wandering ghost outside of the main civilization. After all, human beings live in groups. It is against their nature to do so. There are no benefits at all!”

Chapter 3134: Perfect Universe

“Is your so-called ‘master civilization’ to maintain a connection with everyone’s mind and soul all the time?”

Li Yao exclaimed subconsciously, “This is crazy!”

“Madness?”

Gu Wuxin said, “Let’s say that a villager living in a small village in the dark medieval era arrived in the early days of the information era and saw so many terminals, media, and information exchange platforms, only to discover that the people of the era had to learn all the news that was happening in the entire planet, the entire space zone, and even the entire Sector, including dozens of celebrity gossips and murders that took place thousands of kilometers away. I believe that the villager must’ve thought that the era was crazy, too.

“However, you and I are both very clear that the times are not crazy, and society will not collapse because of too much information. On the contrary, it is the process of history and the inevitability of civilization. It is not inappropriate for us to enjoy ourselves in the ocean of information and take it for granted, is it not?

“What’s the saying—the technology born before I was born was all part of the order of the ordinary world; the technology born when I was young would become a revolutionary product that would change the world; and any technology born after I entered my middle age would violate the laws of nature and be punished by the heavens with evil and taboo techniques.

“Don’t you feel that your performance is very similar to the target of the sarcasm?

“Since you can enjoy all the benefits of the information society, discuss difficult problems with your friends in another space zone in real time through the terminal, entertain yourselves, and discuss your life experiences and the emotional changes that you are experiencing right now, everything is out-and-out evil arts in the eyes of the ancient people. Why can’t you open your heart and shatter the limitations in your head? Why can’t you appreciate the super information society of a higher level with a more tolerant and open attitude and join it frankly?

“Don’t be scared, and there’s no need to be vigilant. Believe me, it has benefits. It is of great benefit to everyone, including our civilization.

“First of all, by connecting everyone’s mind to their brain, we can concentrate all their wisdom and focus on the questions that are of paramount importance for the civilization and the universe. Due to the limitations of the brains of the carbon-based intelligent creatures, if every brain is able to calculate and think independently, we will never be able to crack the mysteries of a higher level, such as the truth of the four-dimensional space, the origin and end of the universe, and the more mysterious and weird five-dimensional and six-dimensional space.

“Such problems can only be solved by gathering the brains of thousands of people and forming super bodies.

“Secondly, as I said just now, joining a super body can make the life of a carbon-based intelligent life billions of times richer and heavier. It can give the originally pale and powerless life of billions of people a gorgeous color. Admit it. In any civilization with a carbon-based intelligent life, the lives of the vast majority of people are meaningless in the first place. They just live and die muddleheadedly. They are nothing more than animals that can talk and think. Maybe they are living a life that is even more carefree and happy than animals! If so, what’s wrong with giving them some color and meaning of life?

“Lastly, and most importantly, the super bodies can resolve the chronic diseases shared by every carbon-based civilization. They can put an end to our internal strife and self-destruction. They can eliminate the ‘chain of suspicion’ that exists between different civilizations and turn the entire sea of the universe into a perfect paradise of harmony and brightness.

“At the end of the day, why does every civilization with carbon-based intelligence decline when it reaches its peak, leading to civil wars and the destruction of the civilization? Isn’t it because of the selfish nature of individuals because of the continuation and diffusion of genes, which turns into infinite greed? The irreconcilable contradiction between infinite greed and limited resources has led to countless similar tragedies.

“Selfishness is perhaps the most important factor for the evolution of all creatures. It is also the first motivation for the birth and rise of a civilization.

“However, when a civilization develops to a very high level, the selfishness of the individual becomes the biggest obstacle to the further development of the civilization, if not the sharp claws that strangle the throat of the civilization.

“Selfishness caused every individual to harbor ill intentions. Every moment, they would have the urge to compete with their own kind, eliminate their own kind, and even eliminate their own kind. The miscommunication of information would lead to the interconnecting of ‘chain of suspicion’ within the civilization, seriously interfering with the normal functioning of society. In the end, the impulse to eliminate their own kind would expand infinitely and fall into a vicious cycle. A ‘war of everyone versus everyone’ would occur, resulting in mutual destruction. The third world war of the Earth Era, the thermonuclear war that nearly destroyed the civilization of mankind, was the most typical example.

“However, in the super bodies, none of this is a problem.

“Because all the information is shared. When you enjoy delicious food, billions of people will enjoy delicious food together with you. When you are in love with a girl of the opposite sex, billions of people will feel the pleasure of innocence and sweetness. When you are engaged in soul-stirring adventures, when your blood is boiling, and when thousands of people are fighting side by side with you, you will also be able to feel everything they get, whether it is fame and fortune, the luck of surviving a disaster, or the pleasure of solving a difficult problem.

“In such an era, the concepts of individuals have been diluted to the minimum. There is no need for ‘selfishness’ at all. Therefore, there is no jealousy, hatred, anger, or the urge to eliminate other people. Is there any reason to scheme and kill each other?

“On the other hand, because the soul and soul of every individual are connected, everyone’s evil thoughts and malice towards other people are as clear as a candle flame. If some sociopaths and natural-born homicidal maniac really have a tiny bit of distracting thoughts, they will be perceived by thousands upon thousands of people in a flash. Billions of bright and kind thoughts will immediately surge over and help him purify or isolate them, killing the threat in the cradle.

“Therefore, the ‘Transcendent Era’ is the most peaceful, the brightest, and the most harmonious era since the birth of the Universe Ocean. The human civilization that created the super bodies, the primitive civilizations and the alien species that are connected to the super bodies, all trust each other and are very united. There are no barriers, disagreements, or wariness at all. Of course, there is no room for schemes or wars. If the universe is a dark forest, isn’t the ‘super body—unified super information of the universe’ the best way to crack the dark forest?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment.

Tilting his head and thinking for a long time, he replied, “There seems to be nothing wrong with it.”

“Of course it is. All the questions and concerns that you can think of have been thought through by the experts and scholars of the Heavenly Palace after thousands of years of exploration and research.

Gu Wuxin said, “Thousands of years ago, when the expedition army of the Pangu Civilization was caught off guard by the Flood Tide Legion outside the black wall and was barely defeated, some captives of the Flood Tide Legion extended an invitation to our experts and asked them to join their super bodies.

“At that time, our experts and the researchers who came later were naturally full of wariness and prejudice. It was impossible for them to agree immediately. Instead, they regarded the invitation of the captives of the Flood Tide Legion as a special kind of enchantment and brainwashing. They scoffed and continued their research.

“However, the temporary failure of marching into the Universe Ocean has led to a shortage of resources and internal instability.

“The discovery of a civilization of a hundred generations of pioneers in the primeval relics and their experience of destruction made us realize, even more desperately, that the ultimate contradiction between resources and desires is a dead knot that no civilization can get around. For billions of years, all civilizations have been destroyed because of this point. Can our civilization be an exception?

“Only by establishing our own ‘unified super-information system of the universe’ and connecting it to the ‘master-civilization super bodies’ created by the civilization of mankind in the near future can we achieve the harmony of the entire sea of the universe and resolve the wars, poverty, crimes, and other problems caused by selfish, ambitious, and internal strife.

“Even if the experts and scholars in the Heavenly Palace can bury their heads in the sand and pretend that they are blind to all the problems, the Pangu civilization outside of the Heavenly Palace is indeed corrupting, degenerating, and splitting, just as the captives of the Flood Tide Legion said. Ugly black spots have grown on the seemingly splendid body of the civilization. The incurable virus has penetrated into their bones and spread to their internal organs.

“Under such undercurrents, a few seniors of the Heavenly Palace were finally tempted.

“At first, they only connected their minds according to the training arts provided by the captives of the Flood Tide Legion.

“Since the Pangu Clan has always been good at large-scale telepathic thoughts, the universal way of communication for the Pangu civilization is the combination of language and brainwaves. Such a secret art of ‘telepathic connection’ is not much better than the methods of the Pangu Clan. Naturally, there are no dangers or taboos.

“The reason why the predecessors connected their minds was not to solve the high-level problems such as the civilization crisis. They were merely faced with a series of inexplicable mysteries in the primeval relics and needed higher-level wisdom.

“According to the captives of the Flood Tide Legion, the wisdom released when the two telepathic thoughts are connected is far more than the sum of the wisdom of the two telepathic thoughts, which means ‘one plus one equals more than two’.

“Besides, the more brains that are connected to the system, the more profound the wisdom of the system will be. Even the most marvelous problems will be solved easily.

“The captives of the Flood Tide Legion were not lying.

“The seniors who have been connected in secret have indeed received the wisdom of the super experts. They have tackled the topics that nobody has been able to answer for hundreds of years and analyzed the mysteries of the demise of more and more pioneers. Of course, they have a deeper understanding about the Flood Tide Legion and the so-called ‘Flood Tide’ itself.

Chapter 3135: When The Super Body Met The Primeval Lord

Gu Wuxin paused for a moment. Then, his facial muscles twitched uncontrollably, and his expression turned extremely weird.

He seemed to be laughing and crying at the same time. Every muscle fiber was twisted in different directions, as if a hundred, a thousand, and ten thousand faces were covering his face at the same time with different expressions.

“Very soon, my predecessors realized that the ‘super-body technology’ far exceeded the scope of the large-scale telepathy of the Pangu Clan. Not only did it increase their wisdom, it also involved the construction of brand-new social and civilization ideologies, new life forms, and a higher relationship between life and the universe!

Gu Wuxin’s voice seemed to be coming from ten thousand mouths and ten thousand brains at the same time, echoing in Li Yao’s head like a surging tide. “We are both independent individuals and co-born a small, hopeful new life. We can also let go of ourselves and integrate into it. We can perceive each other’s thoughts about the ancient relics and our civilization. Countless ideologies and paths have been melded perfectly. The barriers, doubts, and negative emotions that we had in the past have all vanished. We are now living in the purest, clearest, and brightest happiness. Even when a certain expert passed away, his wisdom, his memories, his emotions, and his thinking patterns did not die out. Instead, they continued to exist in the super bodies as information.

/ .

“From this point of view, you can think of it as an individual gaining an ‘immortal body’ after being connected to a super body. Even if your body rots, your soul will still shine brightly in other people’s brains and be eternal!

“Of course, of course, as I said, we were not controlled by the super body of the main civilization outside of the Pangu Universe. Even though we formed our own super bodies, we retained our independent will and thinking ability. At that time, we were not prepared to fully open the Pangu Universe and let the main civilization march in.

“Moreover, at that time, our super bodies were still very weak. We only recruited a small number of open-minded experts and scholars. Most of the people in the Heavenly Palace were radical ‘liberal fundamentalists’ who were entangled by the bad habits of the carbon-based intelligent creatures. It was impossible for them to understand or agree with everything we did.

“However, as you know, as time went by, the chronic disease of the Pangu civilization became more and more obvious. All kinds of conflicts and disasters broke out. Small-scale conflicts, famine, and even massacres took place one after another. In the end, a total civil war between the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan took place.

“Although we are in a celestial palace that is isolated from the rest of the world, we are still very concerned about the situation of the entire Pangu Universe. It is our home and homeland. We chose to burn our life and everything in this lifeless, desolate ancient tomb to save our civilization.

“It’s a pity that, however hard we try, a lot of ancient technology has been analyzed and duplicated and sent to our civilization, only to no avail. We cannot stop the total decline and collapse of our civilization. We can only watch the civilization we love walk on the path of destruction that a hundred pioneers once walked on.

“The primeval relics and the heritage of the Primeval Clan can’t save our civilization. The righteous, the righteous, and the kind paths can only treat the symptoms but not the root cause and buy us more time. Only the ‘unified body of super information in the universe—the super body’—is the ultimate answer to save our civilization!

“The apocalyptic civil war has taught us a lesson. If the destruction of the Pangu civilization is inevitable, let’s plant new seeds with the super bodies so that our civilization will be reborn in raging flames and bring eternal brightness and peace to the Pangu Universe!

Gu Wuxin was almost moved by his sincerity and enthusiasm when he said that. He was apparently enjoying himself.

The four monsters around him, and even the big faces that popped up from the ocean of foam, had exactly the same expression as his. It was quite a creepy scene.

“Well said!”

Li Yao braced himself and said, “Wars to be extinguished, violence and oppression, eternal light and peace… In the end, didn’t the Flood Tide Legion annihilate everyone who didn’t agree with you with brute force?”

“This is ‘evil that is necessary’. Peace cannot bring peace. Only violence can bring peace. Have you never resorted to violence on the path of justice?”

Gu Wuxin said casually, “The civilization of mankind in the old days created the first generation of the super bodies when the earth was destroyed. Then, it made up its mind that such a tragedy could not be allowed to happen again. It was determined to create a new era in the dark, cold sea of the universe where there was no war, violence, darkness, or evil. There was only peace, kindness, happiness, justice, and brightness.

“That’s what they thought, and that’s what they did. When they finally grasped the space navigation technology and marched out of the solar system and even the Milky Way to explore the entire sea of stars, they encountered countless low-level civilizations and alien species. But they barely had any conflicts with the ‘outsiders’. Instead, they invited the ‘outsiders’ to join their ranks and join their super families.

“Of course, there were countless twists and turns and conflicts in the beginning, but the earthlings resolved them with their unique patience and thoughtfulness.

“When the alien species were connected to the super bodies, they shared the history of human civilization and the life on Earth instantly. They also shared the feelings, memories, and ways of thinking of billions of people. In the meantime, the earthlings shared the origins of the alien species and the glamorous scenes of the low-level civilizations through the super bodies. They all realized the importance of communication and integration. One plus one was always greater than two. They were all the children of the universe and even part of the vast universe. They were the tiny cells on the body of the universe. Was there a need to kill each other?

“From then on, the super body was like a snowball, growing larger and larger in the sea of the universe. Its tentacles extended to every sub-universe and every space zone. Thousands of species were willing to be connected to the super body and share everything with each other.

“At this stage, the number of alien species and low-level civilizations connected to the supernaturals far exceeds the number of earthlings. It is no longer appropriate to call them ‘earthlings’ or ‘human beings’. Let’s just call them ‘supernaturals’.

“In this way, the super body civilization really achieved the ‘eternal peace’ within a certain range. All the races that were connected to the super bodies, regardless of whether or not their appearances were hideous and ugly, regardless of how the strong preyed on the weak in history, regardless of how cruel and cruel they were, regardless of whether or not there was a blood feud in the past, regardless of whether or not there were endless conflicts, let go of everything, be reborn, and become part of the super bodies in a brand-new form. There were no selfish desires, no hatred, no violence, and naturally, there was no war. Even after several generations of evolution, the boundary of the race no longer existed… What a perfect new civilization this was!

“It was a pity that the good times did not last long. Although the Universe Ocean was vast and boundless, it could not accommodate the endless desires of certain ambitious species. When the super body civilization explored and invited more species to join the peaceful, friendly, and equal family in the Universe Ocean in their own way, they finally encountered another ferocious, dark civilization—the Primeval Civilization, their ‘brother’ on Earth in the old days!

“The super body civilization and the Primeval Civilization are like duplicates in the mirror.

“If the super body civilization represents brightness, friendship, and peace, then the Primeval Civilization represents darkness, hatred, and conquest.

“Wherever the super body civilization goes, it will extend its friendly tentacles and invite all the intelligent creatures with carbon bases to join its family. There will be absolutely no discrimination, exploitation, or oppression. Even the most primitive alien species in space will be able to receive the ‘invitation code’ that is connected to the super body with a spark of wisdom and be taught selflessly by the other species in the super body.

“Wherever the Primeval Civilization went, they butchered the intelligent alien species in space, wiped out all the low-level civilizations that could threaten them emotionlessly, destroyed the homes of the civilizations, enslaved the citizens of the civilizations, and modified the prosperous planets to the point that they were unrecognizable just so that they could live a comfortable life.

“Moreover, after they conquered most of the natural paradises in the sea of stars, when there were no more low-level civilizations or alien species for them to kill, they would pour their dark and evil lust for killing on their own kind and wage a prolonged, destructive civil war.

“The civil war of the Primeval Civilization almost destroyed one third of the natural paradises in the center of the universe and the future of countless civilizations and intelligent creatures, including themselves.

“They are simply the disgrace of the civilization of mankind in the past. They are also the most evil destroyers in the sea of the universe. They are the ‘cancer of the universe’!

“Despite their darkness, evilness, and cruelty, when the super civilization and the Primeval Civilization met for the first time, the super civilization still had no intention of attacking the Primeval Civilization.

“The history of the ancient Earth has long been buried in the smoke of time. The super civilization is a civilization that values peace and does not care about the grudges of the past.

“What’s more, during the long exploration and growth, the super body civilization was used to peace, friendship, selflessness, and sharing. They had long forgotten the methods of war, slaughter, and conquest.

“In accordance with their usual attitude, the super civilization sent out the tentacles of endless information and invited the Primeval Civilization to join their family. They hoped that the Primeval Civilization could learn the spirit of selfless sharing and resolve the grudges of the past in such a way, thereby changing the nature of the Primeval Civilization.

“It’s a pity that, hehe, the Primeval Civilization is used to their own methods.

Chapter 3136: The Immune System Of The Universe

At this point, the left half of Gu Wuxin’s mouth curled into a sneer, while the right half of his mouth exhaled a mouthful of dirty air and sighed helplessly. His entire face was twisted into two halves, making him look extremely weird.

“In the era of Earth billions of years ago, there was a saying that ‘measuring a gentleman’s heart with his own’. It was a perfect description of the attitude of the Primeval Civilization toward the super body civilization.

“The super civilization is a ‘gentleman’ who has no ill intentions toward the Primeval Civilization. They are hoping to abandon their past and build a better tomorrow in the Sea of Universe.

“The Primeval Civilization, on the other hand, has been living in sorrow for a long time. The guilt of the destruction of the Earth in the past has been fermenting on their black path of conquest. It has become a sore point for the entire Primeval Race. It is even part of their way of life and logic. They seek revenge for the smallest grievance. They are scared witless. They have conquered more than half of the sea of stars, but they have never had a good night’s sleep. They think that the entire universe is black because they are black. They don’t believe that the super civilization came for ‘peace’ and ‘friendship’. They don’t believe that there is a real ‘good-will civilization’ in the universe. Even though the good-will civilization used to be their compatriots, they regard it as the most dangerous enemy right now.

“Will the super body civilization still remember the hatred of being abandoned on Earth tens of thousands of years ago and only want revenge?

“Even if they don’t care about the hatred of the past, right now, they are both on different paths and have developed into a quasi-god level civilization that dominates the Universe Ocean. Will the super body civilization want to destroy the Primeval Civilization and dominate the entire Universe Ocean? As the saying goes, ‘two tigers cannot live on one mountain’.

“Whether or not the super body civilization is malicious to us, at least in the sea of the universe today, the super body civilization is the only existence capable of threatening the Primeval Civilization. For the long-term development and eternal glory of the Primeval Civilization, the threat of the super body civilization must be ‘removed’.

“Look, the three above are the most typical ways of thinking of the Primeval Lord of Heaven. After the prolonged conquest of the sea of stars and the brutal civil war, the Primeval Lord of Heaven has all turned into twisted, frightened birds. The last bit of kindness and brightness in the depths of their humanity has been sealed by the ‘chain of suspicion’ and their inflated desires. They are beyond redemption.

“In the end, the Primeval Civilization was the first to launch an attack against the super body civilization—a surprise attack and a shameless sneak attack.

“Huh?”

Li Yao blinked quickly. “This is different from the history I heard in the ‘ultimate test’!”

“It’s quite normal. Other than darkness, evil, and coldness, the most important feature of the Primeval Civilization is hypocrisy.

Gu Wuxin smiled and said, “Do you want them to admit that they are digging their own graves?

“All in all, the first batch of ‘supernaturals’ who got in touch with the ‘Venerable Ones’ were relatively small in scale. They did not carry any weapons, nor were they prepared for a battle. Therefore, the Primeval Civilization misjudged the capability of the supernaturals and thought that we were too large for our own good.

“Under the surprise attack, the first batch of ‘supernaturals’ were soon defeated and captured by the ‘Primeval Beings’.

“The Primeval Lord of Heaven did not kill the ‘superfigures’. They locked the superfigures in laboratories and conducted all kinds of cruel and inhumane experiments, hoping to discover the secrets of the ‘unified super information system of the universe’ from the superfigures so that they could further improve their capability and become a real ‘god-level civilization’ that would rule the Universe Ocean forever.

“However, the Primeval Lord of Heaven underestimated the reliability and confidentiality of the ‘super-body network’. The ‘super-body network’ is not an ordinary network of spiritual energy, electric waves, or magnetic fields, but one that involves quantum theory and gravitational wave theory. It is directly established in the ripples of the four-dimensional space. No matter how the Primeval Lord of Heaven jammed and blocked it, a Superhuman sent all his life information back to the headquarters of the Superhuman Civilization.

“You know that everything between the supernaturals is shared. One’s happiness is the happiness of the entire civilization, and one’s pain is the pain of the entire civilization.

“On that day, all the individuals of the super body civilization, from the human beings of the past to the low-level civilizations to the alien species in space, felt the excruciating pain of being dissected and dissected alive.

“The entire super body civilization was shocked!

“Before that, many low-level civilizations and alien species had their homes occupied by the Primeval Civilization. Most of their compatriots had been butchered, and they had no choice but to wander in the sea of stars and flee to the super body civilization, bringing the dark, evil, and brutal message of the Primeval Civilization.

“However, at that time, the kind-hearted super body civilization still believed that everything the Primeval Civilization did was just the natural instinct of carbon-based intelligent creatures to reproduce and expand their territory driven by genes. All civilizations would do the same when they developed to a certain stage, and all the problems could be resolved by communication and understanding.

“The terrifying experiment that was almost a live stream of the entire super body civilization changed the opinion of most super humans.

“As they got in touch with the Primeval Civilization more and more and learned more about it, they gradually painted a shocking, unimaginable picture of the dark civilization. At that moment, the super Cultivators finally reached a cold consensus.

“The Primeval Civilization has gone too far on the road of darkness. It has brought the evilness and darkness of human beings to maximum. It is impossible for us to be saved.

“Judging from the conquests and civil wars that they’ve waged, this is a civilization that is prone to self-destruction. It is irresponsible of the entire sea of the universe to allow them to continue living and developing. One day, when the Primeval Civilization reaches its peak, it will perish together with the entire universe.

“The only way to protect the peace, justice, and brightness of the universe is to destroy the Primeval Civilization.

“The individuals of the Primeval Civilization can be connected to the super bodies and be redeemed through the transmission and purification of tremendous information. But the Primeval Civilization as a whole, or according to its essence, is known as the ‘Dark Human Civilization’. It should not exist any longer. Only the ‘Super Civilization’, the ‘Light Human Civilization’, is the sole dominator of the sea of the universe. It is the inevitable path and the ultimate answer!

“You should know everything that happened next from the ‘ultimate test’, right?

“Although everything you learned in the ‘ultimate test’ must’ve been twisted and exaggerated by the Primeval Lord of Heaven, their glib tongue will not change the outcome of their failure.

“Justice will prevail over evil. Light will drown the darkness eventually. After hundreds of sub-universes, tens of thousands of galaxies, and a long, magnificent war, the Primeval Civilization failed miserably. Their reign over the darkness of the Universe Ocean collapsed, and all that was left were some stray dogs fleeing in the sea of stars. We are the descendants of one of the refugees.

“Although the Primeval Civilization got what it deserved, a centipede never dies. The sea of the universe is too vast and deep, and the Primeval Beings are all sordid, cunning, and full of tricks. It is hard to say whether or not some survivors of the Primeval Civilization are hiding in some unknown sub-universe and accumulating their strength, ready to rise again and stir more trouble.

“Think of the entire sea of stars as a gargantuan, super-large creature, a perfect life form of a higher level beyond our imagination and our understanding. Then, the remnants of the Primeval Civilization and all the selfish, ambitious, carbon-based intelligent creatures will be the viruses, bacteria, and cancer cells inside the body of the creature. The Flood Tide Legion that you fear is nothing more than an immune system that is similar to white blood cells.

“The remnants of the Primeval Civilization and all the selfish, greedy, carbon-based intelligent creatures will only bring destruction, oppression, deception, war, insurrection, and so on. The darkness, evil, and negative things will eventually destroy the future of the entire universe. Shouldn’t we eliminate them once and for all?

“Right now, I’ve told you the whole story. Come to your senses, new generation of human beings. Although you and I are the descendants of the survivors of the Primeval Civilization, and we are burdened by the bloody original sin and the unstoppable instincts of conquest and destruction, we will still be accepted by the entire universe as long as we repent sincerely and let go of ourselves. We will be connected to the super bodies and be embraced by the families of the super bodies.

Li Yao’s chin, which had fallen to the back of his foot, drooped for half a minute before it returned to his chin.

“Wow… What you said makes so much sense that it’s completely irrefutable. I was simply enlightened. My entire body sublimated. Without me knowing, I believed everything you said.”

Li Yao looked at Gu Wuxin’s back and said, “In principle, we are definitely not going to be the loyal subjects of the primeval civilization. If the ‘super body’ you mentioned is really so awesome, there’s no harm in giving it a try. However, the new generation of human civilization has a strong belief in democracy and the legal system. It is impossible for us to decide the affairs of the entire civilization.

“Since everyone is talking so happily, and we are very curious about the super body civilization, why don’t we do this? First, you can stop the ‘Flood Tide Warship’ and the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ behind you, which have such brilliant acoustic and photoelectric effects. Then, you can follow us back to the interior of the human civilization. We can go to our parliament, universities, laboratories, research institutes, and other places. We can communicate in depth, have a good exchange, and improve our understanding and communication with each other. You can also preach the benefits of being linked to the super body to the entire human race. You can also have a clash of ideologies with our experts and scholars, engage in a competition of the Great Dao, and find some volunteers to conduct small-scale experiments… In short, we can spend 300-500 years to completely figure out the whole thing. Then, we can have a universal vote and decide whether or not to be linked to the super body. This seems to be the safest way, right?”

“After all, billions of years have passed since the survivors of the Primeval Civilization fled to the Pangu Universe. Hundreds of thousands of years have passed since you were frozen in the ripples of time. We can wait billions of years. It doesn’t matter if we have to wait another three to five hundred years. Hahahaha!”

Chapter 3137: The Doubts Of The Experts!

“… I can’t.”

Gu Wuxin’s face changed countless times before it was condensed into a complicated, anxious, and angry look. “It is exactly because we have been waiting in the dark sea of stars for billions of years that we cannot waste even one more second.

“You haven’t been connected to a super body yet. You don’t understand how uncomfortable it is for me, who has been connected to a super body and is used to the interaction of tremendous information, to be trapped in this small, cold, enclosed universe, unable to absorb the information from the big universe!

“It was like… although I had eyes, I could not see. Although I had ears, I could not hear. Although I had a mouth, I could not tell the entire Universe Ocean my feelings. Pain. Extreme pain. Pain that could not be described with words!

“I can’t stand such a feeling for even half a second. Believe me, you will understand me when you are all connected to the super bodies.

“Right now, I can more or less understand it… You are probably like a drug addict. In the beginning, a tiny amount of information was enough to satisfy you, but gradually, you became the captive and slave of information. You needed more and more information in order to stimulate your soul. As the ‘threshold’ of your soul grew higher and higher, you needed more and more very stimulating information, to the point that the entire Pangu Universe could not satisfy you—it was like a drug addict who could do anything insane when he was not satisfied. You also reached the stage where you would not hesitate to destroy your own civilization even if you had to resort to robbery!

Li Yao said, “After all, you are talking to yourself. Our choice is not important at all. There is no room for us to express our opinions, is there?”

“From any perspective—from the perspective of the super body civilization, from the perspective of the Pangu civilization, or from my own perspective—I am qualified to call you ‘child’.

Gu Wuxin said, “Child, you will understand it when you are connected to a super body. Your civilization is still young and ignorant. It will destroy itself and other people in the confusion. Have you ever seen a parent asking for their child’s opinion before they educate their child?

“The so-called ‘education’, or ‘guidance’, is a form of coercion in the first place. Right now, you are still the ‘host’ whose genes are inherently bad. It is impossible for you to strip away the darkness and evil in the depths of your genes willingly. You must have a bright and strong civilization of kindness to help you purify them.

“Otherwise, you will not be able to control the immense strength that you possess. Not only will you destroy yourself, you will also destroy the entire Pangu Universe and even cause greater damage in the sea of the multiverse. Do you understand?

Gu Wuxin’s words were sincere, but they were not. He extended his hands to Li Yao, and his body suddenly expanded like a soap bubble under the scorching sun, making him both splendid and illusionary.

“I understand.”

Li Yao nodded. “My companions… have figured it out, too!”

Hardly had he finished his sentence when dazzling flames burst out of not only the four monsters and the deities under Gu Wuxin’s control but also the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ not far away and the research base of the captives of the Flood Tide Legion.

It was Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, Li Jialing, the Fist King, and the other experts on Li Yao’s side. They had analyzed the countermeasures through the tactical network hundreds of times and locked onto the loopholes of Gu Wuxin and the Bifrost. They had shocked the world with a single brilliant feat and launched the most fatal attack.

Li Yao and Gu Wuxin did not communicate with each other through language. Instead, a lot of information was transmitted through brainwaves.

Therefore, what seemed to be a long story was actually just a matter of a few thoughts.

The conversation between the two of them lasted less than a minute. Even though Gu Wuxin was trying to buy himself more time, Li Yao had hinted Ding Lingdang and the rest of them to steer the Giant God Soldiers around Gu Wuxin and destroy the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ from the source.

“Cut the nonsense. I’ve seen too many guys who think that brightness and justice can save the civilization and the universe.

Ding Lingdang shouted and was the first to lunge at Gu Wuxin. Since the long-range attack just now didn’t have much effect on the Indigo Devil God, he might as well let her pull out her most beloved iron fist. “No matter how extravagant the words of scumbags like you are, no matter how many empty promises you make, you will inevitably give yourselves away in the end—you simply won’t respect the opinions and feelings of others, and you have never treated others as equals like you. In your eyes, others are just chess pieces, ants, dust, and paint that depicts a ‘bright future’.

“Stop your sweet talk. No matter what you say, we will not believe you. We will find the answers to our questions on our own. Even if we are doomed, we do not need answers that are imposed upon us. Never!”

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

108,000 streaks of lightning seemed to be hiding in the gaps of Ding Lingdang’s fingers. They congregated into two crimson storms and rushed at the super-large octopus that Gu Wuxin was standing on without any mercy. Even the transparent spiritual energy barriers that were condensed in front of Gu Wuxin seemed to be unable to stop Ding Lingdang’s unstoppable attack.

“A civilization of absolute brightness and justice? Hehe. Hehehehe. Why don’t I believe it?”

Boss Bai launched an attack at Gu Wuxin from the flank like a white ghost. “Although the Primeval Civilization in the ‘ultimate test’ must’ve boasted a lot of things about themselves, they admitted the darkness and evilness of their ancestors after all. The bloody and cruel history of conquest sounded a bit more real.
