“But when you mentioned the rise of the super body civilization and the path of conquest, you glossed over everything with the words ‘snowball’. ‘More and more low-level civilizations and alien species in space have joined the families of the super body civilizations’. Haha. Is that a fairy tale? Are all the civilizations and all the alien species so naïve, kind, and unsuspecting that they are all scrambling to join you?

“If a low-level civilization or an alien species does not want to join you because they want to maintain their independent, ‘selfish’ social form and mindset, what will you do? If a civilization changes their mind and wants to quit the super body after they are connected to it, will their requirements be met? If a group can only join but cannot quit, it will be even worse than the pirate gang!

Boss Bai’s giant soldier was attacking Gu Wuxin’s feet like a translucent saber. It seemed that he was trying to separate Gu Wuxin from the four demon beasts and take advantage of their advantage in numbers.

“It’s true that there are many unreasonable points about your theory. For me, who is also a ‘cluster life’ in a certain sense, there are a lot of technical problems that I can’t figure out. If possible, I hope you can provide further answers.”

The boxing champion followed behind Ding Lingdang, too, controlling his enormous iron body. On one hand, he was protecting Ding Lingdang from the bombardment, and on the other hand, he had prepared all the long-range firepower, ready to launch a second wave of attacks when Gu Wuxin could not resist his retreat.

In the meantime, hundreds of small battle puppets jumped out of his enormous body of steel. The smallest battle puppet was only the size of a palm and looked like a small drone that carried spiritual energy bullets. The larger ones looked like the six-legged spiders that were commonly used in mining bases. Since they could blow away the hardest ores, they could naturally crush the armor and bones of the enemy, too.

After Li Yao’s modification, the modification of the best crystal processor experts of the Star Glory Federation, the Star Glory Federation, and the Star Glory Federation, together with the assistance of Xiao Ming and Wen Wen and the hard training of the Fist King himself, his computational ability and combat ability had both soared to an unbelievable level. It was not difficult for him to control thousands of clones at the same time.

Of course, the more clones one had, the more pressure it would put on the mainframe crystal processor. If a lot of data that needed to be calculated was allocated to every clone, such as the cloud calculation, the network would be much more demanding.

And this was the problem of the boxing champion.

“‘Unified Universal Superinformation’ sounds like a very advanced and perfect concept. However, any concept that falls from theory to reality will inevitably be greatly undermined.

The Fist King sent out a lot of questions calmly. “If such a super information network does exist, how large and advanced would it be and how much energy would it consume to connect all the intelligent creatures in the universe, the countless Sectors, and the countless galaxies?

“You mentioned ‘Quantum Entanglement’ and ‘Gravity Wave’ just now. I don’t know much about the application of such technologies in the field of information interaction. But I believe that, however advanced the technology is, it is unavoidable that it involves the consumption of energy. It is impossible to absorb energy from the void, is it not?

“In this universe, there is no energy that can be obtained out of thin air. To maintain the tactical network between a thousand clones, I have to consume a lot of crystals and put tremendous pressure on the mainframe crystal processor. Your ‘super body network’ involves an unbelievable number of billions of times. To maintain its existence and stability, the energy consumed per second is astronomical. I’m afraid that the energy generated by a hundred supernovas exploding at the same time will not be enough for you to burn for one minute. After billions of years of consumption, what kind of terrifying changes has your ‘super body civilization’ made to the entire sea of the universe?”

Chapter 3138: Unstoppable Brightness!

Endless cannon fire poured out from the chest of the boxing champion, followed by Ding Lingdang’s storm-like iron fists that drowned Gu Wuxin.

“I don’t believe that the so-called ‘superhuman civilization’ is as good as you described, either. Receiving the brainwaves of billions of the same species instantly and interacting with them through emotions, memories, and wisdom. How enormous and advanced must the brains of every individual of the superhuman civilization be in order to support such unimaginable computations? Perceiving ten thousand kinds of happiness and suffering in the blink of an eye, with countless voices haunting their brain all the time. Are they really not going to go mad?”

Li Jialing, riding his golden giant soldier, rushed close, too. “I once went through a brutal experiment in ‘Red Spider Lily’, a city in the sky. The experimenter poured a lot of information into my brain in the blink of an eye. The pain of my braincells exploding because of the overloading was already excruciating enough to drive me crazy! If I increase the intensity of the information transmission by billions of times, I will only raise a bunch of mentally deranged lunatics or obese monsters whose brains have deformed and expanded. There will be no second outcome!

The golden lion turned into a streak of gold and rushed into the raging flames created by Ding Lingdang and the boxing champion.

“I find it hard to understand the significance of the existence of every individual in the ‘superhuman civilization’.

Long Yangjun said casually, “Light and darkness are two sides of the same coin. Good and evil, good and evil, influence each other, reference and transformation. If the super body civilization has eliminated darkness and evil and achieved the so-called ‘perfect justice’, what is the difference between it and ‘perfect evil’ without reference?

“The simplest logic is that, if you don’t need to do anything, as long as you are connected to a super body, you will be able to enjoy other people’s happiness, excitement, physical comfort, and the exploration of the mental world. Who would be willing to work hard for real?

“In the same way, if the pain of one individual is the pain of the entire civilization, then the super body civilization should think of ways to put an end to the pain of all individuals. However, without pain, how can people measure their happiness?

“Maybe it’s because my imagination is limited, but I can’t think of any significance for every individual of the super body civilization to continue living, let alone the slightest bit of creativity. In the end, because their hearts and souls are connected and influenced by each other, their way of thinking and their mode of action will show a high degree of ‘convergence’. Even if there are thousands upon thousands of seemingly different individuals, they will only have one thought and the same action.

“Although I was a warrior on the side of Nuwa in my previous life, I didn’t understand the significance of ‘Chaos—Chaos’ at all. Or rather, why would anybody worship Chaos and sabotage the order of the stable operation, dragging the entire universe into a mess?

“Today, when I saw you talking about the seemingly perfect super body civilization, I finally understood.

“If the end of ‘order’ and ‘rules’ is one side of a thousand people without darkness and absolute brightness, then chaos is the only salvation. Life and civilization need chaos. Even if chaos brings oppression, deception, slaughter, and destruction, it is still much better than the unification of the multiverse ocean in a single pattern!

‘Heaven Crystal’, Long Yangjun’s giant soldier, released glamorous brilliance that even suppressed the crimson dragon flames on Ding Lingdang’s head.

Uncharacteristically, she triggered all her techniques and condensed four crystal sabers out of thin air, cutting off the connection between Gu Wuxin and the four devilish beasts.

“My fellow Cultivators have said everything that I wanted to say!

All the experts bravely moved forward. Li Yao was not willing to be left behind, either. However, he steered the ‘sun catastrophe’ and triggered flames that looked like supernovas. He roared, “I was going to refute you just now, but my fellow Cultivators beat me to it. There’s no need for me to repeat myself. In short, no matter how perfect your words are, the so-called ‘absolute light and justice’ are absolutely impossible to achieve! Besides, you’ve never seen what a real super civilization looks like. Everything you know is nothing more than what the captives of the Flood Tide Legion told your ancestors in the old days. Your ancestors were mimicking you and adding fuel to the fire, passing on the knowledge from generation to generation. This is a typical false advertisement. It’s like the braised shrimp and braised beef on the packaging of instant noodles. How can there really be such a thing? It would be good enough to give you a few bags of dehydrated vegetables and seasoning powder!

“For any intelligent creature, the most important power is the right to choose and refuse. If it is not their own choice but the brightness imposed by somebody else, what is the difference between brightness and darkness? If they do not have the right to think for themselves, to choose darkness, and to bear the consequences, brightness is meaningless. The new generation of human civilization will not be fooled by you. We will walk out of our own logic. Definitely!”

Led by Li Yao, all the human experts and their Giant God Soldiers joined the battle.

Burning his soul and overdrafting his life, he brought his hot blood and determination to maximum. Every explosive bullet of spiritual energy and every slash of his chainsword carried visible spiritual waves. Even the solid spiritual shields around the four gods and devils under Gu Wuxin’s control were torn apart by the bombardment of human beings. Blood spurted out, and broken organs and limbs flew everywhere.

I made it!

Ding Lingdang punched with all her might, sending enough power to blow up an asteroid into the body of the Colored Jellyfish. Suddenly, she felt that there was nothing in front of her. Finally, the Colored Jellyfish could not withstand her heavy fists that were like a volcanic eruption and exploded.

Shua!

In the other direction, ‘Sword Addict’ Yan Liren, who had been fighting hard without caring about anything else, slashed ninety-nine times within one second, all of which hit the neck of the Indigo Devil precisely, causing further damage to the enemy before the muscle fibers grew out. Not only were the muscles and veins cut open, the high-frequency vibration even blew up the spine of the Indigo Devil and chopped off its head.

“A competition of speed?”

In the third direction, Boss Bai chuckled and stepped aside, jumping onto the back of the skinning wolf that was lunging at him. Two ghost-like blades pierced into the eyes of the wolf and into its brain silently.

In the meantime, in the fourth direction, the Fist King, Little Ming, and Wen Wen threw out all the battle puppets that they had. They jumped onto the wings and head of the nine-headed griffin and enveloped it like a layer of magma. No matter how it jumped up and down and waved its claws, it could not get rid of the cold machines. It could only watch the battle puppets tearing apart its scales, feathers, and skin with drills and digging deeper and deeper into its body until they exploded near its internal organs.

He had summoned almost forty Giant Soldiers to deal with Gu Wuxin and his four companions.

In the meantime, more or less the same number of Giant God Soldiers, led by the members of the Temple of Immortals, bypassed the battlefield and darted toward District 47. Very soon, earthshaking explosions were echoing in the entire District 47. Hundreds of fireballs rose into the sky together with black smoke, as if a dragon hundreds of times thicker was about to swallow the entire Bifrost.

“You are doomed, Gu Wuxin!”

Riding the calamity of the sun, Li Yao locked onto the enemy among the smoke and flames. “Or rather, the monster who calls himself ‘Gu Wuxin’!

The right arm of the calamity of the sun turned into a burning shooting star. It was so fast and so powerful that the space was even twisted in a small area. When the punch hit Gu Wuxin’s chest brutally, the leader of the Temple of Immortals was blown into… a pile of mud!

“What?”

Li Yao was greatly alarmed. Although the punch contained all his dignity as an expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage and the heir of the Supreme Emperor, he had never expected such an outcome. He was very sure that, before his punch destroyed Gu Wuxin’s body tissues, all the cells inside the enemy’s body had collapsed on their own and turned him into a pile of mud, a slug, or a cluster of pink blood and foam.

The floating foam fell upon Li Yao’s giant soldier.

Even the dazzling golden flames could not melt the seemingly fragile bubbles. Instead, the enemy infiltrated through the gaps of the giant soldier gods.

“I admit that the wisdom of me and everyone else in our tiny super bodies is limited. We cannot answer most of your questions.

Gu Wuxin’s voice was not affected at all. It continued coming from Li Yao’s brain. “But I still believe that we cannot save the civilization and the universe by ourselves. Only by tearing apart the seal of the Pangu Universe and ushering in a real super civilization can we get all the answers and ultimate salvation. All the problems that we are faced with today will be solved easily, too.

“It’s useless. Your struggles are futile. The rainbow bridge has been opened. There is no power that can stop me from transmitting the information of the Pangu Universe.

“Besides, what’s the point even if you stop me? You selfish people who are burdened by the original sin are destined to not be able to hide in the dark, cold universe of Pangu forever. Very soon, you will still be found and connected to the super body civilization. I’ve only brought forward the time of your departure.

“As early as billions of years ago, the super body civilization defeated the Primeval Civilization and became the sole dominator of the sea of the multiverse. After billions of years of development, it has become even more brilliant and magnificent, hasn’t it? Do you still want to compete with the entire sea of the multiverse with the power of the Pangu Universe? It’s impossible. Give up the meaningless resistance and welcome the unstoppable brightness!”

Chapter 3139: Revealing Viciousness

With Gu Wuxin’s roar, Li Yao experienced the most intense and ferocious possession in his life.

He used to be a prince-like existence in the world of possession. In the battlefield of his mind and soul, he was not scared of enemies three to five levels higher than himself at all.

After the training of ‘Three Thousand Mortal Dusts, External Clone’, his research on the field of the soul and the consciousness had reached an even deeper level.

His brain was both an unfathomable maze and a fortress full of traps and firing points. Any enemy who launched a mental attack on him recklessly and penetrated into his brain would be committing suicide.

Many of Li Yao’s trump cards were to release his brainwaves and spiritual flames to meld the mental space in his brain with the real world around him. Then, he could drag the enemy’s soul into his own brain and let him do whatever he wanted with it. If he was crueler, he could carve out an independent space in his brain and lock the enemy’s soul inside for fifty or even a hundred years.

Therefore, Li Yao had not paid much attention to Gu Wuxin’s bragging at the beginning. He was even hoping that the guy would have a mental competition and drag the battle into the field that he was best at.

However, when Gu Wuxin’s mental power flooded into his brain like a magma eruption, he immediately realized that something was wrong.

If it were just Gu Wuxin’s soul, no matter how unbelievable it was, even if it was ten times stronger than Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi in the past, Li Yao, who had just inherited the will of the emperor, might have been able to deal with it and suppress it.

However, although Gu Wuxin’s own mental power was not the best, it was not just his own soul that was flooding into Li Yao’s brain crazily. A hundred or even a thousand souls seemed to be flooding into Li Yao’s brain at the same time.

A thousand souls, a thousand devils, and a thousand ghosts attacked Li Yao’s brain simultaneously, trying to possess him!

“Join us!”

“Link into our super bodies and find a way to save our civilization!”

“We are the eternal light, the absolute justice!”

“Will you really not regret turning me down without even trying?”

“Come on. Come on. I was as stubborn as you were in the old days. But now that you have been connected to a super body, you will be a whole new world. Your life and the entire universe will be different. You will be reborn, and the universe will be reborn. Come on!”

“We know the mysteries of the Pioneer Civilization. We know the methods to jump from the three-dimensional world to the four-dimensional world. Endless laws and mysteries. Come and study them with us!”

For a moment, thousands of wandering souls in various shapes and sizes seemed to be jumping out of Li Yao’s brain.

The wandering souls were either staring at Li Yao with their glittering eyes, waving their tentacles that were full of flagella, or sending complicated, obscure signals to Li Yao with all kinds of weird senses and communication organs in a way that was almost a nightmare.

“Ah!”

Although Li Yao was a monster who had been possessed countless times and could counterattack every time, he still felt that his head was about to explode when his brain was suddenly filled with a thousand ghosts that contained tremendous mental power. The fire of his soul was like a candle in a storm that could be extinguished at any moment.

“Hehehehe!”

“Chirp chirp chirp!”

“Hahahaha!”

The thousand ghosts inside Li Yao’s brain burst into soul-stirring laughter.

“Come on. Come on. The entire universe is going to be covered by the Unified Universal Superinformation sooner or later anyway. What are you persisting for?”

They were dancing weirdly like colorful seaweeds and jellyfish. Very soon, they turned into wriggling fungi and corroded Li Yao’s brain and soul, injecting tremendous information into Li Yao’s memories.

For a moment, Li Yao’s soul seemed to have been torn into a thousand pieces. He experienced a thousand different memories at the same time.

In some of his memories, he had turned into a researcher of the Pangu Clan. Faced with the legacy of the primeval era and the mysteries billions of years ago, he had pondered hard and failed to come up with any answers. He had endured the torment of curiosity and desperation.

In other memories, he had become an expert of the Nuwa Clan and conducted all kinds of experiments wholeheartedly.

In some of his memories, he even turned into a captive of the Flood Tide Legion who had been locked in a laboratory and tasted all kinds of inhuman tortures as an experiment subject.

To be fair, with Li Yao’s current Cultivation and willpower, he could barely bear the physical and mental pain.

The problem was that most of the data he received came from the researchers of the Heavenly Palace who had established the ‘minor super entities’. Most of the researchers were from thirteen different species in the primeval era. Although they were all carbon-based intelligent creatures, their perception systems and thinking processes were too different.

It was like Li Yao had turned into a bent earthworm in one moment and a hibernating turtle in the next. He was about to change into a dozen different life forms and modes of thought in the blink of an eye. It was extremely awkward and uncomfortable.

Many prehistoric species, such as the Houyi Clan, which boasted collective wisdom, were even more different from human beings than earthworms and turtles.

Thankfully, Li Yao had trained with the identity of the Yi family for a while at the bottom of the Gold Crystal Tower, and he was used to the calmness when he was training with his clones.

Otherwise, such intense information would certainly have shattered his original consciousness and memories.

Even so, the endless garbage memories and interference still filled up every one of his brain cells, making the storage and computational space of his brain seriously insufficient. His computational ability plummeted at a visible speed and was almost drowned in the mud of the super body.

“Such terrifying data attacks!

“Other than a natural-born weirdo such as myself, it is difficult for someone with a clear mind and a firm will to maintain themselves under the erosion of tremendous data.

“Li Jialing is right. The brain and the soul of a normal carbon-based intelligent life cannot withstand the overloading of information. Without modification or brainwashing, their brains will definitely explode in a flash.

“Long Yangjun is right. Under the crazy interaction of such a huge amount of information, who can maintain the integrity and clarity of their self? Even the existence of individuals is meaningless!

“However, the ‘supernaturals’ that Gu Wuxin came into contact with were merely the descriptions that the captives of the Flood Tide Legion told his ancestors. They were merely the tip of the iceberg. How terrifying was the real ‘supernaturals’ exactly?

Li Yao felt that his spherical brain cells were packed with square or triangular, sharp souls that were piercing his brain cells from inside out.

It was like his round soul had been stuffed into a triangular, square, or bizarre-shaped container and squeezed into a mess.

Under the suffocating pressure of his soul, it was difficult for him to even struggle, let alone counterattack.

It was not until a streak of redness and a streak of gold flashed past his vision that the mental connection between him and Gu Wuxin’s super body was temporarily cut off like two sharp blades.

Huchi! Huchi! Huchi! Huchi!

Li Yao almost fell to his knees in the ‘sun catastrophe’.

It had only been several seconds since the mental game that he was best at, but he felt as tired as if he had been engaged with a main fleet for three days and three nights. His sweat was spurting out like a fountain, and the water inside his body had almost run out. His mouth was dry, and there was also… fear.

Yes. Fear.

Li Yao was a human being, too. However determined and passionate he was, he had been worried about himself, his family, his home, the civilization of mankind, and the future of the Pangu Universe.

Before, no matter what kind of enemy he was faced with, his mysterious and powerful brain was always his last trump card. Even enemies such as Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi or the super crystal processor Fuxi could not shake his soul at all.

But this time, Li Yao had to admit that, faced with the ‘supernaturals’ made of the souls of a thousand ancient experts, his mental power, however strong, might be completely suppressed or even… corrupted!

“Are you kidding me? The guy’s soul power is unbelievable. It is much stronger than ours?”

The mental devil shrieked in disbelief. Li Yao had rarely sensed fear in its soul ripples.

“It was not his own soul power, but the soul power of all the members of the super body that had been lurking and growing in the Heavenly Palace for thousands of years.

Lu Qingchen’s voice was no longer frivolous and arrogant. It was more solemn than ever. “Do you still not understand? He—he is not Gu Wuxin. The real leader of the Heavenly Palace, Gu Wuxin, is already dead. Maybe he died the moment he fell into the magma, or maybe he died the moment he was connected to the super body. What is in front of us right now is just a ‘carrier’ of the super body. It is the soul crystal of the experts and scholars with the strongest mental power in the Heavenly Palace over the past thousands of years.

“No. Maybe it is not as simple as the experts of the Heavenly Palace. There are also the ferocious beasts brought by the ‘Dao of Mass Destruction’ and the ‘Dragon Lotus Seed’. They are the last elites of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan. The flesh and blood of the monsters have all turned into the most fundamental cells. But what about their souls? I’m afraid that they have melted into the super body in front of us in the form of certain ‘nutrition’ and ‘energy’. Gu Wuxin and the other four monsters that look like deities are not their real selves. The overwhelming ocean of foam—or rather, the ocean of cells—is their real appearance!

Chapter 3140: The True Meaning Of "Human Beings"!

Li Yao and the mental devil both felt that their blood was freezing.

On second thought, it did make a lot of sense. Lu Qingchen was also a crazy schemer who was qualified to be the ‘Ultimate Demon King’ at a certain stage. His deduction might be more in line with the ‘Gu Wuxin—Supernatural Body’ state.

Li Yao did not know how long it had been since the researchers of the Heavenly Palace accepted the ‘invitation’ of the captives of the Flood Tide Legion and established their own soul network to invite more people to join them. He did not know how many people had joined this… indescribable group of terrorists over the past thousand years.

But one thing was certain.

Those who joined the super bodies were all the elites of the primeval civilization. They might not have extraordinary combat ability, but their mental power was unimaginably strong.

Li Yao could not help but recall the ‘resurrected Pangu Clan’ that he had met in the Kunlun Mysterious Land.

Although he had surpassed the Divinity Transformation Stage today, he still felt scared when he remembered the battle against the Pangu Clan when he first met the Giant God Soldiers.

If anything went wrong that day, the history of the entire Pangu Universe would change.

The resurrected Pangu Clan member was probably one of the top experts of the primeval era. It was unlikely that he was among the top experts. At the very least, the mental power of the people in the Heavenly Palace was not much weaker than his.

Then, how terrifying would it be if the mental power of a thousand resurrected Pangu Clan members was gathered together? What kind of destructive, sweeping techniques could it summon?

Li Yao could not imagine it.

No wonder he could not free himself from the mental attack of Gu Wuxin’s super body and had to rely on the mental attack of the mental devil and Lu Qingchen to slightly disrupt the enemy’s rhythm.

Even though he was born with special attributes and had experienced a lot of fortuitous incidents, it was still impossible for him to fight a thousand resurrected Pangu Clan members head-on.

Besides, Lu Qingchen did have a point. The monster in front of him definitely had more than just mental power.

Li Yao had seen with his own eyes how terrifying the ferocious beast tide that swallowed the City of Silver and attacked the Tower of Heaven.

But right now, all the ferocious beasts had collapsed and turned into the most basic cells. But the cells did not lose their vitality. Instead, they condensed into a slowly wriggling ocean of foam, as if they were a new, evil, and powerful life form.

Who knew how many variations there were in this overwhelming pile of meat?

It did not need to be too much. As long as the ‘Gu Wuxin’s super body’ could summon the strength equivalent to thirty to fifty ‘Tyrant Armored Dragons’, it would not even need to be maintained for three to five days. As long as the strongest combat ability deep inside every cell was unleashed within one day and one night, there would be no chance of victory.

It was because the enemy was not trying to destroy them, but they were in a hurry to destroy the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ behind the enemy.

The initiative was in the hands of the enemy. The enemy was waiting patiently, but they were like tigers that were faced with a hedgehog and could not find any gaps to attack.

With every second that went by, the Pangu Universe and the human civilization were closer and closer to the tipping point of being pushed into the abyss of light.

Li Yao’s heart was sinking to the bottom.

When he sorted out his messy thoughts and scanned the situation of the battle around him, his heart almost jumped out of his chest.

The squad that bypassed the battlefield and attacked District 47 was unimpeded. They did not encounter any effective obstacles, but it did not mean that they could complete their mission smoothly.

All kinds of dazzling, sonic, and electric attacks were raining down on District 47 like a brilliant fireworks show.

However, when the smoke dispersed, everybody was dumbfounded to find that, no matter how many facilities they destroyed in District 47, they could not hurt the Bifrost at all.

As time went by, the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ became more and more translucent. It was both like an ice block melting in water and like a projection from another world. The attacks of the giant soldier gods passed through the projection like pouring rain. The best they could do was raise a few ‘water splashes’ and a few ‘ripples’. But they failed to cut off the brilliance of the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ or blow up the surging tide warship. It was all futile.

“How did this happen?”

Li Yao quickly thought of what Gu Wuxin had just said.

The rainbow bridge employed the very advanced technology of mass-energy conversion and space jump. At first, the mass in the three-dimensional universe was transformed into energy. Then, the gaps in the four-dimensional space were torn apart, and the energy was sent out directly with the cutting-edge technology of quantum entanglement and gravitational waves.

In other words, the space where the Bifrost was right now was a tiny four-dimensional space ripple. It was no longer in the same universe as the attackers nearby.

It was like an attacker lifting a ten-thousand-ton rock and bombarding the reflection in a pond. Even if the reflection was blown into pieces, it would never be able to be condensed again. How could the real body be hurt at all?

Li Yao had a very direct understanding of the super body—the advanced technology of flood tide.

Human civilization today had grasped the technology of space jumps, too, including the ability to tear apart space and create temporary wormholes.

However, when human warships were tearing apart the four-dimensional space and creating temporary wormholes, they were at their most vulnerable moment. Not to mention carpet bombing, even a single shot might be enough to sink half of a fleet.

Ever since the space jump technology was invented and applied on a large scale, countless brilliant fleets had been ambushed by the enemy during space jumps. Boss Bai, the ‘Pirate Potentate’, was an expert in such ambushes.

It was also the first reason why Li Yao and the other human experts thought of stabilizing Gu Wuxin and sneaking around the battlefield to attack the Bifrost.

He did not expect that the technology of the ‘Supernatural Body—Flood Tide’ was so much more advanced than that of human beings that they were not scared of sneak attacks at all.

Was it really as Gu Wuxin had said? The Bifrost had soared into the sky, and there was no power that could stop him from sending the message of the Pangu Universe?

“It’s impossible. There must be a way to cut off the Bifrost, or at least destroy the Flood Tide warship!”

Li Yao thought to himself, Since the purpose of the Bifrost is to transform matter into energy, and the energy cannot be destroyed by the attacks of the entities in the three-dimensional universe after it enters the space ripples, I can only try to destroy it from the inside by colliding the frequency of its waves with my soul and my life force.

However, how could it be so easy?

At the very least, Li Yao did not think that ‘Gu Wuxin’s super body’ would give them the time.

The four enormous, intimidating devils were wreathed in grey and pink foam after they fell into the ocean of foam, covered in bruises.

When the foam covered their bodies, they immediately turned into the freshest cells and wriggled and proliferated at a crazy speed. Very soon, their wounds were mostly repaired, and their bodies expanded time and time again. Black shells were even formed on the surface of their bodies, and hundreds of floating tentacles rose up from the periphery, waving crazily in defense of human beings.

Very soon, cracking noises echoed inside the black shell. Furry claws, tentacles that were full of thorns and suckers, and weird tails that were emitting electric arcs crawled out of the shell.

The monster, which had been heavily wounded and on the verge of death a moment ago, had been reborn in less than half a minute and become even stronger and more brutal.

In the meantime, all the human experts, including Li Yao, had been jammed by the sharp brainwaves and the torrent of information released by the ‘Gu Wuxin’s super body’. None of the Giant God Soldiers were able to harass the recovery and rebirth of the monsters with their attacks.

Protected by four devil-like monsters, Gu Wuxin, who was floating in midair, had completed his transformation, too.

He had completely gotten rid of the half-human-half-snake form of the Nuwa Clan and freed his strong legs from the shed skin. He looked like a translucent giant more than fifty meters tall.

He was not wearing any armor, not even any clothes. His skin that should’ve been as smooth as jade was now covered in dense synapses, as if the nerve endings were multiplying infinitely.

The synapses that were exposed to the air were probably some kind of ‘outer antennas’. They could strengthen the mental connection between him, the devils, the monsters, and the ocean of foam and improve his control over the entire battlefield. But they could also remove the last bit of ‘sacred and righteous’ brightness around him, making him look like a devil in a nightmare.

“Damn monster!”

Ding Lingdang was the closest to Gu Wuxin, and she was the most hot-tempered one. She did not care how many times she was transformed. She turned into a shooting star together with the giant soldier and lunged at Gu Wuxin with a shocking exhaust flame. “I don’t believe that you can’t be killed. Try the iron fists of human beings!”

BOOM!

The punch rumbled like the main gun of a super arsenal warship. Crimson ripples and streams of flames spread out in the several square kilometers nearby, as if a magma eruption that had no solid body was taking place. Even Li Yao had to admit that his wife’s punch was getting stronger and stronger. She deserved to be the marvelous girl who blew up a tyrannosaur with one punch!

However, the punch that was as fast as lightning and as powerful as a thunderclap did not cause any damage to Gu Wuxin at all. He did not even have the strength to force his opponent to move at all. Yet, Gu Wuxin had grasped his fist steadily.

“Of course, I will die, but I won’t be killed by you. Just like dinosaurs can never be defeated by ants when they are weak.

Gu Wuxin said casually, “Also, after the modification of the super bodies, we—we have abandoned all the unnecessary impurities inside our bodies and optimized the entire gene pool. Right now, we can only burn for twenty-four hours, but we are closer to the essence of ‘human beings’ than you are.

“With such weak fists, do you think you are qualified to call yourselves the ‘human civilization’ that once dominated the sea of stars? Now, let me tell you the real power that ‘human beings’ represent!

Chapter 3141: The Purest Of Violence!

Hardly had he finished his sentence when dozens of helical spots suddenly beamed out of Gu Wuxin’s muscular arms, each of which seemed to be a living creature baring its fangs and brandishing its claws. The furious damage was absorbed into his palm. After a cracking sound, he crushed the right fist of the big Fire Dragon Sparrow!

At this moment, not just Ding Lingdang and Li Yao, all the human experts who were familiar with Ding Lingdang almost stopped breathing.

It must be noted that, although Ding Lingdang’s Giant God Soldier was named ‘Great Fire Dragon Sparrow’, it was completely different from the incomplete ancient Giant God Soldier that had been excavated from the Kunlun Mysterious Realm a hundred years ago.

As the previous Speaker of Parliament, one of the strongest experts of the Combat Department of the Star Glory Federation, and most importantly, Li Yao’s wife, who enjoyed the majority of the shares and profits of the Glorious Sunlight Group, Ding Lingdang had access to resources beyond the imagination of ordinary experts. According to the standards of the imperium, she was as good as any of the four electors.

In name, it was also known as ‘Great Fire Dragon Sparrow’. But after decades of thorough maintenance and upgrades, almost all the core components and magical equipment units had been replaced by the most cutting-edge models. Even the most cutting-edge laboratories and magical equipment factories under the Glorious Sunlight Group had been established. According to Ding Lingdang’s specialties, a lot of components and weapons had been tailor-made for her. The giant soldier, which was as red as fire and burning furiously, boasted amazing comprehensive combat performance. Even though it had not reached the legendary level such as ‘Gold Sun’, ‘Armageddon’, and ‘Heart of Black Hole’, the God of Blood, the latest version of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow was quite competitive.

According to Ding Lingdang’s personality, she barely used any weapons, not to mention long-range weapons such as crystal railguns and storm bolters. Her favorite weapon was still her fists. Therefore, the toughest part of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow was its arms and fists, which were made of super alloys and designed with new double-helix spiritual energy channels.

Pure combat ability values could not be allocated to every part precisely. But over the past ten years, 50% of the upgrade and strengthening funds had been dedicated to the arms and the fists. According to the cost of military research and development of the federation, it was enough to arm an entire fleet of starships. It was evident how much Ding Lingdang valued her ‘main weapon’.

The so-called double-helix spiritual energy channel was inspired by the double-helix gene strands when it was designing the spiritual energy circuits of the two arms. Ding Lingdang’s fury and fighting will could be transmitted to her fists without being damaged at all. The iron fists of the Giant God Soldier would truly become her hands.

Not to mention that Li Yao had personally guided the adaptive modification of the ‘Great Fire Dragon Sparrow’ before they set off to explore the ancient relics. Adjusting the Giant God Soldier for his wife was even more important than adjusting the Giant God Soldier for himself. Even a component as tiny as sand had been adjusted to perfection and harmony.

In short, Ding Lingdang’s iron fist was the same as Yan Liren’s sword. Even the Immortal Cultivators in the Divinity Transformation Stage were unwilling to touch it.

He had thought that the furious punch condensed by Ding Lingdang could at least force the enemy to dodge or condense a spiritual shield to resist it, even if it could not blow the enemy into pieces.

He did not expect that the enemy would resist in such an unbelievable way!

CRACK! CRACK CRACK CRACK!

Under everyone’s horrified gaze, the time of stagnation finally passed by 0.1 seconds.

Not only was the fist of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow crushed, even the arm behind the fist was also shattered by Gu Wuxin’s immense strength. Starting from the bones, gears, and transmission structure in the deepest part of the fist, it exploded all the way out.

It was like an invisible super ore crusher had bitten the right arm of the Fire Dragon Sparrow with the hardest teeth and minced it into pieces.

Ding Lingdang’s grunt echoed in the communication channel.

“Honey!”

Li Yao bellowed, with thick blood leaking out of his eyes.

He was well aware of Ding Lingdang’s personality. As the toughest female warrior in the sea of stars, she would not cry out in pain even if she was being stabbed by thousands of blades.

She did not expect that even Ding Lingdang could not help but reveal such a weak side. It could be seen that the enemy had really dealt a destructive blow to her.

Although it was only the right arm of the Giant God Soldier that had been shattered, the soul of the user and the mainframe crystal processor of the Giant God Soldier were connected in the first place. The sensor chip of the Giant God Soldier was equivalent to the nerve endings of the user. For the sake of precise manipulation and ultimate release, countless sensor chips had been embedded in the right arm of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow. The explosion of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow’s right arm was no different from the shattering of Ding Lingdang’s own right arm. It was very likely that the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow’s future Cultivation would be seriously affected.

Of course, that was only if they had a ‘future’.

Li Yao subconsciously wanted to rush up and rescue Ding Lingdang.

However, he had been the primary target of thousands of ‘Gu Wuxin’s super bodies’ just now and had not recovered from the dizziness yet.

Ding Lingdang’s reaction was even faster than his. She was still attacking as if her life depended on it. Although her right arm was shattered, she concentrated all her remaining strength on the left fist of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow. In the short distance of several meters, her left fist was already thousands of degrees high. It was burnt into a translucent fireball and crashed into Gu Wuxin’s abdomen.

The left arm of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow pierced in unstoppably.

The extremely high temperature even made Gu Wuxin’s body turn crimson instantly, with crimson human-shaped ripples spreading out.

However, the punch that was powerful enough to burn his internal organs to ash and even crush his spine into powder did not change the scornful smile on his opponent’s face at all. It did not look like Ding Lingdang had pierced through Gu Wuxin’s internal organs and grabbed his opponent’s spine, but like Gu Wuxin biting the left arm of the big Fire Dragon Sparrow with his internal organs and spine.

Not good!

Li Yao suddenly realized that Ding Lingdang had made a fatal mistake.

Right now, Gu Wuxin was merely a human being, but it did not mean that he, who was made of countless terrifying cells, still retained the internal organs and spine of a normal human being.

Come to think of it, Gu Wuxin was almost fifty meters tall right now, which was three to five times higher than that of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan, if not higher than that of the fully-armed, super-heavy giant soldier. His mass was at least three to five thousand times that of a normal human being. If the density of his flesh was higher than that of super alloys and strengthened ceramic materials, his mass might be ten thousand times that of a normal human being.

The support structure of the internal organs and bones of human beings was designed for an object weighing around a hundred kilograms. It was definitely not enough to support an object weighing a hundred tons.

Therefore, although Gu Wuxin looked like a human being dozens of times larger than normal, his internal organs, muscles, and bones were definitely not normal.

Chances were that he was simply a cluster of multiple-cell aggregates, a piece of super-dense ‘meat’ that looked like a slug billions of times tensile. There was no telling how the cells were gathered.

Li Yao wanted to remind Ding Lingdang, but it was too late.

The flesh on Gu Wuxin’s abdomen wriggled weirdly, as if it had turned into a bloody mouth. After a crack, the left arm of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow was torn off!

His right arm exploded, and his left arm was torn off. The Fire Dragon Sparrow, which had lost its iron fists and arms, was like a T-rex that had lost its wings and claws.

BOOM!

Gu Wuxin punched the Fire Dragon Sparrow right in the chest.

Without the blockage of his arms, there was no strength that could stop Gu Wuxin from savaging the human beings as if they were toys.

Maybe, he could’ve used more advanced techniques, the kind with dazzling sound, light, and electricity, to achieve his purpose.

However, he would rather show the strength of his super body to all the human beings around him in the most straightforward, brutal, and furious way, like a hungry primitive man, and destroy the pride of mankind by blowing out the candles in a storm.

Li Yao opened his eyes and watched the breastplate of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow collapse as if it were made of paper. The spiritual residence of the user was twisted into weird shapes.

If there were veins in his eyes, all the veins would have exploded.

The Great Fire Dragon Sparrow was blown away like a kite whose string had been cut off. It crashed through three skyscrapers and did not sink into the fourth one until it collapsed.

“Honey!”

Li Yao didn’t care that his soul was still shaking. He held back the excruciating pain of his brain exploding and summoned his spiritual energy to the maximum, rescuing the broken Fire Dragon Sparrow before the skyscraper collapsed.

“Honey, are you alright? Answer me, Ding Lingdang. Answer me!”

Li Yao’s throat felt like it was being torn apart by a hacksaw. His voice was extremely coarse. Looking at the deeply dented breastplate of the Fire Dragon Sparrow, both his arms and those of the calamity of the sun were shivering. He did not dare to dismantle the twisted breastplate of the Fire Dragon Sparrow in case Ding Lingdang was hurt again.

“Cough cough, cough cough cough cough!”

Ding Lingdang was coughing hard in the communication channel and her extremely twisted body. “Ouch! Ouch! Son of a bitch! I’m going to kill him!”

“That’s good.”

Li Yao took a long breath in relief. The pores all over his body opened slightly.

With Ding Lingdang’s cultivation, her body wouldn’t be reduced to a mass of pus and blood on the spot, nor would her soul be burnt to ash. As long as she could breathe, even if her spine was broken, her nerves were seriously damaged, and her entire body was paralyzed, there wouldn’t be much of a problem. With sufficient medicine and time, as well as the appropriate cultivation technique, her fractured nerves and damaged tissues could always be repaired.

Chapter 3142: Terrifying!

Li Yao’s telepathic thoughts turned into invisible ripples and penetrated into the broken ‘Great Fire Dragon Sparrow’s’ spiritual residence, only to discover that Ding Lingdang had twisted her body into a twisted mess and was hiding in the weird-shaped gaps. She had barely escaped from the heavy pressure in such a way.

Even so, fractures, muscle ruptures, and internal bleeding were inevitable.

However, for Ding Lingdang, who had a “head as big as a bowl with a scar” on her face, this kind of “fatal injury” on the level of an ordinary person only slightly affected her anger when she was cursing.

His telepathic thoughts crawled into Ding Lingdang’s veins, nerves, limbs, and bones. He scanned her brain quickly and carefully and did not find any serious bleeding or concussion. Li Yao was finally relieved and dismantled the breastplate of the Fire Dragon Sparrow with his remote manipulation technique.

It was a great fortune that Ding Lingdang was fine.

However, the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow was completely scrapped. Not only had its arms, which carried the main weapon, been torn apart, its entire framework had been seriously damaged, and it was only one step away from being dismantled in the air. The mainframe crystal processor had also turned into a cluster of charred scrap metal. The Giant God Soldier, which had endured a hundred years of hardships, had already lost the value of maintenance. Besides, at the critical moment, where would it have the time to repair and modify?

Ding Lingdang did not bring a second Giant God Soldier.

Naturally, Li Yao would not allow her to fight Gu Wuxin while she was only wearing a crystal suit, no matter how furious Ding Lingdang was.

Besides, the moment Li Yao lunged at Ding Lingdang, the other human experts were infuriated by Gu Wuxin, or rather, their own fear, and surrounded the enemy with earsplitting roars.

Boss Bai and Yan Liren were the fastest. They were even faster than the toughest Immortal Cultivators of the Star Ocean Imperium, who were still suspicious and lucky when they were faced with such terrifying super bodies.

Before, Boss Bai and Yan Liren rarely had the opportunity to practice. But now that their spiritual flames and computational ability had both been brought to maximum, they were cooperating seamlessly like twins.

Boss Bai’s saber was aimed at Gu Wuxin’s neck, while Yan Liren’s longsword swept across Gu Wuxin’s waist, trying to cut him in half.

It seemed that they had all learned their lesson from Ding Lingdang and knew that Gu Wuxin’s body was definitely vastly different from that of human beings, if not from any known carbon-based intelligent life.

However, as long as his body was still made of cells, he would have to follow the general laws of flesh and blood. If his head, body, and legs were cut into three parts, his mobility and combat ability would be greatly undermined even if he was not killed.

Their moves were as marvelous as their ideas.

Boss Bai’s aura was as hazy as a bright moon. It was both vague and uncanny, as if he had slashed hundreds of times from hundreds of angles at the same time and condensed them at the last moment, or as if he had carved the hundreds of changes inside his body into the void after only one slash.

Yan Liren’s attack, on the other hand, seemed obedient and slow.

However, it was merely ‘appearing’.

His soul, his will, and the entire magnetic field of his life seemed to be attached to the long sword, making it a narrow gap that twisted time and space and could swallow and crush everything in its way. Even when other people focused their eyes on his sword, their soul would be absorbed by it.

—— Before his sword could chop off the enemy’s head, the enemy’s will had already been severed by the long sword, and his soul had already been swallowed by the long sword. This was the sword of ‘Sword Freak’ Yan Liren.

Even the well-acknowledged super experts of the Imperium of True Human Beings and the Holy Accord Alliance did not dare boast that they could avoid the joint attack of Boss Bai and Yan Liren without being hurt.

Even the top experts of the primeval era such as ‘Oblivion Sect’ and ‘Dragon Lotus Seed’ would have to concentrate all their fighting will to deal with Boss Bai and Yan Liren, the two ‘monsters’, if they were not killed miserably in the ‘ultimate test’.

But ‘Gu Wuxin’ was not a super expert of the Star Ocean Imperium or the Covenant Alliance, nor was he the strongest expert of the primeval era.

In the thousands of years after the Heavenly Palace was established, most of the experts living in seclusion were experts and scholars who focused on research and exploration. But for the exploration of the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the testing of new techniques, there were always some tough warriors.

As for the specialists and scholars of the primeval species, although they had neglected the training of their bodies, they were not so weak that they could catch a chicken.

Then, what was the limit of the combat ability of the super bodies created by the collective combat experience of thousands of primeval experts and the wisdom of the captives of the Flood Tide Legion?

No one knew, including Boss Bai and Yan Liren.

Everybody saw that a lot of soap-like bubbles were suddenly secreted on the surface of Gu Wuxin’s body, making him extremely slippery.

Then, the attack that Boss Bai and Yan Liren were so confident in slipped past Gu Wuxin’s body.

No. Not just ‘sliding’. Even the edge of the saber became mottled and incomplete the moment it passed by, as if it had been seriously corrupted and twisted by the space ripples.

The Pirate Potentate and the Sword Addict did not have time to change their moves. The two arms of the enemy were like two long whips that had grown exponentially. They tore the air and even the space apart and hit them heavily.

Pa! Pa!

The shoulders and breastplate of the two Giant God Soldiers had been smashed to pieces. Even their heads and arms were leaning to the side. The framework of the two Giant God Soldiers was about to be broken. Naturally, the riders inside were under the attack of the flood, too.

Boss Bai and Yan Liren’s grunts echoed in the communication channel at the same time. Based on their personalities, they would rather die than to be humiliated like this.

After only one clash, the two Giant God Soldiers were almost wasted and collapsed.

But ‘Heaven Crystal’, Long Yangjun’s giant soldier, appeared behind them.

The ‘crystal’ lived up to its name. It turned into a dazzling, shining super crystal.

Even the experts at the edge of the battlefield could clearly feel that Long Yangjun was like a super bomb that was getting closer and closer to the critical state. Her spiritual flames were burning, raging, and exploding at a visible speed, turning the several square kilometers nearby into her territory.

“It’s impossible!”

“How—how did she trigger such a strong spiritual reaction?”

“What level is she exactly? Is she really an ordinary Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivator, or—”

Many human experts exclaimed in disbelief, especially the Immortal Cultivators from the Imperium of True Human Beings. During the civil war of the imperium, Long Yangjun had been active on the black front between the electors and the revolutionaries as ‘Dongfang Mingyue’. She had been an opponent of many Immortal Cultivators.

At that time, all the Immortal Cultivators considered Long Yangjun to be an ordinary ‘president of the Demon Hunters’ Association’. However high their estimation of Long Yangjun’s capability was, they had never expected that she would be so tough.

“As it turned out, she did not reveal even one third of her capability under the pseudonym ‘Dongfang Mingyue’. It was not until this moment when she was faced with the terrifying ‘supernaturals’ that she finally showed her real skills!

Many Immortal Cultivators who had been enemies with Long Yangjun were sweating in fear.

However, a vague hope was raised in their hearts, too. They hoped that Long Yangjun could show his might and counterattack in a desperate situation, especially when they saw a light ball that was surrounded by electric arcs and looked like a supernova explosion in between the crystal arms of ‘Crystal’.

When the furious energy contained in the light ball tore apart the void and released spiritual waves unscrupulously, all the sensing magical equipment of the Giant God Soldiers were screaming, reminding their masters to raise their spiritual shields in time for the destructive attack.

However, the hope that had just popped up in the hearts of many experts was shattered by Gu Wuxin’s fist together with Long Yangjun’s light ball.

All the experts saw an absurd and unreal scene.

Faced with the terrifying light ball that Long Yangjun had unleashed by burning his soul and overdrafting his life, Gu Wuxin simply punched ‘Sky Crystal’ in the chest like a street gangster in a fight.

Then, he detonated Long Yangjun’s life force in advance and shattered the breastplate of the ‘crystal’, blowing the ‘crystal’ into pieces.

The sound of Long Yangjun vomiting blood was clearly audible in the communication channel.

The light ball that was detonated in advance, on the other hand, was kept within a hundred square meters and absorbed by the ocean of foam below his feet after only five seconds.

Gu Wuxin, who was floating out of the embers of the light ball, had all his skin burnt clean.

However, the ‘muscle fibers’ that used to be hidden inside his skin were now exposed to the public, making him even more hideous and disgusting.

Ding Lingdang, Boss Bai, Yan Liren, Long Yangjun—the four experts failed to hurt the enemy at all. They did not even manage to stop the enemy for one second before they paid a heavy price.

Even ‘Vulture Li Yao’, the so-called best expert of the human civilization, seemed to have suffered a minor loss in the mental confrontation just now and had not recovered yet.

The remaining human experts could not help but feel that their blood was freezing. Their movements were somewhat rigid and sluggish.

But Gu Wuxin did not stay where he was. He simply planned to stop them.

He took the initiative to attack the human experts. Li Jialing and Li Linghai were his first targets.

Over the thousands of years, with the wisdom of thousands of ancient experts, Gu Wuxin immediately realized that Li Jialing and Li Linghai were the soul of all the Immortal Cultivators of the imperium. As long as the two of them were defeated, the Immortal Cultivators would be like loose sand without a leader.

Chapter 3143: Weakness Found!

Li Jialing was a man who dared to charge at billions of stars by himself. He deeply believed that he had grabbed the throat of fate.

“I will never lower my head!”

Every hair on the golden lion’s body stood up. The golden flames of war were aimed at Gu Wuxin like ten thousand sharp blades. The young emperor of the Imperium of True Human Beings performed his strongest technique without any hesitation. The dominating aura of the emperor of the world was fully revealed.

“Emperor!”

Li Linghai loved his son very much. He released the technique ‘Purple Fire True Dragon, Emperor Flames Torrent’ to the maximum, too. Before the aftershock of Long Yangjun’s domain dispersed, the aura of the Emperor and the Empress Dowager surged out like a surging tide.

The other Immortal Cultivators of the imperium followed and charged at Gu Wuxin.

Their last bit of hope had been shattered by the invincibility of their enemy. When they were desperate and the Immortal Cultivators had no other options, they would not hesitate to sacrifice themselves, too.

However, their advantage in numbers did not allow them to achieve a better result than Boss Bai, Yan Liren, Long Yangjun, and Ding Lingdang.

Giant foam popped up from the ocean of cells below Gu Wuxin’s feet and exploded among the giant soldiers. The interference of the strong vitality magnetic field savaged the flesh and blood of all the Immortal Cultivators and destroyed their genes like tornadoes on the cellular level.

Many of them had their internal organs cooked by the interference of the magnetic field.

Their cells were decaying at a hundred times the normal speed, but their new cells had been seriously suppressed. Their growth was extremely slow, and their metabolism was disrupted.

Needless to say, the data on the operation menu was bouncing crazily, too.

Like a hungry tiger pouncing on a lamb, Gu Wuxin dashed into the battle formation made of dozens of Giant God Soldiers. His arms turned into two grey trajectories, and wherever they went, the Giant God Soldiers collapsed with earsplitting noises. Some of their bones were broken, and some of their spiritual residences exploded. Even the riders inside were beaten to the point that they were vomiting blood or even exploded into pools of blood.

Li Jialing had only held on for one second in front of Gu Wuxin, but he had paid the price of one of the giant soldier gods’ left legs and fell out. The golden lion had lost all its prestige, and the Purple Fire True Dragon was eclipsed.

Li Linghai was not at an advantage, either. Although the limbs of the giant soldier were intact, the core transmission structure between his waist and his abdomen had been seriously damaged due to the violent vibrations. Sparks were popping up, and he was cramping uncontrollably, which was a major influence on his mobility and combat ability.

Hum! Hum! Hum!

Seeing that the Immortal Cultivators had been blown into pieces by Gu Wuxin like frozen eggplants, the battle puppets under the control of the Fist King, Xiao Ming, and Wen Wen, as well as the army of mini drones, hurried to fill the gaps between them, while the iron torrent surged close like a living tide.

“Hoooo!”

Gu Wuxin did not even bother to look at them. He suddenly opened his mouth, which was literally a bloody mouth. His entire face had been torn apart. The corner of his mouth was all the way to the back of his head. His lower jaw had even melded with his chest, in order to expose his throat as much as possible. No. It was more like a channel of destruction than a throat. After an earsplitting explosion, the ripples from Gu Wuxin’s abdomen spread to dozens of kilometers nearby in the form of visible ripples.

Not only were the battle puppets and the army of mini drones seriously jammed, they were also falling to the ground. The crystal processors of the Fist King, Little Ming, and Wen Wen were all screaming. The three ‘people’ were dazed and frozen. All the Cultivators and Immortal Cultivators nearby had a splitting headache. They felt that their internal organs were on fire, and they were in a living hell.

In only three to five minutes, the strongest experts of the new generation of human beings had experienced the greatest setback and shock in their life.

‘Gu Wuxin—Transcendent’ suppressed Ding Lingdang, Boss Bai, Yan Liren, Li Jialing, Li Linghai, Long Yangjun, the Fist King, and so on all by himself. The federation, the imperium, and the renowned experts of the Covenant Alliance made the arrogant experts realize the horror of ‘primeval’.

Everyone came to a bitter realization.

If this was really a siege, they were not the ones who had the advantage in numbers.

At first, they thought that the dozens of Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators, Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators, and Soul Splitting Cultivators on their side were attacking a primeval expert.

But as it turned out, the thousands of members of the Heavenly Palace who had joined the super bodies were attacking them together.

However, the ghosts shared one of Gu Wuxin’s bodies. However, with the continuous support of the ocean of cells, how strong, terrifying, and weird could the body become?

While they were panicking, intense roars echoed inside their heads.

It was not the soundwaves that could shake one’s eardrums, but the waves that could stimulate one’s brain cells directly. The source of the soundwaves was too fast for the soundwaves to catch up with. The soaring fighting will, on the other hand, assaulted the souls of all the experts at the speed of light.

It was Li Yao!

He finally recovered from the shock of his soul.

The ‘sun catastrophe’ of the Giant God Soldier appeared above Gu Wuxin’s head like the catastrophe caused by the arrival of a star.

The image of him slashing down from the sky with a saber in his hands seemed to have travelled through ten thousand years and overlapped with that of the ‘Supreme Emperor’, the best expert of the human civilization ten thousand years ago. It was deeply etched in the void and in the heart of every expert.

“Chi!”

Li Yao’s saber slashed into the center of Gu Wuxin’s head precisely and unstoppably, cutting the enemy into two halves.

As he expected, there was no blood gushing out, no internal organs exploding, and no broken bones. The only thing that was moving was a lot of tissues that looked like snakes.

Instead of saying that Li Yao split a ‘giant’ from head to toe, it was more accurate to say that he split a giant starfish.

And this was far from the end.

Swish! Swish! Swish! Swish!

Right when Li Yao’s saber ripped open Gu Wuxin’s body and a shocking wound appeared, hundreds of meat sprouts drilled out of the two sides of his body and darted to the opposite side like elastic springs, reconnecting the two halves of his body.

It seemed that the flesh and blood of such a brand-new structure boasted amazing self-healing ability, like the tentacles of a starfish or the internal organs of a sea cucumber. Without interference, he—he could even be restored to his original state in half a minute.

How could Li Yao let him have his way?

The picture of Ding Lingdang crawling out of the broken spiritual residence of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow covered in blood popped up in his head again. The flames bursting out of Li Yao’s nostrils were thousands of degrees hotter than before. The ‘sun catastrophe’ had emptied all the long-range weapons, but it did not stop him from extending his fingers and piercing them into Gu Wuxin’s torn body, unleashing the most furious and the purest destructive energy.

Two dazzling light balls appeared in the palm of the ‘sun catastrophe’ instantly and expanded to almost a hundred meters in diameter in 0.1 seconds.

Despite the protection of the Giant God Soldier, the crystal suit, and the mustard suit, all the human experts couldn’t help but squint their eyes. The muscles and nerves all over their body constricted to the minimum. It was their natural instinct to protect themselves when they were faced with unimaginable strength.

Is this the best that an expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage can do when he is infuriated?

Even an asteroid could be destroyed so easily!

What kind of monster is Li Yao exactly? He seems to be even stronger than before he entered the primeval relics. Even Wu Yingqi, the Black Star Emperor, might not have been able to concentrate the terrifying damage in such a small space when he was at his peak!

“Black Star Emperor is not the only one. Such high-energy reactions, such dominance, and such an aura indicate that he is the Supreme Emperor from ten thousand years ago. He has indeed received the legacy of the Supreme Emperor!”

At this moment, Li Yao became the last hope in everyone’s heart.

Although the light balls and blast he created only covered an area of a hundred square meters, all the super experts beyond the Nascent Soul Stage knew that the so-called ‘range of destruction’ was the same as sound and photoelectricity. The larger and brighter it was, the easier it would be. The smaller and quieter it was, the harder it was to reach the sky.

A Thunder-Palms strike that could cover an area of a thousand square meters was something that even a Nascent Soul Stage Cultivator could do. With the enhancement of a crystal suit, even a Core Formation Stage Cultivator might be able to give it a try. But as to how lethal it could be in every cubic meter, it was all up to fate.

But Li Yao, on the other hand, had created a temperature and a high-energy reaction that were even higher than that of the sun within a hundred square meters. He was even involved in the techniques of nuclear fission and fusion. That was what truly mattered!

Gu Wuxin’s painful roars finally echoed inside the crimson light ball.

Even the overwhelming ocean of cells nearby was shrieking and cramping. The tentacles that were dancing crazily extended to the sky one after another, as if they had been assaulted by powerful electric arcs. The ends of the tentacles exploded one after another, spurting thick, stinky liquids.

Very soon, large black spots appeared on the gray ocean of cells. Wherever the black spots went, all the cells withered. Even the four invincible devils were drained of all their vitality and turned into emaciated zombies.

It was as if… the ocean of cells was dying.

It seemed that, although Gu Wuxin had been bragging about the ‘super network’ and the ‘cloud calculation’ for a long time, their tiny super bodies still had a mainframe inside Gu Wuxin’s body.

Without the control of the mainframe, the entire ocean of cells immediately went out of control. All the cells collapsed and perished.

Maybe, the real super bodies in the sea of the multiverse were not so bad. But the so-called ‘super body’ in front of everyone was just a small, clumsy ‘counterfeit’. How could it be invincible without any weakness?

Chapter 3144: Forced Link

“Li Yao, kill him!”

“Li Yao, hang in there!”

“Li Yao, I have pinned all my hopes on you. You are… a distraction!”

All the experts clenched their fists and bulged their eyes, as if they were trying to pull their soul out of their body and throw it into the light ball to help Li Yao.

Although they were divided into different sides and had different opinions about Li Yao, they had to admit that Li Yao was the last defense line for the self-proclaimed experts such as themselves. If one of their enemies could not defeat Li Yao even after Li Yao tried his best, nobody would be able to.

In the past, Li Yao had defeated enemies ten times stronger than himself in his… creative ways and his own style. It should be the same this time.

The core of the super body was inside Gu Wuxin’s body. As long as Gu Wuxin was destroyed, the overwhelming foam—the ocean of cells—would be frozen and withered, too. That was what was happening right now.

They could only wait, helplessly, uneasily, and almost desperately.

It was a pity that the enemy they were faced with this time was fundamentally different from all the previous ones.

The black spots were about to join together and wither the ocean of cells. Even the tentacles that extended to the sky were all broken and vaporized like broken gene strands. However, when the area of the black spots was about to break the threshold, they gradually slowed down and finally stopped.

The giant light ball that Li Yao had ignited with his life and soul was emitting weird blue colors. It gradually formed a transparent shell and was even shrinking slightly.

Experienced Cultivators all knew that it was the sign of the collapse and contraction of a large-scale magnetic field with high-energy reactions, which meant that Li Yao was already on the brink of death.

“Li Yao!”

Ding Lingdang screamed desperately. She was planning to charge at Gu Wuxin without caring about anything else with a mere crystal suit, only to be pushed down by several Cultivators from the federation and held back by them.

Riding the broken ‘crystal core’, Long Yangjun was on one of his knees, trying to raise his broken arms and condense new blast waves. But after multiple attempts, the Giant God Soldier’s elbows, shoulders, and waist exploded into enormous fireballs, which eventually paralyzed him.

Boss Bai, Yan Liren, the boxing champion, Li Jialing, Li Linghai… Everyone’s attempts had all failed. They could only watch the ‘shell’ covering the light ball peel off and shatter.

Inside the magnetic field of annihilation, Li Yao’s burning soul fire was almost nonexistent.

The only proof that he had just released terrifying damage was the round crater on the ground that was as smooth as a mirror. Colorful stripes were blossoming on the surface of the crater, indicating that it had been glazed and crystallized.

And above the crater…

Gu Wuxin was indeed heavily wounded.

His skin and the flesh on the surface of his body had been burnt to the ground. His body was not entirely blackened. Instead, it was as colorful and translucent as the crater. He seemed to have been sent into a furnace and burnt for forty-nine days in a temperature of almost ten thousand degrees.

Even the lacerations caused by Li Yao from top to bottom could not be fully recovered. From the top of his head to the tip of his nose, from the tip of his nose to his chest, and from his chest to his waist, there was a shocking scar. The scar was deeply dented, as if his body was still divided into two halves and was merely connected by hundreds of granules.

In this form, Gu Wuxin had completely shed the features of ‘Nuwa Clan’ or ‘Human Beings’. He was more like a deity from a different universe.

Although Gu Wuxin did not release his apocalyptic aura on purpose, the hearts of the human experts were still heavy.

It was because the sharp claws on the end of Gu Wuxin’s right arm, which was dozens of meters long, were grabbing Li Yao and his Giant God Soldier, ‘Solar Calamity’!

Gu Wuxin was so strong that his five claws pierced into the head of the ‘sun catastrophe’ and crushed most of the sensors.

The head of the Giant God Soldier was the focus of 50% of the sensory magical equipment on its body. Although the head explosion was not fatal, its combat ability had dropped by at least 30%. Many operators of the Giant God Soldier would rather sacrifice an arm than expose their head to the enemy’s firepower.

However, right now, the ‘sun catastrophe’ seemed to be paralyzed. Its limbs and loins were dangling loosely. Suspicious liquids were flowing out of the joints and the super-compressed crystal reactor. It did not respond to the savaging of the enemy at all.

“Li Yao!”

Ding Lingdang, Boss Bai, Long Yangjun, Li Jialing… All the experts felt that their hearts were being torn apart by Gu Wuxin’s sharp claws, too.

There was nothing but dead silence in the communication channel. There was no sign of Li Yao at all. There was no telling whether it was because he was caught in the enemy’s mental bombardment or because the communication of the Giant God Soldier was seriously jammed and suppressed.

“Do you feel it? This is the power of the super bodies.”

Gu Wuxin continued savaging the head of the ‘sun catastrophe’ unscrupulously and sent a telepathic thought that sounded like a broadcast to the human experts. “In only thousands of years, thousands of researchers and experts in the Heavenly Palace have gathered together and boasted such strength. Can you imagine how strong the real super bodies are outside of the Pangu Universe? Can you resist them?

“Give it up. If I’m no match for you, you are not qualified to be the enemy of the super body civilization at all. I don’t understand what you are still persisting for. It is meaningless, utterly meaningless!

“Come on. Join us. Let’s share all the strength, wisdom, and memories inside our super bodies. All the strength that I demonstrated just now can be shared with you. We will be one. I will be you, and you will be me. We will create a new world without any conflicts, deception, or contradictions together!

“Shut up, you monster!”

Ding Lingdang took off the facial cover of her crystal suit, revealing an infuriated face. She spat a mouthful of blood at Gu Wuxin and said, “We will not join your damn super body even if we die!”

“Then, as you wish.”

Gu Wuxin smiled. The smile on his colorful face, which seemed to be made of ceramic and crystal, was extremely weird. “However, it was not death but rebirth.

His arms, which were dozens of meters long, waved quickly in midair and threw Li Yao and his ‘sun catastrophe’ away. They crashed into more than ten bizarre-shaped piles of wasted iron and copper with earsplitting noises.

In the meantime, the withering and collapsing ocean of cells was ten times more vigorous than before. Hundreds of glittering tentacles extended out of the black spots and stabbed at almost a hundred Giant God Soldiers.

Most of the soldiers had lost part of their mobility in the previous battle.

The framework and transmission structure of many Giant God Soldiers suffered from different degrees of explosion. Even the connection between the upper body and the lower body was out of control. They were essentially ‘paralyzed’ or ‘body paralyzed’. There was no way that they could avoid the sudden tentacles.

The tentacles were not so much the tentacles of super-large octopuses as the nerve endings that were billions of times larger. Once they tied up a Giant God Soldier, they would immediately divide and proliferate crazily. Very soon, the complicated neural network covered every piece of plate armor of the Giant God Soldier. It even leaked into the internal organs of the Giant God Soldier through the gaps and the wounds, passing through the crystal suit and the mustard suit without any trouble.

“Ah!”

Many experts lost their cool when they saw the vipers and nematodes crawling into their spiritual residences.

The colorful, unusually bright ‘neurons’, while releasing paralyzing and hallucinogenic bioelectricity, slithered toward the nostrils, ears, and mouth of the user and crawled into the body of the expert like lightning.

Many experts were struggling hard, trying to drag the hateful bugs out of their bodies.

But with the assault of the ‘neurons’, an astronomical amount of information flooded into their brain like a tsunami. Everything that Li Yao had experienced just now was replayed in the minds of many experts.

Besides, because of the ‘wireless transmission’ just now and the ‘wired transmission’ now, the intensity of the bombardment was ten times higher!

Even Long Yangjun, whose body was the most special among all the experts, did not have an ‘Earth’ deep inside his brain, nor did he have mental power and mental defense like Li Yao did.

Faced with Gu Wuxin’s ‘invitation’, they had no strength to refuse.

All the experts were roaring in excruciating pain. Their roars gradually turned dull and feeble, as if they had fallen into a nightmare that they could never escape from. The fall in the endless abyss had just begun.

Whether they were willing or not, almost all the human experts and the surviving researchers of the Heavenly Palace were tied up, covered, and corrupted by the weird nerves that crawled out of the ocean of cells and connected to a super-large neural network.

More and more gray-brown cells crawled up the legs of the giant soldier like a carpet of fungi that could split and proliferate infinitely and gradually swallowed one giant soldier after another.

BOOM! CRACK!

As if echoing with Gu Wuxin’s strength, thunder was echoing in the sky of the primeval relics, which had remained unchanged for billions of years. Colorful clouds were gathering, and dazzling lightning was chasing and entangling each other like a neural network in the form of energy. When the neural network of the universe was gradually torn apart, a giant gap was torn open in the sky of the primeval relics, through which the brightness of the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ was projected into the distance.

“Finally—”

Gu Wuxin looked at the sky and said solemnly, “It’s time.”

Chapter 3145: Resonance Of The Soul!

“Li Yao! Li Yao!”

He heard someone calling him.

But the excruciating pain that could not be described with words was like a super-heavy mountain hundreds of thousands of meters high pressing him into an endless abyss. His cells, flesh, blood, soul, and soul were all locked, ground, burnt, crushed, and swallowed.

For a moment, he could not remember who he was, where he was, what he was doing, what he was fighting for, or even whether he was alive or dead. If this was the deepest level of hell, then such a dark and painful hell was too cruel.

He only knew that he had truly suffered a crushing defeat this time. There was no way for him to fight back, not even the slightest chance for him to struggle.

Such pain, such desperation, and such a crushing defeat seemed to have happened a long, long time ago in a place far, far away. It was…

“Run, Li Yao. It’s impossible for you to defeat it. The Vulture Plan has failed. It has completely failed. No one can defeat it. No one can destroy ‘Earth’. Run. Go and live a few carefree days before it catches up to you. Run! Run!”

He heard such a sound.
