The desperate voice from the past and far away, together with the voice from the present and his ears, pierced into his heart like an unstoppable blade and woke him up.

“No…”

He let out his first unclear roar.

“No!”

The second roar became clearer and louder.

“No! No! No! No! No! No! No!”

Like an infuriated primitive man, he waved the torch that had just been extinguished by the storm and roared at the vast and deep sky.

“From the center of the multiverse to the Pangu Universe, and from Earth to this place. I am carrying the hope of billions of reincarnations of my companions. I still have unfinished business. The ‘Vulture Plan’ is far from over. I will not admit defeat just like that. I will not allow you, this little monster, or the big monster in the multiverse sea, to ravage and swallow my companions, my compatriots, my family, my home, and my civilization!

“No matter how many mistakes we have made, no matter whether we are righteous or evil, bright or dark, wise or stupid, we are absolutely free. We will use our own will to decide the path that we want to walk on. Even if this path is full of thorns and flames, even if this path leads to a burning abyss, even if 108,000 gods and devils appear in the higher worlds above the multiverse and block our path, no one will be able to change our determination, our will, and our path—because this is the true meaning of ‘life’!

“We are not cells, not puppets, not walking dead. We are human beings, human beings who will explore the truth of the universe and themselves whatever the cost!

“I’m going to stop you. I won’t let you finish the teleportation even if I have to die a hundred times, a thousand times, and ten thousand times!”

Li Yao’s soul seemed to be a shining eagle carved out of marrow crystals. It jumped out of the information turbulence and data abyss that Gu Wuxin had set up and flapped its wings, roaring in his head.

Li Yao regained control over his body and the Giant God Soldier.

Although 50% of the sensor chips of the ‘sun catastrophe’ had exploded, the framework and the transmission structure were seriously twisted, and the plate armor was falling off, the flames, spiritual waves, and twisted force fields that it released turned into visible ‘domains’ and caused bright gold and crimson energy ripples dozens of meters around Li Yao.

Li Yao scanned his surroundings.

The entire battlefield could only be described as miserable.

The sea of foam made of thousands of cells of ferocious beasts was almost solid. However, hundreds of tentacles suddenly crawled out of the ‘underwater’ and pierced through almost all the Giant God Soldiers brutally, as if they were made of super alloys that carried super electric currents.

Billions of hair-thin, glittering, nerve-like rays of light were shot out from the tentacles and interweaved with every Giant God Soldier and every pilot, trapping everyone inside the network, including Li Yao himself.

Countless nerves soared into the sky and extended to the sky of the primeval relics before they congregated in Gu Wuxin’s body.

Colorful light waves were shining on the shell of Gu Wuxin’s body, which looked like a cocoon. Every light wave was made of a swirl of information and data. He looked down and did not even bother to look at Li Yao, but his attention was focused on the rainbow bridge that soared into the sky.

Gu Wuxin flew toward the rainbow bridge and opened his arms. Rainbow-like ripples were spreading out of his body, indicating that he was about to meld with the rainbow bridge.

“I will not let you go. I will drag you down with me!”

Li Yao gritted his teeth and endured the pain of his bones being broken. He opened and closed his hands, and a skyscraper dozens of floors high suddenly exploded into billions of stones and nails mixed with Li Yao’s furious flames, which darted at Gu Wuxin in midair.

“Huh?”

Gu Wuxin frowned slightly, as if he did not expect that a tiny ‘ant’ such as Li Yao could still fight back. He sniffed and waved his hands casually. The torrent of stones and nails that was still in the air immediately hit an invisible iron wall and rolled back at a speed three times higher than before. They all hit the ‘sun catastrophe’, making the Giant God Soldier, which was already riddled with holes, even more shattered and on the verge of collapse.

In the meantime, miserable redness was beaming out of the thousands of nerves that tied up the ‘sun catastrophe’ and transmitted endless pain into Li Yao’s brain.

It was like Li Yao had endured the pain of all the ‘experimental subjects’ over the past thousands of years at the same time. He had been tortured thousands of times in only several seconds.

However, the excruciating pain did not make Li Yao yield. Instead, the ‘Gate of Earth’ deep inside his brain was showing more and more obvious cracks, which reminded him of more things about ‘Earth’ and ‘reincarnation’. His mind was purer and his will more tenacious.

“Ahhh, compared to everything I’ve experienced on Earth, what is this pain worth? It’s just—it’s just sitting in the cool summer afternoon eating ice cream. Ahhhhh, it hurts, it hurts, it hurts, it hurts!”

Li Yao roared, screamed, punched, twitched, and pissed his pants.

“Li Yao!”

Right then, Ding Lingdang’s voice rang in his ears again.

Even his wife’s face, which was filled with hope and anger, was flashing inside his brain.

Li Yao was dazed for a moment, thinking that he was hallucinating.

“Li Yao!”

Then, Long Yangjun’s voice and appearance appeared.

“Huh?”

Li Yao was startled. “How did you pop out of my brain? It’s very likely to cause a misunderstanding. Just go back! Go back!”

“Li Yao!”

Boss Bai, Lei Chenghu, Li Jialing, Li Linghai, the boxing champion, Yan Liren… The consciousness of all his family, friends, comrades, and compatriots was swirling and echoing inside his brain.

“Dad!”

Even Xiao Ming and Wen Wen turned into two pitiful children and extended their hands towards him in his mind.

Li Yao finally understood.

Whether they liked it or not, the link of the super bodies had already begun.

Gu Wuxin planned to combine their souls in such a way so that he could read the memories deep inside their brains and send all the information to somewhere else in the universe.

After all, Gu Wuxin had been hibernating in the primeval relics for hundreds of thousands of years and did not know much about the recent situation of the Pangu Universe. The latest intelligence from the brains of human beings would be more helpful for the invasion of the ‘superhuman civilization’.

In other words, Li Yao, Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, and everyone else’s brain, mind, and soul were all in a certain ‘superlink’ state. They were about to be drowned in the ocean of information and data and lose themselves.

Hearing the calls of his family, friends, and children, Li Yao felt as if a bolt of lightning had struck his brain and left a dazzling trace.

“Everybody’s telepathic link has been established. There is no way to stop it unless we kill Gu Wuxin!

“Since we can’t stop him, can we seize the last opportunity and give him a taste of his own medicine?

“The strength of the mind is what mankind is best at. If we can concentrate all our mental power, can we launch a fatal attack at Gu Wuxin?

“Let me see. Let me see. Right, ‘Soul War Resonance’. The resonance of souls from the Flying Star Sector and Iron Plateau can allow an ordinary Refinement Stage Cultivator to break through the barrier between the Refinement Stage and the Building Foundation Stage and reach the 100th level of the Refinement Stage, the 1,000th level, and the 10,000th level of the Refinement Stage, thereby becoming the resonance of the souls of the qi-trainers!

“Listen to me!”

Through the ‘superlink’, Li Yao shouted to all his companions, “We still have one last chance. The enemy is not invincible, but I need your help—I need your anger, your fighting will, your determination, and your hope. Come on. Gather your courage and burn your soul. Release your fire of life to your heart’s content! Since the enemy wants us to join his network, let’s see if his so-called ‘superbody’ can accommodate the proudest soul of mankind!”

Chapter 3146: Last Resort

Gu Wuxin was trying to drag all the human experts into his super body.

The unavoidable side effect was that the brains of the human experts were connected and melded together first. The speed of information interaction and data transmission was hundreds of times higher than before!

‘Battle Soul Resonance’ was not an unpredictable technique. Otherwise, the qi-trainers on Iron Plateau would not have considered it a fundamental Cultivation art that was universally available on the planet.

With the Cultivation and understanding of the experts who had participated in the exploration of the ancient relics, when Li Yao transmitted the secret art of ‘resonance of soul’ to their brain, they had finished the analysis almost instantly and grasped how to utilize it.

“Li Yao!”

“Li Yao!”

“Li Yao!”

Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, the boxing champion… Everybody gritted their teeth and fought against the information ocean that was falling from the sky. They poured the last bit of their soul power into Li Yao through the ‘neurons’.

“AHHHHHHHHHHHHH!”

Li Yao roared earthshakingly again. All the veins on his body were bulging. Then, the nerves buried deep below his muscle fibers were glittering in various colors, too, as if they had been decorated with glamorous tattoos.

Scorching flames were flowing out of his nostrils, his mouth, his ears, and even his pores. His muscular body was wreathed in steam, and he felt that it was melting. He had truly sacrificed his body of flesh and blood to annihilate countless cells and transform them into the purest energy.

“Earth… Shock Wave!”

Li Yao’s fingers almost pierced into his brain as he sent a stream of information to Gu Wuxin in the sky.

This move had once fought against the super artificial intelligence ‘Fuxi’ and blocked the enemy’s entire thinking module with endless garbage information from Earth.

Right now, Li Yao was hoping to stuff Gu Wuxin’s head in the same way.

Gu Wuxin’s logic circuits and thinking modules, on the other hand, were more advanced than those of the Fuxis. Or rather, he was a real life form and not a rigid program. When he discovered that Li Yao had sent an extremely weird stream of super-compressed information to him, he immediately realized that something was wrong and cut off the ‘neurons’ between him and Li Yao, thereby cutting off the possibility of Li Yao bombarding him with the ‘Earth Data Package’.

Gu Wuxin’s reaction was not slow at all.

However, such a straightforward approach disrupted the mental connection between him and Li Yao temporarily. He finally lost control over Li Yao and the other human experts for half a second.

In that half second—

BOOM!

Li Yao had almost burnt his own body of flesh and blood. The blast he released had shattered the plate armor and shell of the ‘sun catastrophe’. The scraps were blossoming, spurting, and spluttering everywhere. The emaciated giant soldier did not carry any weapons. Even his spiritual residence was exposed to the air. Li Yao’s true self, which was about to turn into a ‘pure body of energy’, could be seen clearly.

“Li Yao, bring my fighting will and punch the monster hard. Beat all his treasures out!”

Ding Lingdang screamed at the top of her lungs. Her soul was transmitted to Li Yao like visible waves.

“Li Yao, the dignity of the Pirate Potentate depends on you. Come on. This is my strength. Take it!”

Boss Bai was also shouting among the broken soldiers.

“Brother Yao, this is the infinite glory of the imperium. Please!”

Bright gold flames spurted out of Li Jialing’s seven orifices and resonated with Li Yao.

All the other experts sent out their strongest souls, their firmest will, and their most furious fury to Li Yao’s side like surging rivers.

Huala! Huala! Huala! Huala!

The sound of rushing rivers and surging tides came from the air.

Hum! Hum! Hum! Hum! Hum!

All the sabers and swords that had been knocked to the ground by Gu Wuxin were roaring as if they were being pulled by an invisible force.

Hiu! Hiu! Hiu! Hiu! Hiu!

When the flames that Li Yao ignited at the cost of his flesh and blood soared to the maximum and pierced through the sky, resonating with his family, friends, and compatriots, the hundreds of super-large sabers and swords that were specially designed for the Giant God Soldiers turned into hundreds of streaks of brightness and were all absorbed by Li Yao’s vitality magnetic field. They span and collided with each other rapidly, turning into an unstoppable metal storm.

Even the scraps of the Giant God Soldier were attracted by Li Yao. They were broken down into the most fundamental components on their way and assembled into brand-new units. The units were then attached to the skeleton of the ‘sun catastrophe’ and turned into new limbs, power units, and attack units.

The essence of dozens of Giant God Soldiers had been perfectly melded into the ‘Solar Catastrophe’.

The experts of the human civilization gathered their most sincere fighting will into Li Yao’s soul.

“We are… the masters of our own wisdom, memories, emotions, hearts, and will!

Li Yao’s face was covered in interweaving, glittering veins and nerves. His eyes were so bright that they could burn through the entire universe. He seemed to be talking to himself, to all his compatriots and comrades, and even more so to Gu Wuxin and the enemies beyond the Pangu Universe. “The flesh and the blood and the mysterious mind are our territory. We are the kings of this tiny territory, the gods of this tiny world, and the supreme masters of our own ‘minor universe’!

“The three armies can seize the commander-in-chief position, but the will of an ordinary person cannot be seized. No matter what kind of excuse, no matter how high-sounding the name, no matter how powerful the forces that can conquer and destroy the entire universe are, they can forget about devouring our will, our life, and our civilization.

Let’s try this. Our last strength!

Li Yao, his giant soldier, and the storm of brightness made of almost a hundred saber and chainswords were all gone.

In the next second, next to the rainbow bridge that soared into the sky, a golden pillar of light almost the same width appeared. Outside of the golden pillar of light was a helical, rising crimson brightness.

The golden pillar of light and the crimson brightness rushed at Gu Wuxin precisely.

It was too fast for Gu Wuxin to dodge. In a hurry, he could only set up dozens of spiritual shields in front of him, only to be blown into tiny ripples within 0.1 seconds.

With everyone’s hope, anger, and hope, Li Yao crashed into Gu Wuxin in the ‘sun catastrophe’, which was a brand-new evolution.

The blast destroyed all the skyscrapers within dozens of square kilometers like a tsunami.

Most of the Giant God Soldiers and the crystal suits had been pressed into the ground and were seriously deformed.

The human experts were so shocked that their internal organs were displaced, their brains were shaking, and they were vomiting blood.

All the Anti-Ship Swords, the super-large chainswords, and the sharp scraps pierced into Gu Wuxin’s body without wasting any.

The force of Li Yao’s collision was so immense that a lot of the broken parts of the ‘sun catastrophe’ were embedded into Gu Wuxin’s body. The two of them became inseparable.

Li Yao’s brain went blank. There was only one thought in his mind. He could not let Gu Wuxin get close to the Bifrost Bridge. He had to push him out as far away as possible.

“I’ll take it!”

Li Yao’s body and soul power turned into the strongest impetus in the form of flames and flames, pushing Gu Wuxin all the way to the dome of the ancient relic.

“AHHHHHHHHH!”

This time, it was Gu Wuxin’s turn to shout in shock and fury. He did not expect that the sealed human beings could wield such tremendous strength when they were fighting for their lives.

Suddenly, Li Yao and Gu Wuxin both felt that their bodies were lighter. They felt that their heads were up and their feet were down.

Their surroundings turned dark and cold. A thin layer of frost quickly formed on their shells and bodies.

Gu Wuxin’s screams, Li Yao’s roars, and the sounds of the power units of the Giant God Soldier exploding one after another were all drowned in the dead silence.

It was true that Li Yao’s collision of heaven and earth had blown Gu Wuxin out of the primeval relics and into the universe through the gaps on the rainbow bridge.

If Gu Wuxin were an ordinary intelligent creature with a carbon-based body that could only survive in an environment with abundant oxygen and normal temperature, he would’ve been killed instantly.

Unfortunately, he was not.

“Is this your trump card?”

Gu Wuxin smiled. The shell of the shell that looked like a scab wriggled weirdly and grew a structure similar to scales and shells. The vacuum could block his voice, but it could not block the brainwaves that he sent to Li Yao in mockery. “Not a bad trick. It has indeed caused me some damage. If the attack of such a level can last another twenty to thirty hours, it might be able to annihilate me completely.

“However, can your body, your soul, and your broken toy survive another twenty to thirty hours?”

Li Yao vomited a mouthful of blood, which was mixed with his burnt internal organs. His vision was blurred, and his soul gradually collapsed.

“Chi!”

The broken saber that had just been embedded into Gu Wuxin’s body was pushed out by him and pierced into the ‘sun catastrophe’, almost cutting Li Yao in half.

Even in the dark universe and the cold vacuum, Gu Wuxin’s wounds were healing at a visible speed.

Although he was still suppressed by Li Yao, his strength was recovering bit by bit. Li Yao, on the other hand, was on the verge of a total collapse. There was not a single dagger or bullet in his body that he could use.

However, it was impossible to kill the enemy by crashing into them in the vacuum of the universe.

Is this… the end?

Li Yao really tried his best. A black haze was popping up in his heart. “Is this our destination, the destination of human civilization?”

“Chi!”

The second sharp blade was pushed out by Gu Wuxin. This time, it pierced into Li Yao’s abdomen through his spiritual residence. Li Yao’s body, which had almost been burnt to the ground, felt no pain at all.

“Li Yao!”

Right then, Lu Qingchen’s thunderous voice echoed in Li Yao’s ears. “Look over there! Look over there! Look over there!”

“What?”

Li Yao’s consciousness became more and more fuzzy. Stimulated by Lu Qingchen, he subconsciously looked in the direction indicated by his old enemy.

He saw it.

He saw the magnificent yet magnificent super-large planet not far away from him and Gu Wuxin.

Ancient superstar.

Chapter 3147: Time To Update The Data

Right now, the ancient star was right in front of him. Its enormous size was even more obvious, as if it had swallowed more than half of the universe and overturned the universe, making Li Yao, Lu Qingchen, the solar catastrophe, Gu Wuxin, and the ‘supernaturals’ fall toward it.

The entrance and exit of the ancient relics were not in the orbit of the ancient giants, but in the orbit of one of their satellites. The distance between the two was quite large. Li Yao had drifted in space for a long time before he reached the entrance of the ancient relics.

But right now, because of the relative movements of the entrances and exits of the relics, the satellites, and the ancient giants, they had reached the point closest to each other. It was not an exaggeration to say that they could pluck the stars with their hands.

It was certainly not a coincidence.

No matter how advanced and mysterious the technology of Gu Wuxin’s ‘Rainbow Bridge’ was, it was still a teleportation array after all. It was impossible to tear apart the stable three-dimensional universe and transmit information billions of lightyears away without consuming enough energy.

Of course, a lot of energy was stored inside the primeval relics. But Li Yao believed that, if he could make use of the ‘tidal force’ between the ancient star and its satellite, he would be able to help the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ break through the threshold, tear apart the sea of stars, and cross the universe faster.

Therefore, it was the time and coordinates that Gu Wuxin had calculated, based on the astronomical data from hundreds of thousands of years ago.

After all, compared to the universe and astronomy, hundreds of thousands of years was just a moment. Half a second was no different.

For a moment, Li Yao thought of many things. Then, he couldn’t help but laugh.

So that was the case.

As it turned out, this was the reason why the other half of the Fuxi Clan, who controlled the Heavenly Palace, strongly suggested freezing time for hundreds of thousands of years.

It was not because it had discovered Gu Wuxin’s anomaly, nor was it because it had foreseen the rise of human beings, but because it had foreseen a new possibility.

The super artificial intelligence created by the Pangu Civilization was indeed impressive. If it had not been divided into two halves, even Li Yao would have been no match for it.

Of course, it was also possible that Li Yao was thinking too much and that ‘Fuxi’ did not calculate any clear possibilities.

However, the birth of new life, new hope, and new and endless possibilities was always unstoppable, was it not?

Li Yao just didn’t understand one thing.

“Why are you helping me?”

Li Yao sent a feeble brainwave to Lu Qingchen. He did not think that he and Lu Qingchen were good friends who had gone through life and death together. It must be noted that the main purpose of the expedition to the primeval relics was to capture the inhuman ‘anti-human’ Lu Qingchen!

“What else could it be?”

Lu Qingchen asked back, “Am I going to mess around with you at such a critical moment, drag everybody down, and ruin the civilization of mankind?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. It did make sense.

“I’m hopeless anyway.”

There was obvious amusement in Lu Qingchen’s brainwaves. He did not seem to be ‘helpless’ at all. “Let’s say that you or I suddenly receive some kind of mysterious power and blow up the monster after we transform ourselves. Then, I will have to go back and face all the human experts, won’t I? I don’t think that I can compete with you, Ding Lingdang, Boss Bai, Long Yangjun, the Fist King, and everyone else after the bloody battle against the enemy.

“In the end, I was still captured and tied up by you like a dumpling back to the Star Glory Federation for trial.

“Like I said, I would rather die ten thousand times than be judged by pigs like you.

“Therefore, this is probably the best ending for me, isn’t it? Haha. Hahahaha. Hahahaha!”

Lu Qingchen suddenly burst into laughter uncontrollably, which was both ironic and arrogant.

“What are you laughing at?”

Li Yao said solemnly.

“I’m wondering how the history books and textbooks of the Star Glory Federation will evaluate me.

Lu Qingchen smiled and said, “It’s true that I’m a cruel, inhuman butcher. Billions of people have been killed by my schemes because of my telepathic thoughts. But I’ve also saved the federation and the human civilization multiple times at the cost of my own life. The final battle against the super artificial intelligence ‘Fuxi’ was the first, and now it’s the second. I really don’t know how to calculate the muddled debt. Some historians in the future will have a headache. Haha. Hahahaha.

“Hey, Li Yao, what kind of trial do you think history will give me?”

“…I don’t know.”

Li Yao was silent for a long time, but he still shook his head and sighed. “What can a beautiful woman do when she is a thief?”

“There is pleasure in being a thief. Don’t tell me you still don’t understand after crawling through so many ventilation tubes and toilets?”

Lu Qingchen paused for a moment. He suddenly lowered his voice and mumbled, “At the very least, I didn’t go against Grandpa’s last words. I will be a patriot until I die, right?”

Li Yao did not know what to say. He managed to say, “From a certain point of view, on a certain level, theoretically speaking, probably!”

“That’s good.”

Lu Qingchen smiled brightly. “Bring it on!”

“Come on!”

Li Yao roared.

“Hey hey hey hey, what the heck is this? You haven’t even asked for my opinion!”

The mental devil shrieked.

“The majority wins. Two to one.”

Li Yao roared, “Come on. Change your trajectory and push the monster toward the ancient star!”

Along with the roars that echoed in the universe, Li Yao’s body of flesh and blood was like a torch that was about to burn out. Supported by pure oxygen, it was hundreds of times brighter than before. The back of the giant soldier spurted out flames hundreds of meters, thousands of meters, or even hundreds of thousands of meters long, which cut through the darkness of the universe and accelerated its speed by ten times!

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Riding the ‘sun catastrophe’, Li Yao hugged Gu Wuxin and rushed toward the ancient star at such a high speed that even the pieces of meteoroids floating between the ancient star and the satellites crashed into the cosmic dust, leaving shocking craters on their bodies.

But that was not all. While he was rushing forward, Li Yao detonated his cells without caring about anything else and threw biochemical bombs at Gu Wuxin.

Gu Wuxin’s head was spinning because of the collision, and his head was dizzy because of the explosion.

He could clearly feel Li Yao’s determination to go all in, but it disrupted his rhythm of healing his wounds and condensing his combat ability.

However, when he accurately determined Li Yao’s intentions, he could not help but laugh.

“Idiot. Is this the ‘last strike’ that you came up with at the cost of your soul, your life, and everything else? Are you trying to knock me into the gravity ring of the ancient star and destroy me with the enormous gravity of the planet? Or at least suppress me? How idiotic!

Gu Wuxin scoffed. “It was truly wishful thinking. Based on the remaining strength of your life force and your soul power, as well as the remaining fuel of the giant soldier, it was impossible for you to push me into the inner layer of the ancient planet’s gravity.

“Besides, the astronomers in the Heavenly Palace have studied all the space zones and galaxies in the trajectories of the ancient relics hundreds of thousands of years ago. I know all the parameters of the planet like the back of my hand.

“You’ve underestimated the power of the super bodies. You think you can suppress me with the gravity of a planet? Impossible! Not to mention that I won’t fall into the planet at all. Even if I am captured by its gravity and fall into the super-dense atmosphere, I will never be trapped.

“As long as these insignificant injuries are repaired, neither the planet nor the stone belts will be able to trap me. By then, do you want me to swallow your soul and take you to the surface of the planet for a soul-stirring adventure? At that time, you will understand how strong the super bodies are and how stupid you are.

“No. No need. I believe you.”

Li Yao was laughing miserably and vomiting blood at the same time. His eyes were deeply hollowed, and his entire face had turned into a terrifying skull. The only thing that was still shining in his eyes was his stubbornness. “I lost. I’m no match for you. What a strong super body. I’m completely convinced now!”

“Huh?”

In the extremely cold vacuum, a layer of ice was frozen on Gu Wuxin’s face. His thoughts seemed to be frozen, too. “Are you planning to surrender on behalf of all your compatriots and join our super bodies?”

“You can talk about bowing to me later.

Li Yao smiled delightedly. “Before that, I have a small suggestion for you. If you have time, update the database of astronomical parameters inside the Heavenly Palace. Your survey of the ancient giants is outdated.

“What?”

Every scale on Gu Wuxin’s body constricted violently, reducing his size significantly.

He turned his head 180 degrees and faced the enormous ancient star. He saw an unbelievable scene that had never been recorded in the database of the Heavenly Palace hundreds of thousands of years ago.

On the surface of the ancient giants that were full of parallel stripes, there were almost a hundred equally enormous red spots, each of which was a cluster of cyclones and storms that could last hundreds of years or even thousands of years. Endless lightning was born, collided, grew, melted, and perished in them.

At this moment, hundreds of thick electric arcs darted out of the dozens of red spots at the same time. No. ‘electric arcs’ were not enough to describe their magnificence and horror. They were like tongues and tentacles of lightning that were tangled together and dancing crazily toward Gu Wuxin, Li Yao, and Lu Qingchen.

Against the background of the ancient star, the tongues and tentacles of the lightning seemed to be moving very slowly.

However, Gu Wuxin, Li Yao, and Lu Qingchen seemed to be frozen in the gravity field of the ancient star. They could only watch the claws and teeth of the lightning reaching for them.

“What is this?”

For the first time, Gu Wuxin and the thousands of ghosts inside his super body screamed uncontrollably at the same time.

Chapter 3148: : See You Later

“Honestly speaking, we don’t know, either.

Li Yao said, “It is possible that they are some kind of life-forms born in lightning—instantaneous, ever-changing, and tens of thousands of times shorter and more brilliant than the civilization of mankind. Although the longevity of every individual is only a few seconds or even a few microseconds, one to two thousand years is enough for them to cross the road of development of a carbon-based intelligent life of tens of thousands of years or even tens of millions of years. Is that so?”

“What?”

Intense confusion and fear were mixed in Gu Wuxin’s overlapping brainwaves.

At this distance, with his keen senses, he could already sense the ‘starving’ smell of the lightning creature.

“Of course, it is just my speculation. In fact, I don’t know what they are exactly. Maybe you and your super body can help me figure it out.

Li Yao couldn’t help but laugh. “Compared to the civilization of mankind or the super bodies, such a brand-new lightning life is so insignificant and short. However, who says that one person cannot defeat the entire universe? Who says that one second cannot be more brilliant than eternity? In short, enjoy your wonderful trip to the ancient giants!”

The ‘scales’ all over Gu Wuxin’s body turned into sharp thorns. He struggled and tore, trying to get rid of the ‘sun catastrophe’ and tear the giant soldier and Li Yao into pieces.

However, his body, which seemed to be made of iron and was not scared of vacuum, had been condensed by billions of cells in only several minutes. It was strong enough, but not tough enough. Although he seemed to be at ease when he took the full-strength strike of Li Yao and all the other human experts, it was impossible for him to not pay a price.

If one was in a sea of foam and had an endless supply of newborn cells to replenish them, naturally, even the most serious injuries could be mostly recovered in the blink of an eye.

However, the place was too far away from his ‘source of strength’. The spiritual link between him and the sea of foam had been jammed by Li Yao’s chaotic life force, too. There was no time for him to catch his breath. How could he free himself in such a hurry?

“Let go! Let go of me!”

Gu Wuxin roared miserably. More and more sharp blades crowded out of his body and pierced into the ‘sun catastrophe’, sabotaging the internal structure of the Giant God Soldier and threatening the spiritual residence of the user.

His arms, on the other hand, turned into two war hammers and smashed at the ‘sun catastrophe’ like a burning storm, crushing the strongest war machine of the human civilization into a pile of garbage.

“I’m sorry. I won’t let go.”

Despite the protection of the Spiritual Nexus, the liquid crystal processor, the crystal suit, and the mustard suit, all the bones inside Li Yao’s body that could be broken had been broken. Under the blast of the immense energy, his face turned from a ‘living skeleton’ into a swollen pig’s head again. However, his smile was brighter than ever, and the brilliance in his eyes was brighter, too. “Don’t you want to be melded with us? Then why are you yelling for me to let go? Come on. Let’s die together!”

“Why?”

Gu Wuxin went on a rampage and tore off the broken head of the ‘sun catastrophe’. However, at this moment, it did not matter how many crystal chips the Giant God Soldier had left. The high-energy magnetic field of the lightning-like life form was already so intense that it could be perceived without any magical equipment. Li Yao tried his best to tie Gu Wuxin up tighter and tighter until he reached a certain direction!

“Isn’t your super body invincible? Doesn’t it represent the ultimate answer of the universe? Then what are you scared of and why are you asking?”

Li Yao smiled miserably and said intermittently, “In fact, you had the opportunity to know why. But unfortunately, you’ve embarked on a completely different path. Even if I tell you what is supporting my battle, you will never understand. Never!”

“I don’t believe it. I don’t believe it.”

Gu Wuxin burst into fury like a crazy devil. “I haven’t been connected to a real super body yet. Enjoy the splendidness of the entire cosmos. I can’t fall here. I will not fall in this small pond at the feet of selfish, insignificant creatures like you!

“Every intelligent creature is their own universe. The so-called ‘cultivation’ is to explore and appreciate the brilliance of their own universe to your heart’s content. You don’t even know what you are exactly, and you are talking about the Universe Ocean?

Li Yao smiled. “Although the Pangu Universe is just a small pond, it is the home that you and I share. You were supposed to protect it, weren’t you?”

Gu Wuxin was dazed for a moment.

Li Yao could clearly see that there was annoyance and regret in the complicated expressions on his face.

Li Yao even sensed that, in the deepest part of the labyrinth, abyss, and cage named ‘Super Entities’, a few ghosts were kneeling on the ground with their hands extended to the sky, shouting in regret, “No!”

But in the next second, Gu Wuxin’s movements were even more intense and furious. He exerted his strength and tore apart the left arm of the ‘sun catastrophe’!

Thankfully, the ‘sun catastrophe’ had embedded countless broken blades and broken thorns into each other’s bodies. Their right arms and legs were tied up, too, which was why Gu Wuxin failed to get rid of them.

At this moment, they had rushed all the way to the inner layer of the ancient star’s gravitational field. They could clearly feel the gravity condensing in front of them.

Thousands of lightning tentacles were only one step away from them.

At the last moment.

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao turned on the self-detonation system of the Giant God Soldier calmly. A dangerous red light immediately appeared in his vision.

But right then, another force invaded the control menu of the Giant God Soldier and hijacked the self-destruction system.

It was Lu Qingchen.

“You…”

Li Yao was somewhat surprised.

“Let me do it.”

Lu Qingchen was still smiling casually. “One person is enough to detonate a Giant God Soldier. There is no need for more people to be buried together with the monster. Besides, it is the well-known ‘father of the nation’ of the Star Glory Federation. Although it is a bit redundant, the monster is not good enough.

“Lu Qingchen, what the hell are you thinking?”

Li Yao had to admit that he did not understand Lu Qingchen at all.

The last time he had the same feeling was when he saw Jin Tuyi.

“As I said a long time ago, although I can kill billions of people without batting an eyelid, I am still a sincere patriot. The only thing between you and me is the competition of the Great Dao. There is no personal grudge between us. At this moment, why should I cling to you and demand that you be buried together with me?

Lu Qingchen said, “Besides, you have a more important mission that belongs to none other than yourself, don’t you?”

The souls of the two quickly collided, producing thousands of dazzling sparks.

From the moment they first met to this moment, the scenes over the past hundred years were flashing in the souls of the two ‘old enemies’.

Li Yao understood what Lu Qingchen meant. He no longer resisted Lu Qingchen’s manipulation.

“All the melodrama that should be told and all the heroic words that should be roared seem to have been performed during the battle against the ‘Fuxi’. This time, let’s leave it at that. See you later, Vulture Li Yao.”

Lu Qingchen paused for a moment and said in a voice that only he could hear, “Goodbye… Uncle Li.”

Huala!

The cover on the top of the high-energy crystal reactor on the back of the Giant God Soldier was blown open, forming an emergency escape channel. Li Yao, who was wearing a crystal suit, was ejected under the protection of Little Black, the liquid crystal processor, and fled out of the gravity field of the ancient planet.

The Giant Soldier, which had lost its mainframe crystal processor, had lost more than 95% of its skills. With a casual tug, Gu Wuxin had torn it into pieces.

But the remaining torso and the high-energy crystal reactor could still complete the most ancient and simplest tactic of self-destruction.

In the dead silence of the sea of stars, a dazzling flower of energy and metal was blossoming.

With the legendary Supreme Emperor’s Giant God Soldier as the main body, the super Giant God Soldier, which was condensed from dozens of components of the Giant God Soldier, was exceptionally gorgeous when it self-detonated.

At this moment, even the ancient giants nearby were eclipsed. The entire universe was the background of the ‘sun catastrophe’. In the middle of the flowers of energy and metal, Li Yao seemed to see Lu Qingchen’s mysterious yet relieved smile.

“…Asshole.”

Li Yao’s eyes seemed to be burning, but there was no telling whether he was cursing Gu Wuxin or Lu Qingchen.

Gu Wuxin struggled hard in the swirl of self-destruction. This time, his body, which had been seriously damaged, was not so easy to repair. His limbs and torso were all messed up, and he looked like a broken clay doll.

Before he was able to fully repair and evolve into a body that was suitable for cruising in the sea of stars, the lightning creatures had already surrounded him from all directions. Countless tentacles of lightning turned into a bloody mouth and swallowed him.

“No!”

Gu Wuxin’s brainwaves turned into visible ripples and spread out in Li Yao’s direction, as if he hated Li Yao’s guts and was determined to drag him down with him.

However, Lu Qingchen had timed the self-detonation of the Giant God Soldier perfectly and pushed Li Yao further away with the force of the explosion. He had been pushed out of the gravity field of the ancient star and the hunting range of the lightning creature.

Maybe Li Yao had been contaminated by the scent of the lightning creature during his previous contact with it, or maybe the lightning creature had already acquired a fat prey such as Gu Wuxin’s super body and did not want to waste more time and energy on hunting Li Yao. In short, all the tentacles of lightning were trying their best to retract and drag Gu Wuxin into the big red spot.

Thousands of electric arcs lunged at Gu Wuxin and crashed into the spiritual shield that he had barely condensed. However, in the next second, ten times more electric arcs crowded close and shattered his spiritual shield, tore apart his dense cells, crawled into his body, and enveloped him.

Chapter 3149: Li Yao'S Death

Gu Wuxin struggled like a drowning man in a swamp.

In the torrent of super plasma, he lost control over his body. The body of a human being that used to be strong and beautiful suddenly turned into an octopus, an Armored Storm Dragon, and various bizarre-shaped apocalyptic beasts. In the end, countless lumps and black spots piled up on his body, making him look indescribably ugly.

The defense of all the cells had completely collapsed. The overly complicated gene pool had completely collapsed. Billions of incompatible genes were growing and consuming each other in the most intense way.

Gu Wuxin was dragged deeper and deeper into the red spot by the lightning creature. His body collapsed completely. With a flash of brightness, thousands of ghosts escaped from the mud-like body, trying to run away in the form of pure energy. But without the protection of the body, they were even less of a match for the lightning creature. Very soon, they were caught by the overwhelming plasma and dragged back into illusionary bubbles, never to be seen again.

Li Yao opened his arms and legs and floated in the boundless space, observing everything emotionlessly.

It was not until the last bit of Gu Wuxin’s brainwaves dispersed inside and outside of the gravity field of the ancient star that he was somewhat relieved.

At the same time.

Inside the primordial relic.

The moment Gu Wuxin was swallowed by the lightning, the overwhelming ocean of cells seemed to have received a mysterious command, too. It stopped wriggling, splitting, and multiplying in the blink of an eye. The black spots that had just dispersed reappeared. The large, brown, and gray cells all collapsed. Visible smoke surged out of the pits and flew into the sky, turning into colorful, splendid bubbles.

Pa! Pa! Pa!

Billions of bubbles exploded at the same time. The ocean of cells was obliterated instantly. Even the last traces of the city were gone, revealing the true appearance of the City of Silver.

The human experts were all dazed and speechless.

“Is it over?”

Holding her wound, Ding Lingdang breathed heavily. She struggled to her feet and stared at the gap leading to the sea of stars in the sky. “Li Yao, Li Yao, can you hear me?”

“No. It’s not over yet.”

Not far behind her, Long Yangjun was staring at District 47. “The Bifrost hasn’t collapsed. The teleportation is still ongoing. We cannot allow the tiniest bit of information from the Pangu Universe to be transmitted to the center of the cosmos.”

In the sea of stars.

Li Yao moved his eyes away from the ancient giants and the red spots. He turned around and stared at the Bifrost that was rushing out of the primeval relics.

The rainbow bridge made of pure energy was truly an endless bridge across the multiverse from the primeval relics to the end of the universe.

One could vaguely see that the tide-like warship that Gu Wuxin had prepared had been dragged into a stream of ‘matter’ hundreds of kilometers long. It was becoming more and more transparent, as if it had turned from hard metal into an illusionary chimney that could vanish into the three-dimensional universe at any moment.

There was no time.

“Cough cough, cough cough cough cough.”

Li Yao coughed softly and spat a mouthful of blood into his palm. Then he found that his palm was translucent, too, which was not a technique but a sign that his body was about to be burnt up due to over-burning of his life.

He could not wait any longer.

He hoped that his body could hold on for a while longer, even if it was burning for an hour, a minute, a second, an inch of light, or even a minute of heat.

While he was thinking, Li Yao activated his crystal suit and turned into a streak of brightness again, rushing forward and covering the distance of tens of thousands of kilometers in the blink of an eye before he crashed into the rainbow bridge brutally.

BOOM!

There was no sound in the vacuum, but Li Yao’s soul seemed to have been hit by a high-speed crystal train. He screamed and moaned at the same time.

The power unit on the left side of the crystal suit was completely wrecked. The helmet was riddled with shocking cracks that could shatter at any moment.

But the Bifrost remained still.

“Li Yao, Li Yao!”

Ding Lingdang’s sobbing voice echoed inside his brain. Maybe it was the remnant of the previous ‘hyperlink’, or maybe it was the telepathic link between her and Li Yao, but the former Speaker of Parliament’s female intuition sent out a rare warning. “What are you doing? Tell me, what are you doing?”

“…I’m intercepting the Bifrost.”

Li Yao flew back, leaving enough distance for his sprint. After a moment of silence, he said to Ding Lingdang, “Although Gu Wuxin and his super body are doomed, the teleportation is still ongoing. Even though it is an empty warship, a lot of information is still stored inside.

“Even we and the Pangu Civilization have grasped a certain level of self-driving technology. The Flood Tide warship will certainly be teleported to the center of the Universe Ocean without Gu Wuxin. As long as the coordinates of the Pangu Universe and its voyage log are stored in the mainframe crystal processor of the Flood Tide warship, we will be screwed.

“Therefore, even if I can’t stop the teleportation, I must at least destroy the Tide Berserker and erase all the information that has been sent out from the Pangu Universe.

“Basically… that’s it.”

BOOM!

Before he was able to finish his sentence, Li Yao crashed into the Bifrost for the second time in space. Other than breaking his crystal suit into even more pieces, it did not do any good.

“What—what are you going to do?”

Ding Lingdang lost her cool. “We tried to attack the Bifrost on the ground, too, but Gu Wuxin seemed to have set up powerful barriers and barriers all around the teleportation array. The damn space folds are involved. We can’t stop it. We can’t!”

“I know.”

Li Yao tried a third time, a fourth time, and a fifth time. He was like a fly that had been locked in a glass bottle and hit its head hard despite the fact that there was no way out. “This was Gu Wuxin’s last resort. Of course, he set up the most solid defense around the teleportation array. Therefore, attacking from the ground is useless. We have to attack the Flood Tide warship from space or even from inside it.

“Attack from the inside?”

Ding Lingdang shouted, “But it seems to have turned into a torrent of pure energy. Nothing can enter it!”

“Yes.”

Li Yao mumbled, “Honey, I’m sorry.”

BOOM!

The sixth time, which was also the fastest and most violent collision, Li Yao was like a meteorite that had been shattered in the atmosphere. His crystal suit, mustard suit, and even his body of flesh and blood were all burnt to the ground. He turned into a shining body of energy, a burning vitality, and stabbed his transparent hands into the Bifrost.

“AHHHHHHHHHHH!”

Li Yao roared crazily on the mental level. His last cell turned into nothingness in the intense collision of energy. He abandoned his crystal suit, burnt his body, and overdrafted all his vitality in order to squeeze into the Bifrost by force so that his soul would burst into the most brilliant fireworks!

“What are you doing?”

Ding Lingdang’s voice was as painful as her own heart being cut off. “Li Yao, don’t. Don’t!”

“I seem to have played ‘Soul Out of Body’ again, but this time, my body of flesh and blood is completely wasted.”

Li Yao smiled bitterly on the mental level. “I’m sorry, honey. I don’t have a choice. You are a Cultivator, too. You should know that there is only one way in such circumstances.

“You—you abandoned your own body and turned yourself into pure energy, forcing yourself into the Bifrost in such a way?”

Ding Lingdang was greatly shocked. “But you don’t have any weapons at all. How are you going to destroy the Flood Tide warship?”

“Yes.”

Li Yao’s soul was struggling to move forward on the Bifrost. He was silent for a long time before he said, “I still have one bomb. One last… powerful bomb.”

Ding Lingdang immediately understood what Li Yao meant.

“Soul explosion?”

The former Speaker of Parliament shrieked, “No, Li Yao. Let’s think of something. There must be a way. Don’t do it!”

“If we had three days and three nights, we might have been able to think of a safer solution. But right now, we might not even have three seconds. The teleportation has already begun. The Flood Tide warship might be teleported to the center of the Universe Ocean with fatal information at any moment. I have to erase all my own information before that happens.

Li Yao’s soul finally stopped the flood-like warship that had turned into a stream of information. There was a smile of relief deep inside his heart. “Honey, don’t be sad. Show us some of the confidence of the Red Fire Dragon King. We are the same kind of people. People like us never planned to live like turtles for hundreds of years. Death is just the beginning of a different journey for us. We’ve been prepared for that, haven’t we?”

“No, no, no!”

Ding Lingdang burst into tears and shouted, “I’m not ready yet. I won’t allow you to die. Li Yao, do you hear me? Come back. Come back now. We will die together!”

“I’m sorry, honey. I’ll listen to everything you say. But this time… I alone will be enough.”

Li Yao said, “You must survive and accept the challenges of the flood with a new mission.

“I won’t let you die!”

Ding Lingdang gnashed her teeth. “Do you hear me? I absolutely won’t let you die. If smashing the deepest level of hell can pull out your soul, then I will smash the deepest level of hell. If reversing time can bring you back to life, then I will find a way to reverse time and space. If I become the strongest expert in the four-dimension, five-dimension, six-seven-eight-nine-dimension, then I will be able to reconstruct your soul. Then, I will definitely blow up all the universes and all the dimensions. If I have to go to the center of the goddamn multiverse sea, the earth at the core of the flood, only then can I see your shifty eyes again, then I will definitely go to the earth. I will definitely complete your mission and destroy the earth!”

“… Getting to know you was the most interesting thing that happened to me in the Pangu Universe. Do you know that every second after we met, a beautiful picture is flashing inside my soul? I really want to stall for ten days and ten nights and talk to you for ten days and ten nights, but how can I finish what I want to say in ten days and ten nights?”

Li Yao mumbled, “I remember the first time I met you in the East Ocean of the Star Glory Federation and the first time I experienced the brilliance of the world of Cultivators. Everything felt like a long dream. I will definitely fight to the end for the dream that is incomparably real.

“Goodbye, Ding Lingdang.”

“Goodbye, friends.

“Goodbye, Star Glory Federation.

“Goodbye… Human civilization!”

BOOM!

The intense quakes in the sea of stars turned into earthshaking noises in the minds of every expert in the primeval relics.

Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, Fist King, Little Ming, Wen Wen, Li Jialing, Li Linghai, Yan Liren… Everybody raised their heads and saw a brilliant fire blossoming in the depths of the Bifrost. In the middle of the endless flames, a glittering vulture soared into the sky and disintegrated at the end of the sea of stars together with the Bifrost, vanishing into nothingness.

At this moment, Li Yao’s soul illuminated the entire Pangu Universe.

Chapter 3150: Future Meeting

Three years later.

Floating Spear City, Star Glory Federation, headquarters of the Glorious Sunlight Group.

“Welcome, distinguished guests. The Meeting of the Future is about to begin. Please take the VIP channel here. Thank you.”

A beautiful, slender female employee took the VIP card from Meng Jiang’s hand and swept it over her palm. The VIP card immediately blossomed with a translucent radiance, revealing the names of three people: Meng Jiang, his grandson Meng Xiaolang, and his granddaughter Meng Lichuan.

After confirming the identities of the three, the female staff’s smile became even more brilliant. She slightly bowed in greeting and invited the three to enter.

“Wow…”

Meng Xiaolang, Meng Jiang’s grandson, who had just been promoted to a level-2 advisor in the Kunlun Fleet of the federal army, opened his eyes wide and clicked his tongue. He could tell that the female worker in front of him was not a real person but an illusion of mystic rays.

This kind of illusionary and real 3D light beam technology was not unusual in the first place. However, although it was just a portrait condensed out of mystic rays, it could scan the real VIP card. He could even vaguely smell the faint scent of camellias on the female staff’s body. When he retrieved the VIP card, the “skin” of the two parties passed off as real, which involved a lot of force field control and anti-gravity technology. It was quite advanced.

Meng Xiaolang knew that the Kunlun Fleet he was in was indeed doing the relevant research, but he did not expect that it would be dedicated to civil affairs so soon. Over the past three years, the technology of the Star Glory Federation, no, the entire civilization of mankind, had advanced by leaps and bounds!

“Sister, what is this so-called ‘Future Meeting’ trying to do? It is indeed impressive!”

Looking at the headquarters of the Glorious Sunlight Group, which was made of countless floating mountains and looked like a futuristic city, Meng Xiaolang sighed again.

No wonder he was so curious about the ‘Future Meeting’.

He was the second-level advisor of the Kunlun Fleet, and the Kunlun Fleet was one level higher than the Burning Prairie Fleet. It was one of the best fleets in the Star Glory Federation.

His sister ‘Meng Lichuan’ was a professional player under the name of ‘Team Moon Fiend’. She had just entered the top 10 in the ‘Virtual Information Contest’ where billions of professional players were competing against each other. She boasted amazing computational and deduction abilities and was the best strategical analyst.

Besides, Team Moon Fiend was backed by the Dark Moon Foundation and the investment of the Glorious Sunlight Group. They were both Meng Lichuan’s ‘owners’.

Even so, Meng Xiaolang and Meng Lichuan were not qualified to accept the invitation of the ‘Future Meeting’. They had only learned from the network and the news media that such an unprecedentedly grand meeting was about to be held and that it would answer all the questions that had been bothering them for three years. However, they were fortunate enough to be present in person because of their grandfather ‘Meng Jiang’. Meng Jiang was the best friend of ‘Vulture Li Yao’, the founder of the Glorious Sunlight Group and a super hero of the federation. That was why he was given a VIP card that was worth a fortune.

“According to the intelligence that I gathered from Team Moon Fiend, the so-called ‘Future Meeting’ should be like the ‘Magical Equipment Exposition’ of the federation, where the latest training and research results are displayed and the future direction of development is illuminated, including testing the latest models of magical equipment.

Meng Lichuan thought for a moment and said, “However, the Meeting of the Future is definitely not as simple as an ordinary Magical Equipment Exposition. It is said that several press conferences will be held in the Meeting of the Future. The experts from the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance, and even… the primeval experts, will show up in public and reveal the truth of the primeval relics and the secrets of the ‘Battle of Fallen Beings’ three years ago. They will also announce earthshaking news that might change the entire civilization of mankind.

“Ah…”

Meng Xiaolang stuck out his tongue. “So, the so-called ‘ancient relics’, ‘paradise’, and ‘pioneer civilization’, are all real?”

Although nobody knew what had happened in the depths of the sea of stars three years ago, all kinds of gossip had spread out through various channels. As a member of the Kunlun Fleet, Meng Xiaolang had participated in some highly confidential missions, too. Naturally, he had heard some half-truths.

“Battle of the Fallen…”

Listening to the conversation between his grandson and his granddaughter, Meng Jiang’s wrinkled face was solemn. “Li Yao perished in that battle!”

Meng Xiaolang and Meng Lichuan looked at each other, not knowing what to say.

When the three of them passed through the VIP channel, they saw that it was packed with people and reporters.

Not only were there local reporters of the Star Glory Federation, there were also reporters from various Sectors of the Imperium of True Human Beings. Some of them were even from the Covenant Alliance. Of course, the social system of the Covenant Alliance was still being rebuilt, and there was no such thing as a ‘reporter’. But a lot of the ‘watchers’ were acting as the eyes of the Covenant Alliance and carrying out the same duties as the reporters.

Not only were the channels on the ground packed full of people, the channels in the air were also packed with all kinds of bizarre small-scale flying machines. Now and then, interference and collisions would take place, and the flying machines would fall down with squeaking noises. In the sky even higher, countless colorful mystic rays were interweaving and crashing into each other, creating ripples that were as brilliant as the aurora. It was a unique scene that could only be produced when countless remote scanning magical equipment were activated at the same time to capture the scene of the convention from various angles.

Meng Xiaolang and Meng Lichuan had both seen big events before. Whether it was the official establishment of the Kunlun Fleet or the championship of the ‘Virtual Information Competition’, they had both seen the surging tide of reporters.

However, compared to the unprecedented scale of the battle today, the ‘tide’ of reporters that they had seen before were merely the trickling streams in the mountains.

“Why are there so many reporters?”

Meng Xiaolang was dumbfounded.

Then, they realized that their question was too silly. The reporters had every reason to swarm over.

Over the past three years, more changes had taken place in the Star Glory Federation and even the entire human civilization.

The source of all the changes, the mysterious ancient relics, was still lurking in the darkness. Nobody knew the truth.

Even Ding Lingdang, Boss Bai, Yan Liren, Long Yangjun, and the other top experts of the human civilization had vanished from the public eye and hidden themselves in the ancient relics.

As far as the public of the Star Glory Federation and most of the soldiers at the grassroots level were concerned, all they knew was that, three years ago, the federal army launched a last-ditch, desperate “Billions-of-Stars Expedition” and achieved a brilliant, miraculous victory, forcing the two superpowers in the center of the sea of stars, the Imperium of True Human Beings and the Covenant Alliance, to sign a peace treaty.

But the war was not over yet. Countless experts and elites, including the super hero Li Yao, marched into the primeval relics at the end of the sea of stars alone to explore the origin of life billions of years ago and fight the formidable enemies that were hundreds of times more terrifying than the Immortal Cultivators and the people of the Covenant Alliance.

They had won the difficult battle, but they had paid a high price, too.

Including ‘Vulture Li Yao’, dozens of Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators and Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators had perished.

Even the ‘Giant God Soldiers’, known as the strongest ultimate weapons in the sea of stars, had lost dozens of them. The top warriors of the human civilization had suffered heavy losses.

Therefore, everybody called the battle ‘the battle of the fallen’.

The deceased had passed away, but those who survived still had to shoulder the heavy responsibility and continue on the road of thorns and flames step by step.

Maybe the fire of life ignited by the victims illuminated the direction of the survivors.

Maybe it was because the new civilization of mankind was far from running out of luck.

The three years after the ‘Battle of Fallen Stars’ were three years of rapid improvement.

Both Meng Xiaolang and Meng Lichuan had experienced the latest research results from the ancient relics in person through their own channels. What kind of brilliance could they bring out?

It was said that the so-called primeval relics were the treasures left by the ancestors of all the intelligent carbon-based creatures billions of years ago. Before Li Yao perished, he had already opened a gap in the treasures with his courage and wisdom.

It was said that the remains, last words, and precious information of almost a hundred civilizations of the pioneers were lying in the depths of the primeval relics, waiting to be explored, studied, and absorbed.

It was said that a batch of intelligent creatures in the primeval era, namely the legendary Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan, had miraculously survived. Although they were individually stronger than human beings, they had lost the foundation of their civilization and could not compete with human beings. Therefore, they could only choose to cooperate with human beings in exchange for the trust of human beings at the cost of the primeval technology.

After assimilating the technology of the Pangu Civilization, human beings were now able to build their own production line of the ‘Giant Gods’. They no longer had to excavate the relics of the primeval era and make do with scrap iron. Very soon, the army of the Giant Gods, which had suffered a great loss in the ‘Battle of Fallen Stars’, would be ten times stronger than before.

It was said that, with the wisdom of the top biochemical experts of the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance, and the guidance of the prehistoric civilization and the pioneers, the civilization of mankind had made great progress in the field of gene reproduction. One day, it was even possible that a living ‘Pioneer’ could be duplicated with the scraps of the dead bodies left by the pioneers and open a brand-new door of ‘infinite possibilities’ from different directions of evolution.

It was said that the space jump technology employed by the civilization of mankind had an endless direction of improvement, too. Maybe in the near future, the energy consumption and damage suffered by the space jumps would be minimized, making it possible to perform multiple short-distance space jumps within a short period of time. At that time, a starship would be able to perform multiple space jumps in the battlefield millions of kilometers around within several seconds, like an unpredictable, ever-changing ghost. Such a ‘Phantom Fleet’ would be the invincible ‘Sword of Human Beings’.

It was said that many rumors were so exaggerated that even the most imaginative people found it hard to believe. Which of the rumors would become reality in the meeting of the future?
